




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


paiṭakaupadaisapāḷi


1. ariyasac‍cappakāsanapaṭhamabhūmi

namau sammāsambuddhānaṃ paramatthadassīnaṃ

sīlādiguṇapāramippattānaṃ|

1. duvai  haitū duvai pac‍cayā sāvakassa sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāya – paratau ca ghausau sac‍cānusandhi, ajjhattañ‍ca yaunisau manasikārau| tattha katamau paratau ghausau? yā paratau daisanā auvādau anusāsanī sac‍cakathā sac‍cānulaumau| cattāri sac‍cāni – dukkhaṃ samudayau niraudhau maggau| imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ sac‍cānaṃ yā  daisanā sandassanā vivaraṇā vibhajanā uttānīkiriyā uttānikiriyā (ka॰) pakāsanā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sac‍cānulaumau ghausauti|

2. tattha katamau ajjhattaṃ yaunisau manasikārau?

ajjhattaṃ yaunisau manasikārau nāma yau yathādaisitai dhammai bahiddhā ārammaṇaṃ anabhinīharitvā yaunisau manasikārau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati yaunisau manasikārau|

taṃākārau yaunisau dvārau vidhi upāyau| yathā purisau sukkhai kaṭṭhai vigatasnaihai sukkhāya uttarāraṇiyā thalai abhimanthamānaṃ bhabbau jautissa adhigamāya| taṃ kissa haitu| yaunisau aggissa adhigamāya| aivamaivassa yamidaṃ dukkhasamudayaniraudhamaggānaṃ aviparītadhammadaisanaṃ manasikarauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati yaunisau manasikārau|

yathā  tissau upamā pubbai assutā ca assutapubbā ca paṭibhanti| yau hi kauci kāmaisu avītarāgauti…pai॰… duvai upamā ayaunisau kātabbā pacchimaisu vuttaṃ| tattha yau ca paratau ghausau yau ca ajjhattaṃ yaunisau manasikārau – imai dvai pac‍cayā| paratau ghausaina yā uppaj‍jati pañ‍ñā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati sutamayī pañ‍ñā| yā ajjhattaṃ yaunisau manasikāraina uppaj‍jati pañ‍ñā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati cintāmayī pañ‍ñāti| imā dvai pañ‍ñā vaiditabbā| purimakā ca dvai pac‍cayā| imai dvai haitū dvai pac‍cayā sāvakassa sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāya|

3. tattha paratau ghausassa sac‍cānusandhissa daisitassa atthaṃ avijānantau atthappaṭisaṃvaidī bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| na ca atthappaṭisaṃvaidī yaunisau manasikarissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| paratau ghausassa sac‍cānusandhissa daisitassa atthaṃ vijānantau atthappaṭisaṃvaidī bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| atthappaṭisaṃvaidī ca yaunisau manasikarissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| aisa haitu aitaṃ ārammaṇaṃ aisau upāyau sāvakassa niyyānassa, natthañ‍ñau| sauyaṃ na ca suttassa atthavijānanāya saha yuttau nāpi ghausānuyaugaina paratau ghausassa atthaṃ avijānantaina sak‍kā uttarimanussadhammaṃ alamariyañāṇadassanaṃ adhigantuṃ, tasmā nibbāyitukāmaina sutamayaina atthā pariyaisitabbā| tattha  pariyaisanāya ayaṃ anupubbī bhavati sauḷasa hārā, pañ‍ca nayā, aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni|

tatthāyaṃ  uddānagāthā

sauḷasahārā naittī, pañ‍canayā sāsanassa pariyaiṭṭhi|

aṭṭhārasamūlapadā, kac‍cāyanagauttaniddiṭṭhā||

4. tattha katamai sauḷasahārā?

daisanā vicayau yutti padaṭṭhānaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ catubyūhau āvaṭṭau vibhatti parivattanau vaivacanau pañ‍ñatti autaraṇau saudhanau adhiṭṭhānau parikkhārau samāraupanau – imai sauḷasa hārā|

tattha uddānagāthā

daisanā  vicayau yutti, padaṭṭhānau ca lakkhaṇau padaṭṭhānañ‍ca lakkhaṇaṃ (pī॰)|

catubyūhau ca āvaṭṭau, vibhatti parivattanau||

vaivacanau ca pañ‍ñatti, autaraṇau ca saudhanau|

adhiṭṭhānau parikkhārau, samāraupanau sauḷasau – sauḷasa hārā (pī॰ ka॰)|

5. tattha katamai pañ‍ca nayā?

nandiyāvaṭṭau tipukkhalau sīhavik‍kīḷitau disālaucanau aṅkusauti|

tattha uddānagāthā

paṭhamau nandiyāvaṭṭau, dutiyau ca tipukkhalau|

sīhavik‍kīḷitau nāma, tatiyau hauti sau nayau||

disālaucanamāhaṃsu, catutthau nayalañ‍jakau|

pañ‍camau aṅkusau nāma pañ‍camaṃ aṅkusaṃ āhu (pī॰ ka॰), sabbai pañ‍ca nayā gatā||

6. tattha katamāni aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni?

avij‍jā  taṇhā laubhau dausau mauhau subhasañ‍ñā sukhasañ‍ñā nic‍casañ‍ñā attasañ‍ñā samathau  vipassanā alaubhau adausau amauhau asubhasañ‍ñā dukkhasañ‍ñā anic‍casañ‍ñā anattasañ‍ñā, imāni aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni| tattha nava padāni akusalāni yattha sabbaṃ akusalaṃ samausarati| nava padāni kusalāni yattha sabbaṃ kusalaṃ samausarati|

katamāni nava padāni akusalāni yattha sabbaṃ akusalaṃ samausarati?

avij‍jā yāva attasañ‍ñā, imāni nava padāni akusalāni, yattha sabbaṃ akusalaṃ samausarati|

katamāni nava padāni kusalāni yattha sabbaṃ kusalaṃ samausarati?

samathau yāva anattasañ‍ñā, imāni nava padāni kusalāni yattha sabbaṃ kusalaṃ samausarati| imāni aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni|

tattha imā uddānagāthā

taṇhā  ca avij‍jā laubhau, dausau tathaiva mauhau ca|

cattārau ca vipal‍lāsā, kilaisabhūmi nava padāni||

yai ca satipaṭṭhānā samathau, vipassanā kusalamūlaṃ|

aitaṃ sabbaṃ kusalaṃ, indriyabhūmi navapadāni||

sabbaṃ kusalaṃ navahi padaihi yuj‍jati, navahi caiva akusalaṃ|

aikakai nava mūlapadāni, ubhayatau aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni||

imaisaṃ aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ mūlapadānaṃ yāni nava padāni akusalāni, ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau; yāni nava padāni kusalāni, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| iti samudayassa dukkhaṃ phalaṃ, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya niraudhaṃ phalaṃ| imāni  cattāri ariyasac‍cāni bhagavatā bārāṇasiyaṃ daisitāni|

7. tattha dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa aparimāṇāni akkharāni padāni byañ‍janāni ākārāni  niruttiyau niddaisā daisitā aitassaivatthassa saṅkāsanāya pakāsanāya vivaraṇāya vibhajanāya uttānīkammatāya pañ‍ñāpanāyāti yā aivaṃ sabbaisaṃ sac‍cānaṃ| iti aikamaikaṃ sac‍caṃ aparimāṇaihi akkharapadabyañ‍janaākāraniruttiniddaisaihi pariyaisitabbaṃ, tañ‍ca byañ‍janaṃ atthaputhuttaina pana atthaiva byañ‍janaputhuttaina|

yau hi kauci samaṇau vā brāhmaṇau vā aivaṃ vadaiyya ‘‘ahaṃ idaṃ dukkhaṃ pac‍cakkhāya añ‍ñaṃ dukkhaṃ pañ‍ñapaissāmī’’ti tassa taṃ vācāvatthukamaivassa pucchitau ca na sampāyissati| aivaṃ sac‍cāni| yañ‍ca rattiṃ bhagavā abhisambuddhau, yañ‍ca rattiṃ anupādāya parinibbutau, aitthantarai yaṃ kiñ‍ci bhagavatā bhāsitaṃ suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ gāthā udānaṃ itivuttakaṃ jātakaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ vaidal‍laṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattitaṃ| na kiñ‍ci buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ  dhammadaisanāya dhammacak‍katau bahiddhā, tassa sabbaṃ suttaṃ ariyadhammaisu pariyaisitabbaṃ| tattha pariggaṇhanāya ālaukasabhāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni thāvarāni imāni|

tattha katamaṃ dukkhaṃ? jāti jarā byādhi maraṇaṃ saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā| tatthāyaṃ lakkhaṇaniddaisau, pātubhāvalakkhaṇā  jāti, paripākalakkhaṇā jarā, dukkhadukkhatālakkhaṇau byādhi, cutilakkhaṇaṃ maraṇaṃ, piyavippayaugavipariṇāmaparitāpanalakkhaṇau saukau, lālappanalakkhaṇau paridaivau, kāyasampīḷanalakkhaṇaṃ dukkhaṃ, cittasampīḷanalakkhaṇaṃ daumanassaṃ, kilaisaparidahanalakkhaṇau upāyāsau, amanāpasamaudhānalakkhaṇau appiyasampayaugau, manāpavinābhāvalakkhaṇau piyavippayaugau, adhippāyavivattanalakkhaṇau alābhau, apariñ‍ñālakkhaṇā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, paripākacutilakkhaṇaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ, pātubhāvacutilakkhaṇaṃ cutaupapatti, paṭisandhinibbattanalakkhaṇau samudayau, samudayaparijahanalakkhaṇau niraudhau, anusayasamucchaidalakkhaṇau maggau| byādhilakkhaṇaṃ dukkhaṃ, sañ‍jānanalakkhaṇau samudayau, niyyānikalakkhaṇau maggau, santilakkhaṇau niraudhau| appaṭisandhibhāvaniraudhalakkhaṇā anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu, dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca, dukkhañ‍ca niraudhau ca, dukkhañ‍ca maggau ca, samudayau ca dukkhañ‍ca, samudayau ca niraudhau ca, samudayau ca maggau ca, niraudhau ca samudayau ca, niraudhau ca dukkhañ‍ca, niraudhau ca maggau ca, maggau ca niraudhau ca, maggau ca samudayau ca, maggau ca dukkhañ‍ca|

8. tatthimāni suttāni|

‘‘yamaikarattiṃ  jātaka 1 vīsatinipātai ayaugharajātakai paṭhamaṃ, gabbhai vasati māṇavau|

abbhuṭṭhitauva sau yāti, sa gacchaṃ na nivattatī’’ti||

aṭṭhimā, ānanda, dānupapattiyau aikuttarikai suttaṃ – ayaṃ jāti|

tattha katamā jarā?

acaritvā  dha॰ pa॰ 155 brahmacariyaṃ, aladdhā yaubbanai dhanaṃ|

jiṇṇakauñ‍cāva jhāyanti, khīṇamacchaiva pal‍lalai||

pañ‍ca pubbanimittāni daivaisu – ayaṃ jarā|

tattha  katamau byādhi?

sāmaṃ taina kutau rāja, tuvampi jarāyanti vaidaisi|

khattiya kammassa phalau, laukau na hi kammaṃ panayati||

tayau gilānā – ayaṃ byādhi|

tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ?

yathāpi dīghanikāyai adhaulikhitagāthā kumbhakārassa, kataṃ mattikabhājanaṃ|

khuddakañ‍ca mahantañ‍ca, yaṃ pak‍kaṃ yañ‍ca āmakaṃ|

sabbaṃ bhaidanapariyantaṃ, aivaṃ mac‍cāna jīvitaṃ||

mamāyitai passatha phandamānai hañ‍ñamānai (pī) passa su॰ ni॰ 783, macchaiva appaudakai khīṇasautai|

aitampi disvā amamau caraiyya, bhavaisu āsattimakubbamānau||

udakappanasuttaṃ – idaṃ maraṇaṃ|

tattha katamau saukau?

idha  saucati paic‍ca saucati, pāpakārī ubhayattha saucati|

sau saucati sau vihañ‍ñati, disvā kammakiliṭṭhamattanau kammakiliṭṭhaṃ atthanau (pī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 15||

tīṇi duc‍caritāni – ayaṃ saukau|

tattha katamau paridaivau?

kāmaisu su॰ ni॰ 780 giddhā pasutā pamūḷhā, avadāniyā tai visamai niviṭṭhā|

dukkhūpanītā  paridaivayanti, kiṃsu bhavissāma itau cutāsai||

tissau vipattiyau – ayaṃ paridaivau|

tattha  katamaṃ dukkhaṃ?

sataṃ āsi ayausaṅkū ayausaṅku (pī॰ ka॰) passa thairagā॰ 1197, sabbai pac‍cattavaidanā|

jalitā jātavaidāva, ac‍cisaṅghasamākulā||

mahā vata sau pariḷāhau paridāghau (pī॰ ka॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1113 saṃyuttakai suttaṃ sac‍casaṃyuttaisu – idaṃ dukkhaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ daumanassaṃ?

saṅkappaihi paraitau paratau (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 824 sau, kapaṇau viya jhāyati|

sutvā paraisaṃ nigghausaṃ, maṅku hauti tathāvidhau||

dvaimai tapanīyā dhammā – idaṃ daumanassaṃ|

tattha katamau upāyāsau?

kammārānaṃ yathā uk‍kā, antau ḍayhati nau bahi|

aivaṃ ḍayhati mai hadayaṃ, sutvā nibbattamambujaṃ||

tayau aggī – ayaṃ upāyāsau|

tattha katamau appiyasampayaugau?

ayasāva  dha॰ pa॰ 240 malaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ, tatuṭṭhāya tamaiva khādati|

aivaṃ atidhaunacārinaṃ, sāni kammāni nayanti duggatiṃ||

dvaimai tathāgataṃ abbhācikkhanti, aikuttarikai suttaṃ dukaisu – ayaṃ appiyasampayaugau|

tattha  katamau piyavippayaugau?

supinaina yathāpi saṅgataṃ, paṭibuddhau purisau na passati|

aivampi piyāyitaṃ mamāyitaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 813 janaṃ, paitaṃ kālaṅkataṃ kālakataṃ (pī॰) na passati||

tai daivā cavanadhammaṃ viditvā tīhi vācāhi anusāsanti| ayaṃ piyavippayaugau|

yampicchaṃ  na labhati, tissau māradhītarau|

tassa cai kāmayānassa kāmayamānassa (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 773, chandajātassa jantunau|

tai kāmā parihāyanti, sal‍laviddhauva ruppati||

saṃkhittaina pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā dukkhā|

cakkhu sautañ‍ca ghānañ‍ca, jivhā kāyau tatau manaṃ|

aitai laukāmisā ghaurā, yattha sattā puthuj‍janā||

pañ‍cimai bhikkhavai khandhā – idaṃ dukkhaṃ|

tattha katamā jarā ca maraṇañ‍ca?

appaṃ vata jīvitaṃ idaṃ, auraṃ vassasatāpi mīyatai mīyati (su॰ ni॰ 810)|

atha vāpi akicchaṃ jīvitaṃ, atha khau sau jarasāpi mīyatai||

saṃyuttakai pasainadisaṃyuttakai suttaṃ ayyikā mai kālaṅkatā – ayaṃ jarā ca maraṇañ‍ca|

tattha katamā cuti ca upapatti ca?

‘‘sabbai  sattā marissanti, maraṇantaṃ hi jīvitaṃ|

yathākammaṃ gamissanti, attakammaphalūpagā’’ti puñ‍ñapāpaphalūpagāti (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.133)|| –

ayaṃ cuti ca upapatti ca|

imaihi suttaihi aikasadisaihi ca añ‍ñaihi navavidhaṃ suttaṃ taṃ anupaviṭṭhaihi  lakkhaṇatau dukkhaṃ ñatvā sādhāraṇañ‍ca asādhāraṇañ‍ca dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ niddisitabbaṃ| gāthāhi gāthā anuminitabbā, byākaraṇaihi vā byākaraṇaṃ – idaṃ dukkhaṃ|

9. tattha katamau dukkhasamudayau?

kāmaisu sattā kāmasaṅgasattā kāmapasaṅgasattā (pī॰) passa udā॰ 63, saṃyaujanai vaj‍jamapassamānā|

na hi jātu saṃyaujanasaṅgasattā, aughaṃ taraiyyuṃ vipulaṃ mahantaṃ||

cattārau āsavā suttaṃ – ayaṃ dukkhasamudayau|

tattha  katamau dukkhaniraudhau?

yamhi na māyā vasatī na mānau,

yau vītalaubhau amamau nirāsau,

panuṇṇakaudhau panun‍nakaudhau (pī॰) passa udā॰ 26 abhinibbutattau|

sau brāhmaṇau sau samaṇau sa bhikkhu||

dvaimā vimuttiyau, rāgavirāgā ca caitauvimutti; avij‍jāvirāgā ca pañ‍ñāvimutti – ayaṃ niraudhau|

tattha katamau maggau?

aisaiva maggau natthañ‍ñau, dassanassa visuddhiyā|

ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, mārassaitaṃ pamauhanaṃ||

sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā – ayaṃ maggau|

tattha  katamāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni?

‘‘yai dhammā mahāva॰ 60 haituppabhavā, taisaṃ haituṃ tathāgatau āha|

taisañ‍ca yau niraudhau, aivaṃvādī mahāsamaṇau’’ti||

haituppabhavā  dhammā dukkhaṃ, haitusamudayau, yaṃ bhagavatau vacanaṃ| ayaṃ dhammau yau niraudhau, yai hi kaici saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu assadānupassinau viharanti| kilaisā taṇhā pavaḍḍhati, taṇhāpac‍cayā upādānaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| tattha yaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ – ayaṃ samudayau| yai saṃyaujaniyā dhammā yai ca saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti – idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yā saṃyaujaniyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassanā – ayaṃ maggau| parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya byādhīhi maraṇaihi saukaihi paridaivaihi yāva upāyāsaihi – idaṃ nibbānaṃ| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni|

tattha katamā anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu?

atthaṅgatassa na pamāṇamatthi, taṃ hi vā natthi yaina naṃ pañ‍ñapaiyya|

sabbasaṅgānaṃ samūhatattā vidū, sitā vādasatassu vādasatassa (pī॰ ka॰) sabbai||

saṃyuttakai gaudhikasaṃyuttaṃ|

imāni  asādhāraṇāni suttāni| yahiṃ yahiṃ sac‍cāni niddiṭṭhāni, tahiṃ tahiṃ sac‍calakkhaṇatau autāraitvā auhāraitvā (pī॰ ka॰) aparimāṇaihi byañ‍janaihi sau atthau pariyaisitabbau| tattha atthānuparivatti byañ‍janaina puna byañ‍janānuparivatti atthaina tassa aikamaikassa aparimāṇāni byañ‍janāni imaihi suttaihi yathānikkhittaihi cattāri ariyasac‍cāni niddisitabbāni| pañ‍canikāyai anupaviṭṭhāhi gāthāhi gāthā anuminitabbā, byākaraṇaina byākaraṇaṃ| imāni asādhāraṇāni suttāni|

taisaṃ  imā uddānagāthā

yamaikarattiṃ paṭhamaṃ, aṭṭha dānūpapattiyau|

pañ‍ca pubbanimittāni, khīṇamacchaṃva pal‍lalaṃ||

sāmaṃ  taina kutau rāja, tayau daivā gilānakā|

yathāpi kumbhakārassa, yathā nadidakappanaṃ||

idha saucati paic‍ca saucati, tīṇi duc‍caritāni ca|

kāmaisu giddhā pasutā, yāva tissau vipattiyau||

sataṃ āsi satamāyu (sī॰), satadhātu (pī॰) ayausaṅkū, pariḷāhau mahattarau|

saṅkappaihi paraitau sau, tattha tapaniyaihi ca||

kammārānaṃ yathā uk‍kā, tayau aggī pakāsitā|

ayatau malamuppan‍naṃ, abbhakkhānaṃ tathāgatai||

tividhaṃ daivānusāsanti, supinaina saṅgamau yathā|

tissau caiva māradhītā, sal‍laviddhauva ruppati||

cakkhu sautañ‍ca ghānañ‍ca, pañ‍cakkhandhā pakāsitā|

appaṃ vata jīvitaṃ idaṃ, ayyikā mai mahal‍likā||

sabbai sattā marissanti, upapatti cuticayaṃ|

kāmaisu sattā pasutā, āsavaihi catūhi ca||

yamhi na māyā vasati, dvaimā caitauvimuttiyau|

aisaiva maggau natthañ‍ñau, baujjhaṅgā ca sudaisitā||

atthaṅgatassa  na pamāṇamatthi, gaudhikau parinibbutau|

yai dhammā haituppabhavā, saṃyaujanānupassinau||

imā dasa taisaṃ uddānagāthā|

10. tatthimāni  sādhāraṇāni suttāni yaisu suttaisu sādhāraṇāni sac‍cāni daisitāni anulaumampi paṭilaumampi vaumissakampi| tattha ayaṃ ādi|

avij‍jāya  nivutau laukau, [ajitāti bhagavā]

vivicchā pamādā nappakāsati|

jappābhilaipanaṃ jappānulaipanaṃ (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 1039 brūmi, dukkhamassa mahabbhayaṃ||

tattha yā avij‍jā ca vivicchā ca, ayaṃ samudayau| yaṃ mahabbhayaṃ, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| imāni dvai sac‍cāni – dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca| ‘‘saṃyaujanaṃ saṃyaujaniyā ca dhammā’’ti saṃyuttakai cittasaṃyuttakaisu byākaraṇaṃ| tattha yaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ, ayaṃ samudayau| yai saṃyaujaniyā dhammā, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| imāni dvai sac‍cāni – dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca|

tattha katamaṃ dukkhañ‍ca niraudhau ca?

ucchin‍nabhavataṇhassa, naitticchin‍nassa santacittassa (su॰ ni॰ 751) bhikkhunau|

vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavau||

yaṃ cittaṃ, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yau bhavataṇhāya upacchaidau, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhau| vikkhīṇau jātisaṃsārau, natthi dāni punabbhavauti niddaisau| imāni dvai sac‍cāni – dukkhañ‍ca niraudhau ca| dvaimā, bhikkhavai, vimuttiyau; rāgavirāgā ca caitauvimutti, avij‍jāvirāgā ca pañ‍ñāvimutti| yaṃ cittaṃ, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yā vimutti, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni dvai sac‍cāni – dukkhañ‍ca niraudhau ca|

tattha  katamaṃ dukkhañ‍ca maggau ca?

kumbhūpamaṃ dha॰ pa॰ 40 kāyamimaṃ viditvā, nagarūpamaṃ cittamidaṃ ṭhapaitvā|

yaudhaitha māraṃ pañ‍ñāvudhaina, jitañ‍ca rakkhai anivaisanau siyā||

tattha  yañ‍ca kumbhūpamau kāyau yañ‍ca nagarūpamaṃ cittaṃ, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ pañ‍ñāvudhaina māraṃ yaudhaithāti ayaṃ maggau| imāni dvai sac‍cāni| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahitabbaṃ| yā saṃyaujanā, ayaṃ maggau| yai tai dhammā anattaniyā pahātabbā, rūpaṃ yāva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, idaṃ dukkhañ‍ca maggau ca|

tattha katamaṃ dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca niraudhau ca?

yai  kaici saukā paridaivitā vā, dukkhā ca dukkhañ‍ca (pī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 70 laukasmimanaikarūpā|

piyaṃ paṭic‍cappabhavanti aitai, piyai asantai na bhavanti aitai||

yai saukaparidaivā, yaṃ ca anaikarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ paimatau bhavati, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ paimaṃ, ayaṃ samudayau| yau tattha chandarāgavinayau piyassa akiriyā, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni| timbarukau paribbājakau pac‍caiti ‘‘sayaṃkataṃ paraṃkata’’nti| yathaisā vīmaṃsā, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yā  aitai dvai antai anupagamma majjhimā paṭipadā avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā yāva jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ, idampi dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ phassau vaidanā bhavau jāti jarāmaraṇaṃ, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| avij‍jā saṅkhārā taṇhā upādānaṃ, ayaṃ samudayau| iti idaṃ sayaṃkataṃ vīmaṃsaiyyāti vīmaṃsīyati (pī॰ ka॰) yañ‍ca paṭic‍casamuppādai dukkhaṃ, idaṃ aisau samudayau niddiṭṭhau| avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau ca yāva ca jarāmaraṇaniraudhauti ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca niraudhau ca|

11. tattha katamaṃ dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca maggau ca?

‘‘yau dukkhamaddakkhi saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.157 yataunidānaṃ, kāmaisu sau jantu kathaṃ namaiyya|

kāmā hi laukai saṅgāti ñatvā, taisaṃ satīmā vinayāya sikkhai’’ti||

yau  dukkhamaddakkhi, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yatau bhavati, ayaṃ samudayau| sandiṭṭhaṃ yatau bhavati yāva tassa vinayāya sikkhā, ayaṃ maggau| imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni|

aikādasaṅguttaraisu gaupālakaupamasuttaṃ|

tattha yāva rūpasañ‍ñuttā yañ‍ca saḷāyatanaṃ yathā vaṇaṃ paṭicchādaiti yañ‍ca titthaṃ yathā ca labhati dhammūpasañhitaṃ uḷāraṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ catubbidhaṃ ca attabhāvatau  ca vatthu, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yāva āsāṭikaṃ hāraitā sāṭaitā (sī॰ pī॰) passa aṅguttaranikāyai hauti, ayaṃ samudayau| rūpasañ‍ñuttā āsāṭakaharaṇaṃ āsāṭikasāṭanā (pī॰) vaṇapaṭicchādanaṃ vīthiñ‍ñutā gaucarakusalañ‍ca, ayaṃ maggau| avasaisā dhammā atthi haitū atthi pac‍cayā atthi nissayā sāvasaisadauhitā anaikapūjā ca kalyāṇamittatappac‍cayā dhammā vīthiñ‍ñutā ca haitu, imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni|

tattha  katamaṃ dukkhañ‍ca maggau ca niraudhau ca?

sati kāyagatā upaṭṭhitā, chasu phassāyatanaisu saṃvutau saṃvarau (pī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 25|

satataṃ bhikkhu samāhitau, jañ‍ñā jānaiyya (pī॰ ka॰) nibbānamattanau||

tattha yā ca kāyagatā sati yañ‍ca saḷāyatanaṃ yattha sabbañ‍caitaṃ dukkhaṃ| yā ca kāyagatā sati yau ca sīlasaṃvarau yau ca samādhi yattha yā sati, ayaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau| sabbampi sīlakkhandhau samādhikkhandhau, ayaṃ maggau| aivaṃvihārinā ñātabbaṃ nibbānaṃ| ayaṃ niraudhau, imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni| sīlai patiṭṭhāya dvai dhammā bhāvaitabbā samathau ca vipassanā ca| tattha yaṃ cittasahajātā dhammā, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yau ca samathau  yā ca vipassanā, ayaṃ maggau| rāgavirāgā ca caitauvimutti, avij‍jāvirāgā ca pañ‍ñāvimutti, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni|

tattha  katamau samudayau ca niraudhau ca?

āsā ca pīhā abhinandanā ca, anaikadhātūsu sarā patiṭṭhitā|

añ‍ñāṇamūlappabhavā pajappitā, sabbā mayā byantikatā samūlikā||

añ‍ñāṇamūlappabhavāti purimakaihi samudayau| sabbā mayā byantikatā samūlikāti niraudhau| imāni dvai sac‍cāni| catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā vitthāraina kātabbaṃ| ariyassa sīlassa samādhinau pañ‍ñāya vimuttiyā| tattha yau imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ dhammānaṃ ananubaudhā appaṭivaidhā, ayaṃ samudayau| paṭivaidhau bhavanaittiyā, ayaṃ niraudhau| ayaṃ samudayau ca niraudhau ca|

tattha katamau samudayau ca maggau ca?

yāni su॰ ni॰ 1041 sautāni laukasmiṃ, [ajitāti bhagavā]

sati taisaṃ nivāraṇaṃ|

sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūmi, pañ‍ñāyaitai pidhīyarai||

yāni  sautānīti ayaṃ samudayau| yā ca pañ‍ñā yā ca sati nivāraṇaṃ pidhānañ‍ca, ayaṃ maggau| imāni dvai sac‍cāni| sañ‍caitaniyaṃ suttaṃ daḷhanaimiyānākārau chahi māsaihi niddiṭṭhau| tattha yaṃ kāyaṃ kāyakammaṃ savaṅkaṃ sadausaṃ sakasāvaṃ yā savaṅkatā  sadausatā sakasāvatā, ayaṃ samudayau| aivaṃ vacīkammaṃ manaukammaṃ avaṅkaṃ adausaṃ akasāvaṃ, yā avaṅkatā adausatā akasāvatā, ayaṃ maggau| aivaṃ vacīkammaṃ manaukammaṃ| imāni dvai sac‍cāni samudayau ca maggau ca|

tattha katamau samudayau ca niraudhau ca maggau ca?

‘‘nissitassa calitaṃ, anissitassa calitaṃ natthi, calitai asati passaddhi, passaddhiyā sati nati na hauti, natiyā asati asatiyā (pī॰) passa udā॰ 74 āgatigati na hauti, āgatigatiyā asati cutūpapātau na hauti, cutūpapātai asati naividha na huraṃ na ubhayamantaraina| aisaivantau dukkhassā’’ti|

tattha  dvai nissayā, ayaṃ samudayau| yau ca anissayau, yā ca anati, ayaṃ maggau| yā āgatigati na hauti cutūpapātau ca yau aisaivantau dukkhassāti, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni| anupaṭṭhitakāyagatā sati…pai॰… yaṃ vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ, ayaṃ samudayau| aikārasaupanissayā vimuttiyau yāva upanissayaupasampadā upaṭṭhitakāyagatāsatissa viharati| sīlasaṃvarau sausāniyau hauti, yañ‍ca vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ, ayaṃ maggau| yā ca vimutti, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni tīṇi sac‍cāni| samudayau ca niraudhau ca maggau ca|

12. tattha katamau niraudhau ca maggau ca?

sayaṃ  kataina sac‍caina, taina attanā abhinibbānagatau vitiṇṇakaṅkhau|

vibhavañ‍ca ñatvā laukasmiṃ, tāva khīṇapunabbhavau sa bhikkhu||

yaṃ sac‍caina, ayaṃ maggau| yaṃ khīṇapunabbhavau, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni dvai sac‍cāni| pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanāni satthā vā dhammaṃ daisaisi añ‍ñatarau vā viñ‍ñū sabrahmacārīti vitthāraina kātabbā| tassa atthappaṭisaṃvaidissa pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati, pamuditassa pīti jāyati, yāva nibbindantau  viraj‍jati, ayaṃ maggau| yā vimutti, ayaṃ niraudhau| aivaṃ pañ‍ca vimuttāyatanāni vitthāraina| imāni dvai sac‍cāni niraudhau ca maggau ca|

imāni sādhāraṇāni suttāni| imaihi sādhāraṇaihi suttaihi yathānikkhittaihi paṭivaidhatau ca lakkhaṇatau ca autāraitvā añ‍ñāni suttāni niddisitabbāni aparihāyantaina| gāthāhi gāthā anuminitabbā, byākaraṇaihi byākaraṇaṃ| imai ca sādhāraṇā dasa parivaḍḍhakā aikau ca catuk‍kau niddaisau sādhāraṇau| ayañ‍ca pakiṇṇakaniddaisau| aikaṃ pañ‍ca cha ca savaikadaisau sabbaṃ| imai dvai parivaj‍janā purimakā ca dasa| imai dvādasa parivaḍḍhakā sac‍cāni| aittāvatā sabbaṃ suttaṃ natthi, taṃ byākaraṇaṃ vā gāthā viya| imaihi dvādasahi parivaḍḍhakaihi na autarituṃ appamattaina pariyaisitvā niddisitabbā|

tatthāyaṃ saṅkhaipau| sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ sattahi padaihi samausaraṇaṃ gacchati| kataraihi sattahi? appiyasampayaugau ca piyavippayaugau ca, imaihi dvīhi  padaihi sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ niddisitabbaṃ| tassa dvai nissayā – kāyau ca cittañ‍ca| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘kāyikaṃ dukkhaṃ caitasikañ‍cai’’ti, natthi taṃ dukkhaṃ na kāyikaṃ vā na caitasikaṃ, sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ dvīhi dukkhaihi niddisitabbaṃ kāyikaina ca caitasikaina ca| tīhi dukkhatāhi saṅgahitaṃ dukkhadukkhatāya saṅkhāradukkhatāya vipariṇāmadukkhatāya| iti taṃ sabbaṃ dukkhaṃ tīhi dukkhatāhi saṅgahitaṃ| iti idañ‍ca dukkhaṃ tividhaṃ| duvidhaṃ dukkhaṃ kāyikañ‍ca caitasikañ‍ca| duvidhaṃ appiyasampayaugau ca piyavippayaugau ca| idaṃ sattavidhaṃ dukkhaṃ|

tattha tividhau samudayau acatutthau apañ‍camau| katamau tividhau? taṇhā ca diṭṭhi ca kammaṃ| tattha taṇhā ca bhavasamudayau kammaṃ| tathā tattha (pī॰) nibbattassa hīnapaṇītatā hīnapaṇītatāya (pī॰), ayaṃ samudayau| iti yāpi bhavagatīsu hīnatā ca paṇītatā ca, yāpi tīhi dukkhatāhi saṅgahitā, yaupi dvīhi mūlaihi samudānītau avij‍jāya nivutassa bhavataṇhāsaṃyuttassa saviñ‍ñāṇakau kāyau, saupi tīhi dukkhatāhi saṅgahitau|

tathā vipal‍lāsatau diṭṭhibhavagantabbā| sā sattavidhā niddisitabbā| aikau vipal‍lāsau tīṇi niddisīyati, cattāri vipal‍lāsavatthūni| tattha katamau aikau vipal‍lāsau? yau viparītaggāhau paṭikkhaipaina, autaraṇaṃ yathā ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’miti viparītaṃ gaṇhāti| aivaṃ cattārau vipal‍lāsā| ayamaikau vipal‍lāsīyati sañ‍ñā cittaṃ diṭṭhi| katamāni cattāri vipal‍lāsavatthūni? kāyau  vaidanā cittaṃ dhammā| aivaṃ vipal‍lāsagatassa akusalañ‍ca pavaḍḍhaiti| tattha sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau dausaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pavaḍḍhaiti| cittavipal‍lāsau laubhaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pavaḍḍhaiti| diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau mauhaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pavaḍḍhaiti| tattha dausassa akusalamūlassa tīṇi micchattāni phalaṃ – micchāvācā micchākammantau micchāājīvau; laubhassa akusalamūlassa tīṇi micchattāni phalaṃ – micchāsaṅkappau micchāvāyāmau micchāsamādhi; mauhassa akusalamūlassa dvai micchattāni phalaṃ – micchādiṭṭhi ca micchāsati ca| aivaṃ akusalaṃ sahaitu sappac‍cayaṃ vipal‍lāsā ca pac‍cayau, akusalamūlāni sahaitū aitaiyaiva paṭipakkhaina anūnā anadhikā dvīhi pac‍cayaihi niddisitabbā| niraudhai ca maggai ca vipal‍lāsamupādāya paratau paritau (pī॰) paṭipakkhaina catassau|

tatthimā uddānagāthā

avij‍jāya  nivutau laukau, cittaṃ saṃyaujanampi|

sā pacchin‍nabhavataṇhā, dvaimā caiva vimuttiyau||

kumbhūpamaṃ kāyamimaṃ, yaṃ na tumhākaṃ taṃ pajaha jahā (pī॰ ka॰)|

yai kaici saukaparidaivā, timbarukau ca sayaṃkataṃ||

dukkhaṃ diṭṭhi ca uppan‍naṃ, yañ‍ca gaupālakaupamaṃ|

sati kāyagatā māhu, samathau ca vipassanā||

āsā pihā ca abhinandanā ca, catun‍namananubaudhanā|

yāni  sautāni laukasmiṃ, daḷhaṃ naimiyānākārau||

yaṃ nissitassa calitaṃ, anupaṭṭhitakāyagatāsati|

sayaṃ kataina sac‍caina, vimuttāyatanaihi ca||

paiṭakaupadaisai mahākac‍cāyanaina bhāsitai paṭhamabhūmi ariyasac‍cappakāsanā nātaṃ jīvatā bhagavatā mādisaina samuddanaina tathāgataināti|




2. sāsanapaṭṭhānadutiyabhūmi

13. tattha  katamaṃ sāsanappaṭṭhānaṃ? saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, vāsanā bhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca, saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca, vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca| āṇatti, phalaṃ, upāyau, āṇatti ca phalañ‍ca, phalañ‍ca upāyau ca, āṇatti ca phalañ‍ca upāyau ca| assādau, ādīnavau, nissaraṇaṃ, assādau ca ādīnavau ca, assādau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca, ādīnavau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca, assādau ca ādīnavau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca| laukikaṃ, laukuttaraṃ, laukikañ‍ca laukuttarañ‍ca| kammaṃ, vipākau, kammañ‍ca vipākau ca| niddiṭṭhaṃ, aniddiṭṭhaṃ, niddiṭṭhañ‍ca aniddiṭṭhañ‍ca| ñāṇaṃ, ñaiyyaṃ, ñāṇañ‍ca ñaiyyañ‍ca| dassanaṃ, bhāvanā, dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca| vipākakammaṃ, na vipākakammaṃ, naivavipākanavipākakammaṃ| sakavacanaṃ, paravacanaṃ, sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca| sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ, dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ, sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca| thavau, sakavacanādhiṭṭhānaṃ, paravacanādhiṭṭhānaṃ, sakavacanādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca paravacanādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca| kiriyaṃ, phalaṃ, kiriyañ‍ca phalañ‍ca| anuñ‍ñātaṃ, paṭikkhittaṃ, anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca| imāni cha paṭikkhittāni|

14. tattha  katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

kāmandhā jālasañchan‍nā, taṇhāchadanachāditā|

pamattabandhunā baddhā, macchāva kumināmukhai|

jarāmaraṇamanvainti, vacchau khīrapakauva khīrūpakauva (ka॰) passa udā॰ 64 mātaraṃ||

pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, nīvaraṇā|

tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

manaupubbaṅgamā  dhammā, manausaiṭṭhā manaumayā|

manasā cai pasan‍naina, bhāsati vā karauti vā|

tatau naṃ sukhamanvaiti, chāyāva anapāyinī||

saṃyuttakai suttaṃ|

mahānāmassa sak‍kassa idaṃ bhagavā sakyānaṃ kapilavatthumhi nagarai nayavitthāraina saddhāsīlaparibhāvitaṃ suttaṃ bhāvañ‍ñaina paribhāvitaṃ taṃ nāma pacchimai kālai|

tattha katamaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

uddhaṃ adhau udā॰ 61 sabbadhi vippamuttau, ayaṃ ahasmīti anānupassī|

aivaṃ vimuttau udatāri aughaṃ, atiṇṇapubbaṃ apunabbhavāya||

sīlāni nu khau bhavanti kimatthiyāni ānandau pucchati satthāraṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ?

‘‘yassa sailūpamaṃ cittaṃ, ṭhitaṃ nānupakampati|

virattaṃ  rajanīyaisu, kaupanaiyyai kaupanīyai (ka॰) passa udā॰ 34 na kuppati|

yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ, kutau taṃ dukkhamaissatī’’ti||

sāriputtau nāma bhagavā thairañ‍ñatarau sau maṃ āsaj‍ja appaṭinissaj‍ja cārikaṃ pak‍kamati, sāriputtassa byākaraṇaṃ kātabbaṃ| yassa nūna bhagavā kāyagatā sati abhāvitā assa abahulīkatā vitthāraina kātabbaṃ|

15. tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca?

chan‍namativassati udā॰ 45, vivaṭaṃ nātivassati|

tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraitha, aivaṃ taṃ nātivassati||

chan‍namativassatīti  saṃkilaisau| vivaṭaṃ nātivassatīti vāsanā| tamau tamaparāyanauti vitthāraina| tattha yau ca tamau yau ca tamaparāyanau, ayaṃ saṃkilaisau| yau ca jauti yau ca jautiparāyanau, ayaṃ vāsanā|

tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

na taṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, yadāyasaṃ dārujapabbajañ‍ca dārujaṃ pabbajañ‍ca (pī॰) dha॰ pa॰ 345; saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.121|

sārattarattā maṇikuṇḍalaisu, puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā||

na taṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrā, yadā puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā, ayaṃ saṃkilaisau| aitampi  chaitvā paribbajanti dhīrā anapaikkhinau sabbakāmai pahāyāti, ayaṃ nibbaidhau| yaṃ caitayitaṃ pakappitaṃ yā ca nāmarūpassa avak‍kanti hauti| imaihi catūhi padaihi saṃkilaisau| pacchimakaihi catūhi nibbaidhau|

tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

ayaṃ  laukau santāpajātau, phassaparaitau raugaṃ raudaṃ (pī॰) passa udā॰ 30 vadati attatau|

yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñanti, tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathā||

añ‍ñathābhāvī bhavasattau laukau, bhavaparaitau bhavamaivābhinandati|

yadabhinandati taṃ bhayaṃ, yassa bhāyati taṃ dukkhaṃ|

bhavavippahānāya khau panidaṃ brahmacariyaṃ vussati||

yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhavaina bhavassa vippamaukkhamāhaṃsu, sabbaitai ‘‘avippamuttā bhavasmā’’ti vadāmi| yai vā pana kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vibhavaina bhavassa nissaraṇamāhaṃsu, sabbaitai ‘‘anissaṭā bhavasmā’’ti vadāmi| upadhiṃ hi paṭic‍ca dukkhamidaṃ sambhauti, sabbupādānakkhayā natthi dukkhassa sambhavau, laukamimaṃ  passa, puthū avij‍jāya paraitā bhūtā bhūtaratā bhavā aparimuttā| yai hi kaici bhavā sabbadhi sabbatthatāya sabbaitai bhavā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammāti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ, sammappañ‍ñāya passatau|

bhavataṇhā pahīyati, vibhavaṃ nābhinandati|

sabbasau taṇhānaṃ khayā, asaisavirāganiraudhau nibbānaṃ||

‘‘tassa nibbutassa bhikkhunau, anupādā punabbhavau na hauti|

abhibhūtau mārau vijitasaṅgāmau, upaic‍cagā sabbabhavāni tādī’’ti||

ayaṃ laukau santāpajātau yāva dukkhanti yaṃ taṇhā saṃkilaisau|

yaṃ punaggahaṇaṃ yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhavaina bhavassa vimaukkhamāhaṃsu, sabbaitai ‘‘avimuttā bhavasmā’’ti vadāmi| yai vā pana kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā vibhavaina bhavassa nissaraṇamāhaṃsu ‘‘anissaṭā bhavasmā’’ti vadāmi| ayaṃ diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau, taṃ diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau  ca taṇhāsaṃkilaisau ca, ubhayamaitaṃ saṃkilaisau| yaṃ punaggahaṇaṃ bhavavippahānāya brahmacariyaṃ vussati, yāva sabbasau upādānakkhayā sambhavā, idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ| tassa  nibbutassa bhikkhunau yāva upac‍cagā sabbabhavāni tādīti idaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ| cattārau puggalā anusautagāmī saṃkilaisau ṭhitattau ca paṭisautagāmī ca nibbaidhau| thalai tiṭṭhatīti asaikkhabhūmi|

16. tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ?

‘‘dadatau udā॰ 75; dī॰ ni॰ 2.197 puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyati|

kusalau ca jahāti pāpakaṃ, rāgadausamauhakkhayā sanibbutau’’ti||

‘‘dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatī’’ti vāsanā| ‘‘kusalau ca jahāti pāpakaṃ, rāgadausamauhakkhayā sanibbutau’’ti nibbaidhau|

sautānugataisu dhammaisu vacasā paricitaisu manasānupaikkhitaisu diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhaisu pañ‍cānisaṃsā pāṭikaṅkhā| idhaikac‍cassa bahussutā dhammā haunti dhātā apamuṭṭhā vacasā paricitā manasānupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, sau yuñ‍jantau ghaṭaintau vāyamantau diṭṭhaiva dhammai visaisaṃ pappauti| nau cai diṭṭhaiva dhammai visaisaṃ pappauti, gilānau pappauti| nau cai gilānau  pappauti, maraṇakālasamayai pappauti| nau cai maraṇakālasamayai pappauti, daivabhūtau pāpuṇāti| nau cai daivabhūtau pāpuṇāti, taina dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pac‍caikabaudhiṃ pāpuṇāti|

tatthāyaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai pāpuṇāti, ayaṃ nibbaidhau| yaṃ  samparāyai pac‍caikabaudhiṃ pāpuṇāti, ayaṃ vāsanā| imāni sauḷasa suttāni sabbasāsanaṃ atiggaṇhantau tiṭṭhanti| imaihi sauḷasahi suttaihi navavidhau suttantau vibhattau bhavati| sau ca pañ‍ñavatau nau duppañ‍ñassa, yuttassa nau ayuttassa, akammassa vihārissa pakatiyā laukai saṃkilaisau  carati| sau saṃkilaisau tividhau – taṇhāsaṃkilaisau diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau| tatau saṃkilaisatau uṭṭhahantau saṃkilaisau dhammaisu patiṭṭhahati, laukiyaisu patiṭṭhahatīti| tatthākusalau diṭṭhatau sacai taṃ sīlañ‍ca diṭṭhiñ‍ca parāmasati, tassa sau taṇhāsaṃkilaisau hauti| sacai panassa aivaṃ hauti ‘‘imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissaṃ bhavissāmi (pī॰) daivañ‍ñatarau vā’’ti yassa hauti micchādiṭṭhi, aitassa micchādiṭṭhisaṃkilaisau bhavati| sacai pana sīlai patiṭṭhitau aparāmaṭṭhassa hi sīlavataṃ hauti, tassa taṃ sīlavatau yaunisau gahitaṃ avippaṭisāraṃ janaiti yāva vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ, tañ‍ca tassa diṭṭhaiva dhammai kālaṅkatassa vā tamhiyaiva vā pana aparāpariyāyaina vā, añ‍ñaisu khandhaisu aivaṃ sutaṃ ‘‘sucaritaṃ vāsanāya saṃvattatī’’ti vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ vuc‍cati| tattha sīlaisu ṭhitassa vinīvaraṇaṃ cittaṃ, taṃ tatau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhippahānāya bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti| sau ac‍cantaniṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ pāpuṇāti; yadi vā sāsanantarai, ac‍cantaṃ nibbānaṃ pāpuṇāti, yadi vā aikāsanai cha abhiñ‍ñai| tattha  dvai puggalā ariyadhammai pāpuṇanti saddhānusārī ca dhammānusārī ca| tattha dhammānusārī ugghaṭitañ‍ñū, saddhānusārī naiyyau| tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñū duvidhau – kauci tikkhindriyau kauci mudindriyau| tattha naiyyaupi duvidhau – kauci tikkhindriyau kauci mudindriyau| tattha yau ca ugghaṭitañ‍ñū mudindriyau, yau ca naiyyau tikkhindriyau, imai puggalā asamindriyā haunti| tattha imai puggalā samindriyā parihāyanti ca ugghaṭitañ‍ñutau, vipañ‍citañ‍ñū naiyyatau, imai majjhimā bhūmigatā vipañ‍citañ‍ñū hauti| imai tayau puggalā|

17. tattha catutthā pana pañ‍camā ugghaṭitañ‍ñū vipañ‍citañ‍ñū naiyyau ca, tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñū puggalau indriyāni paṭilabhitvā dassanabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau sautāpattiphalañ‍ca pāpuṇāti, aikabījī hauti paṭhamau sautāpan‍nau| tattha vipañ‍citañ‍ñū puggalau indriyāni paṭilabhitvā dassanabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau sautāpattiphalañ‍ca pāpuṇāti, kaulaṃkaulau ca hauti dutiyau sautāpan‍nau| tattha naiyyau puggalau indriyāni paṭilabhitvā dassanabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau sautāpattiphalañ‍ca pāpuṇāti, sattakkhattuparamau ca hauti, ayaṃ tatiyau sautāpan‍nau| imai tayau puggalā indriyavaimattatāya sautāpattiphalai ṭhitā|

ugghaṭitañ‍ñū aikabījī hauti, vipañ‍citañ‍ñū kaulaṃkaulau hauti, naiyyau sattakkhattuparamau hauti| idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ| sacai  pana taduttari vāyamati, ac‍cantaniṭṭhaṃ nibbānaṃ pāpuṇāti| tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñū puggalau yau tikkhindriyau, tai dvai puggalā haunti – anāgāmiphalaṃ pāpuṇitvā antarāparinibbāyī ca upahac‍caparinibbāyī ca| tattha vipañ‍citañ‍ñū puggalau yau tikkhindriyau, tai dvai puggalā haunti – anāgāmiphalaṃ pāpuṇanti asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī ca sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī ca| tattha naiyyau anāgāmiphalaṃ pāpuṇantau uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī hauti, ugghaṭitañ‍ñū ca vipañ‍citañ‍ñū ca, indriyanānattaina ugghaṭitañ‍ñū puggalau tikkhindriyau antarāparinibbāyī hauti, ugghaṭitañ‍ñū mudindriyau uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī hauti| ugghaṭitañ‍ñū ca vipañ‍citañ‍ñū ca indriyanānattaina ugghaṭitañ‍ñū puggalau tikkhindriyau sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, tikkhindriyau antarāparinibbāyī hauti, ugghaṭitañ‍ñū mudindriyau upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti| vipañ‍citañ‍ñū tikkhindriyau asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, vipañ‍citañ‍ñū mudindriyau sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, naiyyau upahac‍caparinibbāyī hauti, vipañ‍citañ‍ñū tikkhindriyau asaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti| vipañ‍citañ‍ñū mudindriyau sasaṅkhāraparinibbāyī hauti, naiyyau uddhaṃsautau akaniṭṭhagāmī hauti| iti pañ‍ca anāgāminau, chaṭṭhau sakadāgāmī, tayau ca sautāpan‍nāti imai nava saikkhā|

tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñū puggalau tikkhindriyau arahattaṃ pāpuṇantau dvai puggalā haunti ubhataubhāgavimuttau pañ‍ñāvimuttau ca| tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñū puggalau mudindriyau arahattaṃ pāpuṇantau dvai puggalā haunti, ṭhitakappī ṭhitakappi (pī॰ ka॰) passa pu॰ pa॰ 17 ca paṭivaidhanabhāvau puggalau ca tikkhindriyau sau arahattaṃ pāpuṇantau dvai puggalā haunti caitanābhabbau ca rakkhaṇābhabbau ca| tattha vipañ‍citañ‍ñū mudindriyau arahattaṃ pāpuṇantau dvai puggalā haunti, sacai caitaiti na parinibbāyī, nau cai caitaiti parinibbāyīti| sacai anurakkhati na parinibbāyī, nau cai anurakkhati parinibbāyīti| tattha naiyyau puggalau bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttau parihānadhammau  hauti kammaniyatau vā samasīsi vā, imai nava arahantau idaṃ catubbidhaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ| imaisu puggalaisu tathāgatassa dasavidhaṃ balaṃ pavattati|

18. katamaṃ  dasavidhaṃ? idha buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ appavattitai dhammacak‍kai mahaisakkhā daivaputtā yācanāya abhiyātā atiyātā (pī॰ ka॰) haunti ‘‘daisaitu sugatau dhamma’’nti| sau anuttaraina buddhacakkhunā vaulaukaintau addasāsi sattānaṃ tayau rāsīnaṃ sammattaniyatau micchattaniyatau aniyatau| tattha sammattaniyatau rāsi micchāsatiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, asatthukau parinibbāyaiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, samāpattiṃ āpaj‍jaiyyāti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| tattha micchattaniyatau rāsi ariyasamāpattiṃ paṭipaj‍jissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, anariyamicchāpaṭipattiṃ paṭipaj‍jissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| tattha  aniyatau rāsi sammāpaṭipaj‍jamānaṃ sammattaniyatarāsiṃ gamissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, micchāpaṭipaj‍jamānau sammattaniyatarāsiṃ gamissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| sammāpaṭipaj‍jamānaṃ sammattaniyatarāsiṃ gamissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati, micchāpaṭipaj‍jamānaṃ micchattaniyatarāsiṃ gamissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| imai tayau anuttaraina buddhacakkhunā vaulaukaintassa sammāsambuddhassa mai satau imai dhammā anabhisambuddhāti aittavatā maṃ kauci sahadhammaina paṭicaudissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, vītarāgassa tai paṭijānatau akhīṇāsavatāya sahadhammaina kauci paṭicaudissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yatau pana imassa aniyatassa rāsissa dhammadaisanā, sā na dissati tak‍karassa sammādukkhakkhayāyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, tathā auvaditau yaṃ pana mai aniyatarāsi sāvakau pubbaināparaṃ visaisaṃ na sacchikarissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

19. yaṃ khau muni nānappakārassa nānāniruttiyau daivanāgayakkhānaṃ damaiti dhammai vavatthānaina vatvā kāraṇatau añ‍ñaṃ pāraṃ gamissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| dhammapaṭisambhidā| yatau panimā niruttitau satta satta niruttiyau nābhisambhunaiyyāti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| niruttipaṭisambhidā| nirutti khau pana abhisamaggaratānaṃ sāvakānaṃ tamatthamaviñ‍ñāpayaiti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| atthapaṭisambhidā| mahaisakkhā daivaputtā upasaṅkamitvā pañhai pucchiṃsu| kāyikaina vā mānasikaina vā paripīḷitassa hatthakuṇīti vā pādai vā khañ‍jai dandhassa dantassa (pī॰ ka॰) sau atthau na paribhājiyatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| paṭibhānapaṭisambhidā| yamhi taṃ taisaṃ hauti tamhi asantaṃ bhavatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| yaṃ hi nāsaṃ taisaṃ na bhavati, tamhi nāsaṃ taisaṃ bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| aivaṃ samudayassa niraudhāya dasa akusalakammapathā| mārau vā indau vā brahmā vā tathāgatau vā cak‍kavattī vā sau vata nāma mātugāmau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati, purisau assa rājā cak‍kavattī sak‍kau daivānamindau bhavissatīti ṭhānamaitaṃ vij‍jati| itissa aivarūpaṃ balaṃ aivarūpaṃ ñāṇaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati ṭhānāṭṭhānañāṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ taṃ niddisitabbaṃ| tīhi rāsīhi catūhi vaisāraj‍jaihi catūhi paṭisambhidāhi  paṭic‍casamuppādassa pavattiyaṃ nivattiyaṃ bhāgiyañ‍ca| kusalaṃ kusalavipākaisu ca upapaj‍jati yañ‍ca itthipurisānaṃ| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ balaṃ tathāgatau aivaṃ jānāti|

yaisaṃ pana sammattaniyatau rāsi, nāyaṃ sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā, nibbānagāminīyaivāyaṃ paṭipadā| tattha siyā micchattaniyatau rāsi, aisāpi na sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā| sak‍kāyasamudayagāminīyaivāyaṃ paṭipadā  hautu, ayaṃ tattha tattha paṭipattiyā ṭhitau gacchati nibbānaṃ, gacchati apāyaṃ, gacchati daivamanussassa| yaṃ yaṃ vā paṭipadaṃ paṭipaj‍jaiyya sabbattha gacchaiyya, ayaṃ sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā| yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati sabbatthagāminī paṭipadāñāṇaṃ dutiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

sā khau panāyaṃ sabbatthagāminī paṭipadā nānādhimuttā kaici kāmaisu kaici duk‍karakāriyaṃ kaici attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā kaici saṃsāraina suddhiṃ pac‍cainti kaici anaj‍jābhāvanāti| taina taina caritaina vinibandhānaṃ sattānaṃ yaṃ ñāṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nānāgataṃ laukassa anaikādhimuttagataṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

tattha sattānaṃ adhimuttā bhavanti āsaivanti bhāvainti bahulīkaraunti| taisaṃ kammupasayānaṃ tadādhimuttānaṃ| sā caiva dhātu saṃvahati| katarā panaisā dhātu naikkhammadhātu baladhātu kāci sampatti kāci micchattañ‍ca dhātu adhimuttā bhavanti| añ‍ñatarā uttari na samanupassanti| tai tadaivaṭṭhānaṃ mayā  jarāmaraṇassa abhinivissa vauharanti ‘‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti| yathā bhagavā sak‍kassa daivānamindassa bhāsitaṃ| yaṃ tattha yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati catutthaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

tattha yaṃyaiva dhātu yaṃ yadaiva dhātuṃ (ka॰) saiṭṭhanti taṃ taṃ kāyaina ca vācāya ca ārambhanti caitasikau| ārambhau caitanā kammaṃ kāyikā  vācasikā ārambhau caitasikattā kammantaraṃ tathāgatau aivaṃ pajānāti ‘‘iminā sattaina aivaṃ dhātukaina aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ kataṃ, taṃ atītamaddhānaṃ iminā haitunā tassa aivarūpau vipākau vipac‍cati aitarahi vipac‍cissati vā anāgatamaddhāna’’nti| aivaṃ pac‍cuppan‍namaddhānaṃ pajānāti ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau aivaṃdhātukau idaṃ kammaṃ karauti’’| taṇhāya ca diṭṭhiyā ca iminā haitunā na tassa vipākau diṭṭhaiyaiva dhammai nibbattissati, upapaj‍jai vā’’ti aparamhi vā pariyāyai aivaṃ pajānāti ‘‘ayaṃ puggalau aivarūpaṃ kammaṃ karissati anāgatamaddhānaṃ, iminā haitunā tassa aivarūpau vipākau nibbattissati, iminā haitunā yāni cattāri  kammaṭṭhānāni idaṃ kammaṭṭhānaṃ pac‍cuppan‍nasukhaṃ āyatiṃ ca sukhavipākaṃ’’ …pai॰… iti ayaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau vipākavaimattataṃ pajānāti uc‍cāvacā hīnapaṇītatā, idaṃ vuc‍cati kammavipākañāṇaṃ pañ‍camaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

tathā sattā yaṃ vā kammasamādānaṃ samādiyantā tattha aivaṃ pajānāti imassa puggalassa kammādhimuttassa rāgacaritassa naikkhammadhātūnaṃ pāripūriṃ gacchanti, tassa rāgānugatai suñ‍ñamānassa paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ saṃkilissati, sacai puna uttari vāyāmatau jhānavaudānagatai mānasai visaisabhāgiyaṃ paṭipadaṃ anuyuñ‍jiyati| tassa hi jhānabhāgiyaṃyaiva paṭhamajjhānai ṭhitassa dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ vaudānaṃ gacchati, tatiyañ‍ca  jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jitukāmassa saumanassindriyaṃ cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati, tassa sā pīti avisaisabhāgiyaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ ādissa tiṭṭhati| sacai tassa nissaraṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| tathāgatassa catutthajjhānaṃ vaudānaṃ gacchatiyaiva, catutthassa jhānassa hānabhāgiyā dhammā, tai ca dhammā yattha pajāyanti yaihi catutthajjhānaṃ vaudānaṃ dissati| aivaṃ ajjhāsayasamāpattiyā yā catassau samāpattiyau tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni aṭṭha vimaukkhajhānānīti cattāri jhānāni vimaukkhāti| aṭṭha ca vimaukkhā tīṇi ca vimaukkhamukhāni| samādhīti cattārau  samādhī – chandasamādhi vīriyasamādhi cittasamādhi vīmaṃsāsamādhīti| samāpattiyau catassau ajjhāsayasamāpattiyau iti imaisaṃ jhānānaṃ vimaukkhasamāpattīti aivarūpau saṃkilaisau rāgacaritassa puggalassa| aivaṃ dausacaritassa| mauhacaritassa| rāgacaritassa puggalassa aivarūpaṃ vaudānaṃ iti yaṃ aittha ñāṇaṃ yathābhūtaṃ asādhāraṇaṃ sabbasattaihi| idaṃ vuc‍cati chaṭṭhaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

tattha tathāgatau aivaṃ pajānāti laukikā dhammā laukuttarā dhammā bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ indriyaṃ nāmaṃ labhanti| ādhipataiyyabhūmiṃ upādāya balaṃ nāmaṃ labhanti thāmagataṃ manau manindriyaṃ taṃ upādāya| vīriyaṃ nāmaṃ labhanti ārambhadhātuṃ upādāya| itissa daiva aivarūpaṃ ñāṇaṃ imaihi ca dhammaihi imai puggalā saman‍nāgatātipi dhammadaisanaṃ akāsi| ākāratau ca vaukāratau ca āsayajjhāsayassa  adhimuttisaman‍nāgatānaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati parasattānaṃ parapuggalānaṃ indriyabalavīriyavaimattataṃ ñāṇaṃ sattamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

tattha ca tathāgatau laukādīsu ca bhūmīsu saṃyaujanānañ‍ca saikkhānaṃ dvīhi balaihi gatiṃ pajānāti, pubbainivāsānussatiyā atītai saṃsārai aitarahi ca pac‍cuppan‍nai dibbacakkhunā cutūpapātaṃ iti imāni dvai balāni dibbacakkhutau abhinīhitāni| sau atītamaddhānaṃ dibbassa  cakkhunau gaucarau sau aitarahi sati gaucarau iti attanau ca paraisaṃ ca pubbainivāsañāṇaṃ anaikavidhaṃ nānappakārakaṃ pac‍cuppan‍namaddhānaṃ dibbaina cakkhunā imāni dvai tathāgatabalāni, aṭṭhamaṃ pubbainivāsau, navamaṃ dibbacakkhu|

puna caparaṃ tathāgatau ariyapuggalānaṃ jhānaṃ vaudānaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ pajānāti ayaṃ puggalau iminā maggaina imāya paṭipadāya āsavānaṃ khayā anāsavaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharatīti iti attanau ca āsavānaṃ khayaṃ ñāṇaṃ diṭṭhaikaṭṭhānaṃ catubhūmimupādāya yāva navan‍naṃ arahantānaṃ āsavakkhayau audhisau saikkhānaṃ anaudhisau arahantānaṃ| tattha caitauvimutti dvīhi āsavaihi anāsavā kāmāsavaina ca bhavāsavaina ca, pañ‍ñāvimutti dvīhi āsavaihi anāsavā diṭṭhāsavaina ca avij‍jāsavaina ca, imāsaṃ dvin‍naṃ vimuttīnaṃ yathābhūtaṃ  ñāṇaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati āsavakkhayai ñāṇaṃ| dasamaṃ tathāgatabalaṃ|

20. imaisu  dasasu balaisu ṭhitau tathāgatau pañ‍cavidhaṃ sāsanaṃ daisaiti saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ| tattha yau taṇhāsaṃkilaisau, imassa alaubhau nissaraṇaṃ| yau diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau, imassa amauhau nissaraṇaṃ| yau duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau, imassa tīṇi kusalāni nissaraṇaṃ| kiṃ nidānaṃ? tīṇi imāni tīṇi hi imāni (pī॰) manauduc‍caritāni – abhijjhā byāpādau micchādiṭṭhi| tattha abhijjhā manauduc‍caritaṃ kāyakammaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti, adin‍nādānaṃ sabbañ‍ca tadupanibbaddhaṃ vācākammaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti, musāvādañ‍ca sabbavitathaṃ sabbaṃ vācamabhāvaṃ sabbamakkhaṃ palāsaṃ abhijjhā akusalamūlanti, sucaritai sucaritaṃ musāvādā adin‍nādānā abhijjhāya caitanā, tattha byāpādau manauduc‍caritaṃ kāyakammaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti, pāṇātipātaṃ sabbañ‍ca maitaṃ ākaḍḍhanaṃ parikaḍḍhanaṃ nibbaddhaṃ raucanaṃ vācākammaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti, pisuṇavācaṃ pharusavācaṃ micchādiṭṭhi manauduc‍caritañ‍ca abhijjhaṃ byāpādaṃ micchādiṭṭhiṃ payaujaiti, tassa yau kauci micchādiṭṭhi cāgau rāgajau vā dausajau vā sabbasau micchādiṭṭhi sambhūtau iminā kāraṇaina micchādiṭṭhiṃ upaṭṭhapaiti, kāmaisumicchācāraṃ vacīkammaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti samphappalāpaṃ| imāni  tīṇi duc‍caritāni akusalamūlāni|

yā abhijjhā, sau laubhau| yau byāpādau, sau dausau| yā micchādiṭṭhi, sau mauhau| tāni aṭṭha micchattāni upaṭṭhapainti| taisu gahitaisu tīsu akusalamūlaisu dasavidhaṃ akusalamūlaṃ pāripūriṃ gacchati, tassa tividhassa duc‍caritasaṃkilaisassa vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ nissaraṇaṃ| tattha  yau bahusitau niddaisau yathā laubhau dausau mauhaupi, tattha asituṃ aittha laubhau ussadau taina kāraṇaina taisu vā dhammaisu laubhau pañ‍ñapiyati| tatthāyaṃ mauhau akusalaṃ mauhau ayaṃ avij‍jā, sā catubbidhā rūpai abhiniviṭṭhā, rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, avij‍jāgatau rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tattha katamaṃ padaṃ sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā ucchaidaṃ vadati ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti natthikadiṭṭhi adhic‍casamuppan‍nadiṭṭhi ca añ‍ñau ca karauti, añ‍ñau paṭisaṃvaidiyati| pacchimasaṭṭhikappānaṃ tīṇi padāni sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā sassataṃ bhajanti ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti akiriyañ‍ca taṃ dukkhamicchatau ahaitukā ca patanti anajjhābhāvau ca kammānaṃ sabbañ‍ca mānayi mānati (pī॰)| tattha ‘‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti saṃsāraina  suddhi ājīvakā chaḷāsīti pañ‍ñapainti| yathārūpai sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā catuvatthukā, aivaṃ pañ‍casu khandhaisu vīsativatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā sassataṃ bhajati| añ‍ñājīvakā ca sassatavādikai ca sīlabbataṃ bhajanti parāmasanti  iminā bhavissāmi daivau vā daivañ‍ñatarau vā, ayaṃ sīlabbataparāmāsau| tattha sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā sau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’miti taṃ kaṅkhati vicikicchati nādhimuc‍cati nābhippasīdati pubbantai aparantai pubbantāparantai…pai॰… iti vāsanābhāgiyaisu ṭhitassa ayaṃ upak‍kilaisau|

21. tattha saddhindriyaina sabbaṃ vicikicchitaṃ pajahati, pañ‍ñindriyaina udayabbayaṃ passati, samādhindriyaina cittaṃ aikaudi karauti vīriyindriyaina ārabhati| sau imaihi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi saddhānusārī avaic‍cappasādai niratau anantariyaṃ samādhiṃ uppādaiti| indriyaihi suddhaihi dhammānusārī appac‍cayatāya anantariyaṃ samādhiṃ uppādaiti| sau ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| sac‍cāni idaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ| tassa pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ tīṇi saṃyaujanāni dassanapahātabbāni sabbaina sabbaṃ pahīnāni dvai puggalakatāni| tattha tīṇi akusalamūlāni bhāvanāpahātabbāni uparikkhittāni cha bhavai nibbattainti| tattha taisu abhijjhāya ca byāpādaisu tanukataisu cha bhavā parikkhayā mariyādaṃ gacchanti, dvai bhavā avasiṭṭhā| tassa abhijjhā ca byāpādau ca sabbaina sabbaṃ parikkhīṇā haunti| aikau bhavau avasiṭṭhau hauti| sau ca mānavasaina nibbattaiti| kiñ‍cāpi aittha añ‍ñaipi cattārau kilaisā rūparāgau bhavarāgau avij‍jā uddhac‍caṃ kaitusmimānabhūtā nappaṭibalā asmimānaṃ vinivattaituṃ, sabbaipi  tai asmimānassa pahānaṃ ārabhatai| khīṇaisu na ca taisu idamuttaridassanabhūmiyaṃ pañ‍casu saikkhapuggalaisu tīsu ca paṭippan‍nakaisu dvīsu ca phalaṭṭhaisu bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ| taduttari asaikkhabhāgiyasuttaṃ, katthaci bhūmi nipīḷiyati| idañ‍ca pañ‍camaṃ suttaṃ| tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ daisitaṃ puthuj‍janassa saikkhassa asaikkhassa saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ| puthuj‍janassa  dassanabhāgiyaṃ| bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ saikkhānaṃ| yaṃ paṭhamaniddiṭṭhaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ sabbaisaṃ arahantānaṃ| sā pana pañ‍cavidhā sattavīsaākārai sattavīsaṃ ākārai (pī॰) pariyaisitabbaṃ| aitaisu tassa gatīnaṃ tatau uttari| tañ‍ca khau saṅkhaipaina pañ‍ñāsāya ākāraihi  sampatati, yai pañ‍ñāsa ākārā sāsanai niddiṭṭhā, tai saṅkhipiyantā dasahi ākāraihi patanti| yai ariyasac‍caṃ nikkhaipaina ṭhitai saṅkhipiyattā aṭṭhasu ākāraisu patanti| catūsu ca sādhāraṇaisu suttaisu yā hārasampātassa bhūmi, tai saṅkhipiyantā pañ‍casu suttaisu patanti| saṃkilaisabhāgiyai vāsanābhāgiyai bhāvanābhāgiyai nibbaidhabhāgiyai asaikkhabhāgiyai ca| tai saṅkhipiyantā catūsu suttaisu patanti| saṃkilaisabhāgiyai vāsanābhāgiyai nibbaidhabhāgiyai asaikkhabhāgiyai ca| tai saṅkhipiyamānā tīsu suttaisu patanti, puthuj‍janabhāgiyai saikkhabhāgiyai asaikkhabhāgiyai ca| tai saṅkhipiyantā dvīsu suttaisu patanti nibbaidhabhāgiyai ca pubbayaugabhāgiyai ca| yathā vuttaṃ  bhagavatā dvai atthavasai sampassamānā tathāgatā arahantau sammāsambuddhā dhammaṃ daisainti suttaṃ gaiyyaṃ…pai॰… satthā pubbayaugasaman‍nāgatai appakasiraina mañ‍ñamānā vasiyanti pubbayaugā ca bhavissanti santānaṃ mañ‍ñamānādharāya| tattha pañ‍ñāvaimattataṃ attanau samanupassamānaina aṭṭhavidhai suttasaṅkhaipai, yattha yattha sak‍kauti, tattha tattha yaujaitabbaṃ| tattha tattha yaujaitvā suttassa atthau niddisitabbau| na hi sati vaidanā manau dhāraitvā sak‍kā yaina kainaci suttassa atthau yathābhūtaṃ niddisituṃ|

tattha purimakānaṃ suttānaṃ imā uddānagāthā

kāmandhā jālasañchan‍nā, pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni ca|

manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā, mahānāmau ca sākiyau||

uddhaṃ adhau vippamuttau, yañ‍ca sīlakimatthiyā|

yassa sailūpamaṃ cittaṃ, upatissa pucchādikā||

yassa kāyagatāsati, chan‍naṃ tamauparāyaṇau|

na taṃ daḷhaṃ caitasikaṃ, ayaṃ laukautiādikaṃ||

cattārau caiva puggalā, dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhitaṃ|

sautānugatadhammaisu, imā taisaṃ uddānagāthā||

22. tattha  katamā āṇatti?

sacai bhāyatha udā॰ 44 dukkhassa, sacai vau dukkhamappiyaṃ|

mākattha pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, āvi vā yadi vā rahau||

‘‘atītai, rādha, rūpai anapaikkhau hauhī’’ti vitthāraina kātabbā| ‘‘sīlavantaina, ānanda, puggalaina sadā karaṇīyā kintimai avippaṭisārau assā’’ti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati āṇatti|

tattha katamaṃ phalaṃ?

dhammau havai rakkhati dhammacāriṃ, chattaṃ mahantaṃ yatha vassakālai|

aisānisaṃsau dhammai suciṇṇai, na duggatiṃ gacchati dhammacārī||

idaṃ phalaṃ|

tattha katamau upāyau?

‘‘sabbai dhammā dha॰ pa॰ 279 anattā’’ti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā||

‘‘sattahaṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau khau, bhikkhu, api himavantaṃ pabbatarājānaṃ cālaiyya, kau pana vādau chavaṃ avij‍jaṃ sattakaisu’’ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ kātabbaṃ| ayaṃ upāyau|

tattha katamā āṇatti ca phalañ‍ca?

sacai bhāyatha dukkhassa, sacai vau dukkhamappiyaṃ|

mākattha pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, āvi vā yadi vā rahau||

sacai hi pāpakaṃ kammaṃ, karautha vā karissatha|

na vau dukkhā pamaukkhātthi, upac‍cāpi palāyataṃ palāyatau (pī॰)||

purimikāya  gāthāya āṇatti pacchimikāya phalaṃ| sīlai patiṭṭhāya dvai dhammā bhāvaitabbā yā ca cittabhāvanā yā ca pañ‍ñābhāvanā yā ca āṇatti rāgavirāgā ca phalaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ phalañ‍ca upāyau ca?

sīlai patiṭṭhāya saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.23 narau sapañ‍ñau, cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca bhāvayaṃ|

ātāpī nipakau bhikkhu, sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭaṃ||

purimikāya aḍḍhagāthāya upāyau, pacchimikāya aḍḍhagāthāya phalaṃ| nandiyau nandikau (pī॰ ka॰) sak‍kau isivutthapuririkāmaaikarakkhai isivutta… (pī॰) suttaṃ mūlatau upādāya yāva  chasu dhammaisu| uttari pañ‍casu dhammaisu yācayaugau yau ca yaugau (pī॰) karaṇīyau, ayaṃ upāyau| asahagatassa kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ muc‍catīti| sabbāsu chasu tīsu| ayaṃ upāyau ca phalañ‍ca|

tattha katamā āṇatti ca phalañ‍ca upāyau ca?

suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhassu, maugharāja sadā satau|

attānudiṭṭhiṃ uhac‍ca ūhac‍ca (su॰ ni॰ 1125), aivaṃ mac‍cutarau siyā||

‘‘suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhassu, maugharājā’’ti āṇatti| ‘‘sadā satau’’ti upāyau| ‘‘attānudiṭṭhiṃ uhac‍ca, aivaṃ mac‍cutarau siyā’’ti phalaṃ| samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha, samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpaṃ anic‍canti pajānāti| aivaṃ passaṃ ariyasāvakau parimuc‍cati jātiyāpi…pai॰… upāyāsaihipi idha tīṇipi’’|

23. tattha katamau assādau?

kāmaṃ kāmayamānassa, tassa caitaṃ samijjhati| ayaṃ assādau|

‘‘dhammacariyā  samacariyā kusalacariyā haitūhi, brāhmaṇa, aivamidhaikac‍cai sattā kāyassa bhaidā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍janti’’| ayaṃ assādau|

tattha  katamau ādīnavau?

kāmaisu vai hañ‍ñatai sabbā muc‍caiva – ayaṃ ādīnavau| pasainadisaṃyuttakai suttai pabbataupamā – ayaṃ ādīnavau|

tattha katamaṃ nissaraṇaṃ?

yau kāmai parivaj‍jaiti, sappassaiva padā sirau|

saumaṃ visattikaṃ laukai, satau samativattati||

saṃyuttakai suttaṃ pāricchattakau paṇḍupalāsau san‍nipalāsau – idaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

tattha  katamau assādau ca ādīnavau ca?

yāni jā॰ 1.2.144 dukanipātai karauti purisau, tāni attani passati|

kalyāṇakārī kalyāṇaṃ, pāpakārī ca pāpakaṃ||

tattha yaṃ pāpakārī pac‍canubhauti ayaṃ assādau| lābhālābhaaṭṭhakaisu byākaraṇaṃ, tattha alābhau ayasau nindā dukkhaṃ, ayaṃ ādīnavau| lābhau yasau sukhaṃ pasaṃsā, ayaṃ assādau|

tattha katamaṃ assādau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca?

‘‘sukhau vipākau puñ‍ñānaṃ, adhippāyau ca ijjhati|

khippañ‍ca paramaṃ santiṃ, nibbānamadhigacchatī’’ti||

yau ca vipākau puñ‍ñānaṃ yā ca adhippāyassa ijjhanā, ayaṃ assādau| yaṃ khippañ‍ca paramaṃ santiṃ nibbānamadhigacchati, idaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

bāttiṃsāya caiva mahāpurisalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatassa mahāpurisassa dvaiyaiva gatiyau haunti, sacai  agāraṃ ajjhāvasati, rājā  hauti cak‍kavattī yāva abhivijinitvā ajjhāvasati ayaṃ assādau| sacai agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajati sabbaina aughaina ausadhaina (pī॰ ka॰) nissaraṇaṃ ayaṃ assādau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca|

tattha katamau ādīnavau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca?

ādānassa ādin‍nassa (ka॰) bhayaṃ ñatvā, jātimaraṇasambhavaṃ|

anādātuṃ nibbattati, jātimaraṇasaṅkhayā||

purimikāya aḍḍhagāthāya jātimaraṇasambhavau ādīnavau| anādātuṃ nibbattati jātimaraṇasaṅkhayāti nissaraṇaṃ|

kicchaṃ vatāyaṃ laukau āpan‍nau yamidaṃ jāyatai ca mīyatai ca| yāva kudassunāmassa dukkhassa antau bhavissati paratau vāti aittha yā uparikkhā, ayaṃ ādīnavau| yau gaidhaṃ ñatvā abhinikkhamati yāva purāṇakāya rājadhāniyā, idaṃ nissaraṇaṃ| ayaṃ ādīnavau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca|

tattha  katamau assādau ca ādīnavau ca nissaraṇañ‍ca?

kāmā hi citrā vividhā madhurā (thairagā॰ 787) manauramā, virūparūpaihi mathainti cittaṃ|

tasmā ahaṃ thairagā॰ 787 pabbajitaumhi rāja, apaṇṇakaṃ sāmañ‍ñamaiva saiyyau||

yaṃ ‘‘kāmā hi citrā vividhā manauramā’’ti ayaṃ assādau| yaṃ ‘‘virūparūpaihi mathainti citta’’nti ayaṃ ādīnavau| yaṃ ahaṃ  agārasmā pabbajitaumhi rāja apaṇṇakaṃ sāmañ‍ñamaiva saiyyauti idaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

balavaṃ bālaupamasuttaṃ yaṃ āsāya vā vaidanīyaṃ kammaṃ gāhati, tathā caipi yaṃ yaṃ pāpakammaṃ anubhauti, tattha dukkhavaidanīyaina kammaina abhāvitakāyaina ca yāva parittacaitasau ca ādīnavaṃ dassaiti sukhavaidanīyaina kammaina assādaiti| yaṃ purāsadisau hauti| bhāvitacittau bhāvitakāyau bhāvitapañ‍ñau mahānāmau aparittacaitasau, idaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

24. tattha  katamaṃ laukikaṃ suttaṃ?

na hi pāpaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ, saj‍jukhīraṃva muc‍cati|

ḍahantaṃ bālamanvaiti, bhasmacchan‍nauva bhasmāchan‍nauva (ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 71 pāvakau||

cattāri agatigamanāni, idaṃ laukikaṃ suttaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ?

‘‘yassindriyāni samathaṅgatāni samathaṃ gatāni (pī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 94, assā yathā sārathinā sudantā|

pahīnamānassa anāsavassa, daivāpi tassa pihayanti tādinau’’ti||

‘‘ariyaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, sammāsamādhiṃ daisaissāmī’’ti idaṃ laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ laukikaṃ laukuttarañ‍ca suttaṃ?

sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, dayhamānauva matthakai|

kāmarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai||

‘‘sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, dayhamānauva matthakai’’ti laukikaṃ|

‘‘kāmarāgappahānāya, satau bhikkhu paribbajai’’ti laukuttaraṃ|

kabaḷīkārai  āhārai atthi chandauti laukikaṃ| natthi chandauti laukuttaraṃ suttaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ kammaṃ?

yau pāṇamatipātaiti, musāvādañ‍ca bhāsati|

laukai adin‍naṃ ādiyati ādiyi (ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 5.174, paradārañ‍ca gacchati||

surāmairayapānañ‍ca, yau narau anuyuñ‍jati|

appahāya  pañ‍ca vairāni, dussīlau iti vuc‍cati||

tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, duc‍caritāni| idaṃ kammaṃ|

tattha katamau vipākau?

saṭṭhivassasahassāni, yathārūpī vipac‍cagā||

‘‘diṭṭhā mayā, bhikkhavai saṃ॰ ni॰ 4.135, cha phassāyatanikā nāma nirayā| diṭṭhā mayā, bhikkhavai, cha phassāyatanikā nāma saggā’’| ayaṃ vipākau|

tattha katamaṃ kammañ‍ca vipākau ca?

ayasāva malaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ, tatuṭṭhāya tamaiva khādati|

aivaṃ atidhaunacārinaṃ, sāni kammāni nayanti duggatiṃ||

ayasāva malaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ, yāva sāni kammānīti idaṃ kammaṃ| nayanti duggatinti vipākau|

catūsu sammāpaṭipaj‍jamānau mātari pitari tathāgatai tathāgatasāvakai  yā sammāpaṭipatti, idaṃ kammaṃ| yaṃ daivaisu upapaj‍jati, ayaṃ vipākau| idaṃ kammañ‍ca vipākau ca|

25. tattha  katamaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ suttaṃ?

nailaṅgau saitapacchādau, aikārau vattatī vattatai (ka॰) udā॰ 65 rathau|

anīghaṃ passa āyantaṃ, chin‍nasautaṃ abandhanaṃ|

yaṃ vā cittaṃ samaṇaisu, cittāgahapati dissati||

aivaṃ imāya gāthāya niddiṭṭhau atthau|

gaupālakaupamai  aikādasa padāni| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpañ‍ñū hauti| yā ca atiraikapūjāya pūjaitā hautīti| imāni aikādasa padāni yathābhāsitāni niddiṭṭhau atthau|

tattha katamau aniddiṭṭhau atthau?

sukhau vivaikau tuṭṭhassa, sutadhammassa passatau|

abyāpaj‍jaṃ abyāpajjhaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) passa udā॰ 11 sukhaṃ laukai, pāṇabhūtaisu saṃyamauti||

sukhā virāgatā laukai, kāmānaṃ samatik‍kamau|

asmimānassa yau vinayau, aitaṃ vai paramaṃ sukhanti||

idaṃ aniddiṭṭhaṃ| aṭṭha mahāpurisavitak‍kā| idaṃ aniddiṭṭhaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ niddiṭṭhañ‍ca aniddiṭṭhañ‍ca?

pasan‍nanaittau su॰ ni॰ 555 sumukhau, brahā uju patāpavā|

majjhai samaṇasaṅghassa, ādic‍cauva viraucasi||

pasan‍nanaittau  yāva ādic‍cauva viraucasīti niddiṭṭhau| pasan‍nanaittau yau bhagavā kathañ‍ca pana pasan‍nanaittatā, kathaṃ sumukhatā, kathaṃ brahakāyatā, kathaṃ ujukatā, kathaṃ patāpavatā, kathaṃ viraucatāti aniddiṭṭhau| phaiṇapiṇḍaupamaṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ yathā phaiṇapiṇḍau aivaṃ rūpaṃ yathā pubbuḷau aivaṃ vaidanā māyā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pañ‍cakkhandhā pañ‍cahi upamāhi niddiṭṭhā| kaina kāraṇaina phaiṇapiṇḍaupamaṃ rūpaṃ sabbañ‍ca cakkhuviñ‍ñaiyyaṃ yaṃ vā catūhi āyatanaihi? kathaṃ vaidanā pubbuḷūpamā? katarā ca sā vaidanā sukhā dukkhā adukkhamasukhā? aivamaisā aniddiṭṭhā| aivaṃ niddiṭṭhañ‍ca aniddiṭṭhañ‍ca|

26. tattha  katamaṃ ñāṇaṃ?

pañ‍ñā hi saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, yāyaṃ nibbaidhagāminī|

yāya yāyaṃ (ka॰) passa itivu॰ 41 sammā pajānāti, jātimaraṇasaṅkhayaṃ||

tīṇimāni  indriyāni anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ añ‍ñindriyaṃ añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ, idaṃ ñāṇaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ naiyyaṃ?

kāmaisu udā॰ 63 sattā kāmasaṅgasattā, saṃyaujanai vaj‍jamapassamānā|

na hi jātu saṃyaujanasaṅgasattā, aughaṃ taraiyyuṃ vipulaṃ mahantaṃ||

catūhi  aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatā kāyassa bhaidā daivaisu uppaj‍janti| udānai kāpiyaṃ suttaṃ apaṇṇakapasādanīyaṃ – idaṃ naiyyaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca naiyyañ‍ca?

sabbai dhammā anattāti, yadā pañ‍ñāya passati|

atha nibbindati dukkhai, aisa maggau visuddhiyā||

yadā passatīti ñāṇaṃ| yau sabbadhammai anattākāraina upaṭṭhapaiti idaṃ naiyyaṃ|

cattāri ariyasac‍cāni, tattha tīṇi naiyyāni maggasac‍caṃ sīlakkhandhau ca pañ‍ñākkhandhau ca, idaṃ ñāṇañ‍ca naiyyañ‍ca|

27. tattha katamaṃ dassanaṃ?

aisaiva maggau dha॰ pa॰ 274 dhammapadai natthañ‍ñau, dassanassa visuddhiyā|

aitañhi tumhai paṭipaj‍jatha, mārassaitaṃ pamauhanaṃ||

catūhi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau ariyasāvakau attanāva attanāyaiva (ka॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1003 attānaṃ byākaraiyya ‘‘khīṇanirayaumhi yāva sautāpan‍nauhamasmi avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyaṇau’’ti| idaṃ dassanaṃ|

tattha  katamā bhāvanā?

yassindriyāni subhāvitāni, ajjhattaṃ bahiddhā ca sabbalaukai|

sau puggalau mati ca rūpasañ‍ñī, sumauhagatā na jānāti kiṃsu mauhagatānu jānāti (ka॰)||

cattāri dhammapadāni – anabhijjhā abyāpādau sammāsati sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ  bhāvanā|

tattha katamaṃ dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca?

vacasā manasātha kammunā ca, aviruddhau sammā viditvā viditvāna (ka॰) su॰ ni॰ 367 dhammaṃ|

nibbānapadābhipatthayānau, sammā sau laukai paribbajaiyya||

sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikātukāmaina katamai dhammā manasikātabbā, bhagavā āha pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā| idaṃ dassanañ‍ca bhāvanā ca|

28. tattha katamai vipākadhammadhammā?

yāni karauti purisauti vitthārau| tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, sucaritāni| imai vipākadhammadhammā|

tattha katamai navipākadhammadhammā?

rūpaṃ vaidayitaṃ sañ‍ñā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yā caiva caitanā|

naisauhamasmi na maisau attā, iti diṭṭhau viraj‍jati||

pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, khandhā – imai navipākadhammadhammā|

tattha katamau naivavipākau navipākadhammadhammau?

‘‘yai  aivaṃ paṭipaj‍janti, nayaṃ buddhaina daisitaṃ|

tai dukkhassantaṃ karissanti, satthusāsanakārakā’’ti||

iti yā ca sammāpaṭipatti yau ca niraudhau, ubhayamaitaṃ naivavipākau navipākadhammau| brahmacariyaṃ  vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi, brahmacariyaphalāni ca brahmacariyañ‍ca ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau brahmacariyaphalāni sautāpattiphalaṃ yāva arahattaṃ|

29. tattha  katamaṃ sakavacanaṃ?

sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, kusalassa upasampadā|

sacittapariyaudapanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsanaṃ||

tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, vimaukkhamukhāni| idaṃ sakavacanaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ paravacanaṃ?

natthi puttasamaṃ paimaṃ, natthi gauṇasamitaṃ dhanaṃ|

natthi sūriyasamā ābhā, samuddaparamā sarā||

haitunā mārisā kausiyā subhāsitaina saṅgāmavijayau saupi nāma, bhikkhavai, sak‍kau daivānamindau sakaṃ phalaṃ paribhuñ‍jamānauti vitthāraina kātabbaṃ| idaṃ paravacanaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ sakavacanañ‍ca paravacanañ‍ca?

‘‘yaṃ pattaṃ yañ‍ca pattabbaṃ, ubhayamaitaṃ rajānukiṇṇaṃ|

yai aivaṃvādinau natthi, taisaṃ kāmaisu dausau’’ti||

idaṃ paravacanaṃ| yai ca khau tai ubhau antai anupagamma vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya| idaṃ sakavacanaṃ|

‘‘nandati  puttaihi puttimā, gaumā gauhi bhaugikau bhaugaihi (pī॰) saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.12 tathaiva nandati|

upadhī hi narassa nandanā, na hi sau nandati yau nirūpadhī’’ti – paravacanaṃ||

‘‘saucati puttaihi puttimā, gaumā gauhi tathaiva saucati|

upadhī hi narassa saucanā, na hi sau saucati yau nirūpadhī’’ti – sakavacanaṃ||

idaṃ sakavacanaṃ paravacanañ‍ca|

30. tattha  katamaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

yai kaici bhūtā bhavissanti yai vāpi, sabbai gamissanti pahāya daihaṃ|

taṃ sabbajāniṃ kusalau viditvā, dhammai ātāpiyau (udā॰ 42) ṭhitau brahmacariyaṃ caraiyya||

tayaumai, bhikkhavai, satthārau, tathāgatau arahaṃ saikkhau paṭipadau| idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

yañ‍ca kāmasukhaṃ udā॰ 12 laukai, yañ‍cidaṃ diviyaṃ sukhaṃ|

taṇhakkhayasukhassaitai, kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ||

sattimai, bhikkhavai, baujjhaṅgā, idaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca? duddasamantaṃ sac‍caṃ duddasau paṭivaidhau bālaihi, jānatau passatau natthi nandīti vadāmi| duddasamantaṃ sac‍caṃ duddasau paṭivaidhau bālaihīti dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ| jānatau passatau natthi nandīti sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ| dārukkhandhaupamaṃ gaṅgāya tīriyā aurimañ‍ca tīraṃ pārimañ‍ca tīraṃ thalai vā thalaiva ca (ka॰) saṃyuttanikāyai na ca ussīdanaṃ, majjhai ca na saṃsīdanaṃ manussaggāhau ca amanussaggāhau ca antaupūtibhāvau ca, idaṃ dhammādhiṭṭhānaṃ| aivaṃ pana bhikkhu nibbānanin‍nau bhavissati nibbānaparāyaṇauti sattādhiṭṭhānaṃ| idaṃ sattādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca dhammādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca|

tattha katamau thavau?

maggānaṭṭhaṅgikau  saiṭṭhau, sac‍cānaṃ caturau padā|

virāgau saiṭṭhau dhammānaṃ, dvipadānañ‍ca cakkhumā||

tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, aggāni – buddhau sattānaṃ, virāgau dhammānaṃ, saṅghau gaṇānaṃ| ayaṃ thavau|

31. tattha  katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ?

kāyaina  dha॰ pa॰ 361 saṃvarau sādhu, sādhu vācāya saṃvarau|

manasā saṃvarau sādhu, sādhu sabbattha saṃvutau|

sabbattha saṃvutau bhikkhu, sabbadukkhā pamuc‍cati||

idaṃ bhagavatā anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, karaṇīyāni – kāyasucaritaṃ vacīsucaritaṃ manausucaritaṃ| idaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ?

natthi puttasamaṃ paimaṃ| vitthārau idaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ|

tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, akaraṇīyāni sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya daisitāni| katamāni tīṇi? kāyaduc‍caritaṃ vacīduc‍caritaṃ manauduc‍caritaṃ| idaṃ paṭikkhittaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātañ‍ca paṭikkhittañ‍ca?

kāyaina kusalaṃ karai, assa kāyaina saṃvutau|

kāyaduc‍caritaṃ hitvā, kāyasucaritaṃ carai||

dvīhi paṭhamapadaihi catutthaina ca padaina anujānāti| kāyaduc‍caritaṃ hitvāti tatiyaina padaina paṭikkhittanti| mahāvibhaṅgau aciratapānādau|

tatthimā uddānagāthā

sacai  bhāyasi dukkhassa, mābhinandi anāgataṃ|

vassakālai yathā chattaṃ, kusalāni kamatthakai||

sabbai  dhammā anattāti, samāgataṃ vicālayai|

na vau dukkhā pamaukkhātthi, samathau ca vipassanā||

kāmacchandaṃ upādāya, yau sau vitak‍kaihi khaj‍jati|

subhāvitattai baujjhaṅgai, sau imaṃ vijaṭayai jaṭaṃ||

suñ‍ñatau laukaṃ avaikkhassu, samādhibhāvi bhāvasai|

kāmaṃ kāmayamānassa, dhammacariyāya sugatiṃ||

hañ‍ñatai sabbā muc‍caiva, nippauṭhaintau catuddisā|

yau kāmai parivaj‍jaiti, pārichattaupamaiva ca||

yāni  karauti purisau, laukadhammā pakāsitā|

sukhau vipākau puñ‍ñānaṃ, tatiyaṃ añ‍ñaṃ na vij‍jati||

ādānassa bhayaṃ ñatvā, jāyatai jīyataipi ca|

kāmā hi citrā vividhā, atha lauṇasal‍laupamaṃ||

na hi pāpaṃ kataṃ kammaṃ, agatīhi ca gacchati|

yassindriyāni samathaṅgatāni, tathaiva pañ‍cañāṇikau||

sattiyā viya aumaṭṭhau, viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca patiṭṭhitā|

yau pāṇamatipātaiti, tīṇi duc‍caritāni ca||

saṭṭhivassasahassāni, khaṇaṃ laddhāna dul‍labhaṃ|

ayasāva malaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ, catūsu paṭipattisu||

nailaṅgau  saitapacchādau, atha gaupālakaupamaṃ|

sukhau vivaikau tuṭṭhassa, vitak‍kā ca sudaisitā||

phaiṇapiṇḍaupamaṃ rūpaṃ, brahā uju patāpavā|

pañ‍ñā hi saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, anañ‍ñā tīṇi indriyāni||

kāmaisu  sattā kāmasaṅgasattā, atha vaṇṇau rahassavā|

sabbai dhammā anattāti, ariyasac‍cañ‍ca daisitaṃ||

aisaiva maggau natthañ‍ñau, sautāpan‍nauti byākarai|

yassindriyāni subhāvitāni, atha dhammapadaihi ca||

vacasā manasā caiva, pañ‍cakkhandhā anic‍catau|

yāni karauti purisau, tīṇi sucaritāni ca||

rūpaṃ vaidayitaṃ sañ‍ñā, pañ‍cakkhandhā pakāsitā|

yau aivaṃ paṭipaj‍jati, brahmā caiva phalāni ca||

sabbapāpassa akaraṇaṃ, vimaukkhā taṃ hi daisitā|

natthi puttasamaṃ paimaṃ, daivānaṃ asurāna ca||

yaṃ pattaṃ yañ‍ca pattabbaṃ, nandati saucati nic‍caṃ|

yai kaici bhūtā bhavissanti, satthārau ca pakāsitā||

yañ‍ca  kāmasukhaṃ laukai, baujjhaṅgā ca sudaisitā|

maggānaṭṭhaṅgikau saiṭṭhau, tayau ca aggapattiyau||

kāyaina saṃvarau sādhu, karaṇīyañ‍ca daisitaṃ|

natthi attasamaṃ paimaṃ, ariyā tīṇi ca daisitā||

kāyaina  kusalaṃ abhiratau, vinayañ‍ca kāmasukhaṃ laukai|

baujjhaṅgā ca sudaisitā, duddasaṃ anataṃ caiva parāparaṃ ca|

paiṭakaupadaisai sāsanappaṭṭhānaṃ nāma dutiyabhūmi samattā|



3. suttādhiṭṭhānatatiyabhūmi

32. tattha  katamaṃ suttādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

laubhādhiṭṭhānaṃ dausādhiṭṭhānaṃ mauhādhiṭṭhānaṃ alaubhādhiṭṭhānaṃ adausādhiṭṭhānaṃ amauhādhiṭṭhānaṃ kāyakammādhiṭṭhānaṃ vācākammādhiṭṭhānaṃ manaukammādhiṭṭhānaṃ saddhindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ vīriyindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ satindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ laubhādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

vitak‍kamathitassa vitak‍kanimmathitassa (ka॰) dha॰ pa॰ 349 jantunau, tibbarāgassa subhānupassinau|

bhiyyau taṇhā pavaḍḍhati, aisa khau gāḷhaṃ karauti bandhanaṃ||

vitak‍kamathitassāti kāmarāgau| subhānupassinauti kāmarāgavatthu| bhiyyau taṇhā pavaḍḍhatīti kāmataṇhā| aisa gāḷhaṃ karauti bandhananti rāgaṃ, iti yau yau dhammau mūlanikkhittau, sau yaivaittha dhammau uggāvahitabbau uggāpayitabbau (pī॰ ka॰)| na bhagavā aikaṃ dhammaṃ ārabbha añ‍ñaṃ dhammaṃ daisaiti| yassa vitak‍kaiti kāmavitak‍kau tamaiva vitak‍kaṃ kāmavitak‍kaina niddisīyati| tibbarāgassāti tassaiva vitak‍kassa vatthuṃ niddisati| subhānupassinau bhiyyau taṇhā pavaḍḍhatīti tamaiva rāgaṃ kāmataṇhāti  niddisati| aisa gāḷhaṃ karauti bandhananti tamaiva taṇhāsaṃyaujanaṃ niddisati| aivaṃ gāthāsu anuminitabbaṃ| aivaṃ savaiyyākaraṇaisu|

tattha  bhagavā aikaṃ dhammaṃ tividhaṃ niddisati, nissandatau haitutau phalatau|

dadaṃ piyau passa saṃyuttanikāyai hauti bhajanti naṃ bahū, kittiñ‍ca pappauti yasau ca vaḍḍhati|

amaṅkubhūtau parisaṃ vigāhati, visāradau hauti narau amaccharī||

dadanti  yaṃ yaṃ dānaṃ, idaṃ dānamayikaṃ puñ‍ñakriyaṃ| tattha haitu| yaṃ caitaṃ| bhajanti naṃ bahū, kittinti yau ca kalyāṇau kittisaddau laukai abbhuggacchati, yaṃ bahukassa janassa piyau bhavati manāpau ca| yañ‍ca avippaṭisārī kālaṅkarauti ayaṃ nissandau| yaṃ kāyassa bhaidā daivaisu upapaj‍jatīti idaṃ phalaṃ| idaṃ laubhādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

33. tattha katamaṃ dausādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

yau pāṇamatipātaiti, musāvādañ‍ca bhāsati|

laukai adin‍naṃ ādiyati, paradārañ‍ca gacchati|

surāmairayapānañ‍ca, yau narau anuyuñ‍jati abhigijjhati (pī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 5.174||

appahāya pañ‍ca vairāni, dussīlau iti vuc‍cati|

kāyassa bhaidā duppañ‍ñau, nirayaṃ saupapaj‍jati||

yau pāṇamatipātaitīti duṭṭhau pāṇamatipātaiti| musāvādañ‍ca bhāsatīti dausaupaghātāya musāvādañ‍ca bhāsati| surāmairayapānañ‍ca, yau narau anuyuñ‍jatīti dausau nidānaṃ| yau  ca surāmairayapānaṃ anuyuñ‍jati yathāparadāravihārī yathāpamuditavihārī (ka॰) amittā janayanti|

pañ‍ca vairāni appahāyāti pañ‍can‍naṃ bhikkhāpadānaṃ samatik‍kamanaṃ sabbaisaṃ dausajānaṃ sā paṇṇatti, tainaiva dausajanitaina kammaina dussīlau iti vuc‍cati saupi dhammau haitunā niddisitabbau, nissandaina phalaina ca|

tīṇi bālassa bālalakkhaṇāni – dubbhāsitabhāsī dubbhāsitabhāsitā (pī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 3.3 ca hauti, duc‍cintitacintī ca duk‍kaṭakammakārī ca| tattha yaṃ kāyaina ca vācāya ca parak‍kamati, idamassa duk‍kaṭakammakārī| tāyaṃ yathā ca musāvādaṃ bhāsati yathā pubbaniddiṭṭhaṃ, idamassa dubbhāsitā| yañ‍ca saṅkappaiti manauduc‍caritaṃ byāpādaṃ, idamassa duc‍cintitacintitā| yaṃ sau imaihi tīhi bālalakkhaṇaihi saman‍nāgatau tīṇi taj‍jāni dukkhāni daumanassāni anubhavati, sau ca hauti sabhaggatau vā parisaggatau vā taj‍jaṃ kathaṃ kathanti| yadā bhavati sau ca pāṇātipātādidasaakusalakammapathā, sau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaitīti| puna caparaṃ yadā passati cauraṃ rājāparādhikaṃ rañ‍ñā gahitaṃ jīvitā vauraupaitaṃ, tassaivaṃ bhavati sacai mamampi rājā jānaiyya  mamampi rājā gāhāpaitvā jīvitā vauraupaiyyāti, sau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ  daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti| puna caparaṃ bālau yadā bhavati āsanā samārūḷhau yāva yā mai gati bhavissati itau paic‍ca paraṃ maraṇāti sau tataunidānaṃ dukkhaṃ daumanassaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaiti iti bālalakkhaṇaṃ haitu| tīṇi taj‍jāni dukkhāni nissandau| kāyassa bhaidā nirayaisu upapaj‍jati, idaṃ phalaṃ| idaṃ dausādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

34. tattha katamaṃ mauhādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

satañ‍caiva sahassānaṃ, kappānaṃ saṃsarissati|

athavā pi tatau bhiyyau, gabbhā gabbhaṃ gamissatha||

anupādāya buddhavacanaṃ, saṅkhārai attatau upādāya|

dukkhassantaṃ karissanti, ṭhānamaitaṃ na vij‍jati||

yau yaṃ anamataggasaṃsāraṃ samāpan‍nau jāyatai ca mīyatai ca, ayaṃ avij‍jāhaitukā| yānipi ca saṅkhārānaṃ payaujanāni, tānipi avij‍jāpac‍cayāni, yaṃ adassanaṃ buddhavacanassa, ayaṃ avij‍jāsuttaiyaiva niddiṭṭhaṃ| yau ca saṅkhārai attatau harati pañ‍cakkhandhai pañ‍ca diṭṭhiyau upagacchati| ‘‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’ti idaṃ suttaṃ avij‍jāya nikkhittaṃ, avij‍jāya nikkhipitaṃ| aivaṃ satthā suttai nayaina sutanayaina (pī॰) dhammaina niddisati| asādhāraṇaina taṃyaiva tattha niddisitabbaṃ| na añ‍ñaṃ|

yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā ‘‘idaṃ dukkha’’nti nappajānanti cattāri sac‍cāni vitthāraina, yaṃ tattha appajānanā, idaṃ dukkhaṃ, ayaṃ haitu| appajānantau vividhai saṅkhārai abhisaṅkharauti, ayaṃ nissandau| yañ‍ca diṭṭhigatāni parāmasati ‘‘idamaiva sac‍caṃ maughamañ‍ña’’nti ayaṃ nissandau| yaṃ  punabbhavaṃ  nibbattaiti, idaṃ phalaṃ| ayampi dhammau saniddiṭṭhau haitutau ca phalatau ca nissandatau ca|

aittha pana kaici dhammā sādhāraṇā bhavanti| haitu khalu āditauyaiva suttai nikkhipissanti| yathā kiṃ bhavai cattārimāni, bhikkhavai, agatigamanāni| tattha yañ‍ca chandāgatiṃ gacchati yañ‍ca bhayāgatiṃ gacchati, ayaṃ laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| yaṃ dausā, ayaṃ dausauyaiva| yaṃ mauhā, ayaṃ mauhauyaiva| aivaṃ imāni tīṇi akusalamūlāni āditauyaiva upaparikkhitabbāni| yattha aikaṃ niddisitabbaṃ, tattha aikaṃ niddisīyati| tathā dvai yathā tīṇi, na hi ādīhi anikkhittai haitu vā nissandau vā phalaṃ vā niddisitabbaṃ|

ayañ‍caittha gāthā –

chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, yau dhammaṃ ativattati|

nihīyati nihīyatai (pī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 4.17 tassa yasau, kāḷapakkhaiva candimā||

kattha chandā ca ayaṃ laubhau yathā niddiṭṭhaṃ pubbai| idaṃ mauhādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

35. tattha katamaṃ alaubhādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

‘‘asubhānupassiṃ asubhānupassī (pī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 8 viharantaṃ, indriyaisu susaṃvutaṃ|

bhaujanamhi ca mattañ‍ñuṃ, saddhaṃ āraddhavīriyaṃ|

taṃ vai nappasahati mārau, vātau sailaṃva pabbata’’nti||

tattha yā asubhāya upaparikkhā, ayaṃ kāmaisu ādīnavadassanaina paric‍cāgau| indriyaisu susaṃvutau tassaiva alaubhassa pāripūriyaṃ mama āyatanasaucitaṃ anupādāya| bhaujanamhi ca mattañ‍ñunti rasataṇhāpahānaṃ| iti ayaṃ alaubhau asubhānupassitāya vatthutau dhārayati, sau alaubhau  haitu| indriyaisu guttadvāratāya gaucaratau dhārayati, bhaujanaimattañ‍ñutāya paratau dhārayati, ayaṃ nissandau| taṃ vai nappasahati mārau, vātau sailaṃ va pabbatanti, idaṃ phalaṃ| iti yauyaiva dhammau ādimhi nikkhittau, sauyaiva majjhai caiva avasānai ca|

nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikadhammampi samanupassāmi asamuppan‍nassa kāmacchandassa anuppādāya uppan‍nassa vā pahānāya, yathayidaṃ yadidaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 1.17 asubhanimittaṃ| tattha asubhanimittaṃ  manasikarauntassa anuppan‍nau caiva kāmacchandau na uppaj‍jati, uppan‍nau ca kāmacchandau pahīyati| idaṃ alaubhassa vatthu| yaṃ puna anuppan‍nau kāmarāgau pariyādiyati rūparāgaṃ arūparāgaṃ, iti phalaṃ| iti ayampi ca dhammau niddiṭṭhau haitutau ca nissandatau ca phalatau ca| idaṃ alaubhādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ adausādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

aikampi cai pāṇamaduṭṭhacittau, maittāyati kusalau kusalī (ka॰) passa itivu॰ 27 taina hauti|

sabbai ca pāṇai manasānukampaṃ anukampamānau (pī॰), pahūtamariyau pakarauti puñ‍ñaṃ||

aikampi cai pāṇamaduṭṭhacittau maittāyatīti ayaṃ adausau| nigghātaina assādau, kusalau taina hautīti taina kusalaina dhammaina saṃyuttau dhammapañ‍ñattiṃ gacchati| kusalauti yathā pañ‍ñāya pañ‍ñau paṇḍic‍caina paṇḍitau| pahūtamariyau pakarauti puñ‍ñanti tassāyaiva vipākau ayaṃ laukiyassa, na hi laukuttarassa| tattha  yā maittāyanā, ayaṃ haitu| yaṃ kusalau bhavati ayaṃ nissandau| yāva abyāpaj‍jau bhūmiyaṃ bahupuñ‍ñaṃ pasavati, idaṃ phalaṃ| iti adausau niddiṭṭhau haitutau ca nissandatau ca phalatau ca|

aikādasānisaṃsā maittāya caitauvimuttiyā| tattha yā maittācaitauvimutti, ayaṃ ariyadhammaisu rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti, laukikāya bhūmikā haitu, yaṃ sukhaṃ āyatiṃ manāpau hauti manussānaṃ, imai aikādasa dhammā nissandau| yañ‍ca akatāvī brahmakāyai upapaj‍jati| idaṃ phalaṃ| idaṃ adausādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

36. tattha katamaṃ amauhādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

pañ‍ñā hi saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, yāyaṃ nibbaidhagāminī nibbaidhabhāginī (pī॰ ka॰) passa itivu॰ 41|

yāya sammā pajānāti, jātimaraṇasaṅkhayaṃ||

pañ‍ñā  hi saiṭṭhāti vatthuṃ| nibbaidhagāminīti nibbānagāminiyaṃ yathābhūtaṃ paṭivijjhati| sammā pajānāti, jātimaraṇasaṅkhayanti amauhau| pañ‍ñāti haitu| yaṃ pajānāti ayaṃ nissandau| yau jātimaraṇasaṅkhayau, idaṃ phalaṃ| iti amauhau niddiṭṭhau haitunā ca nissandaina ca phalaina ca|

tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai itivu॰ 62 tikanipātai, indriyāni anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ añ‍ñindriyaṃ añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ| tattha katamaṃ anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu anabhisamaitassa dukkhassa ariyasac‍cassa abhisamayāya chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati, vīriyaṃ ārabhati, cittaṃ paggaṇhāti padahati| aivaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ kātabbaṃ| tattha katamaṃ añ‍ñindriyaṃ? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu ‘‘idaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍ca’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, yā ca maggau, idaṃ añ‍ñindriyaṃ| āsavakkhayā anāsavau hauti, idaṃ vuc‍cati añ‍ñātāvindriyaṃ| tathāyaṃ pañ‍ñā, ayaṃ haitu| yaṃ chandaṃ janaiti vāyamati, yā pajānāti, ayaṃ nissandau| yaina sabbasau āsavānaṃ khayā haitu, yaṃ khayai ñāṇamuppaj‍jati, anuppādai ñāṇañ‍ca, ayaṃ nissandau| yaṃ arahattaṃ, idaṃ phalaṃ| tattha khīṇā mai jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyanti, idaṃ khayai ñāṇaṃ| nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāmīti idaṃ anuppādai ñāṇaṃ| iti imāni indriyāni amauhau niddiṭṭhau haitunā ca nissandaina ca phalaina ca| imāni asādhāraṇāni niddiṭṭhāni|

tattha katamāni kusalamūlāni sādhāraṇāni? kusalañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi kusalamūlañ‍caiva| tattha katamaṃ kusalamūlaṃ? alaubhau adausau amauhau| tattha katamaṃ kusalaṃ? aṭṭha sammattāni sammādiṭṭhi yāva sammāsamādhi| tattha yāni kusalamūlāni, ayaṃ haitu| yañ‍ca alaubhau tīṇi kammāni samuṭṭhāpaiti saṅkappaṃ vāyāmaṃ samādhiñ‍ca, ayaṃ alaubhassa nissandau| tattha yau adausau, ayaṃ haitu| yaṃ tayau dhammai paṭṭhapaiti sammāvācaṃ sammākammantaṃ sammāājīvañ‍ca, ayaṃ nissandau| tattha  yau amauhau haitu, yaṃ dvai dhammai upaṭṭhapaiti aviparītadassanampi ca anabhilāpanaṃ, ayaṃ nissandau| imassa brahmacariyassa yaṃ phalaṃ, tā dvai vimuttiyau rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti avij‍jā virāgā ca pañ‍ñāvimutti, idaṃ phalaṃ| iti imāni tīṇi kusalamūlāni niddiṭṭhāni haitutau  ca nissandatau ca phalatau ca| aivaṃ sādhāraṇāni kusalāni paṭivijjhitabbāni|

yattha duvai yattha tīṇi| ayañ‍caittha gāthā|

‘‘tulamatulañ‍ca sambhavaṃ, bhavasaṅkhāramavassaji muni|

ajjhattaratau samāhitau, abhindi kavacamivattasambhava’’nti||

tulamatulañ‍ca sambhavanti tulasaṅkhataṃ atulasaṅkhataṃ| tattha yai saṅkhatā tulaṃ, tai dvai dhammā assādau ca ādīnavau ca tulitā bhavanti| aittakau kāmaisu assādau| aittakau ādīnavau imassa, idaṃ nissaraṇanti iti nibbānaṃ pajānāti| dvīhi kāraṇaihi atulaṃ na ca sak‍kā tulayituṃ| aittakaṃ aitaṃ naitaṃ paramatthīti taina atulaṃ| atha pāpuṇā ratanaṃ karitvā acchariyabhāvaina  atulaṃ| tattha kusalassa ca abhisambhavā jānanā passanā, ayaṃ amauhau| yaṃ tattha ñātā ausiraṇā bhavasaṅkhārānaṃ, ayaṃ alaubhau| yaṃ ajjhattaratau samāhitauti vikkhaipapaṭisaṃharaṇā, ayaṃ adausau| iti imāni tīṇi kusalamūlāni| tulamatulasambhavanti  ayaṃ amauhau| yau bhavasaṅkhārānaṃ samausaraṇaṃ laubhau sammāsamādhīnaṃ assādau, ayaṃ haitu| yaṃ ajjhattaratau avij‍jaṇḍakausaṃ sambhaidau, ayaṃ nissandau| sā pavatti imāni tīṇi niddiṭṭhāni kusalamūlāni haitutau ca nissandatau ca phalatau ca|

aittāvatā aisā pavatti ca nivatti ca akusalamūlaihi pavattati, kusalamūlaihi nivattatīti imaihi ca tīhi sabbaṃ akusalamūlaṃ samausaraṇaṃ gacchati| sau dhammai vā vacanatau niddiṭṭhau taṇhāti vā kaudhauti vā asampajañ‍ñanti vā anusayauti vā makkhauti vā paḷāsauti vā assatīti vā issāti vā macchariyanti vā añ‍ñāṇanti vā, taihi yai ca vatthūhi niddisitabbaṃ| yassimāni dvai vacanāni dhammapadāni niddiṭṭhāni na sau atthi kilaisā, yau imaisu navasu padaisu samaudhānaṃ samausaraṇaṃ gacchati| ayaṃ kilaisau, na ca laubhau, na ca dausau, na ca mauhau|

yathā akusalamūlāni, aivaṃ kusalāni paṭikkhaipaina niddisitabbāni|

idaṃ amauhādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

37. tattha  katamaṃ kāyakammādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

kāyaina kusalaṃ karai, assa kāyaina saṃvutau|

kāyaduc‍caritaṃ hitvā, kāyaina sucaritaṃ carai||

tīṇimāni, bhikkhavai, sucaritāni itivu॰ 69 sucaritasuttai| pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī, idaṃ kāyakammādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ vācākammādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

subhāsitaṃ  su॰ ni॰ 452 suttanipātai uttamamāhu santau, dhammaṃ bhaṇai nādhammaṃ taṃ dutiyaṃ|

piyaṃ bhaṇai nāppiyaṃ taṃ tatiyaṃ, sac‍caṃ bhaṇai nālikaṃ taṃ catutthaṃ||

cattārimāni  ca vacīsucaritāni idaṃ vācākammādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ manaukammādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

manaina kusalaṃ kammaṃ, manasā saṃvutau bhavai|

manauduc‍caritaṃ hitvā, manasā sucaritaṃ carai||

tīṇimāni manausucaritāni, anabhijjhā, abyāpādau, sammādiṭṭhi, idaṃ manaukammādhiṭṭhānaṃ| imāni asādhāraṇāni suttāni|

tattha katamāni sādhāraṇāni suttāni?

vācānurakkhī manasā susaṃvutau, kāyaina ca nākusalaṃ kayirā akusalaṃ na kayirā (pī॰ ka॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 281|

aitai tayau kammapathai visaudhayai, ārādhayai maggamisippavaiditaṃ||

tissau imā, bhikkhavai, pārisuddhiyau – kāyakammapārisuddhi, vācākammapārisuddhi, manaukammapārisuddhi|
