tattha katamā kāyakammapārisuddhi? pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī, adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī, kāmaisumicchācārā vairamaṇī| tattha katamā vacīkammapārisuddhi? musāvādā vairamaṇī…pai॰… samphappalāpā vairamaṇī| tattha katamā manaukammapārisuddhi? anabhijjhā abyāpādau sammādiṭṭhi| idaṃ sādhāraṇasuttaṃ|

iti  sādhāraṇāni ca suttāni asādhāraṇāni ca suttāni paṭivijjhitabbāni| paṭivijjhitvā vācāya kāyaina ca suttassa atthau niddisitabbau|

38. tattha katamaṃ saddhindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

yassa  saddhā saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.260; thairagā॰ 507 aṭṭhakanipātai ca passitabbaṃ tathāgatai, acalā suppatiṭṭhitā|

sīlañ‍ca yassa kalyāṇaṃ, ariyakantaṃ pasaṃsitaṃ||

saṅghai pasādau yassatthi, ujubhūtañ‍ca dassanaṃ|

adaliddauti taṃ āhu, amaughaṃ tassa jīvitaṃ||

saddhā vai nandikā ārādhikau, nau tassa saddhauti|

sabbaṃ siyāti bhagavantaṃ, tathārūpau dhammasampasādau||

idaṃ saddhindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ vīriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

ārambhatha ārabhatha (pī॰) passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.185 nik‍kamatha, yuñ‍jatha buddhasāsanai|

dhunātha mac‍cunau sainaṃ, naḷāgāraṃva kuñ‍jarau||

cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, sammappadhānā, idaṃ vīriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ||

tattha katamaṃ satindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

satīmatau sadā bhaddaṃ, bhaddamatthu satīmatau|

satīmatau sadā suvai (saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.238) saiyyau, satīmā sukhamaidhati||

cattārau satipaṭṭhānā vitthāraina kātabbā, idaṃ satindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamaṃ samādhindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

ākaṅkhatau tai naradammasārathi, daivā manussā manasā vicintitaṃ|

sabbaina jañ‍ñā kasiṇāpi pāṇinau, santaṃ samādhiṃ araṇaṃ nisaivatau||

tayaumai, bhikkhavai, samādhī – savitak‍kau savicārau, avitak‍kau vicāramattau, avitak‍kau avicārau| idaṃ samādhindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha  katamaṃ pañ‍ñindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ?

pañ‍ñā hi saiṭṭhā laukasminti vitthāraina|

tissau imā, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñā – sutamayī, cintāmayī, bhāvanāmayī, idaṃ pañ‍ñindriyādhiṭṭhānaṃ suttaṃ, imāni indriyādhiṭṭhānāni asādhāraṇāni suttāni|

39. tattha katamāni sādhāraṇāni indriyādhiṭṭhānāni suttāni?

avītarāgau a॰ ni॰ 6.54 kāmaisu, yassa pañ‍cindriyā mudū|

saddhā sati ca vīriyaṃ, samathau ca vipassanā|

tādisaṃ bhikkhumāsaj‍ja, pubbaiva upahañ‍ñati||

pañ‍cimāni indriyāni| saddhindriyādiindriyaṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ| tīsu avaic‍cappasādai vitthāraina suttaṃ kātabbaṃ| imāni sādhāraṇāni indriyādhiṭṭhānāni suttāni| yaṃ yassa sambandhaṃ kusalassa vā akusalassa vā taina taina adhiṭṭhānaina taṃ suttaṃ niddisitabbaṃ, natthañ‍ñau dhammau niddisitabbau| tattha sādhāraṇaṃ kusalaṃ nāpi kusalaṃ akusalaṃ yathā sādhāraṇāni ca kusalamūlāni sādhāraṇāni ca akusalamūlāni uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ pajahati…pai॰… cattārau sammappadhānā kusalaṃ akusalañ‍ca|

tatthimā uddānagāthā

vitak‍kau hi mamatthikau pamatthikau (pī॰), dadaṃ piyau narau iti|

yau  pāṇamatipātaiti, tīṇi tassa bālalakkhaṇaṃ||

satañ‍caiva sahassānaṃ, yai ca samaṇabrāhmaṇā|

chandā dausā bhayā mauhā, catūhi agatīhi ca||

asubhānupassiṃ viharantaṃ, nimittaisu asubhā ca|

aikampi cai piyaṃ pāṇaṃ, mittā sacai subhāsitā||

pañ‍ñā hi saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, anuñ‍ñā tīṇi indriyāni|

kusalākusalamūlāni ca, tulamatulañ‍ca sambhavaṃ||

kāyaina kusalaṃ karai, tīṇi sucaritāni ca|

subhāsitaṃ uttamamāhu, santau vacīsucaritāni ca||

kāyaina  ca kusalaṃ kayirā, manauduc‍caritāni ca|

kāyānurakkhī ca sadā, tissau ca pārisuddhiyau||

yassa saddhā tathāgatai, samuppādai ca daisitau|

ārambhatha nik‍kamatha, yā ca sammappadhānatā||

satīmatau sadā bhaddaṃ, satipaṭṭhānabhāvanā|

ākaṅkhatau ca anañ‍ñāṇaṃ, yai ca tīṇi samādhayau||

pañ‍ñā  hi saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, tissau pañ‍ñā pakāsitā|

avītarāgau kāmaisu, tathaiva pañ‍cindriyā||

iti thairassa mahākac‍cāyanassa

jambuvanavāsinau paiṭakaupadaisai

tatiyabhūmi suttādhiṭṭhānaṃ nāma|



4. suttavicayacatutthabhūmi

40. tattha  katamau suttavicayau?

tattha  kusalaihi dhammaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi pubbāparasau sādhukaṃ upaparikkhiyati| kiṃnu khau idaṃ suttaṃ ārabhi…pai॰… taihi suttaihi saha adhisan‍naṭṭhaihi yuj‍jati udāhu na yuj‍jatīti?

yathā bhagavā kilaisai ādimhi tattha daisaiti| kiṃ daisitaṃ? taisaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānaṃ udāhu nau daisitanti upaparikkhitabbaṃ| yadi na daisitaṃ bhagavati taisaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānaṃ kusalā dhammā pariyaisitabbā yattha tai akusalā pahānaṃ gacchanti| sacai saman‍naihamānau na labhati| tattha akusalā dhammā apakaḍḍhitabbā vīmaṃsitabbā, saṃkilaisabhāgiyasuttaṃ, yadi kilaisā apakaḍḍhiyantā| yai vā na dainti tattha upaparikkhitabbā ariyamaggadhammā tāsu bhūmīsu kilaisā pahānaṃ gacchanti, udāhu na gacchantīti| yattakā pana kilaisā daisitā| na tattakā ariyadhammā daisitā| yattha kilaisā pahānaṃ gacchanti, tattha yai kilaisā ariyadhammānaṃ paṭipakkhaina na yuj‍janti, tai apakaḍḍhitabbā, sacai apakaḍḍhiyantā yaujanaṃ daiti| tattha aivaṃ vīmaṃsitabbaṃ| dvai tīṇi vā taduttari vā kilaisā aikaina ariyamaggaina pahānaṃ gacchantīti| sacai aivaṃ vīmaṃsiyantā yaujanaṃ daiti, tattha upaparikkhitabbaṃ| paramparāya vā piṭakasampadānaina vā suttassa atthau ca natthau ca| yaṃ vā na sak‍kā suttaṃ  niddisituṃ naiva suttaṃ vicikicchitabbaṃ| aivaṃ yathā ādimhi kusalā dhammā haunti| yai kilaisā  tai pahīnaiyyāti| tai upaparikkhitabbā| purau vā kusalau paṭipakkhaina vā purau daisanā, anūnā anadhikā uggahaitabbā| yathā paṭhamau uttilau yaisamidāni kilaisānaṃ yai ariyadhammā daisitā imai kilaisā imaihi ariyadhammaihi pahīyanti, udāhu nappahīyantīti vicinitabbā| yadi upaparikkhiyamānā yuj‍janti, gahaitabbā| atha na yuj‍janti, yai kilaisā apaṭipakkhā haunti, tai kilaisā aparipakkhitabbā| yai ca ariyadhammā paṭipakkhā haunti, tai ariyadhammā apakaḍḍhitabbā| na hi ariyadhammā anāgāmikilaisappahānaṃ gacchanti, nāpi ariyadhammā sabbakilaisānaṃ pahānāya saṃvattanti| yathā kusalā maittā akusalau rāgau na tu kusalā maittāti kāraitvā akusalassa rāgassa pahānāya sambhavati byāpādau maittāya pahānaṃ gacchati| tasmā ubhau kilaisā upaparikkhitabbā| yau yau ca dhammau upadisiyati kusalau vā akusalau vā sau apakaḍḍhitabbau| sacai tai yuj‍janti apakaḍḍhiyamānau natthi upaparikkhitabbaṃ| dvai vā kilaisā aikaina ariyadhammaina pahīnaiyyāti dvīhi vā ariyadhammaihi aikau vā kilaisau pahīyatīti|

atha vā aivampi upaparikkhiyamānaṃ yuj‍jati, tattha vīmaṃsitabbaṃ vā yathā yuj‍jati tattha vīmaṃsitabbaṃ vā, yathā nanu sak‍kā suttaṃ niddisituṃ, na hi suttai vicikicchitabbaṃ| kilaisau  maṃ ariyadhammaisu daisitaisu ubhayatau upaparikkhitabbaṃ| kira yai vā imai kilaisā daisitā yai ca ariyadhammā daisitā gāthāya vā byākaraṇaina vā, kiṃ nu khau imai kilaisā imaihi ariyadhammaihi pahīyanti, udāhu nappahīyanti? imai vā ariyadhammā imaisaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānāya saṃvattantīti| kiñ‍cāpi kusalaihi dhammaihi akusalā dhammā pahānaṃ gacchanti| na tu sabbaihi ariyadhammaihi sabbākusalā pahānaṃ gacchanti| yathā maittā kusalau akusalau ca rāgau na tu kusalā maittā akusalau rāgauti kāraitvā maittāya rāgau pahānaṃ, byāpādau maittāya pahānaṃ gacchanti| aivaṃ kilaisauti kāraitvā suttaina pahānaṃ gacchati| na suttau dhammauti kāraitvā sabbaṃ kilaisassa pahānāya saṃvattati| yaṃ tu suttassa ariyadhammau saṃkilaisapaṭipakkhau, sau taina pahānaṃ gacchatīti|

41. tattha kusalai daisitai suttai byākaraṇai vā saṃkilaisā na yuj‍janti ariyadhammā vā, tai mahāpadaisai niddisitabbāvayavaina apakaḍḍhitabbā| tattha kilaisaihi ca daisitaihi ariyadhammaisu ca yadipi taina ariyadhammaina tai kilaisā pahānaṃ gacchanti| tatthapi uttari upaparikkhitabbaṃ| kaina kāraṇaina aitai kilaisā pajahitabbā, kaina kāraṇaina ariyadhammā daisitāti? yaina yaina vā ākāraina ariyadhammā daisitā, taina taina pakāraina ayaṃ kilaisau ṭhitau| atthi hi aikau kilaisau, taina vā ariyadhammā na añ‍ñathā añ‍ñathā pahātabbau, yathā diṭṭhi rāgau avij‍jā ca dassanaina pahātabbā| sā cai aivañ‍ca avij‍jā bhāvanāya bhūmi vā dhammā bhāvanāya pahātabbā| sāyaiva uddhaṃbhāgiyaṃ asaṅkhatadassanāya vimuttiyā animittaina caitausamādhinā  amanasikāraina pahīyati| aivaṃ sātthaṃ sabyañ‍janaṃ upaparikkhitabbaṃ| yai dassanaina pahātabbā kilaisā dassanākāraina ariyadhammau daisitau, bhāvanāya  pahātabbā bhāvanākāraina ariyadhammau daisitau, patisaivanā pahātabbā patisaivanākāraina ariyadhammau daisitau, aivaṃ vinaudanapahātabbā yāva satta āsavā kātabbā, yāvañ‍ñathā| añ‍ñathā haisa dhammau pahātabbau añ‍ñainākāraina ariyadhammau daisitau, sau ariyadhammau añ‍ñathā pariyaisitabbau| yadi ayaṃ dhammau pariyaisatau yau ca daisaiti yaina yainākāraina, sau ariyadhammau pariyaisitabbau, tainākāraina kilaisau pahīyati| sau tattha upaparikkhitabbau| atha na yuj‍jati yadi hi taina suttaina vihitaṃ suttaṃ vīmaṃsitabbaṃ| yathā yuj‍jati, tathā gahaitabbaṃ| yathā na yuj‍jati, tathā na gahaitabbaṃ, addhā aitaṃ bhagavatā na bhāsitaṃ, āyasmatā vā duggahitaṃ, yathā mahāpadaisai niddisitabbaṃ, bhagavatā yathābhūtaṃ daisitaṃ, yau ca dhammau daisitau kusalau ca akusalau ca tassa dhammassa pac‍cayau pariyaisitabbau| na hi pac‍cayā vinā dhammau appac‍cayau uppaj‍jati| tattha kau ākārau pariyaisanāya?

tattha tathārūpaṃ sahaitu sappac‍cayaṃ sauyaṃ dhammau vuttauti idaṃ vīmaṃsitabbaṃ| sau ca pac‍cayau tividhau – mudu majjhau adhimattau| tattha mudumhi pac‍cayai mududhammau gahaitabbau, aivaṃ satyaisa pac‍cayau duvidhau paraṃparāpac‍cayau ca samanantarapac‍cayau ca| sau pac‍cayau mudutaina byādhimattaṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? añ‍ñataraupi pac‍cayau añ‍ñaihi pac‍cayaihi pariyattiṃ vā pāripūriṃ vā gacchati| tattha yau dhammau daisitau, tassa dhammassa  aitaina vā kāraṇaina vā haitu pariyaisitabbau| yathā pac‍cayau haitunā pac‍cayaina ca, sau tassa dhammassa nissandau pariyaisitabbau| yathā niddiṭṭhau adhiṭṭhānai padhānaṃ pariyaisati, sau pac‍cayau pariyaisitabbau| na hi mudussa dhammassa adhimattau nissandau adhimattassa vā nissandassa mududhammau, atha mudussa mudu majjhāya majjhau adhimattassa adhimattau yuj‍jati, taṃ gahaitabbaṃ, atha na yuj‍jati na gahaitabbaṃ| yañ‍ca bhagavā ārabhati dhammaṃ daisaituṃ, taṃyaiva dhammaṃ majjhantapariyausānaṃ daisaiti, yathā suttādhiṭṭhānai dhammā ādimhi niddisati, taṃyaiva bahu tassa suttassa pariyausānaṃ| tassa hi dhammassa vasaina taṃ suttaṃ hauti gāthā vā byākaraṇaṃ khuddakaṃ mahantaṃ vā, yathā pana duvidhā anurūpanti vā thapanā ca daisanāthapanā| rūpantipi dhammassa pariyaisitabbā| yathā ca bhagavatā pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ saṃvaraṇaṃ daisitaṃ taṇhāya niggahaṇatthaṃ icchāva hauti| daisaiti yathā gaupālakaupamai suttai añ‍ñaihipi suttaihi bhagavā bhāsati icchāva hauti majjhimanikāyai vitak‍kau ayaṃ bhagavatau daisanānurūpanti iti sau dhammau añ‍ñaisupi vaiyyākaraṇaisu pariyaisitabbau| na hi aikaṃ hi suttai daṭṭhabbau| yuj‍janaṃ taṃ gahaitabbaṃ|

42. tattha katamaṃ anuñ‍ñātaṃ? yaṃ kiñ‍ci suttaṃ bhagavatā na bhāsitaṃ tañ‍ca suttaisuyaiva  ndissati, aivamaitaṃ dhāraitabbaṃ| yathā asukaina bhāsitanti, taṃ suttaṃ vīmaṃsitabbaṃ| kiṃ nu khau imaṃ suttaṃ  anuñ‍ñātaṃ khamaṃ bhagavatau udāhu nānuñ‍ñātaṃ khamaṃ, kiñ‍ci rūpañ‍ca suttaṃ bhagavatau anuñ‍ñātaṃ khamaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpañ‍ca nānuñ‍ñātaṃ khamaṃ? yaṃ sabbasau anautāraitvā dasabalau gaucaraṃ daisaiti, taṃ sabbaṃ suttaṃ bhagavatau nānuñ‍ñātaṃ khamaṃ| atthipi sau sāvakau dasabalānaṃ gaucaraṃ jānāti audhisau anaudhisau, taṃ pana balaṃ sabbasau na jānāti añ‍ñathā nāma savanaina, yathā āyasmatā sāriputtaina yaina brāhmaṇau auvaditau, tassa āyasmatau natthi indriyabalavaimattañāṇaṃ, taina puggalaparau puggalau parauparañ‍ca (pī॰) parañ‍ca taṃ ajānantau sati uttarikaraṇīyai uppāditau, sau bhagavatā apasāditau| yathāva āyasmā mahākassapau bhāginaiyyaṃ auvadati anantariyasaman‍nāgatau iddhipāṭihīraina aṅguliyau adīpaitvā  yaṃ sabbaisaṃ dhammānaṃ kammasamādānānaṃ haitusau ṭhānasau yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇaṃ, tassa āyasmatau saṃvij‍jatai, taina naṃ auvadati, taṃ bhagavā karauti|

‘‘sacaipi dasa paj‍jautai, dhārayissasi kassapa|

naiva dakkhati rūpāni, cakkhu tassa na vij‍jatī’’ti||

api ca khau yathā dūtau rājavacanaina sattamanusāsati, aivaṃ saisānugau añ‍ñātakaṃ ghausaṃ paraisaṃ daisaiti| anuñ‍ñātakhamasuttaṃ gahaitabbaṃ| ananuñ‍ñātakhamaṃ na gahaitabbaṃ|

tattha katamau suttasaṅkarau? pañ‍cavidhaṃ  suttaṃ, saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ vāsanābhāgiyaṃ dassanabhāgiyaṃ bhāvanābhāgiyaṃ asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ| añ‍ñaṃ ārādhaiyya añ‍ñaṃ daisaiti añ‍ñassa ca suttassa atthaṃ añ‍ñamhi suttai niddisati| suttassa vā hi anaikākāraṃ atthaṃ niddisati| ariyadhammasādhanai atthaṃ vivarati| vāsanābhāgiyassa atthaṃ dassanabhāgiyaisu niddisati| aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ atthaṃ uddhaṃbhāgiyaisu niddisati| mudumajjhānaṃ indriyānaṃ adhimattaisu suttaisu niddisati| iti ayaṃ suttaṃ sambhaidaṃ haitunā ca nissandaina ca phalaina ca niddaisaina ca mudumajjhādhimattatāyapi ca atthaina ca byañ‍janaina ca yau sambhaidau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati suttasaṅkarau| yau asambhaidau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati suttavicayau|

tatthāyaṃ uddānagāthā

purimānaṃ  akkhaṇḍaṃ, yathābhūtassa pac‍cayau|

nissandau vāsanāsaddhi, anuñ‍ñā suttasaṅkarau||

thairassa mahākac‍cāyanassa

suttavicayau nāma catutthabhūmi|



5. pañ‍camabhūmi

43. tattha  katamau hāravibhaṅgau? yattha sauḷasa hārā akkharasau bhaidaṃ gacchanti| tattha ādimhi daisanāhārau| tattha ayaṃ gāthā kusalā vā akusalā vā sac‍cāni vā sac‍caikadaisau vā| kiṃ daisitanti? suttai vīmaṃsā daisanāhārau| yathā ariyasac‍cāni nikkhaipau cattāri sac‍cāni  sādhāraṇāni asādhāraṇāni ca| yāni ca aṭṭhārasa padāni dukkhatau satta padāni saṅkhaipaina kāyikaina caitasikaina dukkhaina, appiyasampayaugaina piyavippayaugaina ca tīhi ca saṅkhatāhi| tattha tīṇi saṅkhatalakkhaṇāni tissau dukkhatā uppādau saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, saṅkhāradukkhatāya dukkhatā ca saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, vipariṇāmadukkhatāya dukkhatāti añ‍ñathatthaṃ ca saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, dukkhadukkhatāya ca dukkhatā, imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇānaṃ tīsu vaidanābhūmīsu adukkhamasukhā vaidanā uppādau saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, saṅkhāradukkhatāya ca dukkhatā tayau saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, sukhā vaidanāya ca vipariṇāmadukkhatāya ca dukkhatāti añ‍ñathattaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, dukkhāvaidanā dukkhadukkhatā ca dukkhatā imamhi imaisu navapadaisu paṭhamakaisu sattasu padaisu sauḷasasu padaisu dukkhā pariyaisitabbā, aikādasa dukkhatāya ca lakkhaṇaṃ  niddaisai niddiṭṭhaṃ| pātubhāvalakkhaṇā jātiyā ca pātubhāvacutilakkhaṇau cutauti vitthāraina pan‍narasapadāni kattabbāni, aivaṃ sādhāraṇāni asādhāraṇāni ca sattasu dasasu padaisu sañ‍ñāsa tividhai ca sāsanappaṭṭhānai aṭṭhārasavidhaisu ca suttādhiṭṭhānaisu dasavidhaisu ca suttavidhaiyyaisu sauḷasavidhaisu ca hāraisu aikavīsatividhāya ca pavicayavīmaṃsāyāti idaṃ daisitaṃ| yathābhūtañ‍ca daisitanti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati daisanāhārau|

44. tattha katamau vicayau hārau?

padaṃ pañhā ca pucchā ca, kiṃ pubbaṃ kiñ‍ca pacchimaṃ|

anugīti sā ca vicayau, hārau vicayauti niddiṭṭhau||

padanti  paṭhamaṃ padaṃ| tassa kau atthau? yaṃ bhagavā puṭṭhau āyasmatā ajitaina taṃ gahaitabbaṃ, katipadāni puṭṭhāni yathākiṃ kainassu nivutau laukauti gāthā, imāni katipadāni cattāri iti visaj‍janāya pucchā| yattakaihi padaihi bhagavatā visaj‍jitāni padāni iti pucchāya ca yā padānaṃ saṅkāsanā, idaṃ vuc‍cati padanti|

pañhāti imāni cattāri padāni| kati pañhā? aikau vā dvai vā taduttari vā imāni cattāri padāni aikau pañhau, atthānuparivatti byañ‍janaṃ hauti, sambahulānipi padāni aikamaivatthaṃ pucchati| imāni cattāri padāni anuparivattīni taṃ byañ‍janaina aikau pañhauva hauti| kainassu  nivutau laukauti  laukaṃ sandhāya pucchati, kainassu nappakāsati kissābhilaipanaṃ brūsīti taṃyaiva pucchati| kiṃsu tassa mahabbhayanti taṃyaiva pucchati| aivaṃ atthānuparivatti byañ‍janaṃ aikau pañhau hauti, sau pañhau catubbidhau aikaṃsabyākaraṇīyau vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau paṭipucchābyākaraṇīyau ṭhapaniyauti| tattha cakkhu anic‍canti aikaṃsabyākaraṇīyau, yaṃ anic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau, siyā anic‍caṃ na cakkhu, yānipi āyatanāni ca na cakkhu, tānipi anic‍canti na cakkhuyaiva, ayaṃ vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau, yaṃ cakkhu taṃ cakkhundriyaṃ naiti paṭipucchābyākaraṇīyau, taṃ cakkhu tathāgatauti ṭhapaniyau| añ‍ñatra cakkhunāti ṭhapaniyau pañhau| idaṃ pañhaṃ bhagavā kiṃ pucchitau, laukassa saṃkilaisau pucchitau| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? tividhau hi saṃkilaisau taṇhāsaṃkilaisau ca diṭṭhisaṃkilaisau ca duc‍caritasaṃkilaisau ca| tattha avij‍jāya nivutauti avij‍jaṃ dassaiti, jappāti taṇhaṃ dassaiti, mahabbhayanti akusalassa kammassa vipākaṃ dassaiti, sautaṃ nāma sukhavaidanīyassa kammassa dukkhavaidanīyau vipākau bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jatīti bhagavā visaj‍jaiti, catūhi yau padaihi avij‍jāya nivutau laukauti…pai॰… aivaṃ vuc‍cati|

45. taduttari paṭipucchati, savanti sabbadhi sautāti gāthā, cattāri padāni pucchati taṃ bhagavā dvīhi padaihi visaj‍jaiti|

yāni  sautāni laukasmiṃ, sati taisaṃ nivāraṇaṃ|

sautānaṃ saṃvaraṃ brūmi, pañ‍ñāyaitai pidhīyarai||

imāni cattāri padāni dvīhi padaihi visaj‍jaiti| idaṃ padanti pucchitau, tassa saṃkiliṭṭhassa  laukassa vaudānaṃ pucchitau, sautāni cha taṇhākāyā bahulādhivacanaina niddiṭṭhā bhavanti sabbaihi āyatanaihi| tāni sautāni kaina nivāriyantīti pariyuṭṭhānapahānaṃ pucchati, kaina sautā pidhīyaraiti anusayasamugghātaṃ pucchati| tattha bhagavā chasu dvāraisu satiyā daisaiti, yau hi sampajānau viharati satidauvārikai ca tassa indriyāni guttāni sambhavanti| tattha guttaisu indriyaisu yā yā vipassanā, sā sā taisaṃ taisaṃ sautānaṃ tassā ca avij‍jāya yau laukau nivutau ac‍cantapahānāya saṃvattati| aivaṃ sautāni pihitānipi bhavanti tatau uttari pucchati|

pañ‍ñā  ca sati ca nāmarūpassa khau tassa bhagavantaṃ puṭṭhumāgamma katthaitaṃ upasammati imāni cattāri padāni bhagavā aikaina padaina visaj‍jaiti|

yamaitaṃ pañhaṃ apucchi pucchasai pañhaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 1043, ajita taṃ vadāmi tai…pai॰…|

viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhaina, aitthaitaṃ upasammati||

iminā pañhaina kiṃ pucchati? anupādisaisanibbānadhātuṃ pucchati, taṃ bhagavā anupādisaisāya nibbānadhātuyā visaj‍jaiti| tattha  paṭhamaina pañhaina saṃkilaisaṃ pucchati| dutiyaina pañhaina vaudānaṃ pucchati| tatiyaina pañhaina saupādisaisanibbānadhātuṃ pucchati| catutthaina pañhaina anupādisaisanibbānadhātuṃ paṭipucchati tatau uttari paṭipucchati|

yai ca saṅkhātadhammāsai, yai ca saikhā saikkhā (ka॰) passa su॰ ni॰ 1044 puthū idha|

taisaṃ mai nipakau iriyaṃ, puṭṭhau pabrūhi mārisa||

imāni cattāri padāni pucchati| kati ca pana tai pañhai saṅkhātadhammā ca arahantā saikkhā ca? kiṃ pubbaṃ kiñ‍ca pacchimanti ayamatthau| tattha kataraṃ paṭhamaṃ pucchati, kataraṃ pacchā? arahantaṃ paṭhamaṃ pucchati| saikkhadhammai tattha kaina padaina saṅkhātadhammāti arahantau gahitā, puthūti saikkhā gahitā| taisaṃ mai nipakauti sādhāraṇaṃ padaṃ bhagavantaṃ pucchati| tassa sādhāraṇāni ca asādhāraṇāni ca pañhaisu pucchitabbāni| taṃ bhagavā visaj‍jaiti| na tathā puṭṭhaṃ, paṭhamaṃ puṭṭhaṃ, taṃ pacchā visaj‍jaiti| yaṃ pacchā pucchitaṃ paṭhamaṃ visaj‍jaiti| kiñ‍ca idaṃ pucchitaṃ visuddhānaṃ visujjhantānañ‍ca kā iriyāti idaṃ pucchi, taṃ kāmaisu nābhigijjhaiyya| manasānāvilau siyāti pariyuṭṭhānāni vitak‍kaina ca bhagavā nivāraiti, dvai pana vitak‍kaanāvilatāya  pariyuṭṭhānaṃ, yathā nīvaraṇaisu niddiṭṭhaṃ| kusalā sabbadhammaisūti arahantaṃ visaj‍jaiti|

kainassu tarati aughanti gāthā, imāni cattāri padāni| cattārauyaiva pañhā| kiṃ  kāraṇaṃ, na hi aittha atthānuparivatti byañ‍janaṃ yathānuparivatthivaj‍jaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) yathā paṭhamaṃ ajitapañhaisu, tassa na aikaṃsaina bahūni visaj‍janāni, bahukā pañhā, aikauva na cāpi, sabbai pucchati, pubbai visaj‍jitau, yathā catutthau ajitaupañhai, yaṃ  aittha yathābhūtaṃ pariyaisanāpadabandhaina visaj‍janāyau aivaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pariyaisati| yau puna aittha yaṃ aivaṃ pucchati tattha ayamākārau pucchanāyaṃ antaujaṭā bahijaṭāti gāthā saṃ॰ ni॰ 1.29 pucchitavisaj‍janāya maggitabbā| kathaṃ visaj‍jitāti bhagavāti visaj‍jaiti? sīlai patiṭṭhāya narau sapañ‍ñauti gāthā| tattha cittabhāvanāya samathā, pañ‍ñābhāvanāya vipassanā| tattha aivaṃ anumīyati, yai dhammā samathaina ca vipassanāya ca pahīyanti, tai imai antaujaṭā bahijaṭā| tattha visaj‍janaṃ samathaina rāgau pahīyati, vipassanāya avij‍jā| ajjhattavatthukau rāgau antaujaṭā, bāhiravatthukau rāgau bahijaṭā| ajjhattavatthukā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi, ayaṃ antaujaṭā| aikasaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni ca bāhiravatthukāni bahijaṭā, yā hi ajjhattavatthukā yā diṭṭhibhāgiyaina bhavissati, ayaṃ jaṭā| tathā saṃkhittaina yā kāci ajjhattavatthukā taṇhā ca diṭṭhi ca, ayaṃ antaujaṭā| yā kāci bāhiravatthukā taṇhā ca diṭṭhi ca, ayaṃ bahijaṭā|

yathā daivatā bhagavantaṃ pucchati ‘‘catucak‍kaṃ navadvāra’’nti gāthā saṃyuttanikāyai| tattha bhagavā visaj‍jaiti ‘‘chaitvā naddhiṃ varattaṃ cā’’ti gāthā, idaṃ bhagavā dukkhaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ visaj‍jaiti| imāya visaj‍janāya bhagavā anumīyati  kilaisai aittha purimāya gāthāya niddisitabbaina| taṃ hi catucak‍kanti cattārau vā hatthapādā| navadvāranti nava vaṇamukhāni| yathā catucak‍kanti cattārau upādānā, upādānappac‍cayā bhavau, upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau| navadvāranti nava mānavidhā, mānajātikāya hi dukkhaṃ saiyyainamhi parasau tīṇi tikāni puṇṇaṃ| tikaina saṃyuttaṃ hi pañ‍cakāmaguṇikau rāgau| tattha naddhīti taṇhā visaj‍jīyati| varattanti mānaṃ visaj‍jaiti, icchā laubhau ca pāpakauti pañ‍cakāmaguṇikau rāgau| tattha visamalaubhau pāpakauti niddisiyati samūlataṇhanti| añ‍ñāṇamūlakā taṇhāti añ‍ñāṇamūlakā taṇhā, taṇhāya ca diṭṭhiyā ca pahānaṃ| yai ca puna añ‍ñaipi kaici catucak‍kayaugaina tainaiva kāraṇaina ca yuj‍janti, saṃsāragāminau dhammā sabbai niddisitabbā| tatthāyaṃ gāthā visaj‍janā pucchāya  ca visaj‍janāya samaiti samaṃti (pī॰)| yaṃ yadi sandaina atha saha byākaraṇaina anugītiyaṃ ca sau vicayauti bhagavā yattakāni padāni nikkhipati, tattakaihi anugāyati|

46. aṭṭhahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahati kātumarahati (pī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 8.16| imāni aṭṭha padāni nikkhittāni| chahi padaihi bhagavā anugāyati|

‘‘yau vai na byathati byāthati (ka॰) patvā, parisaṃ uggavādiniṃ|

na ca hāpaiti vacanaṃ, na ca chādaiti sāsanaṃ||

‘‘asandiddhiṃ ca bhaṇati, pucchitau na ca kuppati|

sa vai tādisakau bhikkhu, dūtaiyyaṃ gantumarahatī’’ti||

tattha pana bhagavā yattakāni padāni nikkhipati, tattakaihi anugāyati| sattahi, bhikkhavai, aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau kalyāṇamittau piyau garubhāvanīyauti vitthāraina, idaṃ bhagavā sattahi padaihi anugāyati| iti bahussutavā anugāyati, appatarakathaṃ padaṃ vā nikkhaipau, bahussutavā nava padāni nikkhaipau, appatarikā anugītiyā bahutarikā anugāyati| ayaṃ vuc‍cati tai anugīti ca vicayau, ayaṃ  vicayau nāma hārau|

tattha katamau yuttihārau?

sabbaisaṃ hārānaṃ, yā bhūmī yau ca gaucarau taisaṃ|

yuttāyutti parikkhā, hārau yuttīti niddiṭṭhau||

hārānaṃ sauḷasan‍naṃ yathā daisanā yathā vicayau yau ca niddisiyati, ayaṃ niddaisau| ayaṃ pucchā suttaisu na yuj‍jatīti yā tattha vīmaṃsā, ayaṃ yutti|

yathā hi sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti, atthi haitu atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya, sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti, atthi haitu atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā| sīlavatā, ānanda, puggalaina na vaiyyākaraṇiyā kinti mai vippaṭisārau uppādaiyya…pai॰… abyākaraṇaṃ kattabbaṃ, ayaṃ visuddhiyā maggau| tassa haitu kau pac‍cayau, sīlakkhandhassa  cattāri cattāri haitu ca pac‍cayau ca| sappurisasaṃsaivau yau ca patirūpadaisavāsau ca, ayaṃ upādāpac‍cayatā sappac‍cayau| yaṃ paurāṇakammaṃ assa vipākau pac‍cayau, tāya pac‍cayāya attasammāpaṇidhi, ayaṃ  haitu| iti sīlakkhandhau sahaitu sappac‍cayauti idaṃ laukikaṃ sīlaṃ|

yaṃ pana laukuttaraṃ sīlaṃ, tassa tīṇi indriyāni pac‍cayau – saddhindriyaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ samādhindriyaṃ – ayaṃ pac‍cayau| satindriyañ‍ca pañ‍ñindriyañ‍ca haitu| pañ‍ñāya nibbaidhagāminiyā, yaṃ sīlaṃ jāyati| sautāpan‍nassa ca sīlaṃ taināyaṃ haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau| yaṃ puna samādhinau passaddhi ca pīti ca pāmauj‍jaṃ pac‍cayau| yaṃ sukhaṃ haitu taina samādhikkhandhau sahaitu sappac‍cayau| yaṃ  samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ pañ‍ñā| tassa parataughausau ajjhattaṃ ca yaunisau manasikārau haitu ca pac‍cayau ca, iti imai tayau khandhā sahaitū sappac‍cayā aivaṃ satta pañ‍ñā| sattabyākaraṇīsu ca suttaisu na yuj‍jati| ayaṃ yuttihārau| sau catūsu mahāpadaisaisu daṭṭhabbau|

47. tattha katamaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ?

dhammaṃ daisaiti jinau, tassa ca dhammassa yaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ|

iti yāva sabbadhammā, aisau hārau padaṭṭhānau||

tattha pañ‍cakāmaguṇā kāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci kāmarāgau uppaj‍jati uppan‍nau vā uppaj‍jissati vā, aitaisu yaipi pañ‍casu rūpaisu āyatanaisu nāñ‍ñatra aitaihi kāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānanti| vuc‍catai, taina pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā kāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍cindriyāni rūparāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| manindriyaṃ bhavarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍cakkhandhā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| aikasaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni diṭṭhirāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāmadhātu kāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| arūpadhātu arūparāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| sukhasañ‍ñā kāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| byāpādasañ‍ñā byāpādassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| asampajañ‍ñatā sammauhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| nava āghātavatthūni byāpādassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| navavidhaṃ mānaṃ navamānaṃ vidhamānassa (pī॰ ka॰) mānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| sukhā vaidanā rāgānusayassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| dukkhā vaidanā paṭighānusayassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| adukkhamasukhā vaidanā avij‍jānusayassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| attavādupādānañ‍ca musāvādau ca laubhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| pāṇātipātau ca pisuṇavācā ca pharusavācā ca byāpādassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| micchattañ‍ca samphappalāpau ca mauhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| bhavaṃ bhaugañ‍ca  vaukārau ahaṃkārassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| bāhirānaṃ pariggahau  mamaṃkārassa  padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāyassa saṅgaṃ kāyavaṅkaṃ (pī॰) diṭṭhiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāyikadausau dausassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāyikakāsāvau laubhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| yau yau vā pana dhammau yaina yaina ārammaṇaina uppaj‍jati sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaina vā dhammādhiṭṭhānaina vā anusayanaina vā, sau dhammau tassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| taina sārammaṇaina sau dhammau uppaj‍jati|

yathā manussau purimassa padassa padaṭṭhānaṃ alabhantau dutiyaṃ padaṃ uddharati, sau pacchānupadaṃ saṃharati| yadi pana yau na dutiyapadassa padaṭṭhānaṃ labhati, aparaṃ padaṃ uddharati| tassa yau caisau pac‍cayau bhavati| aivaṃ dhammau kusalau vā akusalau vā abyākatau vā padaṭṭhānaṃ alabhantau na pavattati| yathā payuttassa dhammassa yaunilābhau yaunisau lābhau (pī॰), ayaṃ vuc‍cati padaṭṭhānau hārau|

48. tattha katamau lakkhaṇau hārau?

vuttamhi aikadhammai, yai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā taina|

sabbai bhavanti vuttā, sau hārau lakkhaṇau nāma||

yaisañ‍ca susamāraddhā, nic‍caṃ kāyagatāsatīti gāthāya vuttāya kāyagatāsatiyā vuttā vaidanāgatā cittagatā dhammagatā ca sati catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ aikaina satipaṭṭhānaina| na hi cittaṃ aikasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyā pavattati, nānāsu gatīsu pavattati, kāyagatāsatiyā vuttāya vuttā vaidanāgatā cittadhammagatā ca| na hi kāyagatāsatiyā bhāvitāya satipaṭṭhānā cattārau bhāvanāpāripūriṃ na gacchanti| aivaṃ  tassadisaisu dhammaisu vuttaisu sabbadhammā vuttā ca bhavanti|

sacittapariyaudāpanaṃ, aitaṃ buddhāna sāsananti gāthā caitasikā dhammā vuttā, cittai rūpaṃ vuttaṃ| idaṃ nāmarūpaṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ| tatau sacittapariyaudāpanā yaṃ yaṃ audapaiti, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaina audapaiti, sau maggau| yatau audapanā, sau niraudhau| cakkhuṃ ca paṭic‍ca rūpai ca uppaj‍jati cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, tattha sahajātā vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā phassau manasikārau aitai tai dhammā aikalakkhaṇā uppādalakkhaṇaina| yau ca rūpai nibbindati, vaidanāya sau nibbindati, sañ‍ñāsaṅkhāraviñ‍ñāṇaisupi sau nibbindati| iti  yai aikalakkhaṇā dhammā, taisaṃ aikamhi dhammai niddiṭṭhai sabbai dhammā niddiṭṭhā haunti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati lakkhaṇau hārau|

tattha  katamau catubyūhau hārau?

nirutti adhippāyau ca, byañ‍janā daisanāya ca|

suttatthau pubbāparasandhi, aisau hārau catubyūhau||

tattha katamā nirutti, sā kathaṃ pariyaisitabbā passitabbā (pī॰ ka॰)? yathā vuttaṃ bhagavatā aikādasahi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgatau bhikkhu khippaṃ dhammaisu mahattaṃ pāpuṇāti, atthakusalau ca hauti, dhammakusalau ca hauti, niruttikusalau ca hauti, itthādhivacanakusalau ca hauti, purisādhivacanakusalau ca, vipurisādhivacanakusalau ca, atītādhivacanakusalau ca, anāgatādhivacanakusalau ca, pac‍cuppan‍nādhivacanakusalau ca| aikādhippāyaina kusalau nānādhippāyaina kusalau| kimhi  daisitaṃ, atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ| itthādhivacanaina purisādhivacanaina vipurisādhivacanaina sabbaṃ yathāsuttaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ| taṃ byañ‍janatau niruttikausal‍latau yau yaṃ suttassa suniruttidun‍niruttitaṃ avaikkhati, idaṃ aivaṃ niraupayitabbaṃ| idampi na niraupayitabbaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍catai niruttikausal‍laṃ|

49. tattha katamaṃ adhippāyakausal‍laṃ? yathādaisitassa suttassa sabbassa vāraṃ gacchati imaina bhagavatā daisitabbanti| yathā kiṃ appamādau amataṃ padaṃ, pamādau mac‍cunau padanti gāthā| aittha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai asītimaiva ākaṅkhanti tai appamattā viharissanti, ayaṃ adhippāyau|

yaugassa kālaṃ na nivattati yā ca, sau na tattha pāpintavai bhavanti|

vaidanāmaggaisinā vaidanāmaggaṃ isinā (pī॰) pavaiditaṃ, dhutarajāsavā dukkhā pamaukkhātā||

aittha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai dukkhai nāssādakā dukkhaina sādhakā (pī॰), tai vīriyamārabhissanti dukkhakkhayāyāti| ayaṃ tattha bhagavatau adhippāyau| iti gāthāya vā byākaraṇaina vā daisitai iminā suttaina sādhakā, yau  aivaṃ dhammānudhammaṃ paṭipaj‍jatīti sau adhippāyau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati daisanādhippāyau|

tattha katamau pubbāparasandhi? yaṃ  gāthāyaṃ vā suttaisu vā padāni asīti tāni bhavanti aivaṃ vā aivamaiti tassā gāthāya suttassa vā yāni purimāni padāni yāni ca pacchimakāni, tāni samausāraitabbāni| aivaṃ sau pubbāparaina sandhi ñāyati| yā aikā samāraddhā gāthā dvai tīṇi vā tassa maikadaisai bhāsitānaṃ abhāsitāhi gāthāhi aniddiṭṭhau atthau bhavati tadupadhāritabbaṃ| yaṃva sabbā yaṃ vattabbaṃ (pī॰) itissa pariyaisamānassa pariyaisanā kaṅkhā, tassa vā puggalassa pañ‍ñattīnaṃ aparai pariyaisitabbaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍catai pubbāparaina sandhi| kausal‍lanti vatthutau nidānakausal‍laṃ| byañ‍janatau niruttikausal‍laṃ| daisanādhippāyakausal‍laṃ| pubbāparaina sandhikausal‍laṃ| tattha tassa gāthā pariyaisitā nidānaṃ vā| upalabbhituṃ na atthau niddisitabbau vatthutau nidānakausal‍laṃ atthakausal‍laṃ imaihi catūhi padaihi atthau pariyaisiyantau yathābhūtaṃ pariyiṭṭhau hauti| atha ca sabbau vatthutau vā nidānaina vā yau adhippāyau byañ‍janau nirutti sandhi ca anuttarau aisau pubbāparaina aivaṃ suttatthaina daisitabbaṃ| ayaṃ catubyūhau hārau|

50. tattha katamau āvaṭṭau hārau?

aikamhi padaṭṭhānai, pariyaisati saisakaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ|

āvaṭṭati paṭipakkhai, āvaṭṭau nāma sau hārau||

yathā kiṃ un‍naḷānaṃ pamattānanti gāthāyau| yaṃ pamādau, idaṃ kissa padaṭṭhānaṃ? kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ ausaggassa| kusaladhammausaggau pana kissa padaṭṭhānaṃ? akusaladhammapaṭisaivanāya| kissa padaṭṭhānaṃ, kusaladhammapaṭisaivanāya? kissa padaṭṭhānaṃ, kilaisavatthupaṭisaivanāya? iti pamādaina mauhapakkhiyā diṭṭhi avij‍jā chandarāgapakkhiyā| tattha taṇhā ca diṭṭhi cattārau āsavā taṇhā kāmāsavau ca bhavāsavau ca diṭṭhāsavau ca avij‍jāsavau ca| tattha cittai atthīti diṭṭhi caitasikaisu nic‍canti pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu ajjhāvahanaina kāmāsavau, upapattīsu āsatti bhavāsavau| tattha rūpakāyau  kāmāsavassa bhavāsavassa ca padaṭṭhānaṃ| nāmakāyau diṭṭhāsavassa avij‍jāsavassa ca padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha al‍liyanāya ajjhattavāhanaṃ kāmāsavassa lakkhaṇaṃ| patthanaganthanaabhisaṅkhārakāyasaṅkhāraṇaṃ bhavāsavassa lakkhaṇaṃ, abhinivaisau ca parāmāsau ca diṭṭhāsavassa lakkhaṇaṃ| appaṭivaidhau dhammaisu asampajañ‍ñā ca avij‍jāsavassa lakkhaṇaṃ| imai cattārau āsavā cattāri upādānāni| kāmāsavau kāmupādānaṃ, bhavāsavau bhavupādānaṃ, diṭṭhāsavau diṭṭhupādānaṃ, avij‍jāsavau attavādupādānaṃ, imaihi catūhi upādānaihi pañ‍cakkhandhā| tattha avij‍jāsavau cittai pahātabbau, sau cittai cittānupassissa pahīyati| diṭṭhāsavau dhammaisu pahātabbau, sau dhammaisu  dhammānupassissa pahīyati| bhavāsavau āsattiyā pahātabbau, sau vaidanāsu vaidanānupassissa pahīyati| kāmāsavau pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu pahātabbau, sau kāyai kāyānupassissa pahīyati| tattha kāyānupassanā dukkhamariyasac‍caṃ bhajati| vaidanānupassanā pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ pac‍cayau sukhindriyassa  dukkhindriyassa saumanassindriyassa daumanassindriyassa upaikkhindriyassa, sattakilaisaupacārau taina samudayaṃ bhajati| cittai cittānupassanā niraudhaṃ bhajati| dhammaisu dhammānupassanā maggaṃ bhajati| tainassa catūsu ca dassanaina tassaiva sabbai pahīyanti, yaina niddiṭṭhā paṭhamaṃ un‍naḷānaṃ pamattānaṃ taisaṃ vaḍḍhanti āsavā| jānatau hi passatau āsavānaṃ khayau dukkhaṃ samudayau niraudhau maggau hi akusalā dhammā| aivaṃ pariyaisitabbā| yāva tassa akusalassa gati tatau paṭipakkhaina akusalai dhammai pariyaisati taisaṃ kilaisānaṃ hāraina āvaṭṭati| ayaṃ vuc‍catai āvaṭṭau hārau| aivaṃ suk‍kāpi dhammā pariyaisitabbā| akusaladhammai āgamissa|

tattha āvaṭṭassa hārassa ayaṃ bhūmi sati upaṭṭhānā ca vipal‍lāsā ca cattāri ñāṇāni sak‍kāyasamuppādāyagāminī ca paṭipadā sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā|

51. tattha katamau vibhatti hārau? yaṃ kiñ‍ci vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyaṃ vuc‍cati vibhatti hārau| yathā kiṃ āgantvā ca puna puggalau hauti, nau vāgataṃ  na paribhāsati nau vā na paribhāsati (pī॰), na tāvāyaṃ paribhāsi (ka॰) paripucchatāya pañhāya atiyanaṃ aikassa kiñ‍ci – ayaṃ vuc‍catai vibhatti hārau|

tattha katamau parivattanau hārau| yaṃ kiñ‍ci paṭipakkhaniddaisau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati parivattanau hārau| yathā vuttaṃ  bhagavatā sammādiṭṭhikassa purisapuggalassa micchādiṭṭhi nij‍jiṇṇā hautīti vitthāraina sabbāni maggaṅgāni| ayaṃ vuc‍catai parivattanau hārau|

tattha katamau vaivacanau hārau?

vaivacanaihi anaikaihi, aikaṃ dhammaṃ pakāsitaṃ|

suttai yau jānāti suttavidū, vaivacanau nāma sau hārau||

yathā āyasmā sāriputtau aikamhi vatthumhi vaivacanaina nānāvuttaina bhagavatā pasaṃsitau ‘‘mahāpañ‍ñau sāriputtau hāsapañ‍ñau javanapañ‍ñau’’ti idaṃ pañ‍ñāya vaivacanaṃ| yathā ca maggavibhaṅgai  niyyānatthau aikamaikaṃ maggaṅgaṃ vaivacanaihi niddiṭṭhaṃ| aivaṃ avij‍jāya vaivacanā| aikaṃ akusalamūlaṃ tadaiva santaṃ taisu taisu janapadaisu taina taina pajānanti| na hi anaina tadaivapi ālapiyanti añ‍ñaṃ bhajati| sabbakāmajahassa bhikkhunauti kāmā ālapitā| yassa nitthiṇṇau saṅkauti taiyaiva kāmai saṅkāti ālapati| suṇamānassa puraitaraṃ raj‍janti taiyaiva kāmai raj‍janti ālapati| aivaṃ suttamhi yau dhammau daisiyati tassa pariyaiṭṭhi ‘‘katamassa dhammassa idaṃ nāmaṃ katamassa idaṃ vaivacana’’nti| sabbañ‍ñū hi yaisaṃ yaisaṃ yā nirutti hauti, yathāgāmi taina taina daisaitīti tassa vaivacanaṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ| ayaṃ vaivacanau hārau|

52. tattha katamau pañ‍ñatti hārau? cattāri  ariyasac‍cānīti suttaṃ niddisati, nikkhaipapañ‍ñatti| yā samudayapañ‍ñatti| kabaḷīkārai āhārai atthi chandau atthi rāgau yāva patiṭṭhitaṃ| tattha viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pabhavapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| kabaḷīkārai āhārai natthi chandau…pai॰… samugghāti pañ‍ñatti|

tassa kāmāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, bhavāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati, avij‍jāsavāpi cittaṃ vimuc‍catīti pahānapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| taṇhā yassa purakkhatā pañ‍ñā  parivattati gāthā manāpapañ‍ñattiṃ pañ‍ñapaiti| aivaṃ pana manāpapañ‍ñattīti aikadhammaṃ bhagavā pañ‍ñapaiti| na hi taṇhā dukkhasamudayauti kāraitvā sabbattha taṇhāsamudayau niddisitabbau| yathā uppan‍naṃ kāmavitak‍kaṃ nādhivāsaiti vinaudaiti pajahatīti paṭikkhaipapañ‍ñatti| aivaṃ sabbaisaṃ dhammānaṃ kusalānañ‍ca akusalānañ‍ca yañ‍cassa dhammakkhaittaṃ bhavati, sau caiva dhammau tattha pavattati| tadavasiṭṭhā dhammā tassānuvattakā haunti| sā duvidhā pañ‍ñatti – parādhīnapañ‍ñatti ca sādhīnapañ‍ñatti ca| katamā sādhīnapañ‍ñatti? samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 3.5, bhāvaitha, samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ‘‘rūpaṃ anic‍ca’’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ sādhīnapañ‍ñatti parādhīnapañ‍ñatti ca, sā pañ‍ñatti pañ‍ñāya ca sīlassa ca, yathā cattāri jhānāni bhāvaitha| tassa atthi samādhindriyaṃ mudūni cattāri indriyāni tāni catuparādhīnāni, tīṇi avaic‍cappasādaiti  parādhīnaṃ samādhindriyaṃ cattāri indriyāni parādhīnāti catūsu ariyasac‍caisu aparādhīnaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaisu sammappadhānaisu vīriyindriyaṃ| iti sakai padaṭṭhānai sakai khaittasādhīnau sau dhammau, sau ca tattha pañ‍ñāpaitabbau| tassa paṭipakkhā nighātau niddisitabbau| aitthāyaṃ anaikākārapañ‍ñatti kaina kāraṇaina ayaṃ dhammau pañ‍ñattauti| ayaṃ vuc‍catai pañ‍ñatti|

53. tattha  katamau autaraṇau hārau? chasu dhammaisu autāraitabbaṃ| katamaisu chasu? khandhaisu dhātūsu āyatanaisu indriyaisu sac‍caisu paṭic‍casamuppādaisu| natthi taṃ suttaṃ vā gāthā vā byākaraṇaṃ vā| imaisu chan‍naṃ dhammānaṃ añ‍ñatarasmiṃ na sandissati| aittāvatā aisa sabbā daisanā yā tā khandhā vā dhātuyau vā āyatanāni vā sac‍cāni vā paṭic‍casamuppādau vā, tattha pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ vaidanākkhandhau rāgadausamauhānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| tattha tissau vaidanāyau tassa sukhāya vaidanāya saumanassau savicārau, dukkhāya vaidanāya daumanassau savicārau, adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya upaikkhau savicārau| yaṃ puna tattha vaidayitaṃ idaṃ dukkhasac‍caṃ, khandhaisu saṅkhārakkhandhau tattha kāyau pamattaṃ saupavattati, tañ‍ca saṅkhāragatau dvidhā ca bhavaṅgautaraṇaṃ kammaṃ tīṇi ca saṅkhārāni  puñ‍ñābhisaṅkhārā vā apuñ‍ñā vā ānaiñ‍jā vā haitu sabbasarāgassa nau vītarāgassa, dausassa abhisaṅkhārāni ca avītarāgau caitaiti ca pakappaiti ca, vītarāgau  pana caitaiti ca nau abhisaṅkharauti, yaṃ uṇhaṃ vajiraṃ kaṭṭhai vā rukkhai vā añ‍ñattha vā patantaṃ bhindati ca ḍahati ca, aivaṃ sarāgacaitanā caitaiti ca abhisaṅkharauti ca| yathā sataṃ vajiraṃ na bhindati na ca ḍahati, aivaṃ vītarāgacaitanā caitaiti na ca abhisaṅkharauti| tattha pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ aikau khandhau anindriyasarīraṃ sañ‍ñākkhandhau|

tattha dhātūnaṃ aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau| tattha yā rūpī dasa dhātuyau, tāsu daisiyamānāsu rūpakkhandhau niddisitabbau, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ| yaipi ca cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā manaudhātusattamā, tattha viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau ca niddisitabbau, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ| dhammadhātu pana dhammasamausaraṇā, sau dhammau haitunā ca nissandaina ca phalaina ca kic‍caina ca vaivacanaina ca yaina yaina upalabbhati, taina taina niddisitabbau| yadi vā kusalā yadi vā akusalā yadi vā abyākatā yadi vā asaṅkhatā| dvādasan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ dasa āyatanāni rūpāni taṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ niddisitabbaṃ| rūpakkhandhau ca manāyatanañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaina niddisitabbaṃ, dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ| dhammāyatanaṃ nānādhammasamausaraṇaṃ| tattha yai dhammā indriyānaṃ indriyaisu niddisitabbā, yai anindriyānaṃ anindriyaisu niddisitabbā| pariyāyatau ca  autāraitabbā| yathā sā dhammadhātu tathā dhammāyatanaṃ pariyaisitabbaṃ| yāyaiva hi dhammadhātu tadaiva dhammāyatanaṃ anūnaṃ anadhikaṃ|

tattha paṭic‍casamuppādau atthi tividhau, atthi catubbidhau, atthi duvidhau| tattha tividhau paṭic‍casamuppādau haituphalanissandau| avij‍jā saṅkhārā taṇhā upādānaṃ ca ayaṃ haitu, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  nāmarūpaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ phassau vaidanā ca ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yau bhavau ayaṃ vipākau, yā jāti maraṇaṃ ayaṃ nissandau|

kathaṃ catubbidhau haitu pac‍cayau vipākau nissandau ca? avij‍jā ca taṇhāsaṅkhārā ca upādānaṃ ca – ayaṃ haitu| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpassa pac‍cayau| nāmarūpaṃ upapaj‍jati, tathā upapan‍nassa saḷāyatanaṃ phassau vaidanā ca – ayaṃ pac‍cayau| yau bhavau ayaṃ vipākau| yā jāti yā ca jarāmaraṇaṃ – ayaṃ nissandau|

kathaṃ duvidhau paṭic‍casamuppādau? avij‍jā saṅkhārā taṇhā upādānaṃ – ayaṃ samudayau| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ phassau vaidanā bhavau jāti maraṇañ‍ca  – idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ pana avij‍jāniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau imāni tappaṭipakkhaina dvai sac‍cāni| tasmā paṭic‍casamuppādau yaina ākāraina niddiṭṭhau, taina taina niddisitabbau|

tathā bāvīsati indriyāni| dvādasa indriyāni cakkhundriyāni cakkhundriyaṃ yaina daumanassindriyaṃ, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| purisindriyaṃ ca diṭṭhiyā ca taṇhāpadaṭṭhānaṃ| yatau purisau purisakānaṃ taṃ aivaṃ kātabbatā| atha ajjhattaṃ sāraj‍jati| ayaṃ ahaṃkārau taṃ yasā sārattau bahiddhā pariyaisati, ayaṃ mamaṃkārau aivaṃ itthī, tattha  sukhindriyaṃ ca saumanassindriyaṃ ca purisindriyassānuvattakā haunti| tassa adhippāyaparipuṇṇā laubhadhammā kusalamūlai pavaḍḍhainti| tassa cai ayamadhippāyau na pāripūriṃ gacchati| tassa dukkhindriyaṃ ca daumanassindriyaṃ ca vattati| dausau ca akusalamūlaṃ pavaḍḍhati| sacai pana upaikkhā bhāvaiti upaikkhindriyassa anuvattakāmā bhavati| amauhau ca kusalamūlaṃ pavaḍḍhati| iti satta indriyāni kilaisavatthumupādāya ananvaimāni avamāni sabbassa vaidanā itthindriyaṃ purisindriyaṃ| tattha aṭṭha indriyāni saddhindriyaṃ yāva añ‍ñātāvinau indriyaṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā| dasan‍naṃ pañ‍ñindriyānaṃ kāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| manindriyaṃ bhavarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍ñindriyāni rūparāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| itthindriyaṃ ca purisindriyaṃ ca satta pañ‍ñattiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| tattha yaina yaina indriyaina yuttaṃ vā gāthāya autāraituṃ sak‍kauti, taina taina niddisitabbau| aivaṃ khandhaisu dhātūsu āyatanaisu sac‍caisu paṭic‍casamuppādaisu ayaṃ autaraṇau hārau|

54. tattha katamau saudhanau hārau? yau gāthā aikaina ārambhau bhāsissanti| tattha aikissā bhāsitāya avasiṭṭhāsu bhāsitāsu sau atthau na niddisitabbau| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? na hi  tāva sau atthau bhāsitau, sau abhāsitau na sak‍kā niddisituṃ| yathā  kiṃ appamādau amataṃ padanti gāthā ayamaikā gāthā niddisitabbā| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ, atthikkhātāva imassa ārambhassa anabhāsitaṃ?

aivaṃ aitaṃ (pī॰) passa dha॰ pa॰ 22 visaisatau ñatvā, appamādamhi paṇḍitā|

appamādai pamaudanti, ariyānaṃ gaucarai ratāti||

idaṃ  abhāsitaṃ| imissāpi gāthāya bhāsitāya atthau niddisitabbau| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ, atthi tattha avasiṭṭhaṃ? tai jhāyinau dha॰ pa॰ 23 sātatikā, nic‍caṃ daḷhaparak‍kamāti gāthā, aivaṃ imā gāthāyau upadhāritā yadā bhavanti, tadā atthau niddisitabbau| aivaṃ assutapubbaisu suttaisu byākaraṇaisu vā aikuddaisau bhāsitau| yā vīmaṃsā tulanā idaṃ atthi kic‍caṃ, idaṃ suttaṃ bhāsitaṃ tassa vaivacanaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ vā na vāti| tattha yā vīmaṃsā, ayaṃ vuc‍catai saudhanau hārau|

55. tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau hārau? aikattatā ca vaimattatā ca| tattha kitapañ‍ñatti ca kic‍capañ‍ñatti ca| sā aikattatā ca vaimattatā ca yathā pañ‍ñatti aikavaivacanaina vaimattatā pajānātīti pañ‍ñā, sā ca ādhipataiyyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñatti| yaṃ anaumattiyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñattanti| taṃ anaumattiyaṭṭhaina pañ‍ñābalaṃ| tanubhūtā  gaucarattavasā saivasati tīsu ratanaisu anussati buddhānussati dhammānussati saṅghānussati aviparītānussaraṇatāya| sammādiṭṭhi dhammānaṃ pavicayaina dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau abhinīhāratau abhiñ‍ñāti| saṅkhaipaina maggā kā vatthu avikaupanatāya aikattā, yathā uṇhaina saṃsaṭṭhaṃ uṇhaudakaṃ, sītaina saṃsaṭṭhaṃ sītaudakaṃ khāraudakaṃ guḷhaudakanti, idaṃ aikattatā vaimattatā ca|

atthi  puna dhammau nānādhammasaṅghatau aikatau yathārūpaṃ cattārau vāraitabbā, tañ‍ca rūpanti aikattatā| pathavīdhātu āpau taijau vāyaudhātūti vaimattatā| aivaṃ sabbā catassau dhātuyau rūpanti aikattatā, pathavīdhātu āpau taijau vāyaudhātūti vaimattatā| pathavīdhātūti lakkhaṇatau aikattatā, saṃkiṇṇavatthutau vaimattatā| yaṃ kiñ‍ci kakkhaḷalakkhaṇaṃ, sabbaṃ taṃ pathavīdhātūti aikattatā| kaisā laumā nakhā dantā chavi cammanti vaimattatā| aivaṃ sabbaṃ catassau dhātuyau rūpanti aikattaṃ| saddā gandhā rasā phauṭṭhabbāti vaimattatā|

atthi puna dhammau vaimattatā añ‍ñau nāmaṃ labhati| yathā kāyānupassanāya navasañ‍ñā vinīlakasañ‍ñā uddhumātakasañ‍ñā, ayaṃ asubhasañ‍ñā, yā aikattatā ārammaṇatau vaimattatau, sā aivaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidanāsu ādīnavaṃ samanupassatau tathādhiṭṭhānaṃ samādhindriyaṃ ca sāyaiva  dhammaisu tattha sañ‍ñābhāvanā vīriyindriyaṃ ca dhammaisu dhammānupassanā cittai attasañ‍ñaṃ pajahatau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ ca cittai cittānupassanā| (iti) ( ) natthi pī॰ pautthakai yau kauci ñāṇapacārau sabbasau  pañ‍ñāya gaucarau pañ‍ñā, ayaṃ vaimattatā, yathā kāmarāgau bhavarāgau diṭṭhirāgauti vaimattatā taṇhāya| iti yaṃ aikattatāya ca vaimattatāya ca ñāṇaṃ vīmaṃsanā tulanā| ayaṃ adhiṭṭhānau hārau|

56. tattha katamau parikkhārau hārau? sahaitu sappac‍cayaṃ vaudānañ‍ca saṃkilaisau ca, yaṃ tadubhayaṃ pariyaiṭṭhi, sa parikkhārau hārau| iti dhammānaṃ sahaitukānaṃ haitu pariyaisitabbau, sappac‍cayānaṃ pac‍cayau pariyaisitabbau|

tattha kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ, haitussa ca pac‍cayassa ca? sabhāvau haitu, parabhāvau pac‍cayau| parabhāvassa pac‍cayau haitupi, sabhāvassa haituyā parabhāvassa kassaci pac‍cayau avuttau haitu, vuttau pac‍cayau| ajjhattikau haitu, bāhirau pac‍cayau| sabhāvau haitu, parabhāvau pac‍cayau| nibbattakau haitu, paṭiggāhakau pariggāhakau (ka॰) pac‍cayau| naivāsikau haitu, āgantukau pac‍cayau| asādhāraṇau haitu, sādhāraṇau pac‍cayau| aikauyaiva haitu, aparāparau pac‍cayau|

haitussa upakaraṇaṃ samudānaitabbau| samudānaṃ haitu, tattha duvidhau haitu| duvidhau pac‍cayau – samanantarapac‍cayau ca paramparapac‍cayau ca| haitupi duvidhau – samanantarahaitu ca paramparahaitu ca| tattha katamau paramparapac‍cayau? avij‍jā nāmarūpassa paramparapac‍cayau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanantarapac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| yadi ādimhi avij‍jāniraudhau bhavati nāmarūpassa niraudhaupi| tattha samanantaraṃ kiṃ kāraṇaṃ paramparapac‍cayau samanantarapac‍cayau samuddānitau, ayaṃ pac‍cayatau| tattha  katamau paramparahaitu? vijānantassa paramparahaitutāya haitu, añ‍ñākārau samanantarahaitutāya haitu| yassa hi yaṃ samanantaraṃ nibbattati, sau tassa haitupi jātiniraudhā bahi ākāraniraudhau, ākāraniraudhā daṇḍaniraudhau, daṇḍaniraudhā khaṇḍaniraudhau| aivaṃ haitupi dvidhā sau tāhi passitabbau|

paṭic‍casamuppādau  yathā avij‍jāpac‍cayau tassa puna kiṃpac‍cayau, ayaunisau manasikārau| sau kassa pac‍cayau saṅkhārānaṃ, iti pac‍cayau ca samuppan‍naṃ ca tassa kau haitu avij‍jāyaiva| tathā hi purimā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati| tattha avij‍jānusayau avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānassa haitu purimā haitu pacchā pac‍cayau, sāpi avij‍jāsaṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau catūhi kāraṇaihi sahajātapac‍cayatāya samanantarapac‍cayatāya abhisandanapac‍cayatāya patiṭṭhānapac‍cayatāya|

57. kathaṃ sahajātapac‍cayatāya avij‍jāsaṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau? yaṃ cittaṃ rāgapariyuṭṭhaṃ, tattha avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaina sabbaṃ pañ‍ñāya gaucaraṃ hanti| tattha saṅkhārā tipac‍cayaṭṭhikā addhābhūmikāramahattassa laddhā bhūmikaramahattassa (pī॰ ka॰) ayaṃ avij‍jāsahasamuppan‍naṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍latamāpaj‍jantī catūhi kāraṇaihi pañ‍ñā pahīyati| katamaihi catūhi? anusayau pariyuṭṭhānaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ upādānaṃ| tattha anusayau pariyuṭṭhānaṃ jāti pariyuṭṭhitā saṃyuj‍jati saṃyuttā upādiyati upādānapac‍cayā bhavau| aivaṃ  tai saṅkhārā tividhā uppan‍nā bhūmigatā nāsañ‍ñattha ayaṃ maggaina vinītattāyāti vinibhattāya (pī॰), vinibhattatāya (ka॰) tai thāmagatā apativinītātipi tai saṅkhārāti vuc‍cati, aivaṃ sahaitusamuppan‍naṭṭhaina atthi maiva pac‍cayā saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau niddiṭṭhaṃ apanaitvā kusalaṃ akusalaṃ kusalau ca akusalau ca pakkhipitabbau, vipākadhammā apanaitvā vacanīyaṃ avacanīyaṃ vacanīyañ‍ca avacanīyañ‍ca pakkhipitabbaṃ, bhavaapaivirittā, sabbasuttaṃ parikkhipitabbaṃ|

dasa tathāgatabalāni cattāri vaisāraj‍jāni puñ‍ñāni anañ‍ñākataṃ avij‍jā samanantarapac‍cayatāya saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau yaina cittaina saha samuppan‍nā avij‍jā tassa cittassa samanantaracittaṃ samuppan‍nanti, tassa yaṃ samanantaracittaṃ samuppan‍nanti, tassa pacchimassa cittassa purimacittaṃ haitupac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau, taina avij‍jā haitu taina cittaina upādānaṃ anaukāsakatā ñāṇaṃ na uppaj‍janti| yā tassa appamādā dhātu abhijjhābhisanditā tahiṃ vipal‍lāsā uppaj‍janti ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti, tattha saṅkhārā uppaj‍janti rattā duṭṭhā mūlassa caitanā rāgapariyuṭṭhānaina byāpādapariyuṭṭhānaina avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānaina diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau vatthuniddaisai niddisitabbau, yaṃ viparītacittau vijānāti ayaṃ cittavipal‍lāsau, yā viparītasañ‍ñā upaggaṇhāti ayaṃ sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau| yaṃ viparītadiṭṭhi abhinivisati ayaṃ diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| aṭṭha  micchattāni vaḍḍhanti, tīṇi akusalāni ayaunisau manasikārai uppan‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca vij‍jañ‍ca karaunti| iti pubbāparantai akusalānātaritarau saṅkhārā vuddhiṃ vaipul‍lataṃ gacchanti| tai ca mahatā ca appaṭividitā paunaubhavikā paunaubbhavikā (ka॰) saṅkhārā bhavanti| iti aivaṃ avij‍jā sahajātapac‍cayatāya saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau samanantarapac‍cayatāya ca|

58. kathaṃ  abhisandanākāraina avij‍jā saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau? sā avij‍jā tai saṅkhārai abhisan‍naiti parippharati| saiyyathāpi nāma uppalaṃ vā padumaṃ vā taṃ udakai vaḍḍhaṃ assa, sītaina vārinā abhisan‍naṃ parisandanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍lataṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ abhisandanaṭṭhaina avij‍jā saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau|

kathaṃ patiṭṭhahanaṭṭhaina avij‍jā saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau? tai saṅkhārā avij‍jāyaṃ nissāya vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍lataṃ āpaj‍janti| saiyyathāpi nāma uppalaṃ vā padumaṃ vā pathaviṃ nissāya pathaviṃ patiṭṭhāya vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍lataṃ āpaj‍jati| aitai saṅkhārā avij‍jāyaṃ patiṭṭhitā avij‍jāyaṃ nissāya vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍lataṃ gacchanti| aivaṃ patiṭṭhahanaṭṭhaina avij‍jā saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau|

puna rāgasahagatassa kammassa vipākaina paṭisandhimhi bhavau nibbattati, taṃ kammassa kāmassa (pī॰) sabbaṃ abhiniviṭṭhaṃ añ‍ñāṇavasaina paunaubhavikā saṅkhārāti vuc‍canti, aivampi avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā atthi| puna  pañ‍casu yai ca saikkhā puggalā, yai ca asañ‍ñisamāpattiṃ samāpan‍nā, yai ca bhavagatā, yai ca antaugatāyaiva saṃsaidajā, yai ca vā pana añ‍ñau hi kauci anāgāmibhūtā na caitainti na ca patthainti, taisaṃ kiṃ pac‍cayā saṅkhārā| puna rāgā atthi taisaṃ saṅkhārāni upādānāni cittamanussarantiyaiva avipak‍kavipākasamūhatā asamucchin‍napac‍cayā taisaṃ puna ca gatau bhavati| aivampi hi avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā| puna sā tai na upādānā napi saṅkhārā atthi, puna taisaṃ satta anusayā asamūhatā asamucchin‍nā tadārammaṇaṃ bhavati| viñ‍ñāṇassa patiṭṭhāya viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ| aivampi avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā| puna sā yaṃ kiñ‍ci kammaṃ ācayagāmi sabbaṃ taṃ avij‍jāvasaina abhisaṅkhariyati  taṇhāvasaina ca al‍līyati añ‍ñāṇavasaina ca tattha ādīnavampi na jānāti| tadaiva viñ‍ñāṇabījaṃ bhavati, sāyaiva taṇhāsinaihau bhavati| sāyaiva avij‍jā sammauhauti| aivampi avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā vattabbā| iti imaihi ākāraihi avij‍jā saṅkhārānaṃ pac‍cayau|

tattha avij‍jāya haitu ayaunisau manasikārau pac‍cayau hauti| tattha abhicchaidau ayaṃ tattha tatiyaṃ balaṃ phalaṃ (pī॰) nivatti, ayaṃ paṭisandhi| tattha punabbhavau yau avaicchaidau asamugghātanaṭṭhaina ayaṃ anusayau| yathā  paṭākaṃ vā sāṭakaṃ vā dvai janā pīḷaisu ca aikā vā balaṃ vā assa nivāṭassaisu, na pana pīḷaisu sausaiyya| tattha yaṃ sinaihā āpaudhātu anupul‍lanā sausaitabbā| uṇhadhātumāgamma sacai puna taṃ ākāsai nikkhipaiyya taṃ ussāvaina yaibhuyyataraṃ sinaihamāpaj‍jaiyya,  na hi anāgamma taijaudhātuṃ parisaisaṃ gacchaiyya| aivamaiva bhavaggaparamāpi samāpatti na anurūpassa samugghātāya saṃvattati| tai hi ālayanti sammasanti, na ca taṇhāya taṇhāpahānaṃ gacchanti| tattha sau asamugghātau| avij‍jāya anusayau ca cittassa sampalibaudhau, idaṃ pariyuṭṭhānaṃ| yathābhūtaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa appaṭivaidhau ayaṃ avij‍jāāsavau avij‍jāviñ‍ñāṇabījaṃ bhavati| yaṃ bījaṃ sau haitu na samucchij‍jati, asamucchij‍jantau paṭisandaihati| paṭisandahantau na samugghātaṃ gacchati| asamugghātaṃ cittaṃ pariyaunahati, pariyaunaddhacittau yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, iti sañ‍ñāṇassa sāsavatthau, avij‍jatthau, haituatthau, avacchaidatthau, anivattiatthau, phalatthau paṭisandhiatthau, punabbhavatthau, asamugghātatthau, anusayatthau, pariyuṭṭhānatthau, apaṭivaidhanatthau| aittāvatā avij‍jāya khaittaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ bhavati| ayaṃ vuc‍catai parikkhārau nāma hārau|

59. tattha katamau samāraupanau hārau? ugghaṭitamhi  tamhi santañ‍caiva ca naṃ vitthāraṃ pana vattabbaṃ| vitthāravidhaṃ cittañ‍ñā ayaṃ samāraupanau hārau| tattha nāmaniddaisau upaghaṭakā ugghaṭakā (pī॰) vatthuniddaisau vaivacanaṃ vatthubhūtau vitthārau| yathā kiṃ, yā bhikkhūnaṃ vattatau nivattatau (pī॰) pahātabbau, ayaṃ upaghaṭanā|

tattha  katamau samāraupanau? kiñ‍ci na vattabbaṃ, rūparāgaṃ vā nāmavantapahātabbaṃ nāmamantapahātabbaṃ (ka॰)| yāva viñ‍ñāṇanti vitthāraina kātabbāni| avij‍jā tā aupammaina pañ‍ñāpaitabbā, ayaṃ samāraupanau| nissitacittassa ca mattikau ca nissayau taṇhā ca diṭṭhi ca| tattha diṭṭhi avij‍jā taṇhā saṅkhārā| tattha diṭṭhipac‍cayā taṇhā imai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā| tattha nissitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ idaṃ saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yāva jarāmaraṇaṃ, idaṃ saṃkhittaina bhāsitai avasiṭṭhaṃ paraupayati|

anissitassa passa udā॰ 74 calitaṃ natthīti tassa aivaṃ diṭṭhiyā taṇhāya ca pahānaṃ tattha diṭṭhiavij‍jāniraudhāya bhūtaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sarāgaṭṭhāniyaisu dhammaisu taṃ taṃ dhammaṃ upaic‍ca añ‍ñaṃ dhammaṃ dhāvati mak‍kaṭaupamatāya, atha khvassa parittaisu dhammaisu sarāgaṭṭhāniyaisu chandarāgau natthi kutau tatau calanā, adhimattaisu sattaisu cittaṃ nivaissayati taṃ apatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anāhāraṃ nirujjhati viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau yāva jarāmaraṇaniraudhau| ayaṃ samāraupanau|

tattha  rāgavasaina viñ‍ñāṇassa calitaṃ sapariggahau, tasmiṃ calitai asati yau parikilaisaupacārau  tividhau aggi paṭippassaddhau bhavati| taināha calitai asantai passaddhi hauti| tattha yaṃ samāraupanā passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati| yāva vimuttitamiti ñāṇadassanaṃ bhavati| sau āsavānaṃ khayā ca vimutti nau upapaj‍jati| tassa upapattissa āgatigatiyā asantiyā naividha na huraṃ na ubhayamantaraina| aisaivantau dukkhassāti anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu| idamassa suttassa majjhai samāraupitaṃ paṭic‍casamuppādai ca vimuttiyaṃ ca yaugau na ca aitaṃ tassa saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ vibhaj‍janti| ayaṃ vuc‍catai samāraupanau hārau| na ca saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina suttaina saṃkilaisabhāgiyau yai ca dhammā samāraupayitabbā nāñ‍ñai| aivaṃ vāsanābhāgiyai nibbaidhabhāgiyai, ayaṃ samāraupanau hārau| imai sauḷasa hārā|

suvīrassa mahākac‍cāyanassa jambuvanavāsinau paiṭakaupadaisai

pañ‍camā bhūmi|



6. suttatthasamuc‍cayabhūmi

60. buddhānaṃ  bhagavantānaṃ sāsanaṃ tividhaina saṅgahaṃ gacchati, khandhaisu dhātūsu āyatanaisu ca| tattha pañ‍cakkhandhā rūpakkhandhau yāva viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau| dasa rūpaāyatanāni cakkhu rūpā ca yāva kāyau phauṭṭhabbā ca, ayaṃ rūpakkhandhau| tattha cha vaidanākāyā vaidanākkhandhau cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā yāva manausamphassajā vaidanā, ayaṃ vaidanākkhandhau| tattha cha sañ‍ñākāyā sañ‍ñākkhandhau, rūpasañ‍ñā yāva dhammasañ‍ñā imai cha sañ‍ñākāyā, ayaṃ sañ‍ñākkhandhau| tattha cha caitanākāyā saṅkhārakkhandhau, rūpasañ‍caitanā yāva dhammasañ‍caitanā imai cha caitanākāyā, ayaṃ saṅkhārakkhandhau| tattha cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ yāva manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ imai cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau| imai pañ‍cakkhandhā|

taisaṃ kā pariñ‍ñā? anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ sañ‍ñā anattāti aisā aitaisaṃ pariñ‍ñā| tattha katamau khandhatthau? samūhatthau khandhatthau, puñ‍jatthau khandhatthau, rāsatthau khandhatthau| taṃ  yathā dabbakkhandhau vanakkhandhau dārukkhandhau aggikkhandhau udakakkhandhau vāyukkhandhau iti aivaṃ khandhaisu sabbasaṅgahauva aivaṃ khandhatthau|

tattha aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau cakkhudhātu rūpadhātu cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇadhātu…pai॰… manaudhātu dhammadhātu manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu| aitāyau aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau| tāsaṃ pariñ‍ñā anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ sañ‍ñā anattāti aisā aitāsaṃ pariñ‍ñā| tattha kau dhātuatthau? vuc‍catai avayavatthau dhātuatthau| avayavauti cakkhu nau pasādau cakkhudhātu| aivaṃ pañ‍casu dhātūsu puna rāgavavacchaidatthau dhātuatthau| vavacchin‍nā hi cakkhudhātu| aivaṃ pañ‍casu punarāha aikantipakatyatthaina dhātuatthauti vuc‍catai| taṃ yathā, pakatiyā ayaṃ purisau pittikau saimhikau vātikau san‍nipātikauti aivaṃ pakaticakkhudhātu dasan‍naṃ piyā ca sabbaisu indriyaisu…pai॰… visabhāgatthau dhātuatthau|

tattha  dvādasāyatanāni katamāni? cha ajjhattikāni cha bāhirāni| cakkhāyatanaṃ yāva manāyatananti ajjhattikaṃ, rūpāyatanaṃ yāva dhammāyatananti bāhiraṃ| aitāni dvādasa āyatanāni| aitaisaṃ kā pariñ‍ñā? anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ sañ‍ñā anattāti, aisā aitaisaṃ pariñ‍ñā| api  ca dvidhā pariñ‍ñā ñātapariñ‍ñā ca pahānapariñ‍ñā ca| tattha ñātapariñ‍ñā nāma anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ sañ‍ñā anattāti, aisā ñātapariñ‍ñā| pahānapariñ‍ñā pana chandarāgappahānā, aisā pahānapariñ‍ñā| tattha katamau āyatanatthau? vuc‍catai ākāratthau āyatanatthau| yathā suvaṇṇākarau dubbaṇṇākarau, yathā dvīhi taihi ākāraihi tai tai gāvā uttiṭṭhanti| aivaṃ aitaihi cittacaitasikā gāvā uttiṭṭhanti kammakilaisā dukkhadhammā ca| punarāha āyadānatthau āyatanatthau| yathā rañ‍ñau āyadānaihi āyau bhavati, aivaṃ āyadānatthau āyatanatthau|

61. cattāri ariyasac‍cāni dukkhaṃ samudayau niraudhau maggau ca| dukkhaṃ yathā samāsaina dhammācariyaṃ mānasañ‍ca, samudayau samāsaina avij‍jā ca taṇhā ca, niraudhau samāsaina vij‍jā ca vimutti ca, maggau samāsaina samathau ca vipassanā ca|

tattha sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhikā dhammā katamai? cattārau satipaṭṭhānā yāva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, aivamaitai sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhikā dhammā| yai dhammā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ buddhānaṃ bhagavantānaṃ pac‍caikabuddhānaṃ sāvakānaṃ ca nibbānāya saṃvattantīti, sau maggau cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| katamai cattārau? idha bhikkhu kāyai kāyānupassī viharati, sammappadhānaṃ…pai॰… iddhipādaṃ…pai॰… indriyāni…pai॰… balāni…pai॰… tattha kau indriyatthau? indatthau  indriyatthau, ādhipataiyyatthau indriyatthau, pasādatthau indriyatthau, asādhāraṇaṃ kassa kiriyatthau indriyatthau anavapariyatthau balatthau, thāmatthau balatthau, upādāyatthau balatthau, upatthambhanatthau balatthau|

tattha katamai satta baujjhaṅgā? satisambaujjhaṅgau yāva upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgau| tattha katamau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau? sammādiṭṭhi yāva sammāsamādhi| tattha aṭṭhaṅgikau maggauti khandhau sīlakkhandhau ca samādhikkhandhau ca pañ‍ñākkhandhau ca| tattha yā ca sammāvācā yau ca sammākammantau yau ca sammāājīvau, ayaṃ sīlakkhandhau| yā ca sammāsati yau ca sammāvāyāmau yau ca sammāsamādhi, ayaṃ samādhikkhandhau| yau ca sammāsaṅkappau yā ca sammādiṭṭhi, ayaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau| aivaṃ tāyau tissau sikkhā| aivaṃ tīhākāraihi dasa padāni…pai॰…|

tattha  yaugāvacarau sīlakkhandhai ṭhitau dausaṃ akusalaṃ na upādiyati, dausānusayaṃ samūhanati, dausasal‍laṃ uddharati, dukkhavaidanaṃ parijānāti, kāmadhātuṃ samatik‍kamati| samādhikkhandhai ṭhitau laubhaṃ akusalaṃ na upādiyati, rāgānusayaṃ samūhanati, laubhasal‍laṃ uddharati, sukhavaidanaṃ parijānāti, rūpadhātuṃ samatik‍kamati| pañ‍ñākkhandhai ṭhitau mauhaṃ akusalaṃ na upādiyati, avij‍jānusayaṃ samūhanati, mauhasal‍laṃ diṭṭhisal‍lañ‍ca uddharati, adukkhamasukhavaidanaṃ  parijānāti, arūpadhātuṃ samatik‍kamati| iti tīhi khandhaihi tīṇi akusalamūlāni na upādiyati, cattāri sal‍lāni uddharati, tissau vaidanā parijānāti, taidhātukaṃ samatik‍kamati|

62. tattha katamā avij‍jā? yaṃ catūsu ariyasac‍caisu añ‍ñāṇanti vitthāraina yathā sau pāṇasaj‍jaisu kathaṃkathā kātabbaṃ| tattha katamaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā vaidanā sañ‍ñā caitanā phassau manasikārau, idaṃ nāmaṃ| tattha katamaṃ rūpaṃ? cātumahābhūtikaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ upādāyarūpassa pañ‍ñattiṃ| iti purimakañ‍ca nāmaṃ idañ‍ca rūpaṃ tadubhayaṃ nāmarūpanti vuc‍cati| tattha chaḷāyatananti cha ajjhattikāni āyatanāni, cakkhu ajjhattikaṃ āyatanaṃ yāva manau ajjhattikaṃ āyatanaṃ| phassauti cha phassakāyā cakkhusamphassau yāva manausamphassauti phassau| cha vaidanākāyā vaidanā| taṇhāti cha taṇhākāyā taṇhā| upādānanti cattāri upādānāni kāmupādānaṃ diṭṭhupādānaṃ sīlabbatupādānaṃ attavādupādānanti upādānaṃ| bhavauti tayau bhavā kāmabhavau rūpabhavau arūpabhavau| tattha katamā jāti? yā paṭhamaṃ khandhānaṃ paṭhamaṃ dhātūnaṃ paṭhamaṃ āyatanānaṃ uppatti jāti sañ‍jāti auk‍kanti abhinibbatti khandhānaṃ pātubhāvau, ayaṃ jāti| tattha  katamā jarā? jarā nāma yaṃ taṃ khaṇḍic‍caṃ pālic‍caṃ valittacatā pavivittaṃ catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ vivaṇṇataṃ bhaggau taṃ jarā hīyanā pahīyanā āyunau hāni saṃhāni indriyānaṃ paribhaidau upanāhau paripākau, ayaṃ jarā| tattha katamaṃ maraṇaṃ? maraṇaṃ nāma yaṃ tasmiṃ tasmiṃ sattanikāyai taisaṃ taisaṃ sattānaṃ cuti cavanatā maraṇaṃ kālaṅkiriyā uddhumātakānaṃ bhaidau kāyassa jīvitindriyassa upacchaidau, idaṃ maraṇaṃ| iti purimikā ca jarā idañ‍ca maraṇaṃ tadubhayaṃ jarāmaraṇaṃ|

tattha  andhakāratimisā yathābhūtaṃ appajānanalakkhaṇā avij‍jā saṅkhārānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ ha| abhisaṅkharaṇalakkhaṇā saṅkhārā, upacayapunabbhavābhiraupanapac‍cupaṭṭhānā| tai viñ‍ñāṇassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| vatthu saviñ‍ñattilakkhaṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, taṃ nāmarūpassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| anaikasan‍nissayalakkhaṇaṃ nāmarūpaṃ, taṃ saḷāyatanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| indriyavavatthāpanalakkhaṇaṃ saḷāyatanaṃ, taṃ phassassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| san‍nipātalakkhaṇau phassau, sau vaidanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| anubhavanalakkhaṇā vaidanā, sā taṇhāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| ajjhausānalakkhaṇā taṇhā, sā upādānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ādānaparihananalakkhaṇaṃ upādānaṃ, taṃ bhavassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| nānāgativikkhaipalakkhaṇau bhavau, sau jātiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| khandhānaṃ pātubhāvalakkhaṇā jāti, sā jarāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| upanayaparipākalakkhaṇā jarā, sā maraṇassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| āyukkhayajīvitauparaudhalakkhaṇaṃ maraṇaṃ, taṃ dukkhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāyasampīḷanalakkhaṇaṃ dukkhaṃ, taṃ daumanassassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| cittasampīḷanalakkhaṇaṃ daumanassaṃ, taṃ saukassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saucanalakkhaṇau saukau, sau paridaivassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| vacīnicchāraṇalakkhaṇau paridaivau, sau upāyāsassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| yai āyāsā tai upāyāsā|

nava padāni yattha sabbau akusalapakkhau saṅgahaṃ samausaraṇaṃ gacchati| katamāni nava padāni? dvai mūlakilaisā, tīṇi akusalamūlāni, cattārau vipal‍lāsā| tattha dvai mūlakilaisā avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca, tīṇi akusalamūlāni laubhau dausau mauhau ca| cattārau vipal‍lāsā a॰ ni॰ 4.49 – ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau, ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau, ‘‘anattani attā’’ti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau, ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau cittavipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau|

63. tattha avij‍jā nāma catūsu ariyasac‍caisu yathābhūtaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ, ayaṃ avij‍jā| bhavataṇhā nāma yau bhavaisu rāgau sārāgau icchā mucchā patthanā nandī ajjhausānaṃ aparic‍cāgau, ayaṃ bhavataṇhā|

tattha  katamau laubhau akusalamūlaṃ?

laubhau nāma sau taisu taisu paravatthūsu paradabbaisu paraṭṭhānaisu parasāpataiyyaisu parapariggahitaisu  laubhau lubbhanā icchā mucchā patthanā nandī ajjhausānaṃ aparic‍cāgau, ayaṃ laubhau akusalamūlaṃ| kassaitaṃ mūlaṃ? laubhau laubhajassa akusalassa kāyakammassa vacīkammassa manaukammassa ca, tathā yathā taṃsampayuttānaṃ cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ mūlaṃ|

tattha  katamau dausau akusalamūlaṃ?

sau sattaisu āghātau akkhanti appac‍cayau byāpādau padausau anatthakāmatā caitasau paṭighātau, ayaṃ dausau akusalamūlaṃ|

kassaitaṃ mūlaṃ?

dausajassa kāyakammassa vacīkammassa manaukammassa sampayuttānañ‍ca cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ mūlaṃ|

tattha katamau mauhau akusalamūlaṃ?

yaṃ catūsu ariyasac‍caisu anabhisamayau asampaj‍jaggāhau appaṭivaidhau mauhau muyhanā sammauhau sammuyhanā avij‍jā tamau andhakārau āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ chadanaṃ acchadanaṃ avaicchadanaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) apasacchāgamanaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, ayaṃ mauhau akusalamūlaṃ|

kassaitaṃ mūlaṃ?

mauhajassa akusalassa kāyakammassa vacīkammassa manaukammassa ca taṃsampayuttakānañ‍ca cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ mūlaṃ|

tattha  vipal‍lāsā jānitabbā, vipal‍lāsānaṃ vatthu jānitabbaṃ| yaṃ vipal‍lāsaṃ siyā, taṃ jānitabbaṃ| tattha aikau vipal‍lāsau tīṇi vipal‍lāsāni cattāri vipal‍lāsavatthūni| katamau aikau vipal‍lāsau ca, yaina paṭipakkhaina vipal‍lāsitaṃ gaṇhāti?

‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti, ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti, ‘‘anattani attā’’ti, ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti, ayaṃ aikau vipal‍lāsau|

katamāni cattāri vipal‍lāsavatthūni?

kāyau vaidanā cittaṃ dhammā ca| imāni cattāri vipal‍lāsavatthūni|

katamāni tīṇi vipal‍lāsāni?

sañ‍ñā cittaṃ diṭṭhi ca| imāni tīṇi vipal‍lāsāni|

tattha  manāpikai vatthumhi indriyavatthai vaṇṇāyatanai vā yau nimittassa uggāhau, ayaṃ sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau| tattha viparītacittassa vatthumhi sati viñ‍ñatti, ayaṃ cittavipal‍lāsau| tattha viparītacittassa tamhi rūpai ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti yā khanti ruci upaikkhanā nicchayau diṭṭhi nidassanaṃ santīraṇā, ayaṃ diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau| tattha vatthubhaidaina kāyaisu dvādasa vipal‍lāsā bhavanti| tayau  kāyai tayau vaidanāya tayau cittai tayau dhammai, cattārau sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsā cattārau cittavipal‍lāsā cattārau diṭṭhivipal‍lāsā, āyatanūpacayatau cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasañ‍ñāsamaṅgissa rūpaisu dvādasa vipal‍lāsā yāva manau sañ‍ñāsamaṅgissa, dhammaisu dvādasa vipal‍lāsā cha dvādasakā cattāri vipal‍lāsā bhavanti| ārammaṇanānattatau  hi aparimitasaṅkhaiyyānaṃ sattānaṃ attānaṃ (ka॰) aparimitamasaṅkhaiyyā vipal‍lāsā bhavanti hīnuk‍kaṭṭhamajjhimatāya|

64. tattha pañ‍cakkhandhā cattāri attabhāvavatthūni bhavanti| yau rūpakkhandhau, sau kāyau attabhāvavatthu| yau vaidanākkhandhau, sau vaidanā attabhāvavatthu| yau sañ‍ñākkhandhau ca saṅkhārakkhandhau ca, tai dhammā attabhāvavatthu| yau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau, sau cittaṃ attabhāvavatthu| iti pañ‍cakkhandhā cattāri attabhāvavatthūni| tattha kāyai ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau bhavati| aivaṃ vaidanāsu…pai॰… cittai…pai॰… dhammaisu ca attavipal‍lāsau bhavati| tattha catun‍naṃ vipal‍lāsānaṃ samugghātanatthaṃ bhagavā cattārau satipaṭṭhānai daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti kāyai kāyānupassī viharatau ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsaṃ samugghātaiti, aivaṃ vaidanāsu, cittai, dhammaisu ca kātabbaṃ|

tattha andhakāratimisā appaṭivaidhalakkhaṇā avij‍jā, tassā vipal‍lāsapadaṭṭhānaṃ| ajjhausānalakkhaṇā taṇhā, tassā piyarūpasātarūpaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| attāsayavañ‍canālakkhaṇau laubhau, tassa adin‍nādānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| idha vivādalakkhaṇau dausau, tassa pāṇātipātau padaṭṭhānaṃ| vatthuvippaṭipattilakkhaṇau mauhau, tassa micchāpaṭipatti padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṅkhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ avināsaggahaṇalakkhaṇā nic‍casañ‍ñā, tassā sabbasaṅkhārā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sāsavaphassaupagamanalakkhaṇā sukhasañ‍ñā, tassā mamaṅkārau padaṭṭhānaṃ| dhammaisu upagamanalakkhaṇā attasañ‍ñā, tassā ahaṅkārau padaṭṭhānaṃ| vaṇṇasaṅgahaṇalakkhaṇā subhasañ‍ñā, tassā indriyaasaṃvarau padaṭṭhānaṃ| aitaihi navahi padaihi uddiṭṭhaihi sabbau akusalapakkhau niddiṭṭhau bhavati, sau ca khau bahussutaina sak‍kā jānituṃ nau appassutaina, pañ‍ñavatā nau duppañ‍ñaina, yuttaina nau ayuttaina|

nava  padāni kusalāni yattha sabbau kusalapakkhau saṅgahau samausaraṇaṃ gacchanti| katamāni nava padāni? samathau vipassanā alaubhau adausau amauhau anic‍casañ‍ñā dukkhasañ‍ñā anattasañ‍ñā asubhasañ‍ñā ca|

tattha katamau samathau? yā cittassa ṭhiti saṇṭhiti avaṭṭhiti ṭhānaṃ paṭṭhānaṃ upaṭṭhānaṃ samādhi samādhānaṃ avikkhaipau avippaṭisārau vūpasamau mānasau aikaggaṃ cittassa, ayaṃ samathau|

tattha katamā vipassanā? khandhaisu vā dhātūsu vā āyatanaisu vā nāmarūpaisu vā paṭic‍casamuppādaisu vā paṭic‍casamuppan‍naisu vā dhammaisu dukkhaisu vā samudayaisu vā niraudhai vā maggai vā kusalākusalaisu vā dhammaisu sāvaj‍jaanavaj‍jaisu vā kaṇhasuk‍kaisu vā saivitabbaasaivitabbaisu vā sau yathābhūtaṃ vicayau pavicayau vīmaṃsā paravīmaṃsā gāhanā aggāhanā pariggāhanā cittaina paricitanā tulanā upaparikkhā ñāṇaṃ vij‍jā vā cakkhu buddhi maidhā pañ‍ñā aubhāsau ālaukau ābhā pabhā khaggau nārācau nāraj‍jau (pī॰ ka॰) dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau  sammādiṭṭhi maggaṅgaṃ, ayaṃ vipassanā| tainaisā vipassanā iti vuc‍cati vividhā vā aisā vipassanāti, tasmā aisā vipassanāti vuc‍cati| dvidhā caisā hi vipassanā dhammavipassanāti vuc‍cati, dvidhā imāya passati subhañ‍ca asubhañ‍ca kaṇhañ‍ca suk‍kañ‍ca saivitabbañ‍ca asaivitabbañ‍ca kammañ‍ca vipākañ‍ca bandhañ‍ca vimaukkhañ‍ca ācayañ‍ca apacayañ‍ca pavattiñ‍ca nivattiñ‍ca saṃkilaisañ‍ca vaudānañ‍ca, aivaṃ vipassanāti vuc‍cati| atha vā viiti upasaggau passanāti atthau tasmā vipassanāti vuc‍catai, ayaṃ vipassanā|

65. tattha dvai raugā sattānaṃ avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca, aitaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ raugānaṃ nighātāya bhagavatā dvai bhaisaj‍jāni vuttāni samathau ca vipassanā ca| imāni dvai bhaisaj‍jāni paṭisaivaintau dvai araugai sacchikarauti rāgavirāgaṃ caitauvimuttiṃ avij‍jāvirāgañ‍ca pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ| tattha taṇhāraugassa samathau bhaisaj‍jaṃ, rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti araugaṃ| avij‍jāraugassa vipassanābhaisaj‍jaṃ avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti araugaṃ| aivañhi bhagavā cāha, ‘‘dvai dhammā pariñ‍ñaiyyā passa dī॰ ni॰ 3.352 nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, dvai dhammā pahātabbā avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca, dvai dhammā bhāvaitabbā samathau ca vipassanā  ca, dvai dhammā sacchikātabbā vij‍jā ca vimutti cā’’ti| tattha samathaṃ bhāvaintau rūpaṃ parijānāti, rūpaṃ parijānantau taṇhaṃ pajahati, taṇhaṃ pajahantau rāgavirāgā  caitauvimuttiṃ sacchikarauti, vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau nāmaṃ parijānāti, nāmaṃ parijānantau avij‍jaṃ pajahati, avij‍jaṃ pajahantau avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimuttiṃ  sacchikarauti| yadā bhikkhunau dvai dhammā pariñ‍ñātā bhavanti nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca, tathāssa dvai dhammā pahīnā bhavanti avij‍jā ca bhavataṇhā ca| dvai dhammā bhāvitā bhavanti samathau ca vipassanā ca, dvai dhammā sacchikātabbā bhavanti vij‍jā ca vimutti ca| aittāvatā bhikkhu katakic‍cau bhavati| aisā saupādisaisā nibbānadhātu| tassa āyupariyādānā jīvitindriyassa uparaudhā idañ‍ca dukkhaṃ nirujjhati, añ‍ñañ‍ca dukkhaṃ na uppaj‍jati| tattha yau imaisaṃ khandhānaṃ dhātuāyatanānaṃ niraudhau vūpasamau añ‍ñaisañ‍ca khandhadhātuāyatanānaṃ appaṭisandhi apātubhāvau, ayaṃ anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu|

tattha katamaṃ alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ? yaṃdhātukau alaubhau alubbhanā alubbhitattaṃ anicchā apatthanā akantā anajjhausānaṃ| ayaṃ alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ| kassaitaṃ mūlaṃ? alaubhajassa kusalassa kāyakammassa vacīkammassa manaukammassa taṃsampayuttānañ‍ca cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ mūlaṃ| atha vā ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau kusalanti vuc‍cati, sau tiṇṇaṃ maggaṅgānaṃ mūlaṃ| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ, sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvāyāmassa sammāsamādhissa ca imaisaṃ mūlanti, tasmā kusalamūlanti vuc‍cati|

tattha katamaṃ adausau kusalamūlaṃ? yā  sattaisu vā saṅkhāraisu vā anaghātau appaṭighātau abyāpatti abyāpādau adausau maittā maittāyanā atthakāmatā hitakāmatā caitasau pasādau, ayaṃ adausau kusalamūlaṃ| kassaitaṃ mūlaṃ? adausajassa kusalassa kāyakammassa vacīkammassa manaukammassa taṃsampayuttānañ‍ca cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ mūlaṃ| atha vā tiṇṇaṃ maggaṅgānaṃ mūlaṃ| katamaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ? sammāvācāya sammākammantassa sammāājīvassa ca imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ maggaṅgānaṃ mūlaṃ, tasmā kusalamūlanti vuc‍cati|

tattha katamaṃ amauhau kusalamūlaṃ? yaṃ catūsu ariyasac‍caisu yathābhūtaṃ ñāṇadassanaṃ abhisamayau sammā ca pac‍cāgamau paṭivaidhau amauhau asammuyhanā asammauhau vij‍jāpakāsau ālaukau anāvaraṇaṃ  saikkhānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ, ayaṃ amauhau kusalamūlaṃ| kassaitaṃ mūlaṃ? amauhajassa kusalassa kāyakammassa vacīkammassa manaukammassa taṃsampayuttānañ‍ca cittacaitasikānaṃ dhammānaṃ mūlaṃ| atha vā dvin‍naṃ maggaṅgānaṃ aitaṃ mūlaṃ| katamaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ? sammādiṭṭhiyā ca sammāsatiyā ca imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ maggaṅgānaṃ mūlaṃ, tasmā kusalamūlanti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ  imaisaṃ tīhi kusalamūlaihi aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau yaujaitabbau|

66. tattha  katamā anic‍casañ‍ñā? ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā uppādavayadhamminau’’ti ca yā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā vavatthapanā uggāhau, ayaṃ anic‍casañ‍ñā| tassā kau nissandau? anic‍casañ‍ñāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya aṭṭhasu laukadhammaisu cittaṃ nānusandhati na sandhati na saṇṭhahati, upaikkhā vā paṭik‍kūlatā vā saṇṭhahati, ayamassā nissandau|

tattha katamā dukkhasañ‍ñā? ‘‘sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhā’’ti yā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā vavatthapanā uggāhau, ayaṃ dukkhasañ‍ñā| tassā kau nissandau? dukkhasañ‍ñāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya ālassai saṃpamādai vimhayai ca cittaṃ nānusandhati na sandhati na saṇṭhahati, upaikkhā vā paṭik‍kūlatā vā saṇṭhahati, ayamassā nissandau|

tattha katamā anattasañ‍ñā? ‘‘sabbaisu dhammaisu anattā’’ti yā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā vavatthapanā uggāhau, ayaṃ anattasañ‍ñā| tassā kau nissandau, anattasañ‍ñāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya ahaṅkārau cittaṃ nānusandhati na sandhati, mamaṅkārau na saṇṭhahati, upaikkhā vā paṭik‍kūlatā vā saṇṭhahati, ayamassā nissandau|

tattha katamā asubhasañ‍ñā? ‘‘satta saṅkhārā  asubhā’’ti yā sañ‍ñā sañ‍jānanā vavatthapanā uggāhau, ayaṃ asubhasañ‍ñā| tassā kau nissandau? asubhasañ‍ñāya bhāvitāya bahulīkatāya subhanimittai cittaṃ nānusandhati na sandhati na saṇṭhahati, upaikkhā vā paṭik‍kūlatā vā saṇṭhahati, ayamassā nissandau|

tattha pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ pariñ‍ñā bhagavatā daisitā, yau tattha asubhasañ‍ñā rūpakkhandhassa pariñ‍ñattaṃ, dukkhasañ‍ñā vaidanākkhandhassa pariñ‍ñattaṃ, anattasañ‍ñā sañ‍ñākkhandhassa saṅkhārakkhandhassa pariñ‍ñattaṃ, anic‍casañ‍ñā viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa pariñ‍ñattaṃ| tattha samathaina taṇhaṃ samugghātaiti, vipassanā avij‍jaṃ  samugghātaiti, adausaina dausaṃ samugghātaiti, amauhaina mauhaṃ samugghātaiti, anic‍casañ‍ñāya nic‍casañ‍ñaṃ samugghātaiti, dukkhasañ‍ñāya sukhasañ‍ñaṃ samugghātaiti, anattasañ‍ñāya attasañ‍ñaṃ samugghātaiti, asubhasañ‍ñāya subhasañ‍ñaṃ samugghātaiti|

cittavikkhaipapaṭisaṃharaṇalakkhaṇau samathau, tassa jhānāni padaṭṭhānaṃ| sabbadhammaṃ yathābhūtaṃ paṭivaidhalakkhaṇā vipassanā, tassā sabbanaiyyaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| icchāpaṭisaṃharaṇalakkhaṇau alaubhau, tassa adin‍nādānā vairamaṇī padaṭṭhānaṃ| abyāpādalakkhaṇau adausau, tassa pāṇātipātā vairamaṇī  padaṭṭhānaṃ| vatthuappaṭihatalakkhaṇau amauhau, tassa sammāpaṭipatti padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṅkhatānaṃ dhammānaṃ vināsaggahaṇalakkhaṇā anic‍casañ‍ñā, tassā udayabbayau padaṭṭhānaṃ| sāsavaphassasañ‍jānanalakkhaṇā dukkhasañ‍ñā, tassā vaidanā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sabbadhammaanupagamanalakkhaṇā anattasañ‍ñā, tassā dhammasañ‍ñā  padaṭṭhānaṃ| vinīlakavipubbakauddhumātakasamuggahaṇalakkhaṇā asubhasañ‍ñā, tassā nibbidā padaṭṭhānaṃ| imaisu navasu padaisu upadiṭṭhaisu sabbau kusalapakkhau upadiṭṭhau bhavati, sau ca bahussutaina sak‍kā jānituṃ nau appassutaina, pañ‍ñavatā nau duppañ‍ñaina, yuttaina nau ayuttaināti|

67. tattha nic‍casañ‍ñādhimuttassa aparāparaṃ cittaṃ paṇāmaintau satimapac‍cavaikkhatau anic‍casañ‍ñā na upaṭṭhāti, pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu sukhassādādhimuttassa iriyāpathassa agatimapac‍cavaikkhatau dukkhasañ‍ñā na upaṭṭhāti, khandhadhātuāyatanaisu attādhimuttassa nānādhātuanaikadhātuvinibbhaugamapac‍cavaikkhatau anattasañ‍ñā na upaṭṭhāti, vaṇṇasaṇṭhānābhiratassa kāyai subhādhimuttassa ca vippaṭicchan‍nā asubhasañ‍ñā na upaṭṭhāti|

avippaṭisāralakkhaṇā saddhā, saddahanā pac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ| tassa cattāri sautāpattiyaṅgāni padaṭṭhānaṃ| aivañhi vuttaṃ bhagavatā passa saṃyuttanikāyai saddhindriyaṃ bhikkhavai, kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, catūsu sautāpattiyaṅgaisu kusalaisu dhammaisu|

sūrāapaṭikkhaipanalakkhaṇaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ, vīriyindriyārambhau pac‍cupaṭṭhānaṃ| tassa atītā cattārau sammappadhānā padaṭṭhānaṃ| yathā vuttaṃ bhagavatā passa saṃyuttanikāyai vīriyindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, catūsu sammappadhānaisu|

sati  saraṇalakkhaṇā, asammauhapac‍cupaṭṭhānā| tassa atītā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā padaṭṭhānaṃ| yathā vuttaṃ bhagavatā  satindriyaṃ bhikkhavai, kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, catūsu satipaṭṭhānaisu|

aikaggalakkhaṇau samādhi, avikkhaipapac‍cupaṭṭhānau, tassa cattāri ñāṇāni padaṭṭhānaṃ| yathā vuttaṃ bhagavatā samādhindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, catūsu jhānaisu|

pajānanalakkhaṇā pañ‍ñā, bhūtatthasantīraṇā pac‍cupaṭṭhānā, tassa cattāri ariyasac‍cāni padaṭṭhānaṃ| yathā vuttaṃ bhagavatā passa saṃyuttanikāyai pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kuhiṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ, catūsu ariyasac‍caisu|

cattāri  cak‍kāni passa a॰ ni॰ 4.31 patirūpadaisavāsau cak‍kaṃ, sappurisūpanissayau cak‍kaṃ, attasammāpaṇidhānaṃ cak‍kaṃ, pubbai katapuñ‍ñatā cak‍kaṃ| tattha ariyasan‍nissayalakkhaṇau patirūpadaisavāsau, sau sappurisūpanissayassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ariyasan‍nissayalakkhaṇau sappurissūpanissayau, sau attasammāpaṇidhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| sammāpaṭipattilakkhaṇaṃ attasammāpaṇidhānaṃ, taṃ puñ‍ñānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| kusaladhammaupacayalakkhaṇaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, taṃ sabbasampattīnaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ|

aikādasasīlamūlakā dhammā sīlavatau avippaṭisārau bhavati…pai॰… sau vimuttiñāṇadassanaṃ ‘‘nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti pajānanā| tattha vairamaṇilakkhaṇaṃ sīlaṃ, taṃ avippaṭisārassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| na attānuvādalakkhaṇau avippaṭisārau, sau pāmauj‍jassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| abhippamaudanalakkhaṇaṃ pāmauj‍jaṃ, taṃ pītiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| attamanalakkhaṇā pīti, sā passaddhiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| kammaniyalakkhaṇā passaddhi, sā sukhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| abyāpādalakkhaṇaṃ sukhaṃ, taṃ samādhinau padaṭṭhānaṃ| avikkhaipanalakkhaṇau samādhi, sau yathābhūtañāṇadassanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| aviparītasantīraṇalakkhaṇā pañ‍ñā, sā nibbidāya padaṭṭhānaṃ anālayanalakkhaṇā nibbidā, sā virāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| asaṃkilaisalakkhaṇau virāgau, sau vimuttiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| akusaladhammavivaikalakkhaṇā vimutti, sā vimuttinau vaudānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ|

68. catassau ariyabhūmiyau cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni| tattha yau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, aisā dassanabhūmi| sautāpattiphalañ‍ca sau yathābhūtaṃ  pajānitvā nibbindati, idaṃ tanukāmarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ byāpādānaṃ| sakadāgāmiphalañ‍ca saṇhaṃ viraj‍jati, ayaṃ rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti| anāgāmiphalañ‍ca yaṃ avij‍jāvirāgā vimuc‍cati, ayaṃ katābhūmi| arahattañ‍ca sāmañ‍ñaphalānīti kau vacanatthau, ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sāmañ‍ñaṃ, tassaitāni phalāni sāmañ‍ñaphalānīti vuc‍cati| kissa brahmañ‍ñaphalānīti vuc‍cantai? brahmañ‍ñaariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau, tassa tāni phalānīti brahmañ‍ñaphalānīti vuc‍cantai|

tattha sautāpan‍nau kathaṃ hauti? saha sac‍cābhisamayā ariyasāvakassa tīṇi saṃyaujanāni pahīyanti sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāsau ca, imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānā parikkhayā ariyasāvakau hauti sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau yāva dukkhassantaṃ karauti|

tattha  katamā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi? assutavā bālau puthuj‍janau yāva ariyadhammai akauvidau, sau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati yāva viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ attānaṃ, sau imaisu pañ‍casu khandhaisu attaggāhau vā attaniyaggāhau vā aisauhamasmi aikasmiṃ vasavattikau avattitau (pī॰ ka॰) pakkhittau anuggahau anusayantau aṅgamaṅganti parati| yā tathābhūtassa khanti ruci paikkhanā ākāraparivitak‍kau diṭṭhinijjhāyanā abhippasan‍nā, ayaṃ vuc‍catai sak‍kāyadiṭṭhīti|

tattha pañ‍ca diṭṭhiyau ucchaidaṃ bhajanti| katamāyau pañ‍ca? rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, yāva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, imāyau pañ‍ca ucchaidaṃ bhajanti, avasaisāyau pan‍narasa sassataṃ bhajanti| iti sak‍kāyadiṭṭhipahānā dvāsaṭṭhidiṭṭhigatāni pahīyanti| pahānā ucchaidaṃ sassatañ‍ca na bhajati| iti ucchaidasassatappahānā ariyasāvakassa na kiñ‍ci diṭṭhigataṃ bhavati, añ‍ñā vā laukuttarāya sammādiṭṭhiyā| kathaṃ pana sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na bhavati? idha ariyasāvakau sutavā hauti, sabbau suk‍kapakkhau kātabbau, yāva ariyadhammaisu kauvidau rūpaṃ anattatau samanupassati, yāva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamassa samanupassantassa sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na bhavati|

kathaṃ  vicikicchā na bhavati? idha ariyasāvakau buddhai na kaṅkhati, na vicikicchati abhippasīdati, itipi sau bhagavāti sabbaṃ| dhammai na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati sabbaṃ| yāva taṇhakkhayau virāgau niraudhau nibbānanti, iminā dutiyaina ākaṅkhiyaina dhammaina  saman‍nāgatau hauti| saṅghai na kaṅkhati…pai॰… yāva pūjā daivānañ‍ca manussānañ‍cāti, iminā tatiyaina ākaṅkhiyaina dhammaina saman‍nāgatau hauti|

sabbai saṅkhārā dukkhāti na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati abhippasīdati| taṇhā dukkhasamudayauti na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati| taṇhāniraudhā dukkhaniraudhauti na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati| ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadāti na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati adhimuc‍cati abhippasīdati| yāva buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā dukkhai vā samudayai vā niraudhai vā maggai vā kaṅkhāyanā vimati vicikicchā dvaidhāpathā āsappanā appanā (pī॰ ka॰) dha॰ sa॰ 1008 nikkhaipakaṇḍai passitabbaṃ parisappanā anavaṭṭhānaṃ adhiṭṭhāgamanaṃ aniṭṭhāgamanaṃ (ka॰) anaikaṃsau anaikaṃsikatā, tai tassa pahīnā bhavanti paṇun‍nā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā|

69. tattha sīlabbataparāmāsau dvidhā – sīlassa vā suddhassa vā| tattha sīlassa sīlabbataparāmāsau  imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā daivau vā bhavissāmi daivañ‍ñatarau vā tattha kapautapādāhi accharāhi saddhiṃ kīḷissāmi ramissāmi paricarissāmīti| yathābhūtadassananti rucivimutti rāgau rāgaparivattakā diṭṭhirūpanā passanā asantussitassa sīlabbataparāmāsau| tattha katamau suddhassa sīlabbataparāmāsau? idhaikac‍cau sīlaṃ parāmasati, sīlaina sujjhati, sīlaina nīyati, sīlaina muc‍cati, sukhaṃ vītik‍kamati, dukkhaṃ vītik‍kamati, sukhadukkhaṃ  vītik‍kamati anupāpuṇāti uparimaina| tadubhayaṃ sīlavataṃ parāmasati tadubhayaina sīlavataina sujjhanti muc‍canti nīyanti, sukhaṃ vītik‍kamanti, dukkhaṃ vītik‍kamanti, sukhadukkhaṃ vītik‍kamanti, anupāpuṇantīti avisucikaraṃ dhammaṃ avimuttikaraṃ dhammaṃ visucitau vimuttitau pac‍cāgacchantassa yā tathābhūtassa khanti ruci mutti paikkhanā ākāraparivitak‍kau diṭṭhinijjhāyanā passanā, ayaṃ suddhassa sīlabbataparāmāsau| aitai ubhau parāmāsā ariyasāvakassa pahīnā  bhavanti yāva āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā, sau sīlavā bhavati ariyakantaihi sīlaihi saman‍nāgatau akkhaṇḍaihi yāva upasamasaṃvattanikaihi| imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānā sutavā ariyasāvakau bhavati sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau, sabbaṃ|

sahasac‍cābhisamayā, iti kau vacanatthau? cattārau abhisamayā, pariñ‍ñābhisamayau pahānābhisamayau sacchikiriyābhisamayau bhāvanābhisamayau|

tattha ariyasāvakau dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, samudayaṃ pahānābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, niraudhaṃ sacchikiriyābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, maggaṃ bhāvanābhisamayaina abhisamaiti| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? dukkhassa pariñ‍ñābhisamayau, samudayassa pahānābhisamayau, niraudhassa sacchikiriyābhisamayau, maggassa bhāvanābhisamayau| samathavipassanāya kathaṃ abhisamaiti? ārammaṇai cittaṃ upanibandhaitvā pañ‍cakkhandhai dukkhatau passati| tattha yau upanibandhau, ayaṃ samathau| yā pariyaugāhanā, ayaṃ vipassanā| pañ‍cakkhandhai  dukkhāti passatau yau pañ‍cakkhandhaisu ālayau nikanti upagamanaṃ ajjhausānā icchā mucchā paṇidhi patthanā pahīyati| tattha pañ‍cakkhandhā dukkhaṃ| yau tattha ālayau nikanti upagamanaṃ ajjhausānaṃ icchā mucchā paṇidhi patthanā, ayaṃ samudayau| yaṃ tassa pahānaṃ, sau niraudhau samathau vipassanā ca maggau, aivaṃ taisaṃ catun‍naṃ ariyasac‍cānaṃ aikakālai aikakkhaṇai aikacittai apubbaṃ acarimaṃ abhisamayau bhavati| taināha bhagavā ‘‘sahasac‍cābhisamayā ariyasāvakassa tīṇi saṃyaujanāni pahīyantī’’ti|
