70. tattha  samathavipassanā yuganaddhā vattamānā aikakālai aikakkhaṇai aikacittai cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, yāva maggaṃ bhāvanābhisamayaina abhisamaiti| kiṃ kāraṇā? dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayau, yāva maggaṃ bhāvanābhisamayau| aivaṃ diṭṭhantau yathā nāvā jalaṃ gacchantī cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, pārimaṃ tīraṃ pāpaiti, aurimaṃ tīraṃ jahati, bhāraṃ vahati, sautaṃ chindati; aivamaiva samathavipassanā yuganaddhā vattamānā aikakālai aikakkhaṇai aikacittai cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, dukkhaṃ pariñ‍ñābhisamayaina abhisamaiti, yāva maggaṃ bhāvanābhisamayaina abhisamaiti| yathā vā sūriyau udayantau aikakālai apubbaṃ acarimaṃ cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, andhakāraṃ vidhamati, ālaukaṃ pātukarauti, rūpaṃ nidassīyati, sītaṃ pariyādiyati; aivamaiva samathavipassanā yuganaddhā vattamānā aikakālai…pai॰… yathā padīpau jalantau aikakālai apubbaṃ acarimaṃ cattāri kic‍cāni karauti, andhakāraṃ vidhamati, ālaukaṃ pātukarauti, rūpaṃ  nidassīyati, upādānaṃ pariyādiyati; aivamaiva samathavipassanā yuganaddhā vattamānā aikakālai…pai॰…|

yadā ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau bhavati avinipātadhammau niyatau yāva dukkhassantaṃ karauti, ayaṃ dassanabhūmi| sautāpattiphalañ‍ca sautāpattiphalai ṭhitau uttari samathavipassanaṃ bhāvaintau yuganaddhā vattamānā kāmarāgabyāpādānaṃ yaibhuyyaina pahānā ariyasāvakau hauti| sakadāgāmi pariniṭṭhitattā sakidaiva imaṃ laukaṃ āgantvā dukkhassantaṃ karauti, ayaṃ tanubhūmi|

sakadāgāmiphalañ‍ca yau sakadāgāmiphalai ṭhitau vipassanaṃ bhāvaintau kāmarāgabyāpādai sānusayai anavasaisaṃ pajahati, kāmarāgabyāpādaisu anavasaisaṃ pahīnaisu pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni bhavanti sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi sīlabbataparāmāsau vicikicchā kāmacchandau byāpādau ca, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānā pahānāya (pī॰ ka॰) ariyasāvakau hauti anāgāmī tattha parinibbāyī anāvattidhammau tasmā laukā, ayaṃ vītarāgabhūmi|

anāgāmiphalañ‍ca anāgāmiphalai ṭhitau uttari samathavipassanaṃ bhāvaintau pañ‍ca uddhambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pajahati rūparāgaarūparāgamānauddhac‍caavij‍jañ‍ca| imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ uddhambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānā ariyasāvakau arahā bhavati, khīṇāsavau vusitavā sammadañ‍ñā sampajañ‍ñau (pī॰ ka॰) vimuttau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau anuppattasadatthau, ayaṃ katābhūmi|

arahantauva  ayaṃ saupādisaisā nibbānadhātu| tassa āyukkhayā jīvitindriyāparaudhā idañ‍ca dukkhaṃ nirujjhati, añ‍ñañ‍ca  dukkhaṃ na uppaj‍jati| yau imassa dukkhassa niraudhau vūpasamau, añ‍ñassa ca apātubhāvau, ayaṃ  anupādisaisā nibbānadhātu| imā dvai nibbānadhātuyau| iti sac‍cāni vuttāni| sac‍cābhisamayau vuttau, kilaisavavatthānaṃ vuttaṃ, pahānaṃ vuttaṃ, bhūmiyau vuttā, phalāni vuttāni, nibbānadhātuyau vuttā| aivamimaisu vuttaisu sabbabaudhi vuttā bhavati| aittha yaugau karaṇīyau|

71. tattha katamāyau nava anupubbasamāpattiyau? cattāri jhānāni catassau ca arūpasamāpattiyau niraudhasamāpatti ca| tattha cattāri jhānāni katamāni? idha, bhikkhavai, passa dīghanikāyai bhikkhu vivic‍caiva kāmaihīti vitthāraina kātabbāni| tattha katamā cattārau arūpasamāpattiyau? virāginau vata vattabbau, yāva niraudhasamāpatti vitthāraina kātabbā| imāyau nava anupubbasamāpattiyau|

tattha katamaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? pañ‍caṅgavippayuttaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ| katamaihi pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi vippayuttaṃ? pañ‍cahi nīvaraṇaihi| tattha katamāni pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni? kāmacchandauti vitthāraitabbau| tattha katamau kāmacchandau? yau pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu chandarāgau paimaṃ nikanti ajjhausānaṃ icchā mucchā patthanā aparic‍cāgau anusayau pariyuṭṭhānaṃ, ayaṃ kāmacchandanīvaraṇaṃ| tattha  katamaṃ byāpādanīvaraṇaṃ? yau sattaisu saṅkhāraisu ca āghātau…pai॰… yathā dausai tathā niauṭṭhānā, ayaṃ byāpādau nīvaraṇaṃ| tattha katamaṃ middhaṃ? yā cittassa jaḷatā cittassa garuttaṃ cittassa akammanīyatā cittassa nikkhaipau niddāyanā pacalikatā pacalāyanā pacalāyanaṃ, idaṃ middhaṃ| tattha katamaṃ thinaṃ thīnaṃ (pī॰)? yā kāyassa thinatā jaḷatā kāyassa garuttā kāyassa appassaddhi, idaṃ thinaṃ| iti idañ‍ca thinaṃ purimakañ‍ca middhaṃ tadubhayaṃ thinamiddhanīvaraṇanti vuc‍cati| tattha katamaṃ uddhac‍caṃ? yau avūpasamau cittassa, idaṃ uddhac‍caṃ| tattha katamaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ? yau caitasau vilaikhau alañ‍canā vilañ‍canā hadayalaikhau vippaṭisārau, idaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ| iti idañ‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ purimakañ‍ca uddhac‍caṃ tadubhayaṃ uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍canīvaraṇanti vuc‍cati| tattha katamaṃ vicikicchānīvaraṇaṃ? yau buddhai vā dhammai vā saṅghai vā…pai॰… ayaṃ vicikicchā| api  ca khau pana pañ‍ca vicikicchāyau samanantarāyikā daisantarāyikā samāpattantarāyikā maggantarāyikā saggantarāyikā, imāyau pañ‍ca  vicikicchāyau| idha pana samāpattantarāyikā vicikicchā adhippaitā| imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā|

tattha nīvaraṇānīti kau vacanatthau, kutau nivārayantīti? sabbatau kusalapakkhikā nivārayanti| kathaṃ kiṃ kaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) nivārayanti? kāmacchandau asubhatau nivārayati, byāpādau maittāya maittatau (pī॰) nivārayati, thinaṃ passaddhitau nivārayati, middhaṃ vīriyārambhatau nivārayati, uddhac‍caṃ samathatau nivārayati, kuk‍kuc‍caṃ avippaṭisāratau nivārayati, vicikicchā pañ‍ñātau paṭic‍casamuppādatau nivārayati|

aparau pariyāyau| kāmacchandau alaubhatau kusalamūlatau nivārayati, byāpādau adausatau nivārayati, thinamiddhaṃ samādhitau nivārayati, uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ satipaṭṭhānaihi nivārayati, vicikicchā amauhatau kusalamūlatau nivārayati|

aparau pariyāyau| tayau vihārā dibbavihārau brahmavihārau ariyavihārau| dibbavihārau cattāri jhānāni, brahmavihārau cattāri appamāṇāni, ariyavihārau sattatiṃsa baudhipakkhiyā dhammā| tattha kāmacchandau uddhac‍caṃ kuk‍kuc‍cañ‍ca dibbavihāraṃ nivārayati, byāpādau brahmavihāraṃ nivārayati, thinamiddhaṃ vicikicchā ca ariyavihāraṃ nivārayati|

aparau pariyāyau| kāmacchandau byāpādau uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cañ‍ca samathaṃ nivārayanti, thinamiddhaṃ vicikicchā ca vipassanaṃ nivārayanti, atau nīvaraṇanti vuc‍cantai| imaihi  pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi vippayuttaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ|

katamaihi pañ‍cahi aṅgaihi sampayuttaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ? vitak‍kavicāraihi pītiyā sukhaina ca cittaikaggatāya ca| imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ uppādapaṭilābhasaman‍nāgamau sacchikiriyaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paṭiladdhanti vuc‍cati| imāni pañ‍ca aṅgāni uppādaitvā viharatīti, taina vuc‍catai paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatīti dibbaina vihāraina|

tattha dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ pītisukhaina cittaikaggatāya ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaina imāni cattāri aṅgāni uppādaitvā sampādaitvā viharati, taina vuc‍cati dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatīti|

tattha  pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ satiyā sampajañ‍ñai sukhaina cittaikaggatāya upaikkhāya imāni pañ‍caṅgāni uppādaitvā sampādaitvā viharati, taina vuc‍cati tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharatīti|

tattha catutthaṃ jhānaṃ caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ upaikkhāya satipārisuddhiyā adukkhamasukhāya vaidanāya cittaikaggatā ca, imaihi catūhaṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ| iti imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ aṅgānaṃ uppādau paṭilābhau saman‍nāgamau sacchikiriyā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ paṭiladdhanti vuc‍cati| imāni cattāri jhānāni uppādaitvā sampādaitvā upasampaj‍ja viharati, taina vuc‍cati dibbaina vihāraina viharatīti|

tattha  katamau anic‍caṭṭhau? pīḷanaṭṭhau anic‍caṭṭhau pabhaṅgaṭṭhau sampāpanaṭṭhau vivaikaṭṭhau anic‍caṭṭhau, ayaṃ anic‍caṭṭhau|

tattha katamau dukkhaṭṭhau? pīḷanaṭṭhau dukkhaṭṭhau sampīḷanaṭṭhau saṃvaigaṭṭhau byādhinaṭṭhau, ayaṃ dukkhaṭṭhau|

tattha katamau suñ‍ñaṭṭhau? anupalittau suñ‍ñaṭṭhau, asambhājanaṭṭhau gatapaṭṭhau appaṭṭhau (pī॰) vivaṭṭaṭṭhau, ayaṃ suñ‍ñaṭṭhau|

tattha katamau anattaṭṭhau? anissariyaṭṭhau anattaṭṭhau, avasavattanaṭṭhau, akāmakāriṭṭhau parividaṭṭhau, ayaṃ anattaṭṭhauti|

suttatthasamuc‍cayau nāma saṃvattisantikā paiṭakabhūmi samattā|



7. hārasampātabhūmi

72. jhānaṃ  virāgau| cattāri jhānāni vitthāraina kātabbāni| tāni duvidhāni; baujjhaṅgavippayuttāni ca baujjhaṅgasampayuttāni ca| tattha baujjhaṅgavippayuttāni bāhirakāni, baujjhaṅgasampayuttāni ariyapuggalāni| tattha yaina cha puggalamūlāni taisaṃ nikkhipaitvā rāgacaritau, dausacaritau, mauhacaritau, rāgadausacaritau, rāgamauhacaritau, dausamauhacaritau, samabhāgacaritau, iti imaisaṃ puggalānaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jitānaṃ pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni paṭipakkhau taisaṃ paṭighātāya yathā asamatthau tīṇi akusalamūlāni  niggaṇhāti| laubhaina akusalamūlaina abhijjhā ca uddhac‍cañ‍ca uppilavataṃ alaubhaina kusalamūlaina niggaṇhāti, kuk‍kuc‍cañ‍ca vicikicchā ca mauhapakkhau, taṃ amauhaina niggaṇhāti| dausau ca thinamiddhañ‍ca dausapakkhau, taṃ adausaina niggaṇhāti|

tattha alaubhassa pāripūriyā naikkhammavitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti| tattha adausassa pāripūriyā abyāpādavitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti| tattha amauhassa pāripūriyā avihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ vitak‍kaiti| tattha alaubhassa pāripūriyā vivittau hauti kāmaihi| tattha adausassa pāripūriyā amauhassa pāripūriyā ca vivittau hauti pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi, savitak‍kaṃ  savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati|

vitak‍kāti tayau vitak‍kā – naikkhammavitak‍kau abyāpādavitak‍kau avihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tattha paṭhamābhinipātau vitak‍kau, paṭiladdhassa vicaraṇaṃ vicārau| yathā purisau dūratau purisaṃ passati āgacchantaṃ, na ca tāva jānāti aisau itthīti vā purisauti vā yadā tu paṭilabhati itthīti vā purisauti vā aivaṃ vaṇṇauti vā aivaṃ saṇṭhānauti vā imai vitak‍kayantau uttari upaparikkhanti kiṃ nu khau ayaṃ sīlavā udāhu dussīlau aḍḍhau vā duggatauti vā| aivaṃ vicārau vitak‍kai appaiti, vicārau cariyati ca anuvattati ca| yathā pakkhī pubbaṃ āyūhati pacchā nāyūhati yathā āyūhanā aivaṃ vitak‍kau, yathā pakkhānaṃ pasāraṇaṃ  aivaṃ vicārau anupālati vitak‍kaiti vicarati vicāraiti| vitak‍kayati vitak‍kaiti, anuvicarati vicāraiti| kāmasañ‍ñāya paṭipakkhau vitak‍kau, byāpādasañ‍ñāya vihiṃsasañ‍ñāya ca paṭipakkhau vicārau| vitak‍kānaṃ kammaṃ akusalassa amanasikārau, vicārānaṃ kammaṃ jaiṭṭhānaṃ saṃvāraṇā| yathā palikau tuṇhikau sajjhāyaṃ karauti aivaṃ vitak‍kau, yathā taṃyaiva anupassati aivaṃ vicārau| yathā apariñ‍ñā aivaṃ vitak‍kau| yathā pariñ‍ñā aivaṃ vicārau| niruttipaṭisambhidāyañ‍ca paṭibhānapaṭisambhidāyañ‍ca vitak‍kau, dhammapaṭisambhidāyañ‍ca atthapaṭisambhidāyañ‍ca vicārau| kal‍litā kausal‍lattaṃ cittassa vitak‍kau, abhinīhārakausal‍laṃ cittassa vicārau| idaṃ kusalaṃ idaṃ akusalaṃ idaṃ bhāvaitabbaṃ idaṃ pahātabbaṃ idaṃ sacchikātabbanti vitak‍kau, yathā pahānañ‍ca bhāvanā ca sacchikiriyā ca aivaṃ vicārau| imaisu vitak‍kavicāraisu ṭhitassa duvidhaṃ dukkhaṃ na uppaj‍jati kāyikañ‍ca caitasikañ‍ca; duvidhaṃ sukhaṃ uppaj‍jati kāyikañ‍ca  caitasikañ‍ca| iti vitak‍kajanitaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ pīti kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ kāyikauyaiva| yā tattha cittassa aikaggatā, ayaṃ samādhi| iti paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ pañ‍caṅgavippahīnaṃ pañ‍caṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ|

taisaṃyaiva vitak‍kavicārānaṃ abhikkhaṇaṃ āsaivanāya tassa tappauṇamānasaṃ hauti| tassa vitak‍kavicārā auḷārikā khāyanti| yañ‍ca pītisukhañ‍ca naikkhammañ‍ca auḷārikaṃ bhavati| api ca samādhijā pīti rati ca jāyati| tassa vicārārammaṇaṃ| taisaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ caitau sampasīdati| yai vitak‍kavicārā dvai dhammānussaritabbā| pac‍cuppan‍nā daraṇitabbaṃ| taisaṃ vūpasamā aikaudibhāvaṃ cittaikaggataṃ hauti| tassa aikaudibhāvaina pīti pāripūriṃ gacchati| yā pīti, taṃ saumanassindriyaṃ, yaṃ sukhaṃ, taṃ sukhindriyaṃ| yā cittaikaggatā, ayaṃ samādhi| taṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ| sau pītiyā virāgā yāti aujahi jal‍lasahagataṃ|

73. tattha saumanassacittamupādānanti ca sau taṃ vicinantau upaikkhamaiva manasikarauti| sau pītiyā virāgā upaikkhakau viharati| yathā ca pītiyā sukhamānitaṃ, taṃ kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaiti sampajānau viharati| yaina satisampajañ‍ñaina upaikkhāpāripūriṃ gacchati| idaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ caturaṅgasaman‍nāgataṃ|

tathā  kāyikassa sukhassa pahānāya paṭhamai jhānai saumanassindriyaṃ nirujjhati| dutiyai jhānai dukkhindriyaṃ nirujjhati| sau sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati| tattha catūhi indriyaihi upaikkhā pasādā hauti, dukkhindriyaina daumanassindriyaina sukhindriyaina saumanassindriyaina ca| taisaṃ niraudhā upaikkhāsampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti, tattha sukhindriyaina saumanassindriyaina ca asati hauti, taisaṃ niraudhā satimā hauti, dukkhindriyaina daumanassindriyaina ca asampajañ‍ñaṃ, taisaṃ niraudhā sampajañ‍ñaṃ hauti, iti upaikkhāya ca sañ‍ñā, satau sampajānau cittaikaggatā ca idaṃ vuc‍catai ca catutthaṃ jhānaṃ|

tattha yau rāgacaritau puggalau tassa sukhindriyañ‍ca saumanassindriyañ‍ca; yau dausacaritau puggalau tassa dukkhindriyañ‍ca daumanassindriyañ‍ca; yau mauhacaritau puggalau tassa asati ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca|

tattha  rāgacaritassa puggalassa tatiyai jhānai catutthai ca anunayau nirujjhati, dausacaritassa paṭhamai jhānai dutiyai ca paṭighaṃ nirujjhati, mauhacaritassa puggalassa paṭhamai jhānai dutiyai ca asampajañ‍ñaṃ nirujjhati| tatiyai jhānai catutthai ca asati nirujjhati, aivamaiva taisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ cattāri jhānāni vaudānaṃ gamissanti|

tattha rāgadausacaritassa puggalassa asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca anunayau ca paṭighañ‍ca, taina hānabhāgiyaṃ pahānabhāgiyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) jhānaṃ hauti| tattha  rāgamauhacaritassa puggalassa anunayattaṃ ca ādīnavaṃ dassitā, taṃ tassa hānabhāgiyaṃ jhānaṃ hauti| tattha dausamauhacaritassa puggalassa paṭighau ca asati ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca ādīnavaṃ dassitā taina tassa hānabhāgiyaṃ jhānaṃ hauti|

tattha rāgadausamauhasamabhāgacaritassa puggalassa visaisabhāgiyaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, imāni cattāri jhānāni sattasu puggalaisu niddisitabbāni| catūsu ca samādhīsu chandasamādhinā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, vīriyasamādhinā dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, cittasamādhinā tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, vīmaṃsāsamādhinā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ| appaṇihitaina paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, suñ‍ñatāya dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, animittaina tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, ānāpānassatiyā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ| kāmavitak‍kabyāpādānañ‍ca taṃ taṃ vūpasamaina paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ hauti, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamaina dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, sukhindriyasaumanassindriyānaṃ vūpasamaina tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, kāyasaṅkhārānaṃ vūpasamaina catutthaṃ jhānañ‍ca| cāgādhiṭṭhānaina paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ, sac‍cādhiṭṭhānaina dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ, pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaina tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ, upasamādhiṭṭhānaina catutthaṃ jhānaṃ| imāni cattāri jhānāni saṅkhaipaniddaisaina niddiṭṭhāni, tattha samādhindriyaṃ pāripūriṃ gacchati| anuvattanakāni cattāri, tattha yau paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ nissāya āsavakkhayaṃ pāpuṇāti, sau sukhāya  paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya daumanassindriyapaṭipakkhaina| yau dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, sau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya dukkhindriyapaṭipakkhaina| yau tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, sau sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya saumanassindriyapaṭipakkhaina| yau catutthaṃ jhānaṃ  nissāya āsavānaṃ khayaṃ pāpuṇāti, sau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya sukhindriyapaṭipakkhaina gatau|

pakiṇṇakaniddaisau|

74. yāni  cattāri jhānāni, taisaṃ jhānānaṃ imāni aṅgāni, taisaṃ aṅgānaṃ samūhau sammauhau (pī॰ ka॰) assa aṅgā, ayaṃ jhānabhūmi kau visaisauti assa visaisau| imai sambhārā taihi ayaṃ samudāgamau, tassa samudāgamassa ayaṃ upanisā, tāya upanisāya ayaṃ bhāvanā| tassā bhāvanāya ayaṃ ādīnavau| taina ayaṃ parihāni| kassa parihānīti tadupagajjhāyinau tadupakajjhāyinau (pī॰ ka॰)| taṃ yathā bhaṇitaṃ pac‍cavaikkhantau ayaṃ visaisau| taina visaisaina ayaṃ assādau, sau kassa assādau ajhāniyā jhāyinau, tassā ajhāniyā jhāyinau, idaṃ kal‍litā kausal‍lai ṭhitajjhānaṃ anaumaddiyataṃ gacchati jhānabalaṃ, jhānabalai ṭhitassa ayaṃ pāramippattassa imāni jhānaṅgāni anāvilasaṅkappau paṭhamai jhānai jhānaṅgāni bhāvī| sau pīti tadanusārittāva paṭhamai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ tassaṅgunau ca dhammā tadabhisan‍nitāya ca| pīti dutiyai jhānai jhānaṅgadhammatā khau pana tathā pavattassa sahagataṃ jhānaṅgadhammaṃ sasukhatāya ajjhattaṃ sampasādau dutiyai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ manausampasādanatāya  tadabhisan‍nitāya ca| pīti dutiyai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ samādhitā samādhikā (pī॰) pīti dutiyai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ, caitasau aikaudibhāvau dutiyai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ, upaikkhā phassatā tatiyai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ, sukhaṃ tassa aṅganti ca| caitasau aikaudibhāvau catutthai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ, upaikkhā adukkhamasukhā catutthai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ, abhinisābhūmi upaikkhāsatipārisuddhi catutthai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ| satipārisuddhi ca anaikajjhābhūmīsu jhānaṅgasamāyuttā pīti caitasau aikaudibhāvau catutthai jhānai jhānaṅgaṃ|

tattha katamā jhānabhūmi? savitak‍kai savicārai vivaikā anugatā paṭhamai jhānai jhānabhūmi| avitak‍kai avicārai ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ janitaṃ pītimanugatā dutiyai jhānai jhānabhūmi| sukhasātasamauhitā sappītikā tatiyai jhānai jhānabhūmi| tassa sukhadukkhasahagatā abhinīhārasahagatā catutthai jhānai jhānabhūmi| appamāṇasahagatā sattārammaṇā paṭhamai jhānai jhānabhūmi| abhibhūmiāyatanasahagatā rūpasañ‍ñīsu dutiyai jhānai jhānabhūmi| vimaukkhasahagatānaṃ  vimaukkhaisu tatiyai jhānai jhānabhūmi| anupassanāsahagatā kāyasaṅkhārā sammā catutthassa jhānassa bhūmi|

75. tattha  katamai jhānavisaisā? vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca pāpakaihi akusalaihi dhammaihi cittacaitasikasahagatā kāmadhātusamatik‍kamanatāpi, ayaṃ jhānavisaisau| avitak‍kā caiva avicārā ca sappītikāya satisahagatāya pītisahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti| ayaṃ  jhānavisaisau| avitak‍kāya bhūmiyā avicāraiyaiva sati anugatā upaikkhāsahagatā manasikārā samudācaranti| tadanudhammatāya ca sati saṇḍahati sandahati (pī॰)| tañ‍ca bhūmiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ jhānavisaisau| satipārisuddhisahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, tañ‍ca bhūmiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ jhānavisaisau| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasahagatāya bhūmiyaṃ ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, tañ‍ca bhūmiṃ upasampaj‍ja viharati, ayaṃ jhānavisaisau|

jhānasambhārā naikkhammavitak‍kau sambhārau kāmavitak‍kavinaudanādhippāyatā| abyāpādavitak‍kau sambhārau byāpādavitak‍kapaṭivinaudanādhippāyatā| avihiṃsāvitak‍kau sambhārau vihiṃsāvitak‍kapaṭivinaudanādhippāyatā| indriyaisu guttadvāratā appicchatā sambhārau parisuddhājīvau catun‍naṃ samāpattīnaṃ sambhārau akammassa vihāritā| maggasambhārau samāpattipaj‍janatā| phalasambhārau jhānanibbattitāya jhānasamudāgamau| kusalahaitu yaṃ jhānaṃ samudayaṃ gacchanti kau ca kauci (ka॰) na kutauci naikkhammappattā samudāgacchanti| ālambaniraudhasamādhi santau samudāgacchanti| avītik‍kantā samudāgacchanti| sukhindriyaṃ saumanassindriyaṃ pahānāya tai ca abyāpaj‍jatāya samudāgacchanti| taṃ pana sandhāya samudāgacchanti| aparidāhanāya samudāgacchanti| ayaṃ ñāṇasamudāgamau|

76. tattha katamā upanisā? kalyāṇamittatā  jhānassa upanisā| kalyāṇasampavaṅkatā jhānassa upanisā| indriyaisu guttadvāratā jhānassa upanisā| asantuṭṭhitā kusalaisu dhammaisu jhānassa upanisā| saddhammassavanaṃ jhānassa upanisā| saṃvaijaniyai ṭhānai saṃviggassa yaunisau padhānaṃ| ayaṃ jhānaupanisā|

tattha  katamā bhāvanā? maittāsaivanā abyāpādavitak‍kabhāvanā| karuṇāsaivanā avihiṃsāvitak‍kabhāvanā| muditābhāvanā pītisukhasampajañ‍ñā kāritā| upaikkhābhāvanā passavatā  upaikkhābhāvanā apassavatā upaikkhā ca ajjhupaikkhā ca, asubhasañ‍ñābhāvanā dukkhāpaṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā bhavasandhābhiñ‍ñā bhavasandhānaṃ, sā chabbidhā bhāvanā bhāvitā bahulīkatā anuṭṭhitā vatthukatā yānīkatā paricitā susamāraddhā| ayaṃ bhāvanā|

aivaṃ bhāvayantassa ayaṃ ādīnavau| paṭhamai jhānai saṅkhārasaman‍nāgatau aisau dhammau assutau sāsavau| sacai aisa dhammau ayaṃ sīlau āsan‍napaṭipakkhau ca aisa dhammau kāmau paticārau pativicārau samāpattīnaṃ ca sabbauḷārikau aisa dhammau vitak‍kavicārau ca| tattha cittaṃ khaubhainti, kāyau caittha kilamati, kāyamhi caittha kilantai cittaṃ vihañ‍ñati| anabhinīhārakkhamauva abhiñ‍ñānaṃ imai ādīnavā paṭhamai jhānai|

dutiyai  jhānai imai ādīnavā pītipharaṇasahagatau ca aisau dhammau, na samudācārassaiti cittaṃ| asaudhayaṃ upagamau caisa dhammau upagamiparissayau upagamiparicayau (pī॰) daumanassapac‍catthikau caisa dhammau| tattha tattha yuttīnaṃ pīti paraj‍jatau caisa dhammau duk‍karaṃ hauti, avattasantāsabhūmiparivaj‍jayantau catūsu dukkhatāsu aisa dhammau anuviddhāpanasaddhāya anuviddhā passatiyā (pī॰) dukkhatāya ca na palibaudhadukkhatāya ca abhiñ‍ñādukkhatāya ca raugadukkhatāya ca, imai ādīnavā dutiyai jhānai|

tattha katamai ādīnavā tatiyai jhānai? upaikkhāsukhasahagatāya tattha sātāvīnaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upaikkhāsukhaṃ parivattitau aisa dhammau taina nic‍casañ‍ñitānañ‍ca yaṃ hauti| dukkhaupaniyaṃ sukhaṃ cittassa saṅkhaubhataṃ upādāya sukhadukkhāya gatau savati| sukhadukkhānukatañ‍ca upādāya anabhihārakkhamaṃ cittaṃ hauti| abhiñ‍ñāya sacchikiriyāsu sabbaipi caitai dhammā tīsu jhānasamāpattīsu catūhi ca dukkhatāhi anuviddhānaṃ sā bhayā dukkhatāya palibaudhadukkhatāya ca abhiñ‍ñāya dukkhatāya ca imai ādīnavā tatiyai jhānai|

tattha  katamai  ādīnavā catutthai jhānai? ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāsamāpattikā tai dhammānusamāpattikā aitissā ca bhūmiyaṃ sātānaṃ bālaputhuj‍janānaṃ anaikavidhāni diṭṭhigatāni uppaj‍janti| auḷārikā sukhumaihi ca rūpasañ‍ñāhi anuvidhāni aitāni jhānāni sadā anudayamaittājhānakalānudanukalāya sādhāraṇā, duk‍karā ca sabbai cattārau mahāsambhārā samudāgatāni ca aitāni jhānāni añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ nissāya samudāgacchanti| aittha samudāgatā ca aitai dhammā na samattā haunti| asamuggahitanimittā ca aitai dhammā parihāyanti| nirujjhanti ca aitai dhammā na upādiyanti nirujjhaṅgāni ca, aitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ jhānāni nimittāni na jhānanimittasañ‍ñā  vaukirati| appaṭiladdhapubbā ca jhāyīvasaina ca bhavati jhāyī ca vasaina ca bhavati (pī॰ ka॰)| imaihi ādīnavaihi ayaṃ jhānaparihāni|

77. niraudhasamāpattiyā apaṭisaṅkhāya avasaisasañ‍ñinau ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā sañ‍ñāmanasikārā samudācaranti, sau niraudhasamāpattitau parihāyati| ānaiñ‍jasañ‍ñinau asañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasahagatā manasikārā samudācaranti, tañ‍ca bhūmiṃ na pajānāti, sau tatau parihāyati| ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasañ‍ñā manasikārā samudācaranti, tañ‍ca bhūmiṃ na pajānāti, sau tatau parihāyati| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samāpan‍nassa rūpasañ‍ñāsahagatā| vitthāraina…pai॰… yāva paṭhamai jhānai kāmasañ‍ñāsahagatā kātabbā| sakassa  sā tassa (pī॰ ka॰) parihāyati, kalaṅkajjhānai kalaṅkaṃ jhāyati, parisamantatau jhāyati, bhindantau jhāyati, na sajjhāyati, āyūhantau jhāyati, kiñ‍ci ca niparicitau jhāyati| atividhāvantau jhāyati, atimañ‍ñantau jhāyati, kāyasaṅkhārai appaṭisambhārai jhāyati, pariyuṭṭhānassa nissaraṇaṃ ajānantau jhāyati, nīvaraṇābhibhūtau jhāyati, assāpattimanasikarauntau jhānassa assādau kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhānaṃ pahānaṃ jhānassa assādau kāmarāgahaitūnaṃ dhammānaṃ udayanti, nirujjhaṅgāni aitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ jhānāni uparimā sukhupaikkhā kāmakammakilaisānaṃ pahānaṃ assādau, aivaṃ khau puna jhānassa assādau mahāsaṃvāsamappīḷitai laukasaṃnivāsai asambaudhaukāsā vigamaissamidaṃ jhānappahānā| ayaṃ paliraudhamappaliraudhalaukasan‍nivāsai aisanidhamidaṃ jhānaṃ anamataggasaṃsārasamāpan‍nānaṃ sattānaṃ saṃsārappahānanā ānisaṃsau, yamidaṃ jhānassa  assādau kāyassa ajhāniyajhāyinau bhavati| ajhāniyajhāniyajhāyīhi aparāmasantau ajhāniyajhāyitaṃ jhāyati, yāni kalaṅkajjhāyinau padāni, tāni anudhitāni paṭipakkhai|

78. tattha katamaṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ? samāpattikausal‍laṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ, jhānavisaisakausal‍laṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ, jhānantarikakausal‍laṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ, samāpattivuṭṭhānakausal‍laṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ, jhānai sabhāvakausal‍laṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ, jhānai ādīnavakausal‍laṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ, jhānai nissaraṇakausal‍laṃ jhānakausal‍laṃ, jhānaphalaina  upādāya kausal‍laṃ, jhānaphalaina paṭisaṅkhānaphalai aparihānadhammatā nibbattijhānai ca kīḷitāpi visaisabhāgiyaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilabbhati| idaṃ panassāti bhavahāritā ca ārammaṇānimittaggāhau anabhinīhārabalaṃ, cittaikaggatā nimittāsu gatisahitā samathabalaina asaṃsīdanañ‍ca jhānai maggaphalaṃ samathaṃ pavattai samādhinau upaikkhāpalipubbāparanimittāsayau paggāhinau maggāhinau (pī॰) satibalaṃ taṃ pavattitānañ‍ca vipassanānaṃ samañ‍ñābalai|

tattha  katamā jhānapāramitā? supāramitā maittā kāmaisu sattā kāmasaṅgasattāti udā॰ 63 udānai passitabbaṃ yamhi suttai daisanāya vauhāraina dvai sac‍cāni niddiṭṭhāni, dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca, vicayaina hāraina yai saṃyaujanīyaisu dhammaisu vaj‍jaṃ na passanti, tai aughaṃ tarissantīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| na tarissantīti atthi aisā yutti ca vicayau ca idaṃ nu kissa padaṭṭhānaṃ, kāmaisu sattāti pañ‍ca kāmaguṇā, taṃ kāmataṇhāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṃyaujanai vaj‍jamapassamānāti avij‍jāya padaṭṭhānaṃ, na hi jātu saṃyaujanasaṅgasattā aughaṃ taraiyyuṃ vipulaṃ mahantanti upādānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāmaisu sattāti kāmā dvidhā – vatthukāmā ca kilaisakāmā ca, tattha  kilaisakāmā kāmataṇhā kāmataṇhāya yuttā bhavanti rūpataṇhā bhavataṇhā lakkhaṇaina hāraina, saṃyaujanai vaj‍jamapassamānāti saṃyaujanassa| yau tattha chandarāgau tassa kiṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ? sukhā vaidanā dvai ca indriyāni – sukhindriyañ‍ca saumanassindriyañ‍ca| iti sukhāya vaidanāya gahitāya tayaupi vaidanā gahitā haunti| vaidanākkhandhai gahitai sabbai pañ‍cakkhandhā gahitā haunti| rūpasaddagandharasaphauṭṭhabbā gahitā, vatthukāmaisu  gahitaisu sabbāni cha bāhirāni āyatanāni gahitāni haunti| ajjhattikabāhiraisu āyatanaisu yau satau, ayaṃ vuc‍catai lakkhaṇau hārau, tattha yau auḷārikamhi kilaisai ajjhāvasitau sabbakilaisaisu yau na tatau sukhumataraisu na vītarāgau bhavati| tattha bāhirasaṃyaujanaṃ mamanti ajjhattasaṃyaujanaṃ ahanti| tattha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai aughaṃ taritukāmā tai saṃyaujanīyaisu dhammaisu ādīnavānupassinau viharissantīti ayamaittha bhagavatau adhippāyau| kāmaisu sattāti yaisu ca sattā yaina ca sattā yaisañ‍ca sattā ayaṃ catubbidhau ākārau sabbaisaṃ hārabhāgiyau|

79. tattha katamāni tīṇi vipal‍lāsāni padaṭṭhānāni ca? cittavipal‍lāsassa diṭṭhivipal‍lāsassa sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsassa tayau vipal‍lāsā tīṇi akusalamūlāni padaṭṭhānaṃ| tīṇi akusalamūlāni  hīnappaṇītakāriyakammassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| catun‍nañ‍ca upādānānaṃ dausau akusalamūlaṃ dissati| hīnappaṇītakāriyakammassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| yathā mātuyā vā pitunau vā añ‍ñatarassa vā puna uḷārassa bhikkhunau abhayaṃ daiti| tattha añ‍ñau micchā paṭipaj‍jaiyya kāyaina vā vācāya vā| tattha sau byāpādamupādāya taisaṃ uḷārānaṃ rakkhāvaraṇaguttiyā anupālayantau yau uḷārānaṃ abhayaṃ daiti| taisaṃ abhayai din‍nai yau tattha micchā paṭipaj‍jaiyya| tattha sau byāpādaṃ upādāyantau dausajaṃ kammaṃ karauti| yau tattha asādhu indriyā nīvaraṇaṃ yaṃ taisaṃ abhayaṃ dakkhiṇatau sañ‍ñaṃ idaṃ paṇītaṃ kāraṇaṃ mayā puna tattha micchāpaṭipatti ayaṃ byāpādau hīnagamivakammaṃ laubhau mauhau ca imāni nīvaraṇāni vacanāni tāni cattāri upādānāni  taihi catūhi upādānaihi yau sau upādānau itthī vā purisau vā taisaṃ pañ‍cakkhandhānaṃ taiyaiva upādānau samudayau idaṃ dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca sauyaiva daisanāhārau|

tattha kāmaisu yai na paj‍janti, tai ādīnavānupassanāya paj‍janti| itissā kāmadhātuyā nikkhamitukāmatā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati naikkhammacchandau| yau tattha anabhisaṅkhārānaṃ kiñ‍ci visaudhaiti tassa dhāvarā vā, ayaṃ abyāpādacchandau| kiñ‍ci vihiṃsati, ayaṃ vihiṃsāchandau| iti naikkhammābhinīhatā tayau chandā – naikkhammacchandau abyāpādacchandau avihiṃsāchandau| tattha  naikkhammacchandau alaubhau; abyāpādacchandau adausau; avihiṃsāchandau amauhau| imāni tīṇi kusalamūlāni aṭṭhasu sampattaisu parahitāni, taisaṃyaiva catun‍naṃ upādānānaṃ niraudhāya saṃvattanti| sacai vā puna kammaṃ karaiyya kaṇhaṃ vā suk‍kaṃ vā tassa vipākahānāya saṃvattanti| idaṃ kammaṃ akaṇhaṃ asuk‍kaṃ kammakkhayāya saṃvattati| tattha yau tiṇṇaṃ akusalamūlānaṃ niraudhau, ayaṃ niraudhau| sauyaiva maggau tattha paṭipadāni imāni dvai sac‍cāni imāni cattāri sac‍cāni āvaṭṭau hārau|

kāmaisu sattāti yai saikkhā, tai aikainaivākāraina sattā| yai puthuj‍janā, tai dvīhākāraihi sattā, tassāyaṃ pañhau vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau vattabbau| kiñ‍cāpi sautāpan‍nau paṭisaivanāya, nau ca khau abhinivaisai sattau yau hi apacayāya padahati, na upacayāya| saikkhau hi kilaisavasaina kāmai paṭisaivati| puthuj‍janau pana kilaisasamuṭṭhānāya kāmai paṭisaivati| tattha kāmaisu sattānaṃ catuaughaṃ tarissatīti vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyau, ayaṃ vibhatti|

80. parivattanauti kāmai yai naiva saj‍janti na ca saṃyaujanaihi saṃyuttā, tai aughaṃ tarissanti vipulaṃ mahantanti| ayaṃ suttassa paṭipakkhau|

vaivacananti yau kāmaisu sattau yau ca tattha kāmānaṃ guṇau, tattha visau sattau| yaipi kāmānaṃ āhārā dhammā, tattha visau sattau| tatthimaṃ kāmānaṃ vaivacanaṃ pākau rajau sal‍laṃ gaṇḍau īti upaddavauti| yāni vā pana añ‍ñāni  vaivacanāni tattha visau sattauti vaivacanaṃ| sattau bandhau mucchitau gadhitau ajjhausitau kāmai ajjhāpan‍nā parimuttau tabbahulavihārīti| yāni vā pana añ‍ñāni vaivacanāni, ayaṃ vaivacanau nāma| kāmappacārapañ‍ñattiyā kilaisagaucarapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā cittanti vaivacanaṃ| sattau tabbahulavihārīti yāni vā pana añ‍ñāni| imai kāmappacārapañ‍ñattiyā kilaisagaucara pañ‍ñattiyā  pañ‍ñattā, bījapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā, saṅkhārā saṃyaujanapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā, upādānaṃ haitupañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ, puggalau puthupañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattau|

autaraṇauti imāya paṭic‍casamuppādau dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca| yai kilaisā yai saṅkhārā saṃyaujanāni ca pañ‍casu khandhaisu saṅkhārakkhandhau dhammāyatanaisu  akusalā dhammāyatanāni indriyaisu sukhindriyañ‍ca, saumanassindriyañ‍ca, ayaṃ indriyautaraṇau|

saudhanauti aittakau| aisaiva ārambhau niddisitabbau suttatthau|

adhiṭṭhānauti imai dhammā atthi aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā atthi vaimattatāya| yai sañ‍ñā bāhirau kāmai, tai vaimattatāya pañ‍ñattā| pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu sattāti pariyuṭṭhānavipal‍lāsā vaimattatāya pañ‍ñattā aughaṃ taraiyyuṃ| vipulaṃ mahantanti avij‍jā aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā|

parikkhārauti tassa kau haitu kau pac‍cayau? ārammaṇapac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| ayaunisau ca manasikārau san‍nissayassa pac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| avij‍jā samanantarapac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| rāgānusayau haitupac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| ayaṃ haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau|

samāraupanau  pac‍cayauti yai kāmaisu sattā sugatā surūpāti ayaṃ kāmadhātuyā chandau rāgau tai apuñ‍ñamayā saṅkhārā| tai kiṃ pac‍cayā? avij‍jā pac‍cayā| tai kissa pac‍cayā? viñ‍ñāṇassa pac‍cayā| iti avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā| saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yāva jarāmaraṇaṃ aivamaitassa kaivalassa mahatau dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti aikaṃ suttaṃ gataṃ| pañ‍canīvaraṇikaṃ suttaṃ kātabbaṃ|

81. tattha katamau daisanāhārau nāma? yā ca abhijjhā yau ca byāpādau yañ‍ca uddhac‍caṃ, ayaṃ taṇhā| yañ‍ca thinamiddhaṃ, yañ‍ca kuk‍kuc‍caṃ yā ca vicikicchā, ayaṃ diṭṭhi| yā pana kāyassa akammaniyatā kiñ‍cāpi taṃ middhaṃ nau tu sabhāvakilaisatāya kilaisau, iti yā ca cittasal‍līyanā yā ca kāyākammaniyatā, ayaṃ pakkhaupakilaisau na tu sabhāvakilaisau| tattha attasañ‍ñānupacittaṃ kilamathau kuk‍kuc‍cānupacittaṃ thinaṃ yā cittassa līyanā, iti imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā cattāri nīvaraṇāni sabhāvakilaisā thinamiddhaṃ nīvaraṇapakkhaupakilaisau| yathā cattārau āsavā sabhāvaāsavatāya āsavā nau tu cittasāsavatāya  āsavā| sabhāvatāya āsavā| pakkhai āsavatāya āsavā| atha panāha suttantaṃ yaina tai sampayuttā vā vippayuttā vā āsavā, taiyaiva aitai vattabbā sāsavā vā anāsavā vā|

tattha  katamau vicayau| abhijjhā kāmataṇhā rūpataṇhā bhavataṇhā| yaṃ vā pana kiñ‍ci ajjhausānagataṃ sāsavā abhijjhitassa maittānupassiya yau anatthaṃ carati| tattha yau byāpādaṃ uppādaiti, acari carissatīti| aivaṃ nava āghātavatthūni kattabbāni, tassaivaṃ byāpādānupassissa kilaisau yau paridāhau kāyakilamathau akammaniyatā middhaṃ| cittānupassissa paṭighātaina khiyanā, idaṃ thinamiddhaṃ| tattha adhikaraṇaavūpasamau, idaṃ uddhac‍caṃ| yaṃ kiṃ kasathamīti karathamīti (pī॰ ka॰) idaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ| yaṃ yathā idaṃ santīraṇaṃ, ayaṃ vicikicchā| tattha avij‍jā ca taṇhā ca atthi, idaṃ pariyuṭṭhānaṃ| āvaraṇaṃ nīvaraṇaṃ chadanaṃ upak‍kilaisau ca atthi, idaṃ kāmacchandau kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| byāpādau byāpādapariyuṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| thinamiddhaṃ thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍caṃ avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| vicikicchā vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhānaṃ anusayasaṃyaujanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| byāpādapariyuṭṭhānaṃ paṭighasaṃyaujanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| thinamiddhapariyuṭṭhānaṃ mānasaṃyaujanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| avij‍jāpariyuṭṭhānañ‍ca vicikicchāpariyuṭṭhānañ‍ca diṭṭhisaṃyaujanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau hārau? kāmarāgapariyuṭṭhānai vuttai sabbāni pariyuṭṭhānāni vuttāni hauntīti| saṃyaujanaisu vuttaisu sabbasaṃyaujanāni vuttāni haunti| ayaṃ lakkhaṇau hārau|

82. tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? yai imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā jhānapaṭipakkhau sau dukkhasamudayau| yaṃ phalaṃ, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| tattha kāmacchandassa  naikkhammavitak‍kau paṭipakkhau; byāpādassa abyāpādavitak‍kau paṭipakkhau; tiṇṇaṃ nīvaraṇānaṃ avihiṃsāvitak‍kau paṭipakkhau| iti imai tayau vitak‍kā| naikkhammavitak‍kau samādhikkhandhaṃ bhajati| abyāpādavitak‍kau sīlakkhandhaṃ bhajati| avihiṃsāvitak‍kau pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ bhajati| imai tayau khandhā| ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau nīvaraṇappahānāya saṃvattati| yaṃ nīvaraṇappahānaṃ, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| ayaṃ catubyūhau hārau|

tattha katamau āvaṭṭau hārau? pañ‍ca nīvaraṇāni dasa bhavanti| yadapi ajjhattaṃ sāraj‍jati, tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ| yadapi bahiddhā sāraj‍jati, tadapi nīvaraṇaṃ, aivaṃ  yāva vicikicchā imai dasa nīvaraṇā| ajjhattabahiddhā kilaisā imāni dvai saṃyaujanāni ajjhattasaṃyaujanañ‍ca bahiddhāsaṃyaujanañ‍ca| tattha ahanti ajjhattaṃ, mamanti bahiddhā| sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi ajjhattaṃ, aikasaṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni bahiddhā| yau ajjhattaṃ chandarāgau rūpaisu avītarāgau bhavati avītacchandau| aivaṃ yāva viñ‍ñāṇai, ayaṃ ajjhattā taṇhā| yaṃ chasu bāhiraisu āyatanaisu tīsu ca bhavaisu ajjhausānaṃ, ayaṃ bahiddhā taṇhā| imāni dvai sac‍cāni saṃyaujanāni saṃyaujanīyā ca dhammā| tattha saṃyaujanaisu dhammaisu yā nibbidānupassanā ca, ayaṃ maggau| yaṃ saṃyaujanappahānaṃ, ayaṃ niraudhau| ayaṃ āvaṭṭau hārau|

tattha katamau vibhattihārau? saṃyaujananti na aitaṃ aikaṃsaina| mānasaṃyaujanaṃ diṭṭhibhāgiyanti na taṃ aikaṃsaina adiṭṭhamānaṃ nissāyamānaṃ  na pajahati| yau pañ‍ca uddhambhāgiyau mānau kiñ‍cāpi sau diṭṭhipakkhai siyā| na tu aurambhāgiyaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ tassa pahānāya saṃvattatīti| yau ca ahaṃkārau na paviddhauyaṃ panassa aivaṃ hauti| kadāsu nāmāhaṃ taṃ santaṃ āyatanaṃ sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharissāmi, yaṃ ariyā santaṃ āyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharissantīti, ayaṃ abhijjhā na ca taṃ nīvaraṇaṃ| atthi pana arahatau kāyakilaisamiddhañ‍ca auk‍kamati na ca taṃ nīvaraṇaṃ tassa thinamiddhaṃ nīvaraṇanti| na aikaṃsaina| ayaṃ vibhattihārau|

parivattanauti pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā pañ‍caṅgikaina jhānaina pahānaṃ gacchanti| ayaṃ taisaṃ paṭipakkhau nīvaraṇau asukassa pahīnāti na añ‍ñānuminitabbaṃ, paramatthamajjhattaṃ, ayaṃ parivattanā|

tattha katamau vaivacanau? kāmacchandau chandarāgau paimaṃ nikantīti vaivacanaṃ| nīvaraṇaṃ chadanaṃ upak‍kilaisau pariyuṭṭhānanti vaivacanaṃ|

pañ‍ñattīti avij‍jāpac‍cayā kic‍capañ‍ñattiyā pac‍cāpañ‍ñattiyā (ka॰) pañ‍ñatti, byāpādau vikkhaipapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñatti, thinamiddhaṃ asamugghātapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñatti| aivaṃ sabbaipi aitai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā imamhi suttai vikkhaipapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñatti|

tattha katamau autaraṇau? imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā avij‍jā ca taṇhā ca tattha avij‍jāmūlā  nīvaraṇā| yā taṇhā imai saṅkhārā, tai avij‍jāpac‍cayā  imai  dvai dhammā pañ‍casu khandhaisu saṅkhārakkhandhapariyāpan‍nā, āyatanaisu dhammāyatanaṃ, dhātūsu dhammadhātu, indriyaisu imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ sukhindriyassa ca saumanassindriyassa ca itthindriyassa ca purisindriyassa ca|

tattha katamau saudhanau hārau? idaṃ suttaṃ yathā ārabbha nikkhittaṃ sau atthau bhāsitau imaihi pañ‍cahi padaihi|

tattha kāmacchandau ca byāpādau ca vicikicchā ca na aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā, kāmāti na aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā, atha khalu vaimattatāya pañ‍ñattā| ayaṃ adhiṭṭhānau hārau|

tattha katamau parikkhārau? kāmacchandassa ayaunisau manasikārau subhārammaṇapac‍cayau; subhanimittañ‍ca haitu| byāpādassa ayaunisau manasikārau āghātavatthūni ca pac‍cayau; paṭighānusayau haitu| thinamiddhassa paṭisaṃhārau pac‍cayau; pavattiyā kilamathā calanā tañ‍ca haitu| uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cassa rajanīyaṃ ārammaṇiyaṃ assādiyākindriyaṃ tāva aparipuṇṇañ‍ca ñāṇaṃ pac‍cayau; kāmasañ‍ñā ca diṭṭhianusayau ca haitu| vicikicchāya nava mānavidhā ārammaṇaṃ mānānusayau, sauva pac‍cayau; vicikicchānusayau haitu| aitai pañ‍ca dhammā sahaitu sappac‍cayā uppaj‍janti|

tattha katamau samāraupanau hārau? imai pañ‍ca nīvaraṇā cattāraupi aitai āsavā gaṇḍāpi taṇhāpi (pī॰) aitai sal‍lāpi aitai upādānāni aitai| taisu aiva bāhiraisu dhammaisu saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ suttanti pañ‍ñattiṃ gacchati| ayaṃ samāraupanau hārau|

niddiṭṭhaṃ  saṃkilaisikabhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ|

83. manaupubbaṅgamā dhammāti gāthā|

tattha katamau daisanā hārau? imamhi suttai kau atthau khandhavavatthānaina viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhaṃ daisaiti, dhātuvavatthānaina manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuṃ, āyatanavavatthānaina manāyatanaṃ, indriyavavatthānaina manindriyaṃ| tassa kiṃ pubbaṅgamā dhammā? saṃkhittaina cha dhammā pubbaṅgamā dhammā kusalamūlāni ca  akusalamūlāni ca animittaṃ imamhi suttai kusalamūlaṃ daisitaṃ| tattha katamā manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā? manau taisaṃ pubbaṅgamaṃ, yathāpi balassa rājā pubbaṅgamau, aivamaiva  dhammānaṃ manaupubbaṅgamā| tattha tividhānaṃ pubbaṅgamānaṃ naikkhammacchandaina abyāpādacchandaina avihiṃsāchandaina| alaubhassa naikkhammacchandaina pubbaṅgamā| adausassa abyāpādacchandaina pubbaṅgamā| amauhassa avihiṃsāchandaina pubbaṅgamā| tattha manausaiṭṭhāti manasā imai dhammā ussaṭā manaina vā nimmitā| manauva imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ saiṭṭhauti manauva imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ saiṭṭhajaiṭṭhauti manauva imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ ādhipac‍caṃ karautīti manausaiṭṭhā| manaujavāti  yattha manau gacchati| tattha imai dhammā gacchantīti manaujavā| yathā vātau sīghaṃ gacchati añ‍ñau vā kauci sīghaṃ gāmakau vuc‍catai vātajavauti pakkhigāmikauti, aivamaiva imai dhammā manaina sampajāyamānā gacchanti, tattha imai dhammā gacchantīti manaujavāti| tai tividhā chandasamudānitā anāvilatā ca saṅkappau| sattavidhā ca kāyikaṃ sucaritaṃ vācasikaṃ sucaritaṃ, tai dasa kusalakammapathā| tattha manasā cai pasan‍naināti manaukammaṃ| bhāsati vāti vacīkammaṃ| karauti vāti kāyakammaṃ| imaihi imasmiṃ suttai dasa kusalakammapathā paramāpi santā sīlavatā paramā| sau bhavati vivattiyaṃ na laukaniyyānāya vāsanābhāgiyaṃ suttaṃ bhavati| ayaṃ daisanā|

tattha katamau vicayau hārau? manaupubbaṅgamā dhammāti kusalamūlāni ca aṭṭhaṅgasammattāni| idaṃ suttaṃ|

yuttīti dasan‍naṃ kusalakammapathānaṃ yau vipākau, sau sukhavaidanīyau abyāpādassaṅgamānau| chāyāva anapāyinīti anugacchati atthi aisā yutti|

padaṭṭhānanti aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ manaupavicārānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| manaupubbaṅgamā dhammāti sabbakusalapakkhassa imai dhammā padaṭṭhānaṃ| manasā cai pasan‍naināti yau caitasau pasādau, idaṃ saddhindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| bhāsati vāti sammāvācāya| karauti vāti sammākammantassa ca sammāvāyāmassa ca padaṭṭhānaṃ|

lakkhaṇauti  iti pubbaṅgamā dhammāti vaidanāpubbaṅgamāpi aitai, sañ‍ñāpubbaṅgamāpi aitai, saṅkhārapubbaṅgamāpi aitai| yai kaici dhammā sahajātā sabbai pubbaṅgamā aitaisaṃ dhammānaṃ| tatau naṃ sukhamanvaitīti saumanassamapi naṃ anvaiti yaṃ susukhacchāyā tadāpi naṃ sukhaṃ tadapi anvaiti|

84. tattha  katamau catubyūhau hārau? manaupubbaṅgamāti na idaṃ aikādivacanaṃ| kiṃ kāraṇā? sabbai yaiva imai chaviñ‍ñāṇakāyā, imamhi bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai sukhaina atthikā, tai manaṃ pasādaintīti ayaṃ imamhi suttai bhagavatau adhippāyau| atthau pubbaiyaiva niddiṭṭhau|

yāni hi kusalamūlāni, tāni aṭṭhānisaṃsamattā haitu, ayaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| dasa ṭhānāni daisanāhaitūni daisanāpac‍cayā niddaisanā ca| tattha yaṃ mañ‍ñai dukkhaina saha nāmarūpaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasac‍canti aṅgaina kusalamūlaṃ pahīyati, ayaṃ appahīnabhūmiyaṃ samudayau| yaṃ taisaṃ pahānā, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni| ayaṃ āvaṭṭau hārau|

vibhattīti –

manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā, manausaiṭṭhā manaumayā|

manasā cai pasan‍naina, bhāsati vā karauti vā|

tatau naṃ sukhamanvaiti, chāyāva anapāyinīti||

taṃ  na aikaṃsaina samaṇassa vā brāhmaṇassa vā pana hauti| tassa vā micchādiṭṭhikassa sakasatthai cittaṃ pasādaiti, taina ca pasan‍naina cittaina bhāsati byākarauti na taṃ sukhamanvaiti na chāyāva anugāminī, dukkhamaiva taṃ anvaiti| yathā vahantaṃ cak‍kaṃ padamanvaiti, idaṃ taṃ vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyaṃ| manasā cai pasan‍naina kāyakammaṃ vacīkammaṃ sukhavaidanīyanti samaggatai sukhavaidanīyaṃ micchaggatai dukkhavaidanīyaṃ, ayaṃ vibhatti|

tattha katamau parivattanau hārau? manaupubbaṅgamā dhammāti yaṃ manasā paduṭṭhaina bhāsati vā karauti vā dukkhamassānugāminī, aitāniyaiva dvai suttāni bhāsitāni aisa aiva ca paṭipakkhau| vaivacananti yadidaṃ manaucittaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ manindriyaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

pañ‍ñattīti manaupubbaṅgamā dhammāti ayaṃ manau kiñ‍ci pañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| dhammāti kusalakammapathapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| manausaiṭṭhāti visiṭṭhapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| manaujavāti sahapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| cittanti naikkhammapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| manasā cai pasan‍naināti saddhindriyapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| manasā cai pasan‍naināti anāvilasaṅkappadutiyajjhānapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| manasā cai pasan‍naināti  assaddhānaṃ paṭipakkhapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| bhāsati vāti sammāvācāpañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| karauti vāti sammākammantapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| tatau  naṃ sukhamanvaitīti jhānasamādhānaṃ| indriyaisu manindriyaṃ| paṭic‍casamuppādai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| manaupubbaṅgamā dhammāti maittā ca mudutā ca jhānaisu dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca| khandhaisu saṅkhārakkhandhapariyāpan‍nau| dhātūsu dhammadhātu, āyatanaisu dhammāyatanaṃ| yaṃ kusalaṃ indriyaisu sukhindriyañ‍ca saumanassindriyañ‍ca padaṭṭhānaṃ| imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍nānaṃ phassapac‍cayā sukhavaidanīyau phassau sukhavaidanā manaupavicāraisu saumanassavicārau chattiṃsaisu paṭhamapadaisu cha saumanassanaikkhammassitā| iti ayaṃ autaraṇau hārau|

tattha katamau saudhanau hārau? yaṃ atthaṃ ārabbha idaṃ suttaṃ bhāsitaṃ| sau atthau niyuttau aitamatthaṃ ārabbha suttaṃ| ayaṃ saudhanau hārau|

85. tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau hārau? manaupubbaṅgamā dhammāti vaivacanapañ‍ñatti, na aikattapañ‍ñatti| dhammāti aikatau na vaivacanapañ‍ñatti| manasā cai pasan‍naināti sau pasādau dvidhau ajjhattañ‍ca abyāpādāvikkhambhanabahiddhā ca aukappanatau| sau ajjhattapasādau dvidhau| samugghātapasādau ca vikkhambhanapasādau ca byāpādapariyuṭṭhānaṃ| vighātau na mūlapasādau jātamūlampi vā| pasādau sabyāpādaṃ vighātaina| tatau naṃ sukhamanvaitīti sukhaṃ kāyikañ‍ca caitasikañ‍ca appiyavippayaugaupi piyasampayaugaupi naikkhammasukhampi puthuj‍janasukhampi pītisambaujjhaṅgampi caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ| yampi passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, tampi kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ baujjhaṅgā ca caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ| yampi passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaisi, tampi tañ‍ca sukhapadaṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñattiyā yathāvuttaṃ taṃ aparāmaṭṭhaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ| anvaitīti appanā sandissati na cāyaṃ vā pattabhūtau anvaiti| tadidaṃ suttaṃ dvīhi ākāraihi adhiṭṭhātabbaṃ| haitunā ca yau pasan‍namānasau vipākaina ca yau dukkhavaidanīyau|

parikkhārauti bhagavā pañ‍casataina bhikkhusaṅghaina nagaraṃ pavisati rājagahaṃ| tattha manussau puggalau bhagavantaṃ parivisati, tassa pasādau uppan‍nau kusalamūlapubbayaugāvacaraupi| sau añ‍ñaisañ‍ca akkhāti, idaṃ vācaṃ bhāsati  lābhā taisaṃ, yaisaṃ nivaisanaṃ bhagavā pavisati, amhākampi yadi bhavaiyya mayampi bhagavatau saṃpasādaṃ lacchamhāti| yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā ‘‘namau bhagavatau namau bhagavatau’’ti abyāpādamānau aikamantai aṭṭhāsi| tadanantarai bhagavā imaṃ suttaṃ abhāsittha ‘‘manaupubbaṅgamā dhammā’’ti| sabbaṃ suttaṃ tathā yaṃ paraisaṃ bhāsati idaṃ  vācākammaṃ| yaṃ añ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaiti, idaṃ kāyakammaṃ| yau manaupasādau, idaṃ manaukammaṃ| tattha yaṃ paraisaṃ pakāsaiti bhāsati vaṇṇaṃ| yaisaṃ bhagavā nivaisanaṃ gacchatīti| sabbaṃ tassa alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ| yaṃ bhagavati maittacittau, tassa adausau kusalamūlaṃ| yaṃ añ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaiti mānañ‍ca niggaṇhāti, tatthassa amauhau kusalamūlaṃ pātubhavati| yaṃ uḷārapañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati, idamassa diṭṭhivipal‍lāsappahānaṃ| yaṃ tathāyaiva saṃvarau hauti, idamassa sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsappahānaṃ| yaṃ manassa pasādanaṃ, idamassa cittavipal‍lāsappahānanti akusalavipal‍lāsānaṃ vikkhambhanaṃ pahānaṃ pac‍cayau| tīṇi kusalamūlāni yau anāvilacittasaṅkappau, sau tassa manasikārauti vuc‍cati| yaṃ kilaisaihi vikkhambhanaṃ iti vipal‍lāsā ca ārammaṇā sappac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau kusalamūlāni ca sandissayatāya pac‍cayau, sau ca manasikārau haitunā iminā pac‍cayaina cittaṃ uppan‍naṃ| tattha yaṃ sasatthārammaṇaṃ cittaṃ pavattaṃ ayaṃ buddhānussati| yampi bhagavatau guṇai manasi karauti, ayamassa dhammānussati| tattha satisampajañ‍ñaṃ haitu, ayañ‍ca pac‍cayau| vācā pañ‍ñā haitu vitak‍kavicārā pac‍cayau| kāyasaṅkhārā kammassa abhisaṅkhārau nāma haitu vā appac‍cayau sukhavaidanīyassa kammassa upacayau haitukā kammassa pac‍cayau|

86. tattha katamau samāraupanau hārau? manasāyaiva pasan‍naina satauyaivaittha pasan‍nau api ca cittavaudānā sattā vimuc‍cantīti taina sattā cittapubbaṅgamā cittaina pasan‍naina caitanāpi tattha cittabhūtā bhavantīti paṭighā ayaṃ caitanānaṃ pasādaina kāyau cassa pasādau, sau ca ārabhati pasādaina pasan‍nau sañ‍ñānanti cassa aviparītā, sau pañ‍cavidhau vikkhambhanā, kāyapassambhanāyaivā pasādau cittasitau cittaṃ pana pubbaṃyaiva pasan‍naṃ| ayaṃ samāraupanā| aivaṃ pañ‍can‍nampi pasādau| tatau naṃ sukhamanvaitīti katamaṃ bhagavā niddisati? na hi attasac‍caṃ tassa kammassa vipākau anvaiti| tassa upāyau anugacchati yadā sitapac‍cayā uppaj‍jatai  saumanassaṃ avippaṭisāraupi anvaiti| ayaṃ samāraupanau hārau|

mahānāma sak‍kassa suttaṃ passa saṃ॰ ni॰ 5.1017| tasmiṃ cai samayai assatau asampajānau kālaṃ karaiyya kāmai bhavati| assatau abhisamāhārau yau mā bhāyi, mahānāma, yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ sīlaparibhāvitaṃ sutacāgaparibhāvitanti vitthāraina kātabbaṃ| cāgaina ca pañ‍ñāya ca kiṃ dassaiti? yā saddhā, sā caitasau pasādau| yā anāvilasaṅkappitā, sā saddhā| kiṃ kāraṇā? anāvilalakkhaṇā| anāvilalakkhaṇā hi saddhā| aparai  āhu guṇaparisuddhiniṭṭhāgamanalakkhaṇā, yañ‍ca aparai vā vacanapaṭiggahalakkhaṇā saddhā| aparau pariyāyau  attānaṃ yadi aivaṃ aukappaiti ‘‘nāhaṃ kiñ‍ci jānāmīti aisā ahaṃ tattha anuñ‍ñattā anañ‍ñatā’’ti| ayaṃ saddhāti| aparau pariyāyau aikasaṭṭhiyā diṭṭhigatānaṃ ādīnavānupassanā anic‍caṃ dukkhamanattāti| taina ca padiṭṭhaṃ bhavati yathā gambhīrai udapānai udakaṃ cakkhunā passati na ca kāyaina abhisambhunāti| aivamassa ariyā nijjhānakkhantiyā diṭṭhi bhavati, na ca sacchikatā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati saddhā| sā ca laukikā| aparau pariyāyau khamati puthuj‍janabhūtassa vīsati cāti kau sak‍kāyādhīnā na nivaisau| na aitaṃ aikanti nayasañ‍ñā yathābhūtaṃ diṭṭhiyā tu khalu mudūhi pañ‍cahi indriyaihi dassanamaggaina pahīnā bhavanti| diṭṭhaikaṭṭhā ca kilaisā, ayaṃ saddhā|

sautāpattaṅgamadukkhāyaṃ bhūmiyaṃ paripuṇṇā vuc‍cati| tasmiṃyaiva bhūmiyaṃ saikkhasīlaṃ ariyā dhāranti vuc‍cati| tasmiṃyaiva bhūmiyaṃ mudupañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyanti vuc‍cati| tasmiṃyaiva bhūmiyaṃ khandhaihi anatthikatā, ayaṃ cāgau| tasmā saddhā cāgādhiṭṭhānaina niddisitabbā| yatikaina taina (ka॰) bhiyyau manaina sā hissa viparītā diṭṭhikā assaddhā, sā nayanaupadhīsu pamattā samādin‍nā| tattha saddhindriyaṃ yau kāmaṃ parivissanti iti santapāpapaṭinissaggā na cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaina pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānaṃ, sīlaina upasamādhiṭṭhānaṃ| imai cattārau  dhammā sīlaṃ paribhāvayanti saddhā sīlaṃ cāgau ca pañ‍ñāti| tattha saddhāya aughaṃ tarati| yaṃ sīlaṃ, ayaṃ appamādau| yau cāgau, idaṃ pañ‍ñāya kammaṃ| yā pañ‍ñā, idaṃ pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, tattha yaṃ saddhindriyaṃ| taṃ tīsu avaic‍cappasādaisu| yaṃ sīlaṃ, taṃ saddhindriyaisu| yau cāgau, sau catūsu  jhānaisu| yā pañ‍ñā, sā sac‍caisu, sati sabbatthagāminī| tassa saikkhassa bhaddikā bhati, bhaddikau abhisamparāyau| tassa sammuṭṭhassatikassa sīlaṃ karauntassa na kāyasammuṭṭhassatitāya tāni vā indriyāni taṃ vā kusalamūlaṃ kammavipākaṃ bhavati| tassa tikassa atthaniddaisau| tattha saddhā sīlaṃ cāgau pañ‍ñā cattārau dhammā| yā saddhā yā ca pañ‍ñā, idaṃ manausucaritaṃ| yaṃ sīlaṃ, idaṃ kāyikaṃ vācasikaṃ sucaritaṃ| yau cāgau, idaṃ caitasikaṃ alaubhau sucaritaṃ| iti cittai gahitai pañ‍cakkhandhā gahitā bhavanti| imaihi dhammaihi sucaritaṃ idaṃ dukkhañ‍ca ariyasac‍caṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ maggassa|

87. tattha katamau vicayau hārau? yā ca saddhā yañ‍ca sīlaṃ| taṃ kissa karauti? yā saddhā tāya bhagavantaṃ anussarati mattainapi hatthinā samāgatā, assa bhau kuk‍kurā sabbaṃ sīlaina nappaṭipaj‍jati kāyaina vā vācāya vā ṭhānaṃ visāradau bhavatīti avippaṭisārī pañ‍ñā yassa pañ‍ñattaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti| tassa akhaṇḍassa  sīlaṃ yaṃ na pacchi tassaṃ mauhassa akusalacittaṃ  uppaj‍jati micchādiṭṭhisahagataṃ vā, ayaṃ vicayau hārau| dhammavādinau bhaddikārāti bhavissati atthi aisā yutti|

tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau hārau? yamidaṃ cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ paribhāvitaṃ saddhāya sīlaina cāgaina pañ‍ñāya samādhinā paṭhamajjhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| yā saddhā assa anāvilasaṅkappau, taṃ dutiyajjhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| tīṇi ca avaic‍cappasādā yaṃ sīlaṃ, taṃ ariyakantaṃ, taṃ sīlakkhandhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| yā pañ‍ñā, sā pañ‍ñākkhandhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| imai ca dhammā idañ‍ca cittaṃ aikaudibhūtasamādhissa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saddhā saddhindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| cāgau samādhindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saddhā ca pañ‍ñā ca vipassanā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sīlañ‍ca cāgau ca samathassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saddhā ca pañ‍ñā ca avij‍jā virāgāya pañ‍ñāvimuttiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sīlañ‍ca cāgau ca rāgavirāgāya caitauvimuttiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau hārau? viñ‍ñāṇai vuttai saddhāsatibhāvitai sabbai pañ‍cakkhandhā vuttā bhavanti| saddhāya bhaṇitāya sabbāni satta dhanāni bhaṇitāni haunti saddhādhanaṃ…pai॰… sīlakkhandhai vuttai samādhikkhandhau ca pañ‍ñākkhandhau ca vuttā bhavanti| yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ paribhāvitaṃ pacchimakai  kālai na tadanuparivatti bhavissatīti naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati| tattha  sañ‍ñāpi tadanuparivattinī bhavati| yaipi taj‍jātikā dhammā, taipi tadanuparivattinau bhavanti| rūpasañ‍ñā rūpasañ‍caitanānupassanamanasikārau aivaṃ chan‍naṃ āyatanānaṃ viñ‍ñāṇakāyai, ayaṃ lakkhaṇau hārau|

tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? idha suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai bhaddikaṃ bhatiṃ ākaṅkhaiyya bhaddikañ‍ca abhisamparāyaṃ, tai saddhaṃ sīlaṃ cāgaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca manasi karissanti, ayaṃ adhippāyau| yai cañ‍ñaipi sattā tathāgatassa sammukhaṃ na paṭiyujjhantai, imaṃ dhammaṃ sautā avippaṭisāratau kālaṃ karissantīti, ayaṃ adhippāyau|

88. tattha katamau āvaṭṭau hārau? idampi cattārau dhammā saddhā ca pañ‍ñā ca assaddhiyañ‍ca avij‍jañ‍ca hananti| sīlañ‍ca cāgau ca taṇhā ca dausañ‍ca hananti| tassa dvai mūlāni pahīyanti| dukkhaṃ nivattaiti appahīnabhūmiyañ‍ca dvimūlāni pañ‍cakkhandhā| dvai ariyasac‍cāni samathau ca vipassanā ca| dvin‍naṃ mūlānaṃ pahānaṃ| imāni dvai sac‍cāni niraudhau ca maggau ca| ayaṃ āvaṭṭau hārau|

tattha  katamau vibhatti? yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ saddhāparibhāvitaṃ…pai॰… sacai puthuj‍janassa tassapi bhaddikā bhati bhavissatīti na aikaṃsaina tassa kammaṃ diṭṭhaiyaiva dhammai vipākanti pac‍caissati, aparamhi vā pariyāyai bhavissati| yaṃ vā atītaṃ  vipākāya pac‍cupaṭṭhitaṃ, tappac‍cayāni caitāni, yai yathā mahākammavibhaṅgai ‘‘taināyaṃ vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇiyau niddaisau dhammacārinau yā bhaddikā bhatī’’ti|

tattha katamā parivattanā? assaddhiyaṃ dussīlyaṃ yaṃ macchairaṃ duppañ‍ñaṃ ca duppañ‍ñiyaṃ (ka॰) yañ‍ca paṭipakkhaina pahīnā bhavanti, ayaṃ parivattanā|

tattha katamaṃ vaivacanaṃ? yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ paribhāvitaṃ cittaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… yaṃ saddhābalaṃ saddhindriyaṃ, yaṃ sīlaṃ taṃ sucaritaṃ, saṃyamau niyamau damau khandhatā imāni tassa vaivacanāni| yau cāgau sau paṭinissaggau alaubhau vausaggau cāgauyiṭṭhānaṃ| yā pañ‍ñā sā pañ‍ñattā pañ‍ñappabhā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ pañ‍ñābalaṃ|

tattha  katamā pañ‍ñatti? yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ bījaṃ pañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| paribhāvanā vāsanā pañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñatti| saddhā pasādapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā| sīlaṃ sucaritapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| cāgau puñ‍ñakiriyapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattau| pañ‍ñā vīmaṃsapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā| imai tayau dhammā saddhā sīlaṃ cāgau pañ‍ñavatau pārisuddhiṃ gacchanti|

tattha katamau autaraṇau? yaṃ cittaṃ, taṃ khandhaisu viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau, dhātūsu manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu, āyatanaisu manāyatanaṃ| yai cattārau dhammā, tai khandhaisu saṅkhārakkhandhai pariyāpan‍nā…pai॰… dhātūsu āyatanaisu|

tattha  katamau saudhanau hārau? idaṃ bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ mahānāmaina sak‍kaina pucchitaina sabbaṃ taṃ niyuttaṃ|

tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau? idaṃ cittaṃ vaimattatāya pañ‍ñattaṃ akusalaihi cittaihi aparibhāvitaihi paribhāvitanti yāni puna paribhāvitāni añ‍ñaisampi tattha upādāya pañ‍ñattaṃ sabbaipimai cattārau dhammā aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā| bhaddikā bhatīti kāmabhauginau taiva rūpadhātu arūpadhātu manussāti sabbā bhaddikā bhati tadaiva kathāya pañ‍ñattaṃ, ayaṃ pañ‍ñatti|

tattha  katamau parikkhārau? cittassa indriyāni pac‍cayau ādhipataiyyapac‍cayatāya manasikārau| haitupac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| saddhāya laukikā pañ‍ñā haitupac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| yaunisau ca manasikārau pac‍cayau| sīlassa patirūpadaisavāsau pac‍cayau| attasammāpaṇidhānañ‍ca haitu| cāgassa alaubhau haitu| avippaṭisārau ca haitupac‍cayau| pañ‍ñā paratau ca ghausau ajjhattañ‍ca yaunisau manasikārau haitupac‍cayau ca|

tattha katamau samāraupanau? yaṃ taṃ cittaṃ dīgharattaṃ paribhāvitanti caitasikāpi| aittha sabbai dhammā paribhāvitā bhaddikā tai bhati bhavissati, bhaddikā upapattikau abhisamparāyau| iti yai kaici manussakā upabhaugaparibhaugā sabbai bhaddikā bhatiyaiva, ayaṃ samāraupanau|

89. uddhaṃ  adhau sabbadhi vītarāgauti gāthā passa udā॰ 61 udānai| tattha kiṃ uddhaṃ nāma? yaṃ  itau uddhaṃ bhavissati anāgāmī, idaṃ uddhaṃ| adhau nāma yamatik‍kantamatītaṃ, idamavauca apadānatanti uddhaṃ| tattha atītaina sassatadiṭṭhi pubbantākappikānaṃ aparantadiṭṭhi kaisañ‍ci, ucchaidadiṭṭhiṃ yaṃ ucchaidadiṭṭhiyaṃ (ka॰) vuttakappikānaṃ imā caiva diṭṭhiyau ucchaidadiṭṭhi ca sassatadiṭṭhi ca| tatthāyaṃ sassatadiṭṭhi imāni pan‍narasa padāni sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi sassataṃ bhajanti| rūpavantaṃ mai attā, attani mai rūpaṃ, rūpaṃ mai attāti yaduc‍catai pañ‍ñaṃ paridahanti| yā ucchaidadiṭṭhi sā pañ‍casatāni ucchaidaṃ bhajanti| tai ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti passanti, rūpaṃ mai attāti tathārūpā catubbidhā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi ucchaidaina ca sassataina ca| aivaṃ pañ‍casu khandhaisu vīsativatthukāya diṭṭhiyā pan‍narasa padāni pubbantaṃ bhajanti| sassatadiṭṭhiyā pañ‍ca padāni aparantaṃ bhajanti ucchaidadiṭṭhiyā| tattha ‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti passantā rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, sau ucchaidavādī rūpavantañ‍ca attānaṃ, attani ca rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attāti sau passati cāti iti ucchaidadiṭṭhi ca, attatau paṭissarati sassatadiṭṭhi pubbantatau ca paṭissarati| ‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti na samanupassati| tassa diṭṭhāsavā pahānaṃ gacchanti| yau tīsu addhāsu pubbantai ca aparantai ca taina taina niddiṭṭhānaina uddhaṃ adhau sabbadhi vītarāgau ‘‘ahamasmī’’ti na anupassatīti iminā dvāraina iminā payaugaina iminā upāyaina idaṃ dassanabhūmi ca sautāpattiphalañ‍ca sau ariyau payaugau anabhāvaṃgataina saṃsāraina apunabbhavāti yau kauci ariyau payaugau  punabbhavāya mudūni vā pañ‍cindriyāni majjhāni adhimattāni vā sabbaṃ apunabbhavappahānāya saṃvattanti| ahanti diṭṭhaughau kāmaughau bhavaughau avij‍jaughau ca audhisau| tattha daisanāhāraina cattāri sac‍cāni pañ‍cahi indriyaihi sautāpattiphalaina ca dvai sac‍cāni maggau ca niraudhau ca| sak‍kāyasamudayaina dvai sac‍cāni dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca, ayaṃ daisanā hārau|

tattha  katamau vicayau? ‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti asamanupassantau tīṇi dassanappahātabbāni saṃyaujanāni pajahati| ayaṃ vicayau|

tattha katamā yutti? tividhā puggalā kauci ugghaṭitañ‍ñū kauci vipañ‍citañ‍ñū kauci naiyyau| ugghaṭitañ‍ñū tikkhindriyau ca tatau vipañ‍citañ‍ñū mudindriyau tatau mudindriyaihi naiyyau| tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñū tikkhindriyatāya dassanabhūmimāgamma  sautāpattiphalaṃ pāpuṇāti, aikabījakau bhavati| ayaṃ paṭhamau sautāpan‍nau| vipañ‍citañ‍ñū mudūhi indriyaihi dassanabhūmimāgamma sautāpattiphalaṃ pāpuṇāti, kaulaṃkaulau ca hauti| ayaṃ dutiyau sautāpan‍nau| tattha naiyyau dassanabhūmimāgamma sautāpattiphalaṃ pāpuṇāti, sattakkhattuparamau ca bhavati| ayaṃ tatiyau sautāpan‍nau|

atthi aisā yutti mudumajjhādhimattaihi indriyaihi mudumajjhādhimattaṃ bhūmiṃ sacchikaraiyya sak‍kāyadiṭṭhippahānaina vā diṭṭhigatāni pajahati| ayaṃ  yutti|

tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau? tattha sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi sabbamicchādiṭṭhiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| sak‍kāyau nāmarūpassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| nāmarūpaṃ sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍ca indriyāni rūpīni rūparāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saḷāyatanaṃ ahaṃkārassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| tattha katamau lakkhaṇau? dvīsu diṭṭhīsu pahīnāsu tattha aikā diṭṭhi diṭṭhigatāni pahānaṃ gacchanti| uddhaṃ ca adhau ca vītarāgau sabbarajanīyaisu vītarāgau hauti| taj‍jā parabhūmiyaṃ, idaṃ pac‍cayanti yathābhūtaṃ passati| sau sabbapaṭic‍casamuppādaṃ āmasati| ayaṃ lakkhaṇau hārau|

90. tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? imamhi suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai sattā yai nābhiramissanti, tai diṭṭhippahānāya vāyamissanti| ayamaittha bhagavatau adhippāyau| ayaṃ catubyūhau hārau|

tattha katamau āvaṭṭau hārau? yānimāni mudūni pañ‍cindriyāni tāni aurambhāgiyāni pañ‍cindriyāni| sabbaina sabbaṃ samūhananti abhijjhābyāpādau ca bhāvanākāraina saikkhāya vimuttiyā balaṃ saddhā, uddhambhāgiyāni diṭṭhivasaina balaṃ saddhā, vīriyindriyaṃ ārabhitattā satindriyaṃ paggahitattā ac‍cantaṃ niṭṭhaṃ gacchanti| tattha yāni indriyāni, ayaṃ maggau saṃkilaisappahānaṃ| ayaṃ niraudhau āyatiṃ anuppādadhammau, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| ayaṃ āvaṭṭau hārau|

tattha  katamau vibhatti hārau? ‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti  yau samanupassati, sau ca khau adhimattaina laukikā yaṃ bhūmiyaṃ na tu ariyaina payaugaina sau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi pajahati| yaṃ vuc‍cati taj‍jāya bhūmiyā adhimattāya| tattha taj‍jāya bhūmiyaṃ pañ‍cahi ākāraihi adhimattataṃ paṭilabhati sīlaina vataina bāhussac‍caina samādhinā naikkhammasukhaina| tattha appattai pattasañ‍ñī  adhimānaṃ gaṇhāti| aitasmiṃyaiva vatthuppattiyaṃ bhagavā idaṃ suttaṃ bhāsati| sīlavā vatamattaināti| tattha yau appattai pattasañ‍ñī tassa yau samādhi, sau sāmisau kāpurisasaivitau pana sau kāpurisā vuc‍canti puthuj‍janā| āmisaṃ yañ‍ca ariyamaggamāgamma laukikā anariyaṃ taina samādhi hauti anariyau kāpurisasaivitau| yau pana ariyākāraina yathābhūtaṃ na jānāti na passati jānāti passati (pī॰), sau adhigamanaṃ pajahati yau ariyaina samādhinā akāpurisasaivitaina nirāmisaina nīyati, tattha akāpurisā vuc‍canti ariyapuggalā| yau taihi saivitau samādhi, sau akāpurisasaivitau| tasmā aikaṃ vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyaṃ ‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti asamanupassantau tathā pātaiti|

tattha katamā parivattanā? imāya dassanabhūmiyā kilaisā pahātabbā, taihi pahīyanti aniddiṭṭhāpi bhagavatā niddisitabbā yau|

tattha katamaṃ vaivacanaṃ? yā  sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyā attadiṭṭhiyā| ayaṃ bhūmi| yai kilaisā pahātabbā| tai appahīyanti aniddiṭṭhāpi bhagavatā sassatadiṭṭhi ca ucchaidadiṭṭhi ca, sā pariyantadiṭṭhi ca| yā apariyantadiṭṭhi ca, sā sassatadiṭṭhi ca| yā ucchaidadiṭṭhi, sā natthikā diṭṭhi| yā sassatadiṭṭhi, sā akiriyadiṭṭhi| idaṃ vaivacanaṃ|

tattha katamā pañ‍ñatti? taṇhā saṃyaujanapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā| maggau paṭilābhapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattau| indriyā paṭilābhapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattāti| tattha katamau autaraṇau? sak‍kāyau dukkhaṃ dassanappahātabbau| samudayau maggau| indriyāni tāni ca niddiṭṭhāni khandhadhātuāyatanaisu|

tattha katamau saudhanau hārau? yañhi ārabbha bhagavatā idaṃ suttaṃ bhāsitaṃ, sau ārabbha niddiṭṭhau| tattha katamau parikkhārau? nāmarūpassa haitupac‍cayaupi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ haitu bījaṃ| taina avij‍jā ca saṅkhārā ca pac‍cayau| nivattinayau na aparau pariyāyau sabbabhavau, yai ca sabbabhavassa haitu parabhaṇḍapac‍cayau iti sammādiṭṭhi paratau ca ghausau yaunisau ca manasikārau pac‍cayau| yā pañ‍ñā uppādaiti, aisā haitu sammādiṭṭhiyā sammāsaṅkappau bhavati, yā sammāsamādhi sammādiṭṭhi (pī॰), ayaṃ parikkhārau|

tattha  katamau samāraupanau? ‘‘ayamahamasmī’’ti  asamanupassī dukkhatau raugatau…pai॰… pan‍narasa padāni| sīlāni bhagavā kimatthiyāni kimānisaṃsāni| sīlāni, ānanda, avippaṭisāratthāni yāva vimutti| tattha duvidhau atthau – purisatthau ca vacanatthau ca|

91. tattha katamau purisatthau? yāyaṃ na pacchānutāpitā ayaṃ avippaṭisārau, ayaṃ purisatthau| yathā kauci brūhayati imatthamāsaivati sau bhaṇaiyya, kiñ‍ci mamaittha adhīnaṃ tassatthāya idaṃ kiriyaṃ ārabhāmīti| ayaṃ purisatthau|

tattha katamau vacanatthau? sīlāni kāyikaṃ vā vācasikaṃ vā sucaritaṃ avippaṭisārauti| tattha sīlassa vatassa ca bhāsauyaiva| anañ‍ñā sugatakammatā sucaritaṃ ayaṃ avippaṭisārau| aivaṃ yāva vimuttīti aikamaikasmiṃ padai dvai atthā – purisatthau ca vacanatthau ca, yathā imamhi suttai aivaṃ sabbaisu suttaisu dvai dvai atthā| ayaṃ hi paramatthau uttamatthau ca| yaṃ nibbānasacchikaṃ nissāya yaṃ sakaṃ sacchikātabbaṃ bhavati, sau vuc‍cati katassa satassa (ka॰) katthauti| ayaṃ puna vaivacanaṃ sampajānāti| iminā niyuttatthamabhilabbhanti vacanatthau| tattha yaṃ atthaṃ sāvakau abhikaṅkhati| tassa yau paṭilābhau, ayaṃ purisatthau| yaṃ yaṃ bhagavā dhammaṃ daisaiti, tassa tassa dhammassa yā atthaviñ‍ñatti| ayaṃ atthau, tattha  sīlānaṃ avippaṭisārau atthaupi ānisaṃsaupi| aisau ca ānisaṃsau yaṃ duggatiṃ na gacchati| yathā taṃ bhagavatā aisānisaṃsau dhammai suciṇṇai na duggatiṃ gacchati dhammacārī, ayaṃ atthau|

yaṃ purisau bhāvanābhūmiyaṃ sīlāni ārabbha sīlaina saṃyuttau hauti aivaṃ yāva vimutti tathā sīlakkhandhau| tattha yau ca avippaṭisārau anusayavasaina niddiṭṭhau, tañ‍ca sīlaṃ ayaṃ sīlakkhandhau| pāmauj‍japītipassaddhīti ca samādhindriyaina, ayaṃ samādhikkhandhau| yaṃ samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau| imai tayau khandhā sīlaṃ samādhi pañ‍ñā ca tathā sīlaṃ paripūraiti yaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ taina kāraṇaina sau sīlaṃ paripūraiti, anuppan‍nassa ca akusalassa anuppādāya vāyamati, uppan‍nassa ca pahānāya anuppan‍nassa ca kusalassa uppādāya, uppan‍nassa ca kusalassa bhiyyaubhāvāya  iti vīriyindriyaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ| tattha yau samādhikkhandhau, idaṃ samādhindriyaṃ| pañ‍ñākkhandhau pañ‍ñindriyaṃ, taṃ catūsu sammappadhānaisu daṭṭhabbaṃ| tathā yau anuppan‍nassa ca akusalassa anuppādāya vāyamati, idaṃ paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ| yaṃ uppan‍nassa, idaṃ dutiyaṃ| cattāri sammappadhānāni catūsu jhānaisu passitabbāni| tathā  sīlakkhandhaina naikkhammadhātu ca adhikā ādikā (pī॰), tayau ca vitak‍kā naikkhammavitak‍kau abyāpādavitak‍kau avihiṃsāvitak‍kau ca| sādhāraṇabhūtā| yā piyāyamānassa pāmauj‍jaina idaṃ kāyikaṃ sukhaṃ ānitaṃ aniyamītipaimaina, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| yau tattha avikkhaipau, ayaṃ samādhi| idaṃ pañ‍caṅgikaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ| yā caitasikā passaddhi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ viraudhanaṃ, yau kilaisau ca paridāhau, sau paṭhamai jhānai niruddhau| tathā yā ca kilaisapassaddhi yā ca vitak‍kavicārānaṃ passaddhi, ubhayaipi aitai dhammai passaddhāyaṃ| tattha kāyassa cittassa ca sukhaṃ sukhāyanā, idaṃ pītisukhinau passaddhi| yaupi aikaudibhāvau cittassa, taina aikaudibhāvaina yaṃ cittassa ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ, idaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṅgaṃ| iti ajjhattañ‍ca sampasādau caitasau ca aikaudibhāvau pīti ca sukhañ‍ca, idaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ caturaṅgikaṃ| yau passaddhakāyau sukhaṃ vaidaiti, taina adhimattaina sukhaina pharitvā sukhaṃ caitasikaṃ yaṃ, sau pītivītarāgau aivaṃ tassa pītivītarāgatāya upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sau pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhaṃ paṭilabhati| sukhañ‍ca paṭisaṃvaidaiti| sati ca sammā pañ‍ñāya paṭilabhati| sacai sati aikaggatā idaṃ pañ‍caṅgikaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ| yaṃ  sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati, ayaṃ aikaggatāya parāvidhānabhāgiyā, paṭhamai jhānai atthi cittaikaggatā nau cakkhussa vaidanā sabbaṃ pāripūriṃ gacchati| yathā catutthai jhānai, tathā yā upaikkhā passambhayaṃ satisampajañ‍ñaṃ cittaikaggatā ca, idaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ|

92. yathā samādhi dassayitabbaṃ, tathā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ taṃ catūsu ariyasac‍caisu passitabbaṃ| yaṃ samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, sā pajānanā catubbidhā asubhatau dukkhatau anattatau ca, yadārammaṇaṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ ariyasac‍caṃ, yaṃ pajānantau nibbindati vimuc‍cati tathā yaṃ kāmāsavassa pahānaṃ bhavāsavassa diṭṭhāsavassa avij‍jāsavassa, ayaṃ niraudhau appahīnabhūmiyaṃ  āsavasamudayau| imāni cattāri ariyasac‍cāni yathā pañ‍ñindriyaṃ passitabbaṃ| yathāyaṃ samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dassanabhūmi| sautāpattiphalañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānantau nibbindatīti, idaṃ tanukañ‍ca| kāmarāgabyāpādaṃ sakadāgāmiphalañ‍ca yaṃ nibbindati viraj‍jati, ayaṃ paṭhamajjhānabhāvanābhūmi ca rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti anāgāmiphalañ‍ca| yaṃ vimutti vimuc‍cati, ayaṃ avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti arahattañ‍ca| imai avippaṭisārā ca vīriyindriyañ‍ca cattārau sammappadhānā avippaṭisārā tañ‍ca upari yāva  samādhi, aivaṃ tai cattāri jhānāni samādhindriyañ‍ca yaṃ samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| imai cattārau satipaṭṭhānā sīlapāripūrimupādāya cāgasaṃhitaina ca nibbaidhikānañ‍ca nimittānaṃ anāvilamanā, idaṃ satindriyaṃ cattārau satipaṭṭhānā| yaṃ  puna imāya dhammadaisanāya tīsu ṭhānaisu diṭṭhaugamanakindriyaṃ kilaisapahānaina ca saikkhasīlaṃ, idaṃ saddhindriyaṃ| cattāri ca sautāpattiyaṅgāni phalāni| samādhindriyāni saupaniyāhārīni sabbasuttaisu niddisitabbāni| yaṃ jhānaṃ paṭilabhanaṃ vīriyagahitaṃyaiva ñāṇaṃ paṭissaratau, ayaṃ sutamayī pañ‍ñā| yau samādhi pubbāparanimittābhāsau anaumagatitāya yathākāmau, ayaṃ cintāmayī pañ‍ñā, yaṃ tathāsamāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ bhāvanāmayī pañ‍ñā| ayaṃ suttaniddaisau|

imaṃ suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ bujjhakāradhikaṃ bujjhitabbaṃ| yaihi aṅgaihi saman‍nāgataṃ taṃ bujjhissanti tassa aṅgāni bujjhissanti, taina baujjhaṅgā| tathā āditau yāva sīlaṃ vataṃ caitanā karaṇīyā, kissa sīlāni pāripūraiti| anuppan‍nassa ca akusalassa anuppādāya uppan‍nassa ca akusalassa pahānāya anuppan‍nassa kusalassa uppādāya uppan‍nassa ca kusalassa bhiyyaubhāvāya, idaṃ vīriyaṃ tassa tassa bujjhitassa aṅganti| ayaṃ vīriyasambaujjhaṅgau| iminā vīriyaina dvai dhammā āditau avippaṭisārau pāmauj‍jañ‍ca yā puna pīti avippaṭisārapac‍cayā pāmauj‍japac‍cayā, ayaṃ pītisambaujjhaṅgau| yaṃ pītimanassa kāyau passambhati| ayaṃ passaddhisambaujjhaṅgau| taina  kāyikasukhamānitaṃ yaṃ sukhinau cittaṃ samādhiyati, ayaṃ samādhisambaujjhaṅgau| yaṃ samāhitau yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ayaṃ dhammavicayasambaujjhaṅgau| yā sīlamupādāya pañ‍can‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ upādāyānulaumatā nimittāyanā pītibhāgiyānañ‍ca visaisabhāgiyānañ‍ca  apilāpanatā sahagatā hauti anavamaggau, ayaṃ satisambaujjhaṅgau| yaṃ yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti, ac‍cāraddhavīriyaṃ karauti| uddhac‍cabhūmīti katā abhipatthitaṃ paisaiti| kausaj‍jabhūmīti garahitau rahitaihi aṅgaihi bujjhati yaṃ cakkhusamathapathaṃ, sā upaikkhāti| taina sā upaikkhā tassa baujjhaṅgassa aṅganti karitvā upaikkhāsambaujjhaṅgauti vuc‍catai| aisau suttaniddaisau|

93. tattha katamā daisanā? asmiṃ suttai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitāni| tattha katamau vicayau? sīlavatau avippaṭisārau yāva vimutti imissāya pucchāya minikimatthassamīti dvai padāni pucchā dvai padāni visaj‍janāni dvīhi padaihi dvai abhiñ‍ñaṃ dvīhi caiva padaihi visaj‍janā kiṃ pucchati nibbādhikaṃ kāyabhūmiṃ kammassa tathā hi patiṭṭhā ca asaikkhai dhammai uppādaiti| tattha katamā yutti? sīlavatau  avippaṭisārau bhavati kiṃ nicchandassa  ca virāgau atthi aisā yutti| tattha katamaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ? vīriyaṃ vīriyindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| samādhi samādhindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍ñā pañ‍ñindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| vīriyaṃ adausassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| samādhi alaubhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| pañ‍ñā amauhassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| vīriyindriyaṃ tiṇṇaṃ maggaṅgānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, sammāvācāya sammākammantassa sammāājīvassa| samādhindriyaṃ tiṇṇaṃ maggaṅgānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, sammāsaṅkappassa sammāvācāya sammāsamādhinau| pañ‍ñindriyaṃ dvin‍naṃ maggaṅgānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, sammāsatiyā sammādiṭṭhiyā ca|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau? sīlakkhandhai vuttai sabbai tayau khandhā vuttā bhavanti, sīlamaiva hi sailaupamatā yathā sailau sabbapac‍catthikaihi akaraṇīyau aivaṃ taṃ cittaṃ sabbakilaisaihi na kampatīti, ayaṃ amauhau| virattaṃ passa udā॰ 34 udānapāḷiyaṃ rajanīyaisūti ayaṃ alaubhau| kaupanaiyyai na kuppatīti ayaṃ adausau| tattha pañ‍ñā amauhau kusalamūlaṃ, alaubhau alaubhauyaiva, adausau adausauyaiva| imaihi tīhi kusalamūlaihi saikkhabhūmiyaṃ ṭhitau asaikkhamaggaṃ uppādaiti| saikkhabhūmi sampattikammadhammai uppādaiti, sā ca sammāvimutti, yañ‍ca vimuttirasañāṇadassanaṃ imai dasa asaikkhānaṃ arahattaṃ dhammā| tattha aṭṭhaṅgikaina maggaina catubbidhā bhāvanāpi labbhati| sīlabhāvanā kāyabhāvanā cittabhāvanā  pañ‍ñābhāvanā ca| tattha sammākammantaina sammāājīvaina ca kāyau bhāvitau| sammāvācāya sammāvāyāmaina ca sīlaṃ bhāvitaṃ| sammāsaṅkappaina sammāsamādhinā ca cittaṃ bhāvitaṃ| sammādiṭṭhiyā sammāsatiyā ca pañ‍ñā bhāvitā| imāya catubbidhāya bhāvanāya dvai dhammā bhāvanāpāripūriṃ gacchanti cittaṃ pañ‍ñañ‍ca| cittaṃ bhāvanāya samathau, pañ‍ñā bhāvanāya vipassanā| tattha pañ‍ñā avij‍jāpahānaina cittaṃ upak‍kilaisaihi amissīkatanti| pañ‍ñā bhāvanāya cittabhāvanaṃyaiva paripūraiti| aivaṃ yassa subhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kutau taṃ dukkhamaissatīti| api ca khau pana tassa āyasmatau abyāpādadhātu adhimuttā, na sau paitaṃ samāpan‍nau tassa saṅkhāpahāraṃ daiti, saṅkhāvitak‍kitai sarīrai dukkhaṃ na vaidiyati, ayaṃ suttatthau|

94. tattha katamā daisanā? imamhi suttai dasa asaikkhā arahattadhammā daisitā appamāṇā ca sammā vibhāvanā| tattha katamau vicayau? sailaupamatā yai yai dhammā vaidanīyasukhadukkhaupagatā, tai sabbai nirūpaṃ vānupassantānaṃ vūpagatā kāyatau vaidayitaparikkhārau appavattitau dukkhaṃ na vaidiyati| tattha katamā yutti, yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kutau taṃ naṃ (ka॰) dukkhamaissatīti| tīsu bhāvanāsu dukkhaṃ nakkhamati cittaṃ cittabhāvanāya ca| niraudhabhāvanāya ca  ānantarikā samādhibhāvanāya ca| iti yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittanti samādhi phalassa padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau? yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ passa udā॰ 34 udānapāḷiyaṃ cittanti cittāni bhāvitāni yathā paṭhamaṃ niddiṭṭhāni pañ‍ñā sīlaṃ kāyau cittaṃ, sīlampi subhāvitaṃ kāyikacaitasikañ‍ca ṭhitattā nānupakampatīti vaidanāpi tathā sañ‍ñāpi saṅkhārāpi| kutau taṃ dukkhamaissatīti sukhampi nānugacchati, adukkhamasukhampi nāgatanti|

tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? idha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai dukkhaina adhikā bhavissanti, tai aivarūpāhi samāpattīhi virahissanti| ayamaittha bhagavatau adhippāyau| yai ca appasan‍nā, tai hi bhavissanti, pasan‍nānañ‍ca  pītipāmauj‍jaṃ bhavissati, ayaṃ tattha bhagavatau adhippāyau| āvaṭṭauti natthi āvaṭṭanassa bhūmi|

vibhattīti yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kutau taṃ dukkhamaissatīti duvidhau niddaisau – dukkhahaituniddaisau ca paṭipakkhaniddaisau ca| kau sau dukkhahaitu? yatau dukkhaṃ āgacchati paṭipakkhai vuttai saisadhammānaṃ sīlaṃ haitu ca pac‍cayau ca, tai sabbai dhammā vuttā haunti| aikabaudhipakkhiyai dhammai vuttai sabbai baudhagamanīyā dhammā vuttā bhavanti|

tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? imamhi suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai avippaṭisāraina chandikā, tai sīlapāripūrī bhavanti pāmauj‍jachandikā avippaṭisārīpāripūrī, ayamaittha bhagavatau adhippāyau…pai॰… ayaṃ catubyūhau hārau|

tattha katamau āvaṭṭau? idaṃ  suttaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ| yau nibbaidhau, ayaṃ niraudhau| yaina nibbijjhati, sau maggau| yaṃ nibbijjhati, taṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ nibbaidhagāminā maggaina pahīyati, samudayauyaṃ vuttau|

tattha katamā vibhatti? sīlavatau avippaṭisārauti vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyaṃ, parāmasantassa natthi avippaṭisārau yāva dausakataṃ kāyaina vā vācāya vā akusalaṃ ārabhati| kiñ‍cipissa aivaṃ hauti ‘‘sukatamaitaṃ sucaritamaitaṃ nau cassa taina avippaṭisāraina pāmauj‍jaṃ jāyati  yāva vimutti, tassa sīlavatau avippaṭisārau’’ti vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyaṃ, ayaṃ vibhattihārau|

tattha katamā parivattanā? imaihi sattahi upanisāsampattīhi aikādasa upanisā vibhattiyaṃ pajahānaṃ pajahanti, ayaṃ parivattanā|

tattha katamā vaivacanā? imaisaṃ ariyadhammānaṃ balabaujjhaṅgavimaukkhasamādhisamāpattīnaṃ imāni vaivacanāni|

tattha katamā pañ‍ñatti? sīlavatau avippaṭisārauti sīlakkhandhai naikkhammapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ, nisaj‍japañ‍ñatti ca aivaṃ dasa aṅgāni dvīhi dvīhi aṅgaihi pañ‍ñattāni|

tattha katamau autaraṇau? idaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyasuttaṃ pañ‍casu autiṇṇaṃ yathā yaṃ paṭhamaṃ niddiṭṭhaṃ aivamindriyādikhandhadhātuāyatanaisu niddisitabbāni|

tattha  katamau saudhanau hārau? sīlavatau avippaṭisārauti na tāva suddhau ārambhau avippaṭisārinau pāmauj‍janti na tāva suddhau ārambhau yāni aikādasa padāni daisitāni yadā tadā suddhau ārambhau, ayaṃ saudhanau|

tattha  katamau adhiṭṭhānau? sīlavaimattatāya pañ‍ñattaṃ aivaṃ dasa padāni sabbāni sīlakkhandhassa ānisaṃsau, tai ca patirūpadaisavāsau ca pac‍cayau attasammāpaṇidhānañ‍ca haitu, samādhikkhandhassa sukhaṃ haitu passaddhi pac‍cayau, yaina jhānasahajāti ca ṭhānanti jhānaṅgā aparau pariyāyau kāmaisu ādīnavānupassanā samādhinau pac‍cayau naikkhammai ānisaṃsadassāvitā haitu|

tattha katamā samāraupanā? yaṃ vīriyindriyaṃ, sau sīlakkhandhau| yaṃ sīlaṃ, tai cattārau dhammā padhānā| yaṃ dhammānudhammapaṭipatti, sau pātimaukkhasaṃvarau|

95. yassa sailaupamaṃ cittanti gāthā passa udā॰ 34 udānai, sailaupamanti upamā yathā sailau vātaina na kampati na uṇhaina na sītaina saṃkampati| yathā anaikā acaitanā, tai uṇhaina milāyanti, sītaina avasussanti, vātaina bhajanti| na aivaṃ sailau virattaṃ rajanīyaisu dausanīyai na dussatīti  kāraṇaṃ dausanīyai daumanassantaṃ, na duṭṭhaina vā kampati uṇhaina vā, sau milāyati sītaina vā avasussati, aivaṃ cittaṃ rāgaina nānussati sītaina kampatīti| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? virattaṃ rajanīyaisu dausanīyai na dussati| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? dausanīyai  panassanti na dussati, aduṭṭhaṃ taṃ na kausissanti, taina kuppanīyai na kuppati, yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kutau taṃ dukkhaniddaisau ca  kutau aivarūpassa dukkhaṃ āgamissatīti niddiṭṭhaṃ|

parivattanāti kutau taṃ dukkhamaissatīti yaṃ caitasikaṃ sukhaṃ anupādisaisā ayaṃ natthi saupādisaisā ayaṃ atthi| puna aivamāhaṃsu taṃ khaṇaṃ taṃ muhuttaṃ ubhayamaiva avaidayitaṃ saupādisaisaṃ yañ‍ca anupādisaisaṃ yañ‍ca taṃ khaṇaṃ taṃ muhuttaṃ anupādisaisaṃ yañ‍ca saupādisaisaṃ ca avaidayitaṃ| sukhamāpan‍nassa anāvattikanti ayamaittha visaisau parivattanā|

tattha  katamau vaivacanau? yassaivaṃ bhāvitaṃ cittaṃ vā bhāvitaṃ subhāvitaṃ anuṭṭhitaṃ vatthukataṃ susamāraddhaṃ| cittanti manau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ manindriyaṃ manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātu|

tattha  katamā pañ‍ñatti? cittaṃ manau saṅkhārā vūpasamapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| samādhi asaikkhapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattau| dukkhaṃ ucchin‍napañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ|

tattha katamau autaraṇau? cittai niddiṭṭhai pañ‍cakkhandhā niddiṭṭhā haunti, ayaṃ khandhaisu autaraṇau, manauviñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā niddiṭṭhāya aṭṭhārasa dhātuyau niddiṭṭhā haunti, ayaṃ dhātūsu autaraṇau| manāyatanai niddiṭṭhai sabbāni āyatanāni niddiṭṭhāni haunti| tattha manāyatanaṃ nāmarūpassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| nāmarūpapac‍cayā saḷāyatanaṃ| tathā paṭic‍casamuppādai| ayaṃ autaraṇau| tattha katamau saudhanau suddhauyaiva ārambhau|

tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau? chaḷindriyaṃ bhāvanā aikattāyaṃ pañ‍ñatti chaṭṭhitaina kāyau aikattāya pañ‍ñattau|

tattha katamau parikkhārau? cittassa pubbahaitu samuppādāya manasikārau ca tappauṇatā ca yaṃ asamāhitabhūmiyaṃ ca visaisadhammānaṃ abhāvitattā cittasatataṃ gacchati, sacai samādhinau sukhaṃ haitu avippaṭisārau pac‍cayau, ayaṃ haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau parikkhārau|

tattha  katamā samāraupanā? yassaivaṃ bhāvitanti tassa dhammā samāraupayitabbā| kāyau sīlaṃ pañ‍ñā bhāvitacittanti anabhirataṃ anapaṇataṃ anaikaṃ anutaṃ anāpaj‍jāsattaṃ ayaṃ samañ‍ñāyatanā na tassa saikkhassa sammāsamādhi sabbai asaikkhā dasa arahantadhammā niddiṭṭhā haunti| asaikkhabhāgiyāni suttāni|

96. yassa  nūna, bhantai, kāyagatāsati abhāvitā, ayaṃ sau añ‍ñataraṃ sabrahmacāriṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ (ka॰) āsaj‍ja samāpaj‍ja appaṭinisaj‍ja janapadacārikaṃ pak‍kamaiyya, sau āyasmā imasmiṃ vippaṭijānāti dvai pajāni paṭijānāti cittabhāvanāyañ‍ca diṭṭhiyā pahānaṃ, kāyabhāvanāyañ‍ca diṭṭhippahānaṃ, kāyabhāvanāyañ‍ca taṇhāpahānaṃ, yaṃ paṭhamaṃ upamaṃ karauti| asucināpi sucināpi pathavī naiva aṭṭiyati na jigucchati na pītipāmauj‍jaṃ paṭilabhati, aivamaiva  hi pathavīsamaina sau caitasā anvayaina appakaina avairaina abyāpaj‍jaina viharāmīti| iti sau āyasmā kiṃ paṭijānāti, kāyabhāvanāya sukhindriyapahānaṃ paṭijānāti, cittabhāvanāya saumanassindriyapahānaṃ paṭijānāti| kāyikā vaidanā rāgānusayamanugatānaṃ sukhindriyaṃ paṭikkhipati| na hi vaidanākkhandhaṃ yā caitasikā sukhavaidanā tattha ayaṃ paṭilābhapac‍cayā uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ| sautaṃ paṭikkhipati, na hi manausamphassajaṃ vaidanaṃ| tattha catūsu mahābhūtaisu rūpakkhandhassa anusayapaṭighapahānaṃ bhaṇati| kāmai rūpañ‍ca tañ‍ca asaikkhabhūmiyaṃ| kāyai kāyānupassanā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārañ‍ca| balaina ca ussāhaina ca sabbaṃ manasi katattānaṃ pahānaṃ  maidaṃ katālikāya ca purisaina ca maṇḍanakajātikaina ca, aitaihi imassa mātāpitusambhūtaṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇaṃ, sau kāyaina ca kāyānupassanāya ca cittaina ca cittānupassanāya ca dvai dhammai dhāraiti| kāyakilaisavatthuṃ cittaina ca cittasan‍nissayai cittaina subhāvitaina sattan‍naṃ ca samāpattīnaṃ viharituṃ paṭijānāti|

gahapatiputtaupamatāya ca yathā gahapatiputtassa nānāraṅgānaṃ vatthakaraṇḍakau puṇṇau bhavaiyya, sau yaṃ yadaiva vatthayugaṃ pubbaṇhasamayai ākaṅkhati, pubbaṇhasamayai nibbāpaiti, aivaṃ majjhanhikasamayai, sāyanhasamayai, aivamaiva sau āyasmā cittassa subhāvitattā yathārūpaina vihāraina ākaṅkhati pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharituṃ, tathārūpaina yathārūpaina (pī॰ ka॰) pubbaṇhasamayaṃ viharati, majjhanhikasamayai, sāyanhasamayai| taina vaisa āyasmatā upamāya mai āsitāya pathavī vā anuttarā indriyabhāvanā bhāvitacittaina| taina sau āyasmā idaṃ aṭṭhavidhaṃ bhāvanaṃ paṭijānāti catūsu mahābhūtaisu, kāyabhāvanaṃ upakacaṇḍālaṃ purisamaitakaṃ bhavatalākāsu cittabhāvanaṃ, imāhi bhāvanāhi tāya bhāvanāya ca samathā pāripūrimantaihi| imaihi catūhi pañ‍ñāpāripūrimantaihi|

97. kathaṃ  upakacaṇḍālaṃ paṭikūlaisu dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? kāyau  pakatiyā appaṭikūlaṃ kāyai uddhumātakasañ‍ñā saṃkhittaina nava sañ‍ñā imai paṭikūlā dhammā caisau āyasmā paṭikūlatau ajigucchitau kāyagatāsatiyā bhāvanānuyaugamanuyuttau viharati, na hi tassa jigucchappahāya cittaṃ paṭikūlati|

kathaṃ  appaṭikūlaisu dhammaisu paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharatīti? kāyau sabbalaukassa appaṭikūlau taṃ sau āyasmā asubhasañ‍ñāya viharati| aivaṃ appaṭikūlaisu dhammaisu paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

kathaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharatīti api sabbauyaṃ laukassa yamidaṃ muṇḍau pattapāṇī kulaisu piṇḍāya vicarati, taina ca sau āyasmā suvaṇṇadubbaṇṇaina appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī cittaina ca kāyaina nibbidāsahagataina appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī, aivaṃ paṭikūlaisu appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

kathaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? paṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu subhasañ‍ñinau itthirūpai paṭikūlaisu ca jigucchinau vinīlakavipubbakai tattha sau āyasmā paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

kathaṃ paṭikūlaisu dhammaisu tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jayitvā upaikkhakau viharati satau ca sampajānau ca? appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu subhasañ‍ñinau itthirūpai paṭikūlaisu ca jigucchinau vinīlakavipubbakai tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jayitvā ‘naitaṃ mama’‘naisauhamasmi’‘naisau mai’ attāti viharati| aivaṃ  tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jayitvā upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau|

aparau pariyāyau| taidhātukau laukasan‍nivāsau sabbabālaputhuj‍janānaṃ appaṭikūlasañ‍ñā| tattha ca āyasmā sāriputtau appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati| aivaṃ appaṭikūlaisu dhammaisu paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

kathaṃ paṭikūlaisu dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? paṭikūlasañ‍ñinau sabbasaikkhā idha kā taidhātukai sabbalaukai| tattha katamau bhūmippattau samādhiphalai sacchikatau appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? na hi taṃ atthi yassa laukassa pahānāya paṭikūlasañ‍ñī uppādaiyya|

kathaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? taidhātukai laukasan‍nivāsai yāva kāmalaukabhūmatā hi rāgānaṃ vītarāgānaṃ paṭikūlasamatā rūpārūpadhātuṃ appaṭikūlasamatā| tattha ca āyasmā sāriputtau paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati| aivaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

kathaṃ  paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? yaṃ kiñ‍ci paratau duruttānaṃ durāgatānaṃ vacanapathānaṃ taṃ vacanaṃ appaṭikūlaṃ yāvatā vācasau appatirūpā tathā janassa appaṭikūlasañ‍ñā| tattha āyasmā sāriputtau abhiñ‍ñāya sacchikatau appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati, aivaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

98. kathaṃ  paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jayitvā upaikkhakau ca viharati satau ca sampajānau? yañ‍ca naisaṃ samanupassati yai dhammā duc‍caritā, tai dhammā appaṭikūlā| tattha āyasmā sāriputtau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati yai dhammā duc‍caritā, tai dhammā aniṭṭhavipākā| yai dhammā sucaritā, tai ācayagāminau| sau ca sucaritaṃ ācayagāminiṃ karitvā duc‍caritaṃ aniṭṭhavipākaṃ karitvā tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jayitvā upaikkhakau viharati|

atha paṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlaisu ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati| taṇhā paṭikūladhammā kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? taṇhāvasaina hi sattā dvīhi dhammaihi sattā, kabaḷīkārai āhārai rasataṇhāya sattā, phassai sukhasañ‍ñāya sattā| tatthāyasmā sāriputtau kabaḷīkārai ca āhārai paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati, phassai ca dukkhasañ‍ñī viharati| aivaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca paṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

kathaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? taṇhākkhayaṃ anuttaraṃ nibbānaṃ tathā bālaputhuj‍janānaṃ paṭikūlasañ‍ñā pahatasañ‍ñā ca| tatthāyasmatau  sāriputtassa appaṭikūlasañ‍ñā abyāpādasañ‍ñā ca sāmaṃ pañ‍ñāya passitvā aivaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|

kathaṃ paṭikūlaisu ca appaṭikūlaisu ca dhammaisu appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati? tatiyai  ca nibbānai paṭikūlasañ‍ñinau yasaina ca kittini ca appaṭikūlasañ‍ñinau| tatthāyasmā sāriputtau assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ sammāpañ‍ñāya paṭijānantau paṭikūlañ‍ca appaṭikūlañ‍ca dhammaṃ tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jayitvā appaṭikūlasañ‍ñī viharati|
