kathaṃ  paṭikūlaṃ appaṭikūlañ‍ca dhammaṃ tadubhayaṃ abhinivaj‍jayitvā upaikkhakau viharati? satau ca sampajānau ca, yañ‍ca samanupassati anunayau appaṭikūlau dhammau paṭighau ca paṭikūlau dhammau, tatthāyasmā sāriputtau anunayassa paṭighappahīnattā upaikkhakau viharati satau sampajānau ca| yañ‍cassa samanupassati ayaṃ pañ‍cavidhā anuttarā indriyabhāvanā| ayaṃ suttaniddaisau|

99. tattha katamau daisanāhārau? imamhi suttai kiṃ daisitabbaṃ? tattha vuc‍catai, imamhi suttai diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārau daisitau, tathā vimuttaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhaṇā ca adhipañ‍ñādhammaṃ daisitaṃ|

tattha katamau vicayau? yai kāyai kāyānupassinau viharanti, taisaṃ cittaṃ anunayappaṭighaina na viharati anunayappaṭighaina cābhiramamānassa cittaṃ samaggataṃ bhavissatīti bhāvanāya balamaitaṃ, ayaṃ vicayau hārau|

tattha katamau yuttihārau? kāyabhāvanāya ca cittabhāvanāya ca na kiñ‍ci sabrahmacārī atimañ‍ñissatīti| atthi aisā yutti, ayaṃ yuttihārau|

tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau hārau? kāyabhāvanāya  paṭhamassa sati upaṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| yā pathavīsamacittatā, sā anic‍cānupassanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau? yaṃ pathavīsamaina caitasā viharati attānupassī pathavīsamaina gihī  viharati| kau atthau pathavīsamaināti? yathā yai ca sailaupamatāya akammayuttā aivamaiva pathavīsamau ayaṃ hiriyatāya| ayaṃ lakkhaṇau|

tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? imamhi byākaraṇai kau tassa āyasmatau adhippāyau? yai kaici arahantā indriyabhāvanaṃ ākaṅkhiyanti, tai pathavīsamataṃ uppādayissantīti| ayaṃ adhippāyau|

tattha katamau āvaṭṭauti? natthi āvaṭṭassa bhūmi|

tattha  katamau vibhatti? yau kāyānupassī viharati, sau pathavīsamacittataṃ paṭilabhissatīti na aikaṃsaina| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? yai khaṇḍakādichin‍nakādinau, na tai pathavīsamacittataṃ paṭilabhanti| sabbā kāyagatāsati saikkhabhāvanāya nibbānaṃ phalaṃ, ayaṃ vibhatti|

tattha katamau parivattanau hārau? yai kāyānupassinau viharissanti, taisaṃyaiva kāyapac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyya āsavā vighātapariḷāhā, ayaṃ parivattanau hārau|

tattha katamau autaraṇau? pañ‍cakkhandhā sattaisu ca pañ‍cakkhandhā (pī॰) avitiṇṇā avatiṇṇā (pī॰) bāvīsatindriyāni, tathā yaṃ manindriyaṃ, taṃ manaudhātu manāyatanañ‍ca| yaṃ samādhindriyaṃ, taṃ dhammadhātu dhammāyatanañ‍ca| ayaṃ autaraṇau hārau|

tattha  katamau saudhanau hārau? yai ca manasā cattārau bhāvaitabbā, tai sabbai bhāvitā yaṃ taṃ manaina pahīnai pattabbataṃ sabbattha aitassa ca atthāya ārambhau, sau atthau suddhau| ayaṃ saudhanau hārau|

tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau? ayaṃ samādhi aikattatāya pañ‍ñattau, cha kāyā aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā| pañ‍cindriyāni rūpīni rūpakāyau| cha vaidanākāyā vaidanākāyau| cha sañ‍ñākāyā sañ‍ñākāyau| cha caitanākāyā caitanākāyau| cha viñ‍ñāṇakāyā viñ‍ñāṇakāyau| sabbaipi aitai dhammā dhammakāyautiyaiva saṅkhaṃ gacchanti| ayaṃ adhiṭṭhānau|

parikkhārauti  samāpattikausal‍lañ‍ca vīthikausal‍lañ‍ca dhītikausal‍lañ‍ca (pī॰) haitu| yañ‍ca gaucarakausal‍laṃ yañ‍ca kal‍laṃ taṃ kausal‍laṃ pac‍cayau| vaudānakausal‍laṃ haitu, kal‍laṃ pac‍cayau| sukhaṃ haitu, abyāpaj‍jaṃ pac‍cayau| ayaṃ parikkhārau|

tattha katamau samāraupanauti? yathā pathavī sucimpi nikkhīpantai asucimpi nikkhittai tādisaiyaiva aivaṃ kāyau manāpikaihipi phassaihi amanāpikaihipi phassaihi tādisauyaiva paṭighasamphassaina vā sukhāya vaidanāya tādisaṃ yau cittaṃ| idaṃ suttaṃ vibhattaṃ saaupammaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa puggalassa vibhāgaina| tattha samāraupanāya avakāsau natthi|

100. tattha  katamaṃ suttaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ? yatau ca kusalaihi dhammaihi na viraudhati, na vaḍḍhati, imaṃ ādīnavaṃ bhagavā daisaiti, tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraiyya, vivaṭaṃ nātivassati, tatau ādīnavatau vivaraiyyāti taṃ  tīhi dhammaihi nābhidhaṃsitāti asubhasañ‍ñāya rāgaina nābhidhaṃsiyati| maittāya dausaina nābhidhaṃsiyati| vipassanā mauhaina nābhidhaṃsiyati| aivañ‍cassa yau yau dhammau paṭipakkhau tamhi tamhi dhammai paripūrissati| yau tassa dhammassa akusalau dhammau paṭipakkhau, taina nādhivāsiyati|

aparau pariyāyau| yai imai dhammā attanā na sak‍kauti vuṭṭhānaṃ, tai aitai dhammā daisitā| chan‍namativassatīti taihi vitak‍kaṃ yaina ca sak‍kā puna daisitaṃ cittaṃ vibhāvaituṃ pariyaudāpaituṃ vivaikanin‍nassa vivaikapauṇassa vivaikapabbhārassa vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍lataṃ āpaj‍jati kusalaisu dhammaisu, saiyyathāpi nāma uppalaṃ vā kumudaṃ vā padumaṃ vā udakai suk‍kapakkhai candau yāvaratti yāvadivasau āgacchati, tassa vuddhiyaiva pāṭikaṅkhitabbā, na parihāni, aivaṃvidhaṃ taṃ cittaṃ nābhidhaṃsiyati| aparaupaittha yau akūṭau asaṭhau amāyāvī uju purisau yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikarauti| tattha yau chādaiti tassa akusalā dhammā cittaṃ anudhāvanti| chan‍namativassatīti yau pana hauti asaṭhau akūṭau amāyāvī uju purisau yathābhūtaṃ attānaṃ āvikarauti| tassa cittaṃ akusalaihi dhammaihi na viddhaṃsiyati, ayaṃ suttatthau|

101. tattha katamā daisanā? idha daisitā dasa akusalakammapathā adhivassanatāya dasa kusalakammapathā anadhivassanatāya akusalaihi na  visujjhati| yathā vuttaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘cittasaṃkilaisā, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃkilissantī’’ti|

tattha  katamau vicayau? yassaivaṃ cittaṃ adhivāsiyati, tassa bujjhitassa yaṃ bhavaiyya kūṭaiyya, taṃ ānantariyainapi satthari vā guṇānukampanatāya, ayaṃ vicayau|

tattha katamā yuttīti? aivaṃ anadhivasiyantaṃ cittaṃ vuṭṭhāti| vuṭṭhitaṃ patiṭṭhahati kusalaisu dhammaisūti atthi aisā yutti|

padaṭṭhānanti  chan‍namativassatīti chan‍naṃ asaṃvarānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, vivaṭaṃ nātivassatīti achan‍naṃ saṃvaraṇānaṃ| tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraiyya vivaṭaṃ nātivassatīti daisanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ|

lakkhaṇauti chan‍namativassatīti yai kaici vicittaina chan‍naina aikalakkhaṇā dhammā sabbai tai aviddhaṃsiyanti| tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraiyya| vivaṭaṃ nātivassatīti yai kaici taina acchan‍naina aikalakkhaṇā dhammā sabbai tai nātivassantīti lakkhaṇau hārau|

tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? imamhi suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yaisaṃ kaisañ‍ci cittaṃ akusalā dhammā adhipaṭidaisitā tai yathādhammaṃ paṭikarissantīti ayaṃ tattha bhagavatau adhippāyau| ayaṃ catubyūhau hārau|

āvaṭṭauti  yaṃ chan‍naṃ taṃ duvidhaṃ kampamānaṃ samucchitabbau| ānantariyasamādhīnaṃ| tattha passaddhiyañ‍ca mānau āsavai vaḍḍhaiti, assaddhiyaina ca pamādaṃ gacchati, pamādaina aunamati, un‍naḷabhāvaṃ gacchati| vuttaṃ caitaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘un‍naḷānaṃ pamattānaṃ taisaṃ vaḍḍhanti āsavā’’ti cattāri tāni upādānāni, yāni cattāri upādānāni, tai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā bhavanti| imāni sac‍cāni dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca| tasmā chan‍naṃ vivaraiyyāti yaina haitunā, tai āsavā vaḍḍhanti| taisaṃ pahīnattā āsavā pahīyantai| tattha appamādaina assaddhiyaṃ pahīyati uddhac‍cakuk‍kuc‍cappahānaina auḷārikatā tassa dvai dhammā na samathau ca bhāvanā ca pāripūriṃ gacchanti| yau taisaṃ āsavānaṃ khayau, ayaṃ niraudhau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni, ayaṃ āvaṭṭau|

tattha katamau vibhatti hārau? chan‍namativassatīti na aikaṃsau| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? yassa assā nivattanā yathāpi saikkhānaṃ| yathāvuttaṃ bhagavatā –

‘‘kiñ‍cāpi  saikkhau pakaraiyya pāpaṃ, kāyaina vācāya uda caitasā vā|

abhabbau hi tassa pariguhanāya, abhabbatā diṭṭhapadassa hautī’’ti||

kiñ‍cāpi  taisaṃ nivāraṇaṃ cittaṃ hauti| api tu appac‍cayā samāyai ca tai niddisitabbā, ayaṃ vibhattihārau|

tattha katamau parivattanau hārau| chan‍namativassatīti yassa yai dhammā sabbaṃ anavivaṭaṃ ativassiyati, vivaṭaṃ nātivassati, avaguṇantaṃ nātivassati| ayaṃ parivattanau hārau|

tattha katamau vaivacanau hārau| chan‍nanti āvutaṃ nivutaṃ pihitaṃ paṭikuj‍jitaṃ sañchan‍naṃ paraudhaṃ, vivaṭaṃ nātivassatīti yassa tai dhammā pabbaj‍jitā  vinaudaṃ nādhivassitā vantikatāti, ayaṃ vaivacanau hārau|

tattha katamau pañ‍ñatti hārau| chan‍namativassatīti kilaisabhāgiyapañ‍ñattaṃ vivaṭaṃ nātivassatīti sadhammakic‍caṃ yaṃ paṭipadā pañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ, tasmā hi chan‍naṃ vivaraiyyāti anusāsanapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ, vivaṭaṃ nātivassatīti niddhānapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ, ayaṃ pañ‍ñatti hārau|

tattha katamau autaraṇau hārau? chan‍namativassatīti tayau kilaisā rāgau dausau mauhau, tai khandhaisu saṅkhārakkhandhau…pai॰… tai purā yathā niddiṭṭhaṃ khandhadhātuāyatanaisu, ayaṃ autaraṇau hārau|

tattha katamau saudhanau hārau? yainārambhaina idaṃ suttaṃ bhāsati sau ārambhau niyuttau|

adhiṭṭhānauti chan‍namativassatīti aikattatāya pañ‍ñattaṃ| kiṃkāraṇaṃ? idaṃ hi ativassatīti imassa ca ativassati aivañ‍ca ativassatīti ayaṃ vaimattatāya yā suṇasādhāraṇaihi lakkhaṇaihi pañ‍ñāpiyati, sā aikattapañ‍ñatti|

tattha katamau parikkhārau? yañ‍ca taṃ ativassiyanti, tassa dvai haitū dvai pac‍cayā akusalapasutaiva  vācakattābhirati ca| imai dvai ayaunisaumanasikārau ca kusalā dhammā vaupasaggā ca, imai dvai pac‍cayā|

tattha katamau samāraupanau? chan‍namativassatīti vaimati passatīti chan‍naṃ yaṃ pariggahituṃ yaṃ adaisituṃ appassutaṃ yaṃ kathaṃkathā vibhūtaina  akusalamūlaina yaṃ taṇhāya ca tai vaḍḍhati dausāti san‍nitvā tai appasakkhayaina saṅkhārā| saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yāva jarāmaraṇaṃ, ayaṃ samāraupanau| yaṃ puna tathā daisanā, tassaiva akusalā dhammā vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍latamāpaj‍jati tassa saṅkhārā niraudhā, ayaṃ samāraupanau|

102. cattārau puggalā passa a॰ ni॰ 4.85 tamau tamaparāyanauti…pai॰… tattha katamau vuc‍catai tamau nāma? yau tamau andhakārau, yathā vuttaṃ bhagavatā ‘‘yathā andhakārai tasmiṃ bhayānakai sakampidhātupurisau na passati, aivamaiva añ‍ñāṇatau tamaupanandhakārau pāpakasakammasavipākaṃ na saddhau hauti| iti aivaṃ lakkhaṇatā añ‍ñāṇaṃ tamau avij‍jā mauhau, yaina sattā yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti, iti vuc‍cati tamauti| sau tiṇṇaṃ cakkhūnaṃ tamau maṃsacakkhunau dibbacakkhunau pañ‍ñācakkhunau, imaisaṃ cakkhūnaṃ idha tamau niddisiyati añ‍ñāṇanti| tattha katamaṃ añ‍ñāṇaṃ adassanaṃ? atha nissayai yaṃ pubbantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ aparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ pubbantāparantai añ‍ñāṇaṃ haitumhi añ‍ñāṇaṃ pac‍cayamhi añ‍ñāṇaṃ tassa añ‍ñāṇinau samādhibhūtassa aisau nissandau| yaṃ na jānāti idaṃ saivitabbaṃ idaṃ na manasikātabbanti| sau taina tamaina niddisiyati tamaupi yathā vuc‍cati| mūḷhauti aivaṃ caitanā| taina tamaina sau puggalau vuc‍cati| tamauti  sau taina tamaina asamūhataina asamucchin‍naina tapparamau bhavati tapparāyanau, ayaṃ vuc‍cati puggalau tamau tamaparāyanauti| parāyanauyaiva dhammau manasikātabbau sau tamau dahati añ‍ñacittaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti| tai cassa dhammā nijjhānakkhamanti| sau sutamayāya pañ‍ñāya samanupassati|

tattha katamau tamau jautiparāyanau? sau taina pañ‍ñāvasaina iriyati aivaṃ tassaiva iriyantassa parāyanau bhavati| ayaṃ vuc‍catai puggalau tamau jautiparāyanau|

tattha katamau puggalau jauti jautiparāyanau jautiparāyanau (pī॰)? tattha vuc‍cati jauti nāma yaṃ tassa cai tamassa paṭipakkhaina yai ca dhammai antamasau ñāṇālaukau, sau suṇadhammau puggalau tamau jautiparāyanau, tattha vuc‍catai, yauyaṃ puggalau tamau jautiparāyanau, sau yadi tathārūpaṃ kalyāṇamittaṃ paṭilabhati, yau naṃ akusalatau ca nivāraiti  bhāvitakusalatāva bhāvī niyaujaitīti| aivañ‍ca saddhammaṃ daisaiti| imai dhammā kusalā, imai dhammā akusalā| imai dhammā sāvaj‍jā, imai dhammā anavaj‍jā| imai dhammā saivitabbā, imai dhammā na saivitabbā| imai dhammā bhajitabbā, imai dhammā na bhajitabbā| imai dhammā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā, imai dhammā na upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā| imai dhammā manasikātabbā, imai dhammā na manasikātabbāti| pac‍catai sañ‍ñāya yathā sañ‍ñāyati satindriyāni, sau aivaṃ pajānāti| imai dhammā kusalā, imai dhammā akusalā| imai dhammā sāvaj‍jā, imai dhammā anavaj‍jā| imai dhammā saivitabbā, imai  dhammā na saivitabbā| imai dhammā bhāvaitabbā, imai dhammā na bhāvaitabbā| imai dhammā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā, imai dhammā na upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā| imai dhammā manasikātabbā, imai dhammā na manasikātabbāti| sau tai dhammai susuyyati, sautaṃ audahati, añ‍ñaṃ cittaṃ upaṭṭhapaiti, tai cassa dhammā nijjhānakkhamanti, sau sutamayāya pañ‍ñāya saman‍nāgatau sau taina pac‍cayavasaina iriyati aivaṃ tassaiva iriyanti tapparamau bhavati tapparāyanau| ayaṃ vuc‍catai puggalau tamau tamaparāyanau|

tattha katamau puggalau jauti tamaparāyanau? jauti nāma yā tassaiva tamassa paṭipakkhaina yai dhammā antamasau ñāṇālaukau, sau puna dhammau| katamā uc‍catai? pañ‍ñāyatau paṇḍitauti vuc‍catai, sau aivaṃ pajānāti| imai dhammā kusalā, imai dhammā akusalā| imai dhammā sāvaj‍jā, imai dhammā anavaj‍jā| imai dhammā saivitabbā, imai dhammā na saivitabbā| imai dhammā bhāvitabbā, imai dhammā na bhāvitabbā| imai dhammā upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā, imai dhammā na upasampaj‍ja vihātabbā| imai dhammā manasikātabbā, imai dhammā na manasikātabbā| idha pana pāpamittasaṃsaivanau pāpamittavasānugau akusalai dhammai abhivaḍḍhaiti, kusalai dhammai pajahati| sau taina pamādaina pac‍cayasañ‍ñā amanasikatvā assatiasampajañ‍ñaṃ āsaivati| tayā yau paṭipakkhau tamau, sau pavaḍḍhaiti| sau tamābhibhūtau parāyanau tamaparamau caiva bhavati| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati puggalau jauti tamaparāyanau|

103. tattha katamau puggalau jauti jautiparāyanau? tattha vuc‍catai sauyaṃ puggalau kalyāṇamittassa san‍nissitau bhavati sak‍kā saṃyaugī kusalaṃ  gavaisī, sau kalyāṇamittai upasaṅkamitvā paripucchati, paripañhayati? kiṃ kusalaṃ, kiṃ akusalaṃ? kiṃ sāvaj‍jaṃ, kiṃ anavaj‍jaṃ? kiṃ saivitabbaṃ, kiṃ na saivitabbaṃ? kiṃ bhāvitabbaṃ, kiṃ na bhāvitabbaṃ? kiṃ upasampaj‍ja vihātabbaṃ, kiṃ na upasampaj‍ja vihātabbaṃ? kiṃ manasikātabbaṃ, kiṃ na manasikātabbaṃ? kathaṃ saṃkilaisau hauti, kathaṃ vaudānaṃ hauti? kathaṃ pavatti hauti, kathaṃ nivatti  hauti? kathaṃ bandhau hauti, kathaṃ maukkhau hauti? kathaṃ sak‍kāyasamudayau hauti, kathaṃ sak‍kāyaniraudhau hauti? sau aittha daisitaṃ yathā upaṭṭhitaṃ tathā sampaṭipaj‍jantau sau aivaṃ pajānāti| imai dhammā kusalā, imai dhammā akusalā| aivaṃ…pai॰… yāva kathaṃ sak‍kāyasamudayau hauti, kathaṃ sak‍kāyaniraudhau hautīti vitthāraina kātabbaṃ| sau tai dhammai adhipāṭikaṅkhāti aivaṃ lakkhaṇaṃ ñāṇaṃ vij‍jā ālaukaṃ vaḍḍhaiti| sau puggalau tapparamau bhavati tapparāyanau, ayaṃ vuc‍catai puggalau jauti jautiparāyanau|

tattha katamau puggalau tamau tamaparāyanau? yau akusalaṃ dhammaṃ dīpaiti| taṃ bhāvanāya hīnāsu gatīsu upapattiṃ dassaiti, tapparamau bhavati tapparāyanau| ayaṃ vuc‍catai puggalau tamau tamaparāyanau|

tattha yau puggalau tamau jautiparāyanau? sau tamaina akusalassa kammassa vipākaṃ dassaiti| tamaiti yaṃ cakkhu kalyāṇamittassa yaina akusalai dhammai pajahati, kusalai dhammai abhivaḍḍhati|

tattha  yau ca paṇītāsu gatīsu upapattiṃ dassaiti, tapparamau taina vuc‍catai tamau jautiparāyanau|

tattha yau puggalau jauti tamaparāyanau? kusalassa kammavipākaṃ dassaiti| yaṃ cakkhu pāpamittasaṃsaggaina pāpamittupasaivaina pāpamittavasānugau akusalaṃ dhammaṃ abhivaḍḍhati, taṃ bhāvanāya hīnāsu gatīsu upapattiṃ dassaiti| tapparamau taina vuc‍catai jauti tamaparāyanau|

tattha yau puggalau jauti jautiparāyanau sau jautitā pabhātā jautitabhāvatāya (pī॰) yāva paṇītāsu gatīsu upapattiṃ dassaiti| tapparamau taināha jauti jautiparāyanau|

jautitamaparāyanaina  dasa akusalānaṃ kammānaṃ udayaṃ dassaiti| tamaina puggalaina akusalānaṃ kammānaṃ vipākaṃ dassaiti| na akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ vipākaṃ dassaiti| tamaina aṭṭha micchattāni dassaiti| jautinā aṭṭha sammattāni dassaiti| jautinā tamaparāyanaina dasa akusalakammapathai dassaiti| jautinā paṇītattaṃ dassaiti| tamaina jautiparāyanaina atapanīyaṃ dhammaṃ dassaiti| jautinā tamaparāyanaina tapanīyaṃ dhammaṃ dassaiti| ayaṃ suttatthau|

104. tattha  katamau daisanā hārau? imamhi suttai kiṃ daisitaṃ? tattha vuc‍catai imamhi suttai kusalākusalā dhammā daisitā| kusalākusalānañ‍ca dhammānaṃ vipākau daisitau| hīnappaṇītānañ‍ca sattānaṃ gati nānākāraṇaṃ daisitaṃ| ayaṃ daisanā hārau|

tattha katamau vicayau hārau? akusalassa  kammassa yau vipākaṃ pac‍canubhauti| tattha ṭhitau akusalai dhammai uppādiyati vicayantaṃ yuj‍jati| kusalassa kammassa yau vipākaṃ pac‍canubhauti| tattha ṭhitau kusalai dhammai uppādiyati vicayantaṃ yuj‍jati| ayaṃ vicayau yutti ca|

tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau hārau? yau puggalau jauti, sau pac‍cavaikkhaṇāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| yau puggalau tamau, sau tamādin‍naṃ vānupassanāya padaṭṭhānanti dassaiti| tamaina jautiparāyanaina appamādassa padaṭṭhānaṃ dassaiti, tamau avij‍jāya ca diṭṭhiyā ca padaṭṭhānaṃ dassaiti| jautinā tamaparāyanaina pamādassa ca diṭṭhiyā ca padaṭṭhānaṃ dassaiti| ayaṃ padaṭṭhānau|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau hārau? tamaina tamaparāyanaina tamauti avij‍jāya niddiṭṭhāya sabbakilaisadhammā niddiṭṭhā haunti| tamaina jautiparāyanaina jautivij‍jāya niddiṭṭhāya sabbai baudhipakkhiyadhammā niddiṭṭhā haunti| jautitamaparāyanaina pamādau niddiṭṭhau hauti| tamaina jautiparāyanaina appamādau niddiṭṭhau hauti| ayaṃ lakkhaṇau hārau|

tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? imamhi suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai sattā nīcakulinau, na tai imaṃ sutvā kusalai dhammai samādāya vattissanti| yai sattā uc‍cakulinau, tai imaṃ dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā  bhiyyausau mattāya kusalai dhammai samādāya vattissantīti| ayaṃ catubyūhau hārau| bhūmiyaṃ upadaisau|

tattha katamau āvaṭṭau hārau? yā avij‍jātau pabhūti taṇhā, ayaṃ samudayau| yau tamau tamaparāyanau, idaṃ dukkhaṃ| imāni dvai sac‍cāni dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca jauti yaina suttaina dhammaina pañ‍ñāpiyati, sau  dhammau pañ‍ñindriyassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| taina amauhaina tīṇi kusalamūlāni pāripūriṃ gacchanti saggassa padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamā vibhatti? tamau tamaparāyanauti na aikaṃsaina| kiṃ kāraṇaṃ? atthi tamau ca  bhavau aparāpariyavaidanīyaina ca kusalaina jautinā puggalaina sahaupattibhāvai| atthi jauti ca bhavau aparāpariyavaidanīyaina ca akusalaina tamaina puggalaina sahaupattibhāvai parivattanā tamaisu paṭipakkhauti jautinā tamaparāyanau|

tattha katamau vaivacanau? yau tamau, sau aivaṃ attabyāpādāya paṭipan‍nau, sau assaddhāya bālau akusalau abyattau anādīnavadassī| yau jauti, sau attahitāya paṭipan‍nau paṇḍitau kusalau byattau ādīnavadassī| ayaṃ vaivacanau|

tattha katamā pañ‍ñatti? sau puggalau vipākapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñāpiyati, akusalai pariyādin‍natā pañ‍ñāpiyati| jautikusaladhammupapattipañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñāpiyati kusaladhammavipākapañ‍ñattiyā cāti|

autaraṇauti yai avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā yañ‍ca jarāmaraṇaṃ yā ca avij‍jā, taṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ, niddaisaina vij‍juppādau avij‍jāniraudhau yau yāva jarāmaraṇaniraudhau, imai dvai dhammā saṅkhārakkhandhapariyāpan‍nā| dhammadhātu dhammāyatanañ‍ca padaṭṭhānaṃ niddaisaina dhātūsu|

tattha katamau saudhanau? imassa suttassa daisitassa ārambhau| adhiṭṭhānauti  tamauti bhagavā bravīti, na aikaṃ puggalaṃ daisaiti| yāvatā sattānaṃ gati, tattha yai duc‍caritadhammaina upapan‍nā, tai bahulādhivacanaina tamau niddisati| yā jauti sabbasattaisu kusaladhammaupapatti sabbaṃ taṃ jautīti abhilapati ayamaikatā pac‍cayau yaunisaumanasikārapañ‍ñatti catun‍naṃ mahābhūtānaṃ puggalānaṃ|

tattha  katamau parikkhārau? akusalassa pāpamittatā pac‍cayau, ayaunisau manasikārau haitu| kusalassa kalyāṇamittatā pac‍cayau, yaunisau manasikārau haitu|

tattha katamā samāraupanāti? idhaikac‍cau nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau hautīti nīcai kulai pac‍cājātau rūpaisu saddaisu gandhaisu rasaisu phassaisu, sau upapan‍nau sabbamhi mānussakai upabhaugaparibhaugai| jauti paṇītaisu kusalaisu upapan‍nau sabbamhi mānussakai upabhaugaparibhaugai upapan‍nauti|

105. tattha  katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ nibbaidhabhāgiyaṃ ca suttaṃ? na taṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrāti gāthā| kaina kāraṇaina taṃ bandhanaṃ daḷhaṃ? catūhi kāraṇaihi issariyaina sak‍kā maucaituṃ dhanaina vā añ‍ñaina vā yācanāya vā parāyanaina vā| yaisu ca ayaṃ rāgau maṇikuṇḍalaisu puttaisu dāraisu ca yā apaikkhā, idamassa caitasikabandhanaṃ| taṃ na sak‍kā issariyaina vā dhanaina vā añ‍ñaina vā yācanāya vā parāyanaina vā maucaituṃ| na ca tattha kauci atthi  pāṭibhaugau| iminā bandhanatau maucayitthāti daivau vā manussau vā tadidaṃ bandhanaṃ rāgānusayaina ca chasu bāhiraisu ca āyatanaisu bandhati| rūpaisu rūpataṇhā bandhati, yāva dhammaisu dhammataṇhā| yau idha laukai bandhau paralaukasmiṃ bandhau nīyati| sau bandhau jāyati, bandhau mīyati| bandhau asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ gacchati| na sak‍kā maucaituṃ añ‍ñatra ariyamaggaina imañ‍ca bandhanaṃ| maraṇabhāvañ‍ca upapattibhāvañ‍ca bhayatau viditvā chandarāgaṃ pajahati| sau imaṃ chandarāgaṃ pajahitvā atik‍kamati| ayañ‍ca laukau itau paraṃ dutiyau|

tattha yaṃ bandhanāsaṅkhārānaṃ pahānaṃ idaṃ vuc‍cati ubhayaisu ṭhānaisu vīriyaṃ, gandhaparivātau ganthaparivasau (pī॰) ganthaparivutau (ka॰) sumuni naupalimpati| tathaiva pariggahaisu puttaisu dāraisu ca avūḷhau sal‍lauti tassaiva taṇhāya pahānaṃ dassaiti| ayaṃ taṇhāmūlassa pahānā varai ahanāvarai (pī॰), ahanāvarau (ka॰) appamattauti kāmau pamādavattati pahānāya naikkhammābhiratau appamādavihārī bhavati| tassa āsayaṃ pahānāya naiva imaṃ laukaṃ āsīsati na paralaukaṃ| na idhalaukaṃ nissitaṃ, piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ ākaṅkhati| nāpi paralaukaṃ nissitaṃ piyarūpaṃ sātarūpaṃ  ākaṅkhati, taina vuc‍catai ‘‘nāsīsatai laukamimaṃ paraṃ laukañ‍cā’’ti| yaṃ tassa pahānaṃ taṃ chaidanaṃ aṭṭhakavaggiyaisu muni niddiṭṭhau| sau idha viraudhau aṭṭhakavaggiyaisu nāsīsanaṃ idha anāthā| tathāyaṃ taṇhāya tassa pariggahassa vatthukāmassa  aikagāthāya aitai sabbai kāmā dassitā| taina bhagavā daisaiti ‘‘aitampi chaitvāna paribbajanti anapaikkhinau sabbakāmai pahāyā’’ti| imissā gāthāya dvidhā niddaisau saṃsandananiddaisau ca samayaniddaisau ca, yathā ayaṃ gāthā saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca, aivaṃ tāya gāthāya saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca visaj‍janā| aivaṃ gāthā sabbagāthāsu byākaraṇaisu vā niddiṭṭhaṃ suttaṃ|

106. tattha katamā daisanā? imaṃ suttaṃ kainādhippāyaina daisitaṃ| yai rāgacaritā sattā, tai kāmai pajahissantīti ayaṃ tattha bhagavatau adhippāyau|

tattha katamau vicayau? yassa dasavatthukā kilaisā uttiṇṇā vantā viditā| katamai dasavidhāti, kilaisakāmā ca aurambhāgiyauddhambhāgiyā ca saṃyaujanā dasavatthukāni āyatanāni, ayaṃ vicayau|

tattha katamā yutti? yai sārattā tai gāḷhabandhanaina bandhanti atthi aisā yutti|

tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau? sārattau maṇikuṇḍalaisu mamaṃkārassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| apaikkhāti atītavatthussa sarāgassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| aitampi chaitvāti bhāvanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau? sārattacittau maṇikuṇḍalaisu yau ahaṃkārai visattau mamaṃkārai visattau, yau puttadārai sārattau| khaittavatthusmiṃ sārattau| ayaṃ  lakkhaṇau hārau|

tattha katamau catubyūhau hārau? idha suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau| yai nibbānaina chandikā bhavissanti, tai puttadārai taṇhaṃ pajahissanti| ayaṃ tattha bhagavatau adhippāyau| imāni cattāri sac‍cāni|

tattha katamau āvaṭṭau? yā puttadārai taṇhā, ayaṃ samudayau| yai upādin‍nakkhandhā, tai yai ca bāhiraisu rūpaisu rūpapariggahau, idaṃ dukkhaṃ, yaṃ  tattha chaidanīyaṃ, ayaṃ niraudhau| yaina bhij‍jati, ayaṃ maggau| vibhattīti natthi vibhattiyā bhūmi, parivattanauti paṭipakkhau niddiṭṭhau|

tattha katamau vaivacanau? niddiṭṭhau vaivacanau| tattha katamau autaraṇau? atthi taṇhā aikau sattau autiṇṇau tappac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ yāva jarāmaraṇaṃ| yā tattha vaidanā, ayaṃ avij‍jā vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau yāva jarāmaraṇaniraudhau|

tattha katamau saudhanau? suddhau gāthāya ārambhau| tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau? na taṃ daḷhaṃ bandhanamāhu dhīrāti aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā, na vaimattatāya| cattārau rāgā kāmarāgau rūparāgau bhavarāgau diṭṭhirāgau cāti aikattatāya pañ‍ñattā|

tattha katamau parikkhārau? yaisaṃ rāgau maṇikuṇḍalaisu tassa subhasañ‍ñā haitu, anubyañ‍janasau  ca nimittaggāhitā pac‍cayau| yāya tai chin‍nāni tassa asubhasañ‍ñā haitu, nimittaggahaṇaanubyañ‍janaggahaṇavinaudanaṃ pac‍cayau|

tattha  katamau samāraupanau? sārattau maṇikuṇḍalaisu sammūḷhavidhau duṭṭhātipi aitampi aivampi (pī॰ ka॰) chaitvāna paribbajantīti taṃ pariñ‍ñātatthaṃ parivaj‍jitatthaṃ pajahitā, ayaṃ samāraupanau|

107. yaṃ caitasikaṃ yaṃ pakappitaṃ vitthāraina pac‍cayau, yaṃ vā caitasikaṃ kāyikaṃ caitasikaṃ kammaṃ| kiṃkāraṇā? caitasikā hi caitanā manaukammāti vuc‍catai, sā caitanākammaṃ, yaṃ caitasikaṃ imaṃ kāyikañ‍ca vācasikañ‍ca imāni tīṇi kammāni niddiṭṭhāni| kāyakammaṃ vacīkammañ‍ca tāni kusalāni piyaṃ kāyaina ca vācāya ca ārabhati parāmasati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati sīlabbataparāmāsau| saṅkappanā tai tividhā saṅkhārā puñ‍ñamayā apuñ‍ñamayā ānaiñ‍jamayā, tappac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tai ārammaṇamaitaṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā| yā subhasañ‍ñā sukhasañ‍ñā attasañ‍ñā ca| idaṃ caitasikaṃ| yaṃ rūpūpagaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhati rūpārammaṇaṃ rūpapatiṭṭhitaṃ nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍lataṃ gacchati, ayaṃ saṅkappanā, iti yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīsu  ṭhitaṃ paṭhamābhinibbattiārammaṇavasaina upādānaṃ, idaṃ vuc‍cati caitasikanti|

tattha ṭhitassa arūpassa yā nikanti ajjhausānaṃ, idampi sakampitaṃ manāpikaisu rūpaisu piyarūpasātarūpaisu ābhaugau, idaṃ caitasikaṃ| yaṃ caitaiti sattaisu sattasu (pī॰) manāpikaisu abhijjhākāyaganthau paṭighānusayaisu byāpādakāyaganthau sabbai cattārau ganthā, ayaṃ  pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu paṭhamābhinipātau cittassa yā caitanā yassa tattha assādānupassissa anaikā pāpakā akusalā dhammā cittaṃ arūpavatiyau haunti| puggalau rāgānubandhibhūtau taihi kilaisakāmaihi yathā kāmakaraṇīyau, ayaṃ vuc‍catai kāmaisu pakappanā| aivaṃ sabbai cattārau aughā| yaṃ taihi kāmaihi saṃyuttau viharati bhāvitau ajjhausan‍nau, ayaṃ caitanā| yassa tathāyaṃ avītarāgassa adhigatapaimassa tassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā dukkhānuparivattitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti saritassa vayadhammasamuppādau cittaṃ pariyādiyati, idaṃ vuc‍cati pakappitanti|

aikamaikassa caitaiti ca pakappaiti ca viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhiti yā hauti, sā ca ṭhiti dvidhā ārammaṇaṭṭhiti ca āhāraṭṭhiti ca| tattha yā ārammaṇaṭṭhiti, ayaṃ nāmarūpassa pac‍cayau| yā āhāraṭṭhiti yā punabbhavābhinibbattikā ṭhiti yā ca paunaubhavikā ṭhiti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ārammaṇaṃ| taṃ hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā tassa viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ yāva jarāmaraṇañ‍ca caitaiti, atha ca puna patthayatai yatau na paunaubhavikā anāgatavatthumhi, ayaṃ paṭipakkhau niddiṭṭhau| na caitaiti na patthayati atha ca dūsaitīti duvidhau niddaisau| assa pubbai hauti taṃ caitasikaṃ taṃ pakappitaṃ asamūhataṃ tappac‍cayā, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhiti hauti|

108. atha  vā tassa anusayā āvibhavanti tappac‍cayā tassa punabbhavau nibbattati| atha vā naṃ saṃkiyatai appaitu āgārai vā, sukhumā vā santi vā na saṃkiyatai kāmai taṃ aivaṃ nic‍caisupi āgāraisu jātau hauti| taṃ nayati yaṃ nau kappaituṃ aivaṃ saṅkhārā caititā pakappitā ca ārammaṇabhūtā  haunti, yā ca caitanā yā ca pakappanā yañ‍ca vatthu nibbattaṃ, ubhaupi aitai ārammaṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa tathā caitanāya ca saṅkappanāya ca patthanāya ca bhūtā sattā caitaiti ca saṅkappaiti ca| yaṃ gavaisanā na ca caitaiti na ca saṅkappaiti| katamai ca sattā bhūtā? yai ca tanujātaaṇḍajāpi aṇḍakā anubhin‍nā saṃsaidajā na ca sambhin‍nā imai bhūtā| katamai sambhavaisinau gabbhagatā aṇḍagatā saṃsarantau imai na caitaiti na patthaiti na ca saṅkappaiti| anusayai na ca punabbhavau nibbattīti? yai bhūtā sattā yai sambhavaisinau, tai thāvarā| yai vā satau caitainti patthainti ca yai thāvarā| tai na ca caitainti, na ca patthainti, na ca saṅkappainti, anusayaina ca saṃsaranti|

aparau pariyāyau| yai ariyapuggalā saikkhā, tattha tai na ca caitainti, na ca saṅkappainti, anusayaina puna uppaj‍janti|

aparau pariyāyau| sukhumā pāṇā bhūmigatā udakagatā cakkhunau āpāthaṃ nāgacchanti, tai na ca caitainti, na ca saṅkappainti, anusayaina ca saṃsaranti|

aparau pariyāyau| bāhikā sabbai bhikkhū abhimānikā, tai na ca caitainti, na ca patthayanti, anusayaina ca saṃsaranti, na ca caitainti, na ca saṅkappainti, na ca anusainti| ārammaṇampaitaṃ  na hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā|

na ca caitaitīti pariyuṭṭhānasamugghātaṃ dassaiti| na ca anusaitīti anusayasamugghātaṃ dassaiti| na ca caitaitīti auḷārikānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānaṃ dassaiti| na ca anusaitīti sukhumānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānaṃ dassaiti| na ca caitaitīti yaina bhūmi ca na ca patthayantīti sakadāgāmī anāgāmī, na ca anusaitīti arahaṃ, na ca caitaitīti sīlakkhandhassa paṭipakkhaina pahānaṃ dassaiti, na ca patthayatīti samādhikkhandhassa paṭipakkhaina pahānaṃ dassaiti, na ca anusayatīti pañ‍ñākkhandhassa paṭipakkhaina pahānaṃ dassaiti, na ca caitaitīti apuñ‍ñamayānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ pahānaṃ dassaiti, na ca patthayatīti puñ‍ñamayānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ pahānaṃ dassaiti, na ca anusaitīti ānaiñ‍jamayānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ pahānaṃ dassaiti, na ca caitaitīti anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ, na ca patthayatīti añ‍ñindriyaṃ, na ca anusayatīti añ‍ñātāvinau indriyaṃ| na ca caitaitīti mudukā indriyabhāvanā, na ca patthayatīti  majjhaindriyabhāvanā, na ca anusaitīti adhimattā indriyabhāvanā| ayaṃ suttatthau|

109. tattha katamā daisanā? idha suttai cattāri sac‍cāni daisitāni| yañ‍ca caitayitaṃ yañ‍ca pakappitaṃ atthi aitaṃ ārammaṇaṃ cittaṃ patiṭṭhati vicinati vicinayati (pī॰ ka॰) yuj‍jati| na ca caitaitīti na ca patthayatīti atthi aivaṃ ārammaṇaṃ anusayai viñ‍ñāṇamiti viciniyati yuj‍jati na ca caitaiti na ca patthayati| anusayappahānā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiṃ na gavaisanti, viciyantaṃ yuj‍jati| ayaṃ yuttivicayau|

tattha katamau padaṭṭhānau? caitanā  pariyuṭṭhānaṃ caitanāpariyuṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṅkappanaṃ upādānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| anusayau pariyuṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| taisaṃ chandarāgavināsāya bhāvanā bhavarāgassa pahānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau? yaṃ caitasikanti vaidayitaṃ pakappitaṃ uggahitaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ tabbiñ‍ñāṇaṃ ārammaṇampi pac‍cayaupi|

tattha katamau catubyūhau? idha suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai punabbhavaṃ na icchanti, tai na caitayissanti na ca patthayissantīti, ayaṃ adhippāyau|

āvaṭṭauti yā ca caitanā patthanā ca anusayau ca viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitipahānā ca, imāni dvai sac‍cāni| vibhattīti natthi vibhattiyā bhūmi| parivattanā pana paṭipakkhaṃ suttaṃ|

tattha  katamau vaivacanau? caitanā rūpasañ‍caitanā yāvadhammasañ‍caitanā| yau anusayau, tai satta anusayā|

pañ‍ñattīti caitanāpariyuṭṭhānaṃ pañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā| saṅkappanaṃ upādānapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| anusayau haitupañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattau| viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti upapattihaitupañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā| caitanā saṅkappanā anusayau samucchaidau chandarāgavinayapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattau| paṭhamai kaici dvīhi parivattakaihi paṭic‍casamuppādau idappac‍cayatāya majjhapañ‍ñatti|

autaraṇauti  dvīhi parivattakaihi dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca majjhimakaihi maggau ca niraudhau ca| saudhanauti suttai suttassa ārambhau|

adhiṭṭhānauti  yañ‍caitayitaṃ sabbaṃ adhiṭṭhānaina aikattāya pañ‍ñattaṃ| saṅkappitanti upādānaikattāya pañ‍ñattaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aikattāya pañ‍ñattaṃ|

parikkhārauti subhañ‍ca ārammaṇaṃ ayaunisau manasikārau caitanā haitupac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| viñ‍ñāṇassa patiṭṭhānau dhammau ārammaṇapac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau| tassa manasikārau haitupac‍cayatāya pac‍cayau|

tattha katamau samāraupanau? idaṃ suttaṃ sañ‍ñitaṃ tattha caitaiti visaj‍janā iti niddisitabbā| tassa diṭṭhiyā viñ‍ñāṇapac‍cayā nāmarūpaṃ yāva jarāmaraṇaṃ, ayaṃ samāraupanau| ārammaṇamaitaṃ na hauti viñ‍ñāṇassa ṭhitiyā, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhā nāmarūpaniraudhau, nāmarūpaniraudhā yāva jarāmaraṇaniraudhau|

110. tattha katamaṃ saṃkilaisabhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca asaikkhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ? ayaṃ laukau passa udā॰ 30 udānai santāpajātau yāva yai hi kaici samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā bhavaina bhavassa vippamaukkhamāhaṃsu| saṃkilaisabhāgiyaṃ upadhiṃ hi paṭic‍ca dukkhamidaṃ sambhauti, yā tā pana taṇhā pahīyanti, bhavaṃ nābhinandatīti nibbaidhassa nibbutassa nic‍cutassa (pī॰ ka॰) bhikkhunau anupādāya punabbhavau na hauti| upac‍cagā sabbabhavāni tādīti asaikkhabhāgiyaṃ|

tattha santāpajātauti rāgajau santāpau dausajau mauhajauti| taisaṃ sattānaṃ ṭhānaṃ dassaiti| laukau santāpajātauti phassau tividhau sukhavaidanīyau dukkhavaidanīyau adukkhamasukhavaidanīyau| tattha sukhavaidanīyau phassau rāgasantāpau, dukkhavaidanīyau dausasantāpau, adukkhamasukhavaidanīyau mauhasantāpau| yathā  ca bhagavā āha paṭhamakassa valāhakassa gaumaggai kaumaggai (pī॰ ka॰) passa a॰ ni॰ 3.35 yaihi gahapatiputta rāgajaihi dausajaihi mauhajaihi santāpaihi dukkhaṃ supati, tai mama santāpā na santi|

raugaṃ  vadati attatauti taihi santāpaihi santāpitau tividhaṃ vipal‍lāsaṃ paṭilabhati sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsaṃ cittavipal‍lāsaṃ diṭṭhivipal‍lāsaṃ| tattha asubhai subhanti sañ‍ñāvipal‍lāsau| dukkhai sukhanti cittavipal‍lāsau| anic‍cai nic‍canti anattani attāti diṭṭhivipal‍lāsau|

yathā cittassa vipal‍lāsau sañ‍ñādiṭṭhitai tividhā vitak‍kā – cittavitak‍kau vipal‍lāsau sañ‍ñāvitak‍kau vipal‍lāsau diṭṭhivitak‍kau vipal‍lāsaupi| tattha avij‍jā vipal‍lāsau gaucarā gatipataiyyabhūmi, yathā hi taṃ sañ‍jānāti yathā vijānāti yathā sañ‍jānāti ca vijānāti ca| yathā khanti caitaiti imai cattārau vipal‍lāsā sattā yaihi catubbidhaṃ attabhāvavatthuṃ raugabhūtaṃ gaṇḍabhūtaṃ ‘‘attā’’ti vadanti| raugaṃ vadati attatauti ayaṃ āvaṭṭau| yaina yaina hi mañ‍ñati tatau taṃ hauti añ‍ñathāti subhanti mañ‍ñati na tathā hauti| aivaṃ sukhanti nic‍caṃ attāti sau añ‍ñathā bhavamaiva santaṃ anāgataṃ bhavaṃ patthayati, taina vuc‍cati ‘‘bhavarāgau’’ti| bhavamaivābhinandati, yaṃ abhinandati, taṃ dukkhanti pañ‍cakkhandhai niddisiyati| yañ‍ca tappac‍cayā saukaparidaivadukkhaṃ tassa hi bhāvaissati| aittāvatā saṃkilaisau hauti| pahānatthaṃ khau pana brahmacariyaṃ  vussati| tiṇṇaṃ santāpānaṃ chandarāgavinayau hauti|

upadhiṃ hi paṭic‍ca dukkhamidaṃ bhavatīti yai bhavamaivābhinandanti yassa bhāvaissati, taṃ dukkhaṃ tassa dukkhassa pahānamāha| sabbasau upādānañ‍ca yaṃ natthi dukkhassa sambhavauti cattārau vipal‍lāsā yathā niddiṭṭhaupādānamāha| tassa paṭhamau vipal‍lāsau kāmupādānaṃ, dutiyaṃ diṭṭhupādānaṃ, tatiyaṃ sīlabbatupādānaṃ, catutthaṃ attavādupādānaṃ, taisaṃ yau khayau natthi dukkhassa sambhavau upadhi nidānaṃ dukkhaniraudhamāha| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau vibhavataṇhā na hauti| vibhavaṃ nābhinandatīti dassanabhūmiṃ mantaiti sabbasau taṇhakkhayaṃ nibbānanti dvai vimuttiyau kathaiti rāgavirāgañ‍ca avij‍jāvirāgañ‍ca| tassa bhikkhunauti anupādisaisanibbānadhātuṃ mantaiti| ayaṃ suttassa atthaniddaisau|

111. tattha  katamau vicayau? yassa yattha pariḷāhaiti tassa pariḍayhantassa sau yathābhūtaṃ natthi nibbindati ca, ayaṃ vicayau ca yutti ca| padaṭṭhānau  rāgajau pariḷāhau sukhindriyassa daumanassindriyassa ca padaṭṭhānaṃ| dausajau pariḷāhau sukhindriyassa daumanassindriyassa ca padaṭṭhānaṃ| mauhajau pariḷāhau upaikkhindriyassa daumanassindriyassa ca padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau hārau? phassaparaitau vaidanāparaitau sañ‍ñāparaitaupi saṅkhāraparaitaupi yaina yaina mañ‍ñati yadi subhanimittaina yadi sukhanimittaina yadi nic‍canimittaina yadi attanimittaina asubhai subhanti mañ‍ñati, aivaṃ sabbaṃ rāgajai pariḷāhai vuttai cattārau pariḷāhā vuttā bhavanti| rāgajau dausajau mauhajau diṭṭhijau  ca rāgaṃ vadāmīti attatau vadati| sabbāni pan‍narasa padāni anic‍caṃ dukkhanti|

tattha katamau catubyūhau? idha suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai pariḷāhaina na acchanti tai bhavaṃ nābhinandanti| yai bhavaṃ nābhinandanti, tai parinibbāyissanti| ayaṃ adhippāyau|

tattha katamau āvaṭṭau? saṃkilaisabhāgiyaina dukkhañ‍ca samudayañ‍ca niddisati| nibbaidhabhāgiyaina maggañ‍ca niraudhañ‍ca|

tattha katamā vibhatti? santāpajātau raugajātau raugaṃ vadati attatau taṃ na aikaṃsaina hauti amanasikārā santāpajātau khau na ca raugaṃ attatau vadati|

tattha katamau parivattanau? pakkhapaṭipakkhanidassanatthaṃ bhūmi parivattanāya|

tattha katamau vaivacanau hārau? raugañ‍ca attatau vadati sal‍laṃ attatau vadati| pan‍narasa padāni sabbāni vattabbāni|

tattha katamā pañ‍ñatti? santāpajātauti daumanassapadaṭṭhānaṃ| sabbai vacanapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñapaiti| raugaṃ vadati attatau vipal‍lāsau saṃkilaisapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñapaiti| yaṃ nābhinandati, taṃ  dukkhanti vipal‍lāsanikkhaipapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā| tai akatasattā laukā majjhaina vaimattatāya pañ‍ñattā|

tattha katamau autaraṇau? santāpajātauti tīṇi akusalamūlāni, tai saṅkhārā saṅkhārakkhandhapariyāpan‍nā, dhātūsu dhammadhātu, āyatanaisu  dhammāyatanaṃ| indriyaisu itthindriyaṃ purisindriyañ‍ca padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha  katamau saudhanau? suddhau suttassa ārambhau|

tattha katamau adhiṭṭhānau hārau? pariḷāhauti yai sattā laukā aikattapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā, tai akatasattā laukā majjhaina vaimattatāya pañ‍ñattā|

tattha katamau parikkhārau? santāpajātauti ayaunisau manasikārau haitu, vipal‍lāsañ‍ca pac‍cayau| tattha dvīhi dhammaihi attā abhiniviṭṭhā cittañ‍ca caitasikañ‍ca dhammai ubhayāni tassa viparītaina parāmasatau| aparau pariyāyau, caitasikaihi dhammaihi attasañ‍ñā anattasañ‍ñā samugghātaiti| aparau pariyāyau| anic‍casañ‍ñā caitasikaisu dhammaisu, na tu attasañ‍ñā| idaṃ vuc‍cati cittanti vā manauti vā viñ‍ñāṇanti vā idaṃ dīgharattaṃ abbhuggataṃ aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti| tattha caitasikā dhammānupassanā aisāpi dhammasañ‍ñā| tassa kau haitu, kau pac‍cayau? ahaṃkārau haitu, mamaṃkārau pac‍cayau|

tattha katamau samāraupanau? ayaṃ laukau santāpajātauti akusalaṃ mantaiti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nāmarūpassa pac‍cayau yāva jarāmaraṇanti, ayaṃ samāraupanau|

112. aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ, sammappañ‍ñāya passati akusalamūlānaṃ pahānaṃ| tattha avij‍jāniraudhau avij‍jāniraudhā yāva jarāmaraṇaniraudhau, ayaṃ samāraupanau|

cattārau  puggalā passa a॰ ni॰ 4.5 – anusautagāmī paṭisautagāmī ṭhitattau, tiṇṇau pāraṅgatau thalai tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇauti|

tattha yau anusautagāmī ayaṃ kāmai saivati| pāpañ‍ca kammaṃ karauti yāva kāmai paṭisaivati| idaṃ laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, sau yaiva taṇhā, sau taihi kāmaihi vuyhati anusautagāmīti vuc‍cati| yau puggalau tāhi gamitau tappac‍cayā tassa haitu akusalakammaṃ karauti kāyaina ca vācāya ca, ayaṃ vuc‍cati pāpakammaṃ karautīti| tassa tīṇi sautāni sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi vicikicchā sīlabbataparāmāsau| imaihi tīhi sautaihi  tividhadhātuyaṃ uppaj‍jati kāmadhātuyaṃ rūpadhātuyaṃ arūpadhātuyaṃ| taina paṭipakkhaina yau kāmai na paṭisaivati| yau sīlavataṃ na parāmasati| yau sak‍kāyadiṭṭhīnaṃ pahānāya kāmaisu yathābhūtaṃ ādīnavaṃ passati| yaina ca tai dhammai paṭisaivati| yañ‍ca tappac‍cayā tiṭṭhati brāhmaṇauti arahaṃ kira| tattha arahaṃ tassa pāraṅgatau hauti, pāraṅgatassa thalai tiṭṭhati saupādisaisā nibbānadhātu| anusautagāminīti dassanappahātabbānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ appahānamāha| paṭisautagāminīti phalai diṭṭhaikaṭṭhānañ‍ca kilaisānaṃ pahānamāha, ṭhitattaina pañ‍can‍naṃ aurambhāgiyānaṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ pahānamāha| tattha anusautagāminā maggarūpimāha| paṭisautagāminā ṭhitattaina ca maggamitimāha| pāraṅgataina sāvakā asaikkhā  ca sammāsambuddhā ca vuttā| anusautagāminā sak‍kāyasamudayagāminiṃ paṭipadamāha| paṭisautagāminā ṭhitattaina sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadamāha| pāraṅgataina dasa asaikkhā arahantā dhammā vuttā| ayaṃ suttatthau|

113. tattha katamā daisanā? imasmiṃ hi suttai cattāri ariyasac‍cāni daisitāni| taidhātukalaukasamatik‍kamanañ‍ca|

tattha katamau vicayau hārau? yau kāmai paṭisaivati pāpaṃ pāpakaṃ (pī॰) karaiyyāti yau ca kāmai na paṭisaivati sau pāpakammaṃ na karaiyyāti yau ca imaihi dvīhi bhūmīhi uttiṇṇau pāraṅgatauti yā vīmaṃsā ayaṃ vicayau|

yuttīti yuj‍jati suttaisu, nāyuj‍jatīti yā vīmaṃsāya, ayaṃ yutti| padaṭṭhānauti anusautagāminā sattan‍naṃ saṃyaujanānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| akusalassa kiriyā akusalassa mūlānaṃ padaṭṭhānaṃ| paṭisautagāminā yathābhūtadassanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ṭhitattaina asaṃhāriyāya asahāriyāya (pī॰) padaṭṭhānaṃ| pāraṅgatauti kadāci bhūmiyā padaṭṭhānaṃ|

tattha katamau lakkhaṇau hārau? yau anusautaṃ gacchati taṇhāvasaina| sabbaisampi kilaisānaṃ vasaina gacchati| yau paṭisautaṃ vāyamati| taṇhāya sabbaisampi sau kilaisānaṃ vāyamati  paṭisautaṃ| yau  attanā ṭhitau kāyainapi sau ṭhitau vācācittainapi sau ṭhitau| ayaṃ lakkhaṇau hārau|

tattha katamau catubyūhau? idha suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai anusautagāminiyā paṭipadāya nābhiramissanti, tai paṭisautaṃ vāyamissantīti yāva kadāci bhūmiyaṃ, ayaṃ adhippāyau| āvaṭṭauti  idha suttai cattāri suttāni daisitāni|

tattha katamau vibhatti hārau? yau kāmai paṭisaivati pāpañ‍ca kammaṃ karauti| sau anusautagāmīti na aikaṃsaina sautāpan‍naupi kāmai paṭisaivati| taṃ bhāgiyañ‍ca pāpakammaṃ karauti| kiñ‍cāpi saikkhaupi karaiyya pāpaṃ yathā suttai niddiṭṭhau na ca sau anusautagāmī, idaṃ vibhaj‍jabyākaraṇīyaṃ| na ca kāmai paṭisaivati na ca pāpakammaṃ karauti paṭisautagāmī na ca aikaṃsaina sabbai bāhirakau kāmaisu vītarāgau na ca kāmai paṭisaivati, taina ca pāpakammaṃ karauti anusautagāmī paṭisautagāmī, ayaṃ vibhatti|

tattha katamau parivattanau hārau? niddiṭṭhau paṭipakkhau| vaivacanauti kāmaisu vatthukāmāpi kilaisakāmāpi rūpasaddagandharasaphassaputtadāradāsakammakarapaurisañ‍ca pariggahā|

pañ‍ñattīti sabbai puthuj‍janā aikattāya pañ‍ñattā| anusautagāmīti kilaisasamudācārapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā| yai pana saikkhā puggalā, tai nibbānapañ‍ñattiyā niṭṭhānapañ‍ñattiyā (ka॰) pañ‍ñattā| yai pana anāgāmī, tai asaṃhāriya pañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā, ayaṃ pañ‍ñatti|

autaraṇauti yau anusautagāmī, sau dukkhaṃ| yai tassa dhammā, tai dukkhassa samudayau| yaṃ rūpaṃ, ayaṃ rūpakkhandhau, aivaṃ pañ‍capi khandhā paṭic‍casamuppādau, tai kilaisā saṅkhārakkhandhapariyāpan‍nā dhammāyatanaṃ dhammadhātu indriyaisu ca pañ‍ñattā|

saudhanauti yainārambhaina idaṃ suttaṃ daisitaṃ, sau ārambhau sabbau suddhau|

adhiṭṭhānauti  paṭisautagāminā sabbai sautāpan‍nā aikattaina vā niddiṭṭhā rāgānusayapaṭisautagāminau saikkhāva maggau ca saikkhau ca puggalau ṭhitattauti|

vītarāgau  aikattāya pañ‍ñattau| pāraṅgatauti sabbai arahantau sabbai pac‍caikabuddhā sammāsambuddhā ca aikattāya pañ‍ñattā|

parikkhārauti anusautagāminau pāpamittapac‍cayau kāmapariyuṭṭhānaṃ haitu| paṭisautagāminau dvai haitū dvai pac‍cayā ca yāva sammādiṭṭhiyā uppādāyadiṭṭhi upādāyadiṭṭhi (pī॰), tassa paṭiladdhamaggau haitu ārambhau pac‍cayau kāyikau caitasikassa kauṭṭhāsau ca| samāraupanauti vibhatti idaṃ suttaṃ natthi samāraupanāya bhūmi|

114. pañ‍cānisaṃsā sautānugatānaṃ dhammānaṃ passa a॰ ni॰ 4.191 yāva diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhānaṃ suttaṃ vitthāraina kātabbaṃ| yuñ‍jatau ghaṭaintassa vāyamatau gilānau maraṇakālai daivabhūtau pac‍caikabaudhiṃ pāpuṇāti| sautānugatāti saddhammassavanaina kataṃ hauti| na ca adhipañ‍ñādhammavipassanāya tassa cittaṃ tasitaṃ hauti, na ca anibbiddhattaṃ, idaṃ ca suttaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ puggalānaṃ daisitaṃ, saddhānusārinau mudindriyassa tikkhindriyassa ca dhammānusārinau tikkhindriyassa mudindriyassa ca| yau pana mauhacaritau puggalau na sak‍kauti yuñ‍jituṃ ghaṭituṃ vāyamituṃ yathābhūtaṃ yathāsamādhikā vimutti taṃ khaṇaṃ taṃ layaṃ taṃ muhuttaṃ phalaṃ dassaiti| sādhu parihāyati parau taṃ duyhati, nau tu sukhaavipākinī bhavati| tassa diṭṭhai yaiva ca dhammai upapaj‍jaaparāpariyavaidanīyaṃ| tattha yau puggalau dhammānusārī tassa yadi sautānugatā dhammā haunti sau yuñ‍jantau pāpuṇāti| yau dhammānusārī mudindriyau, sau gilānau pāpuṇāti| yau saddhānusārī tikkhindriyau, sau maraṇakālasamayai pāpuṇāti| yau mudindriyau, sau daivabhūtau pāpuṇāti| yadā daivabhūtau na pāpuṇāti, na sau tainaiva dhammarāgaina tāya dhammanandiyā pac‍caikabaudhiṃ pāpuṇāti| yau sautānugataisu yuñ‍jati ghaṭaiti vāyamati, sau pubbāpan‍naina visaisaṃ sañ‍jānāti, sañ‍jānantau pāpuṇāti| sacai pana gilānassa manasikārau hauti, tattha yuñ‍jantau pāpuṇāti| sacai panassa maraṇakālai saṃviggau hauti, tattha  yuñ‍jantau pāpuṇāti| sacai pana na katthaci kattha (pī॰ ka॰), tattha (ka॰) saṃvaigau hauti, tassa daivabhūtassa sukhinau dhammabhūtā pādā aivaṃ avilapati| sau aivaṃ jānāti ‘‘ayaṃ sau dhammavinayau yattha mayaṃ pubbai manussabhūtā brahmacariyaṃ carimhā’’ti| atha daivabhūtau pāpuṇāti| dibbaisu vā pañ‍casu kāmaguṇaisu ajjhausitau hauti pamādavihārī, sau taina kusalamūlaina pac‍caikabaudhiṃ pāpuṇāti|

yā parataughausaina vacasā suparicitā, ayaṃ sutamayī pañ‍ñā| yai pana dhammā haunti manasā anupaikkhitā, ayaṃ cintāmayī pañ‍ñā| yaṃ diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā, ayaṃ bhāvanāmayī pañ‍ñā| yaṃ sautānugatā vacasā paricitā haunti, sau ca diṭṭhai yaiva dhammai parinibbāyī, ayaṃ arahaṃ puggalau| yau upapaj‍jati daivabhūtau pāpuṇāti, tattha ca parinibbāyati, ayaṃ anāgāmī| yau taina kusalamūlaina pac‍caikabaudhiṃ pāpuṇāti, ayaṃ pubbayaugasambhārasambhūtau puggalau|

sautānugatā  dhammāti paṭhamaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ, vacasā paricitāti dutiyaṃ tatiyañ‍ca vimuttāyatanaṃ, manasā anupaikkhitāti catutthaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhāti pañ‍camaṃ vimuttāyatanaṃ|

sautānugatāya vimuttiyā vacasā yā vācā suppaṭividdhā anupubbadhammassa sautaina sutvā sīlakkhandhai paripūraiti, manasā anupaikkhitā samādhikkhandhaṃ paripūraiti, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhā pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ paripūraiti|

sautānugatā dhammā bahussutā hauntīti vitthāraina kātabbaṃ| idaṃ paṭhamaṃ saddhāpadānaṃ manasā anupaikkhitāti paṭisal‍lānabahulau viharati, vitthāraina kātabbaṃ| idaṃ dutiyaṃ saddhāpadānaṃ diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhāti anāsavā caitauvimuttiyā nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātīti| idaṃ tatiyaṃ saddhāpadānaṃ|

sautānugatā dhammāti saikkhaṃ satthā dassaiti| manasā anupaikkhitāti arahattaṃ satthā dassaiti| diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhāti tathāgataṃ arahantaṃ sammāsambuddhaṃ satthā dassaiti|

sautānugatā  dhammāti kāmānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ dassaiti| manasā anupaikkhitāti rūpadhātuyā nissaraṇaṃ dassaiti| diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhāti taidhātukānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ dassaiti| ayaṃ suttatthau|

115. tattha katamau daisanāhārau? imamhi suttai tayau aisanā daisitā sautānugataihi dhammaihi vacasā paricitaihi kāmaisanāya samathamaggau| diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhaihi brahmacariyaisanāya samathamaggau|

vicayauti yathā suttaṃ manasikarauntau vicinantau sutamayipañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| yathā ca sau  manasikarautīti yathā sutadhammā tadā cintāmayipañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| yathā diṭṭhaiva  dhammai manasikarauti tadā bhāvanāmayipañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| ayaṃ vicayau|

sutaina sutamayipañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| cintāya cintāmayipañ‍ñaṃ bhāvanāya bhāvanāmayipañ‍ñaṃ paṭilabhati| atthi aisā yutti|

padaṭṭhānauti sautānugatā dhammāti dhammassavanassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| vacasā paricitāti yuñ‍janāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| manasā anupaikkhitāti dhammānudhammāya vipassanāya padaṭṭhānaṃ| diṭṭhiyā anupaikkhitāti pañ‍ñāyapi anupaikkhitā diṭṭhiyāpi anupaikkhitā|

catubyūhauti imamhi suttai bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai imāhi dvīhi pañ‍ñāhi saman‍nāgatā taihi…|

sa nibbutauti maggaphalaṃ anupādisaisañ‍ca nibbānadhātuṃ mantaiti, dānaina auḷārikānaṃ kilaisānaṃ pahānaṃ mantaiti| sīlaina majjhimānaṃ, pañ‍ñāya sukhumakilaisānaṃ mantaiti, rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti katā ca bhūmi|

dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyati|

kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti maggau vuttau|

rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti maggaphalamāha||

dadatau  puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatauti tīhi padaihi laukikaṃ kusalamūlaṃ vuttaṃ| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti laukuttaraṃ kusalamūlaṃ vuttaṃ|

dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ mantaiti| kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti saikkhabhūmiṃ mantaiti| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti asaikkhabhūmi vuttā|

dadatau  puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti magganiyā paṭipadā vuttā| kusalau  ca jahāti pāpakanti saikkhavimutti| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti asaikkhavimutti|

dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti dānakathaṃ sīlakathaṃ maggakathaṃ laukikānaṃ dhammānaṃ daisanamāha| kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti laukai ādīnavānupassanā| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti sāmuk‍kaṃsikāya dhammadaisanāyapi paṭividdhā|

dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti pāṇānaṃ abhayadānaina pāṇātipātā vairamaṇisattānaṃ abhayaṃ daiti| aivaṃ sabbāni sikkhāpadāni kātabbāni| saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti sīlai patiṭṭhāya cittaṃ saṃyamaiti, tassa saṃyamatau pāripūriṃ gacchati| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti dvai vimuttiyau| ayaṃ suttaniddaisau|

116. tattha katamā daisanā? imamhi suttai kiṃ daisitaṃ? dvai sugatiyau daivā ca manussā ca, dibbā ca pañ‍cakāmaguṇā, mānussakā ca| dvīhi padaihi niddaisau| dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyati, kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti maggau vuttau| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti dvai nibbānadhātuyau daisitā saupādisaisā ca anupādisaisā ca| ayaṃ daisanā|

vicayauti dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti iminā paṭhamaina padaina dānamayikapuñ‍ñakiriyavatthu vuttaṃ| tainassa ānantariyānaṃ kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ| dutiyaina padaina… yanti, niyyānikaṃ sāsananti, ayaṃ adhippāyau| assavanaina  ca amanasikāraina ca appaṭivaidhaina ca sak‍kāyasamudayagāminī paṭipadā vuttā| savanaina  ca manasikāraina ca paṭivaidhaina ca sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā vuttā| ayaṃ āvaṭṭau|

vibhattīti aikaṃsabyākaraṇīyau| natthi tattha vibhattiyā bhūmi| parivattanāti yai pañ‍cānisaṃsā, tai pañ‍cādinā paṭipakkhaina tainaiva diṭṭhaiva dhammai pāpuṇāti, taṃ upapaj‍jamānā aparau pariyāyau|

vaivacananti sautānugatā dhammāti yaṃ suttaṃ diṭṭhampi pañ‍ñindriyaṃ viñ‍ñattampi diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhampi vibhāvitampi|

pañ‍ñattīti  sautānugatādhammāti daisanā avij‍jāpañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| manasikārau pāmauj‍japañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattau, diṭṭhadhammāpi ānisaṃsapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattā|

autaraṇauti tissau pañ‍ñā vacasā paricitaisu sutamayīpañ‍ñā manasā anupaikkhitaisu cintāmayīpañ‍ñā diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhāsu bhāvanāmayīpañ‍ñā| imāni ariyasac‍cāni indriyāni vij‍juppādā avij‍jāniraudhau paṭic‍casamuppādau indriyaisu tīṇi indriyāni, āyatanaisu dhammāyatanapariyāpan‍nā dhātūsu dhammadhātupariyāpan‍nāti| saudhanauti yau ārambhau suttassa pavaisau niyuttau|

adhiṭṭhānauti pañ‍cānisaṃsāti vaimattatāya pañ‍ñattā ānisaṃsā sautā anugatāti vaimattatāya ariyavauhārau pañ‍ñattau, dhammai ca savananti aikattatāya pañ‍ñattaṃ|

parikkhārauti dhammassavanassa payirupāsanā pac‍cayau, saddhā haitu| manasā anupaikkhitāti atthappaṭisaṃvaiditā pac‍cayau, dhammappaṭisaṃvaiditā haitu, diṭṭhiyā suppaṭividdhāti  saddhammassavanañ‍ca manasikārau ca pac‍cayau, sutamayī cintāmayī pañ‍ñā haitu| samāraupanauti vibhattaṃ suttaṃ aparau pariyāyau nibbatti balai natthi| tattha samāraupanāya bhūmi|

117. tattha katamaṃ vāsanābhāgiyañ‍ca nibbaidhabhāgiyañ‍ca suttaṃ? dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti gāthā| dadatauti dānamayikapuñ‍ñakiriyavatthu vuttaṃ| saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti sīlamayikapuñ‍ñakiriyavatthu vuttaṃ| kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti laubhassa ca mauhassa ca byāpādassa ca pahānamāha| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti laubhassa ca mauhassa ca byāpādassa  ca chandarāgavinayamāhāti| dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti gāthā alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ bhavati| saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti adausau kusalamūlaṃ bhavati| saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti avairā asapattā abyāpādatāya sadā| kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti ñāṇuppādā añ‍ñāṇaniraudhau| catutthapadaina rāgadausamauhakkhayaina rāgavirāgā caitauvimuttimauhakkhayaina avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti, ayaṃ vicayau|

yuttīti dānai ṭhitau ubhayaṃ hi paripūraiti| macchariyañ‍ca pajahati| puñ‍ñañ‍ca pavaḍḍhati| atthi aisā yutti|

padaṭṭhānanti  dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti cāgādhiṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti sac‍cādhiṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti upasamādhiṭṭhānassa padaṭṭhānaṃ| ayaṃ padaṭṭhānau|

tattha  katamau lakkhaṇau? dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyati| dadataupi vairaṃ na kariyāti kusalau ca jahāti pāpakaṃ rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutau rūpakkhayāpi vaidanakkhayāpi, yaina rūpaina diṭṭhaṃ, taina tathāgatau pañ‍ñapaintau pañ‍ñapaiyya rūpassa khayā virāganiraudhāti aivaṃ pañ‍cakkhandhā|

catubyūhau idha bhagavatau kau adhippāyau? yai mahābhaugānaṃ patthayissanti? tai dānaṃ dassanti parissayapahānāya, yai avairābhichandakā, tai pañ‍ca vairāni pajahissanti, yai kusalābhichandakā, tai aṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ bhāvaissanti aṭṭhan‍naṃ micchattānaṃ pahānāya| yai nibbāyitukāmā, tai rāgadausamauhaṃ pajahissantīti ayaṃ bhagavatau adhippāyau|

āvaṭṭauti yañ‍ca adadatau macchariyaṃ yañ‍ca asaṃyamatau vairaṃ yañ‍ca akusalassa pāpassa appahānaṃ, ayaṃ dukkhaniddaisau na samudayau| alaubhaina ca adausaina ca amauhaina ca kusalaina imāni tīṇi kusalamūlāni| taisaṃ pac‍cayau aṭṭha sammattāni, ayaṃ maggau| taisaṃ rāgadausamauhānaṃ khayā, ayaṃ niraudhau|

vibhattīti dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti na aikaṃsaina yau rājadaṇḍabhayaina daiti, yau ca akappiyassa paribhaugaina sīlavantaisu daiti, na tassa puñ‍ñaṃ  pavaḍḍhatīti sau caitaṃ dānaṃ akusalaina daiti, daṇḍadānaṃ satthadānaṃ apuñ‍ñamayaṃ pavaḍḍhati, na puñ‍ñaṃ| saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti na aikaṃsaina kiṃ kāraṇaṃ yañ‍ca yau padaṃ diṭṭhadhammikaṃ passati yadi mama rājānau gahaitvā hatthaṃ vā chindaiyya…pai॰… na taina saṃyamaina vairaṃ na karauti| yau tu aivaṃ samādiyati pāṇātipātassa  pāpakau vipākauti, diṭṭhai yaiva dhammai abhisamparāyai ca aivaṃ sabbassa akusalassa haitutau ārati| iminā saṃyamaina vairaṃ na cīyati|

parivattanāti dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti adadatau puñ‍ñaṃ na pavaḍḍhati| yaṃ dānamayaṃ, taṃ saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyati, asaṃyamatau vairaṃ karīyati| kusalau ca jahāti pāpakaṃ akusalau na jahāti| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sanibbutauti dūtaṃ paisaitvā paṇītaṃ paisaitvāpi na pak‍kausāmi, sau  sayamaiva pana mahābhikkhusaṅghaparivārau amhākaṃ vasanaṭṭhānaṃ sampattau amhaihi ca santhāgārasālā sandhāgārasālā (ka॰) kāritā, aittha mayaṃ dasabalaṃ ānaitvā maṅgalaṃ bhaṇāpaimāti cintaitvā upasaṅkamiṃsu| yaina santhāgāraṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsūti taṃ divasaṃ kira santhāgārai cittakammaṃ niṭṭhāpaitvā aṭṭakā muttamattā haunti| buddhā nāma arañ‍ñajjhāsayā arañ‍ñārāmā antaugāmai vasaiyyuṃ vā nau vāti tasmā bhagavatau manaṃ jānitvāva paṭijaggissāmāti cintaitvā tai bhagavantaṃ upasaṅkamiṃsu| idāni pana manaṃ labhitvā paṭijaggitukāmā yaina santhāgāraṃ, tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| sabbasantharinti yathā sabbaṃ santhataṃ hauti aivaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsūti| aittha pana tai mal‍larājānau santhāgāraṃ paṭijaggitvā nagaravīthiyaupi sammaj‍jāpaitvā dhajai ussāpaitvā suvaṇṇaghaṭikadaliyau ca ṭhapāpaitvā sakalanagaraṃ dīpamālāhi vippakiṇṇatārakaṃ viya katvā khīrapakai khīrupakai (pī॰ ka॰) dārakai khīraṃ pāyaitha, daharai kumārai lahuṃ lahuṃ bhaujāpaitvā sayāpaitha, uc‍cāsaddaṃ mākari, aj‍ja aikarattiṃ satthā antaugāmaiva vasissati, buddhā nāma appasaddakāmā hauntīti bhairiṃ carāpaitvā sayaṃ daṇḍakadīpikā ādāya yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| bhagavantaṃ yaiva purakkhatvāti bhagavantaṃ puratau  katvā, tattha bhagavā bhikkhūnañ‍caiva upāsakānañ‍ca majjhai nisin‍nau ativiya viraucati| samantapāsādikau suvaṇṇavaṇṇau abhirūpau dassanīyau puratthimakāyatau  suvaṇṇavaṇṇā rasmi uṭṭhahitvā gaganatalai asītihatthaṃ ṭhānaṃ gaṇhāti| pacchimakāyatau dakkhiṇahatthatau vāmahatthatau suvaṇṇavaṇṇā haiṭṭhā pādatalaihi pavāḷavaṇṇarasmi uṭṭhahitvā ghanapathaviyaṃ asītihatthaṃ ṭhānaṃ gaṇhāti, aivaṃ samantā asītihatthamattaṃ ṭhānaṃ chabbaṇṇabuddharasmiyau vij‍jautamānā vitaṇḍamānā vidhāvanti, sabbai disābhāgā suvaṇṇacampakapupphaihi vikiriyamānā viya suvaṇṇaghaṭatau nikkhantasuvaṇṇarasadhārāhi siñ‍camānā viya pasāritasuvaṇṇapaṭaparikkhittā vviya vairambhavātasamuṭṭhitakiṃsukakiṃsukārakaṇikārapupphacuṇṇasamaukiṇṇā viya vippakasantaṃ asītianubyañ‍janabyāmappabhā dvattiṃsavaralakkhaṇasamuj‍jalaṃ sarīraṃ samuggatatārakaṃ viya gaganatalaṃ vikasitamiva padumavanaṃ sabbaphāliphul‍lau viya yaujanasatikau pāricchattakau paṭipāṭiyā ṭhapitānaṃ dvattiṃsacandānaṃ dvattiṃsasūriyānaṃ dvattiṃsacak‍kavattīnaṃ dvattiṃsadaivarājānaṃ dvattiṃsamahābrahmānaṃ nibbutau asaikkhassa natthi nibbuti|

vaivacananti dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, anumaudataupi puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati| cittassa samādahataupi vaiyyāvac‍cakiriyāyapi puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti|

pañ‍ñattīti dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, alaubhassa paṭinissayaghātapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| saṃyamatau  vairaṃ na cīyatīti adausassa paṭinissayaghātapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti amauhassa paṭinissayaghātapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ|

autaraṇauti  pañ‍casu indriyaisu dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhati, saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyati saṃyamaina sīlakkhandhau| autiṇṇau chasu indriyaisu saṃvarau, ayaṃ samādhikkhandhau, yaṃ kusalau ca jahāti pāpakaṃ, ayaṃ pañ‍ñākkhandhau, rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti vimuttikkhandhau| dhātūsu dhammadhātu, āyatanaisu manāyatanaṃ|

saudhanauti yainārambhaina idaṃ suttaṃ daisitaṃ sau ārambhau suddhau|

adhiṭṭhānau dānanti aikattatāya pañ‍ñattaṃ| cāgau paric‍cāgau dhammadānaṃ āmisadānaṃ, aṭṭha dānāni vitthāraina kātabbāni, ayaṃ vaimattatā| na ca dadatau  aikattapañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| khantī anavaj‍janti pañ‍ñattiyā pañ‍ñattaṃ| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti raudhavīriyapañ‍ñattiyā yaudha vīriyapañ‍ñattiyā (pī॰ ka॰) pañ‍ñattā|

parikkhārauti dānassa pāmauj‍jaṃ pac‍cayau, alaubhau haitu| saṃyamatau yaunisau manasikārau haitu, paric‍cāgau pac‍cayau| kusalau ca jahāti pāpakanti yathābhūtadassanaṃ pac‍cayau, ñāṇappaṭilābhau haitu| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti paratau ca ghausau ajjhattañ‍ca yaunisau manasikārau maggau ca haitu ca pac‍cayau ca|

samāraupanauti dadatau puñ‍ñaṃ pavaḍḍhatīti gāthā tassa sīlampi vaḍḍhati| saṃyamaupi vaḍḍhati| saṃyamatau vairaṃ na cīyatīti| añ‍ñaipi kilaisā na cīyanti yaipissa tappac‍cayā uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ āsavā vighātā, taipissa na uppaj‍janti| rāgadausamauhakkhayā sa nibbutauti rāgadausassāpi khayā rāgānusayassapi khayā dausassa mauhassāpi sa nibbutauti saupādisaisā nibbānadhātu anupādisaisāpi| ayaṃ samāraupanau|

thairassa mahākac‍cāyanassa paiṭakaupadaisai

hārassa sampātabhūmi samattā|



8. suttavaibhaṅgiyaṃ

118. pubbā  kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jāya ca bhavataṇhāya ca| tattha avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sattānaṃ pubbakauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati| tattha yai sattā taṇhāsaṃyaujanā, tai ajjhausānabahulā mandavipassakā| yai pana ussan‍nadiṭṭhikā sattā, tai vipassanābahulā mandajjhausānā|

tattha taṇhācaritā sattā sattasañ‍ñābhiniviṭṭhā anuppādavayadassinau| tai pañ‍casu khandhaisu attānaṃ samanupassanti ‘‘rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ, attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attāna’’nti| aivaṃ pañ‍cakkhandhā| añ‍ñaihi khandhaihi attānaṃ samanupassanti tassa ussan‍nadiṭṭhikā sattā vipassamānā khandhai ujuṃ attatau samanupassanti| tai rūpaṃ attakau samanupassanti| yaṃ rūpaṃ, sau attā| yau ahaṃ, taṃ rūpaṃ| sau rūpavināsaṃ passati, ayaṃ ucchaidavādī| iti pañ‍can‍naṃ khandhānaṃ paṭhamābhinipātā sak‍kāyadiṭṭhiyau pañ‍ca ucchaidaṃ bhajanti ‘‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’nti| aikamaikamhi khandhai tīhi padaihi pacchimakaihi sassataṃ bhajati ‘‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’nti| itau bahiddhātai pabbajitā taṇhācaritā kāmasukhal‍likānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| taina yai ca nissandaina diṭṭhicaritā attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā  viharanti| taina yaiva diṭṭhisukhaina aittāvatā bāhirakau payaugau|

tattha diṭṭhicaritā sattā yai ariyadhammavinayaṃ autaranti, tai dhammānusārinau haunti| yai taṇhācaritā sattā ariyaṃ dhammavinayaṃ autaranti, tai saddhānusārinau haunti|

tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā, tai kāmaisu dausadiṭṭhī, na ca yai kāmaisu anusayā samūhatā, tai attakilamathānuyaugamanuyuttā viharanti| taisaṃ satthā dhammaṃ daisaiti| añ‍ñau vā sāvakau kāmaihi natthi atthauti tai ca pubbaiyaiva kāmaihi anatthikā iti kāmai appakasiraina paṭinissaj‍janti| tai caitasikaina dukkhaina anajjhausitā| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhā paṭipadā’’ti| yai pana taṇhācaritā sattā, tai kāmaisu ajjhausitā, taisaṃ satthā vā dhammaṃ daisaiti| añ‍ñatarau vā bhikkhu kāmaihi natthi atthauti, tai piyarūpaṃ dukkhaina paṭinissaj‍janti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘dukkhā paṭipadā’’ti| iti imai sabbasattā dvīsu paṭipadāsu samausaraṇaṃ gacchanti dukkhāyañ‍ca sukhāyañ‍ca|

tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā, tai dvidhā mudindriyā ca tikkhindriyā ca| tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā tikkhindriyā sukhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, khippañ‍ca abhisamainti, taina vuc‍cati ‘‘khippābhiñ‍ñā sukhā paṭipadā’’ti| tattha yai diṭṭhicaritā sattā mudindriyā paṭhamaṃ tikkhindriyaṃ upādāya dandhataraṃ abhisamainti, tai sukhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, dandhañ‍ca abhisamainti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘sukhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā’’ti| tattha taṇhācaritā sattā dvidhā tikkhindriyā ca mudindriyā ca| tattha  yai taṇhācaritā sattā tikkhindriyā dukkhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, khippañ‍ca abhisamainti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘dukkhā paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā’’ti| tattha yai taṇhācaritā sattā mudindriyā paṭhamaṃ tikkhindriyaṃ upādāya dandhataraṃ abhisamainti, tai dukkhaina paṭinissaj‍janti, dandhañ‍ca abhisamainti| taina vuc‍cati ‘‘dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā’’ti| imā catassau paṭipadāyau apañ‍camā achaṭṭhā| yai hi kaici nibbutā nibbāyissanti  vā imāhi catūhi paṭipadāhi anañ‍ñāhi ayaṃ paṭipadācatuk‍kaina kilaisai niddisati| yā catuk‍kamaggaina ariyadhammaisu niddisitabbā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati sīhavik‍kīḷitau nāma nayau|

119. tatrimai cattārau āhārā| cattārau vipal‍lāsā upādānā yaugā ganthā āsavā aughā sal‍lā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau agatigamanāti, aivaṃ imāni sabbāni dasa padāni| ayaṃ suttassa saṃsandanā|

cattārau āhārā| tattha yau ca kabaḷīkārau āhārau yau ca phassau āhārau, imai taṇhācaritaina pahātabbā| tattha yau ca manausañ‍caitanāhārau yau ca viñ‍ñāṇāhārau, imai diṭṭhicaritaina pahātabbā|

paṭhamau āhārau paṭhamau vipal‍lāsau, dutiyau āhārau dutiyau vipal‍lāsau, tatiyau āhārau tatiyau vipal‍lāsau, catutthau āhārau catutthau vipal‍lāsau| imai cattārau vipal‍lāsā apañ‍camā achaṭṭhā| idañ‍ca pamāṇā cattārau āhārā|

tattha  paṭhamai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau kāmai upādiyati, idaṃ kāmupādānaṃ| dutiyai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau anāgataṃ bhavaṃ upādiyati, idaṃ sīlabbatupādānaṃ| tatiyai vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau viparītau diṭṭhiṃ upādiyati, idaṃ diṭṭhupādānaṃ| catutthai  vipal‍lāsai ṭhitau khandhai attatau upādiyati, idaṃ attavādupādānaṃ|

tattha kāmupādānai ṭhitau kāmai abhijjhāyati ganthati, ayaṃ abhijjhākāyaganthau| sīlabbatupādānai ṭhitau byāpādaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ byāpādakāyaganthau| diṭṭhupādānai ṭhitau parāmāsaṃ ganthati, ayaṃ parāmāsakāyaganthau| attavādupādānai ṭhitau papañ‍cantau ganthati, ayaṃ idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisau kāyaganthau|

tassa ganthitā kilaisā āsavanti| kiñ‍ci pana vuc‍cati vippaṭisārau| yai vippaṭisārā yau vippaṭisārau (pī॰ ka॰) tai anusayā| tattha abhijjhākāyaganthaina kāmāsavau, byāpādakāyaganthaina bhavāsavau, parāmāsakāyaganthaina diṭṭhāsavau, idaṃ sac‍cābhinivaisakāyaganthaina avij‍jāsavau|

tai  cattārau āsavā vaipul‍labhāvaṃ gatā aughā haunti, taina vuc‍canti ‘‘aughā’’ti| tattha kāmāsavau kāmaughau, bhavāsavau bhavaughau, avij‍jāsavau avij‍jaughau, diṭṭhāsavau diṭṭhaughau|

tai cattārau aughā āsayamanupaviṭṭhā anusayasahagatā vuc‍canti| sal‍lāti hadayamāhac‍ca tiṭṭhantā| tattha kāmaughau rāgasal‍laṃ, bhavaughau dausasal‍laṃ, avij‍jaughau mauhasal‍laṃ, diṭṭhaughau diṭṭhisal‍laṃ|

imaihi catūhi sal‍laihi pariyādin‍naṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ catūsu dhammaisu tiṭṭhati rūpai vaidanāya sañ‍ñāya saṅkhāraisu| imā catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau| tattha rāgasal‍laina nandūpasaicanaṃ rūpūpagaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhati| dausasal‍laina vaidanūpagaṃ mauhasal‍laina sañ‍ñūpagaṃ  diṭṭhisal‍laina nandūpasaicanaṃ saṅkhārūpagaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhati|

catūhi viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitīhi catubbidhaṃ agatiṃ gacchanti chandā dausā bhayā mauhā| rāgaina chandā agatiṃ gacchati, dausaina dausā agatiṃ gacchati, mauhaina mauhā agatiṃ gacchati, diṭṭhiyā bhayā agatiṃ gacchati| iti idañ‍ca kammaṃ imai ca kilaisā| ayaṃ saṃsārassa haitu|

120. tatthimā  catassau disā kabaḷīkārāhārau ‘‘asubhai subha’’nti vipal‍lāsau kāmupādānaṃ kāmayaugau abhijjhākāyaganthau kāmāsavau kāmaughau rāgasal‍laṃ rūpūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti chandā agatigamanaṃ| ayaṃ paṭhamā disā|

phassau āhārau ‘‘dukkhai sukha’’nti vipal‍lāsau sīlabbatupādānaṃ bhavayaugaubyāpādau kāyaganthau bhavāsavau bhavaughau dausasal‍laṃ vaidanūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti dausā agatigamanaṃ, ayaṃ dutiyā disā|

manausañ‍caitanāhārau ‘‘anattani attā’’ti vipal‍lāsau diṭṭhupādānaṃ diṭṭhiyaugau parāmāsakāyaganthau diṭṭhāsavau diṭṭhaughau diṭṭhisal‍laṃ sañ‍ñūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti bhayā agatigamanaṃ| ayaṃ tatiyā disā|

viñ‍ñāṇāhārau ‘‘anic‍cai nic‍ca’’nti vipal‍lāsau attavādupādānaṃ avij‍jāyaugau idaṃsac‍cābhinivaisau kāyaganthau avij‍jāsavau avij‍jaughau mauhasal‍laṃ saṅkhārūpagā viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhiti mauhā agatigamanaṃ, ayaṃ  catutthī disā| iti imaisaṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ paṭhamaina padaina paṭhamāya disāya ālaukanaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati disālaukanā|

catūhi  vipal‍lāsaihi akusalapakkhai disāvilaukanā kilaisaṃ saṃyaujaitvā ayaṃ akusalapakkhai disāvilaukanāya bhūmi pañ‍can‍naṃ dasan‍naṃ suttānaṃ yāni paṭhamāni padāni imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ kau atthau? aikau atthau, byañ‍janamaiva nānaṃ| aivaṃ dutiyā aivaṃ tatiyā aivaṃ catutthī| ayaṃ paṭhamā saṃsandanā|

iminā paiyyālaina sabbai kilaisā catūsu padaisu pakkhipitabbā| tatau kusalapakkhai catassau paṭipadā cattāri jhānāni cattārau satipaṭṭhānā cattārau vihārā dibbau brahmā ariyau ānaiñ‍jau cattārau sammappadhānā cattārau acchariyā abbhutadhammā cattārau adhiṭṭhānā cattārau samādhayau chandasamādhi vīriyasamādhi cittasamādhi vīmaṃsāsamādhi| cattārau dhammā sukhabhāgiyā nāñ‍ñatra baujjhaṅgā nāñ‍ñatra tapasā nāñ‍ñatindriyasaṃvarā nāñ‍ñatra sabbanissaggā cattāri appamāṇāni|

tattha dukkhā paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā bhāviyamānā bahulīkariyamānā paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ vihāraṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamau vihārau paripuṇṇau paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ sammappadhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ paṭhamaṃ acchariyaṃ abbhutadhammaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamau acchariyau abbhutau dhammau paripuṇṇau paṭhamaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ paripūraiti, paṭhamaṃ adhiṭṭhānaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ chandasamādhiṃ paripūraiti, chandasamādhi paripuṇṇau indriyasaṃvaraṃ paripūraiti, indriyasaṃvarau paripuṇṇau paṭhamaṃ maittāappamāṇaṃ paripūraiti| aivaṃ yāva sabbanissaggau catutthaṃ appamāṇaṃ paripūraiti|

tattha paṭhamā ca paṭipadā paṭhamañ‍ca jhānaṃ paṭhamañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ paṭhamau ca vihārau paṭhamañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ  paṭhamau ca acchariyau abbhutau dhammau sac‍cādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca chandasamādhi ca indriyasaṃvarau ca maittā ca appamāṇaṃ| ayaṃ paṭhamā disā|

dukkhā ca dutiyā ca (ka॰) paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ dutiyañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ dutiyau ca vihārau dutiyañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ dutiyau ca acchariyau abbhutau  dhammau cāgādhiṭṭhānaṃ cittasamādhi cattārau iddhipādā karuṇā ca appamāṇaṃ, ayaṃ dutiyā disā|

sukhā ca tatiyā ca (ka॰) paṭipadā dandhābhiñ‍ñā tatiyañ‍ca jhānaṃ tatiyañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ tatiyau ca vihārau tatiyañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ tatiyau ca acchariyau abbhutau dhammau pañ‍ñādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca vīriyasamādhi ca baujjhaṅgā ca muditā ca appamāṇaṃ| ayaṃ tatiyā disā|

sukhā ca catutthī ca (ka॰) paṭipadā khippābhiñ‍ñā catutthaṃ jhānaṃ catutthañ‍ca satipaṭṭhānaṃ catutthau ca vihārau catutthañ‍ca sammappadhānaṃ catutthau ca acchariyau abbhutau dhammau upasamādhiṭṭhānañ‍ca vīmaṃsāsamādhi ca sabbanissaggau ca upaikkhā appamāṇañ‍ca| ayaṃ catutthī disā| imāsaṃ catassan‍naṃ disānaṃ ālaukanā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati disālaukanau nāma nayau|

tatthāyaṃ yaujanā| cattārau ca āhārā catassau ca paṭipadā, cattārau ca vipal‍lāsā cattārau ca satipaṭṭhānā, cattāri ca upādānāni cattāri ca jhānāni cattārau ca yaugā vihārā ca, ganthā ca sammappadhānā ca, āsavā ca acchariyā abbhutadhammā ca, aughā ca adhiṭṭhānāni ca, sal‍lā ca samādhayau, viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau  cattārau ca sukhabhāgiyā dhammā, cattāri ca agatigamanāni cattāri ca appamāṇāni iti kusalākusalānaṃ paṭipakkhavasaina yaujanā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati disālaukanau nayau|

tassa  cattāri sāmañ‍ñaphalāni pariyausānaṃ, yau ca dhammau kusalākusalaniddaisai paṭhamau disāniddaisau, imassa sautāpattiphalaṃ pariyausānaṃ dutiyaṃ sakadāgāmiphalaṃ, tatiyaṃ anāgāmiphalaṃ, catutthaṃ arahattaphalaṃ|

tattha katamau tipukkhalau nayau? yai ca dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti dvai puggalā, yai ca sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti dvai puggalā|

imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ puggalānaṃ yau puggalau sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, yau ca puggalau dukkhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti| imai dvai puggalā bhavanti| tattha yau sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya niyyāti, ayaṃ ugghaṭitañ‍ñū| yau pacchimau puggalau sādhāraṇau, ayaṃ vipañ‍citañ‍ñū| yau puggalau dandhābhiñ‍ñāya dukkhāya paṭipadāya niyyāti, ayaṃ naiyyau| imai cattārau bhavitvā tīṇi haunti, tattha ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa samathapubbaṅgamā vipassanā, naiyyassa vipassanāpubbaṅgamau samathau, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa samathavipassanā yuganaddhā| ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa mudukā daisanā, naiyyassa tikkhā daisanā, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa tikkhamudukā daisanā|

ugghaṭitañ‍ñussa adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā, naiyyassa adhicittasikkhā, vipañ‍citañ‍ñussa adhisīlasikkhā| iti imaisaṃ puggalānaṃ catūhi paṭipadāhi niyyānaṃ|

tattha  ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, tīṇi akusalamūlāni tayau phassā tissau vaidanā tayau upavicārā tayau saṃkilaisā tayau vitak‍kā tayau pariḷāhā tīṇi saṅkhatalakkhaṇāni tissau dukkhatāti|

tīṇi akusalamūlānīti laubhau akusalamūlaṃ, dausau akusalamūlaṃ, mauhau akusalamūlaṃ| tayau phassāti sukhavaidanīyau phassau, dukkhavaidanīyau phassau, adukkhamasukhavaidanīyau phassau| tissau vaidanāti sukhā vaidanā dukkhā vaidanā adukkhamasukhā vaidanā| tayau upavicārāti saumanassaupavicārau daumanassaupavicārau upaikkhaupavicārau| tayau saṃkilaisāti rāgau dausau mauhau| tayau vitak‍kāti kāmavitak‍kau byāpādavitak‍kau vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tayau pariḷāhāti  rāgajau dausajau mauhajau| tīṇi saṅkhatalakkhaṇānīti uppādau ṭhiti vayau| tissau dukkhatāti dukkhadukkhatā vipariṇāmadukkhatā saṅkhatadukkhatā|

tattha laubhau akusalamūlaṃ kutau samuṭṭhitaṃ? tividhaṃ ārammaṇaṃ manāpikaṃ amanāpikaṃ upaikkhāṭhāniyañ‍ca| tattha manāpikaina ārammaṇaina laubhau akusalamūlaṃ samuṭṭhahati| iti manāpikā ārammaṇā sukhavaidanīyau phassau, sukhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai sukhavaidanā, sukhavaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai saumanassūpavicārau, saumanassūpavicāraṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai rāgau, rāgaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai kāmavitak‍kau, kāmavitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai rāgajau pariḷāhau  rāgajaṃ pariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai uppādau saṅkhatalakkhaṇau, uppādaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai vipariṇāmadukkhatā|

dausau  akusalamūlaṃ kutau samuṭṭhitaṃ? amanāpikaina ārammaṇaina dausau akusalamūlaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ| iti amanāpikā ārammaṇā dukkhavaidanīyau phassau, dukkhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai dukkhavaidanā, dukkhavaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai daumanassūpavicārau, daumanassūpavicāraṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai dausau, dausaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai byāpādavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai dausajau pariḷāhau, dausajaṃ pariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai dukkhadukkhatā vaidanā|

mauhau akusalamūlaṃ kutau samuṭṭhitaṃ? upaikkhāṭhāniyaina ārammaṇaina mauhau akusalamūlaṃ samuṭṭhitaṃ| iti upaikkhāṭhāniyā ārammaṇā adukkhamasukhavaidanīyau phassau, adukkhamasukhavaidanīyaṃ phassaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai adukkhamasukhā vaidanā, adukkhamasukhavaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai upaikkhūpavicārau, upaikkhūpavicāraṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai mauhau, mauhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai vihiṃsāvitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai mauhajau pariḷāhau, mauhajaṃ pariḷāhaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai vayau saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ, vayaṃ saṅkhatalakkhaṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jatai saṅkhatadukkhatā, iti ayaṃ tiṇṇaṃ kilaisānaṃ niddaisau, ayaṃ vuc‍catai kusalapakkhai tipukkhalau nayau|

iti tīṇi akusalamūlāni na catutthāni na pañ‍camāni, tayau phassāti tissau vaidanā yāva saṅkhatadukkhatāti, yau  kauci akusalapakkhau, sabbau sau tīsu akusalamūlaisu samausarati|

tattha  katamau kusalapakkhau? tīṇi kusalamūlāni, tissau pañ‍ñā sutamayī pañ‍ñā cintāmayī pañ‍ñā bhāvanāmayī pañ‍ñā| tayau samādhī savitak‍kasavicārau…pai॰… tissau sikkhā adhisīlasikkhā…pai॰… sikkhā| tīṇi nimittāni samathanimittaṃ paggahanimittaṃ upaikkhānimittaṃ| tayau vitak‍kā naikkhammavitak‍kau…pai॰… avihiṃsāvitak‍kau| tīṇi indriyāni anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyanti vitthārau| tayau upavicārā naikkhammūpavicārau abyāpādūpavicārau avihiṃsūpavicārau| tissau aisanā kāmaisanā bhavaisanā brahmacariyaisanā| tayau khandhā sīlakkhandhau samādhikkhandhau pañ‍ñākkhandhau|

tattha  yaṃ alaubhau kusalamūlaṃ, taṃ sutamayipañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, sutamayī pañ‍ñā paripuṇṇā savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ samādhiṃ paripūraiti, savitak‍kau savicārau samādhi paripuṇṇau adhicittasikkhaṃ paripūraiti, adhicittasikkhā paripuṇṇā samathanimittaṃ paripūraiti, samathanimittaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ naikkhammavitak‍kaṃ paripūraiti, naikkhammavitak‍kau paripuṇṇau anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ paripūraiti, anañ‍ñātañ‍ñassāmītindriyaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ naikkhammūpavicāraṃ paripūraiti, naikkhammūpavicārau paripuṇṇau kāmaisanaṃ pajahati, kāmaisanappahānaṃ samādhikkhandhaṃ paripūraiti|

adausau kusalamūlaṃ cintāmayipañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, cintāmayī pañ‍ñā paripuṇṇā avitak‍kavicāramattaṃ samādhiṃ paripūraiti| avitak‍kavicāramattau samādhi paripuṇṇau adhisīlasikkhaṃ paripūraiti, adhisīlasikkhā paripuṇṇā upaikkhānimittaṃ paripūraiti, upaikkhānimittaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ abyāpādavitak‍kaṃ paripūraiti, abyāpādavitak‍kau paripuṇṇau añ‍ñindriyaṃ paripūraiti, añ‍ñindriyaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ abyāpādūpavicāraṃ paripūraiti, abyāpādūpavicārau paripuṇṇau bhavaisanaṃ pajahati, bhavaisanappahānaṃ sīlakkhandhaṃ paripūraiti|

amauhau kusalamūlaṃ bhāvanāmayipañ‍ñaṃ paripūraiti, bhāvanāmayīpañ‍ñā paripuṇṇā avitak‍kaavicāraṃ samādhiṃ paripūraiti, avitak‍kau avicārau samādhi paripuṇṇau adhipañ‍ñāsikkhaṃ paripūraiti, adhipañ‍ñāsikkhā paripuṇṇā paggahanimittaṃ paripūraiti, paggahanimittaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ añ‍ñātāvinau indriyaṃ paripūraiti, añ‍ñātāvinau indriyaṃ paripuṇṇaṃ avihiṃsūpavicāraṃ paripūraiti, avihiṃsūpavicārau paripuṇṇau brahmacariyaisanaṃ paripūraiti, brahmacariyaisanā paripuṇṇā pañ‍ñākkhandhaṃ paripūraiti|

iti imai tayau dhammā kusalapakkhikā sabbai kusalā dhammā tīhi tikaniddaisaihi niddisiyanti  tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni tassa pariyausānaṃ| tattha paṭhamaina appaṇihitaṃ, dutiyaina suñ‍ñataṃ, tatiyaina animittaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati dutiyau tipukkhalau nāma nayau|

tattha yai imai tayau puggalā ugghaṭitañ‍ñū vipañ‍citañ‍ñū naiyyauti| imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ puggalānaṃ yai ca puggalā sukhāya paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya, sukhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, tai dvai puggalā| yai ca dvai puggalā dukkhāya  paṭipadāya khippābhiñ‍ñāya, dukkhāya paṭipadāya dandhābhiñ‍ñāya ca niyyanti, imai cattārau taina visaisaina dvai bhavanti diṭṭhicaritau ca taṇhācaritau ca| imai cattārau bhavitvā tayau bhavanti, tayau bhavitvā dvai bhavanti| imaisaṃ dvin‍naṃ puggalānaṃ ayaṃ saṃkilaisau, avij‍jā ca taṇhā ca, ahirikañ‍ca anauttappañ‍ca, assati ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, nīvaraṇāni ca saṃyaujanāni ca, ajjhausānañ‍ca abhinivaisau ca, ahaṃkārau ca mamaṃkārau ca, assaddhiyañ‍ca dauvacassañ‍ca, kausaj‍jañ‍ca ayaunisau ca manasikārau, vicikicchā ca abhijjhā ca, asaddhammassavanañ‍ca asamāpatti ca|

tattha avij‍jā ca ahirikañ‍ca assati ca nīvaraṇāni ca ajjhausānañ‍ca ahaṃkārau ca assaddhiyañ‍ca kausaj‍jañ‍ca vicikicchā ca asaddhammassavanañ‍ca, ayaṃ aikā disā|

taṇhā ca anauttappañ‍ca asampajañ‍ñañ‍ca saṃyaujanāni ca abhinivaisau ca mamaṃkārau ca dauvacassatā ca ayaunisau manasikārau ca abhijjhā ca asamāpatti ca, ayaṃ dutiyā disā| dasan‍naṃ dukānaṃ dasa padāni paṭhamāni kātabbāni| saṃkhittaina atthaṃ ñāpainti paṭipakkhai kaṇhapakkhassa sabbaisaṃ dukānaṃ dasa padāni dutiyakāni, ayaṃ dutiyā disā|

iti akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ dukkhaniddaisau, ayaṃ samudayau| yaṃ taṃ dhammaṃ ajjhāvasati nāmañ‍ca rūpañ‍ca idaṃ dukkhaṃ iti ayañ‍ca samudayau, idañ‍ca dukkhaṃ, imāni dvai sac‍cāni dukkhañ‍ca samudayau ca nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa paṭhamaniddaisau|

tattha katamau kusalapakkhau? samathau ca vipassanā ca, vij‍jā ca caraṇañ‍ca, sati ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca, hirī ca auttappañ‍ca, ahaṃkārappahānañ‍ca  mamaṃkārappahānañ‍ca, sammāvāyāmau ca yaunisau ca manasikārau, sammāsati ca sammāsamādhi ca, pañ‍ñā ca nibbidā ca, samāpatti ca saddhammassavanañ‍ca, saumanassañ‍ca dhammānudhammappaṭipatti ca|

tattha  samathau ca vij‍jā ca sati ca hirī ca ahaṃkārappahānañ‍ca sammāvāyāmau ca sammāsati ca pañ‍ñā ca samāpatti ca saumanassañ‍ca, imai dhammā aikā disā| vipassanā ca caraṇañ‍ca sampajañ‍ñañ‍ca auttappañ‍ca mamaṃkārappahānañ‍ca yaunisau manasikārau ca sammāsamādhi ca nibbidā ca saddhammassavanañ‍ca dhammānudhammappaṭipatti ca, ayaṃ dutiyā disā| iti kusalapakkhai ca akusalapakkhai ca nandiyāvaṭṭassa pana nayassa catassau disā|

tāsu  yāni akusalapakkhassa paṭhamāni padāni akusalāni kusalaihi pahānaṃ gacchanti, tāni kusalapakkhai dutiyaihi padaihi pahānaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti yāni akusalapakkhassa dutiyāni akusalapadāni pahānaṃ gacchanti, tāni kusalapakkhassa paṭhamaihi padaihi pahānaṃ gacchanti| taisaṃ pahānā avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti pariyausānaṃ| imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ nayānaṃ paṭhamau nayau sīhavik‍kīḷitau nāma| aṭṭha padāni cattāri ca kusalāni cattāri ca akusalāni imāni aṭṭha padāni mūlapadāni, atthanayaina dutiyau tipukkhalau| sau chahi dhammaihi naiti kusalamūlāni ca naiti akusalamūlāni ca, iti imāni cha padāni purimakāni ca aṭṭha mūlapadāni imāni cuddasa padāni aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ mūlapadānaṃ| tattha  yau pacchimakau nayau nandiyāvaṭṭau, sau catūhi dhammaihi naiti| avij‍jāya ca taṇhāya ca samathaina ca vipassanāya ca, imai cattārau dhammā imāni aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni tīsu nayaisu niddiṭṭhāni|

tattha yāni nava padāni kusalāni, tattha sabbaṃ kusalaṃ samausarati| taisañ‍ca navan‍naṃ mūlānaṃ cattāri padāni sīhavik‍kīḷitanayai tīṇi tipukkhalai dvai nandiyāvaṭṭai, ic‍caitai kusalassa pakkhā| tattha yāni nava padāni kusalāni, tattha sabbaṃ kusalaṃ yuj‍jati| tattha sīhavik‍kīḷitai nayai cattāri padāni tīṇi tipukkhalai dvai nandiyāvaṭṭai imāni nava padāni kusalāni niddiṭṭhāni|

tattha yāni nandiyāvaṭṭai nayai cattāri padāni, tattha aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni samausaranti| yathā kathaṃ, samathau ca alaubhau ca adausau ca asubhasañ‍ñā ca dukkhasañ‍ñā ca imāni kusalapakkhai pañ‍ca padāni samathaṃ bhajanti| vipassanā ca amauhau ca anic‍casañ‍ñā ca anattasañ‍ñā ca imāni cattāri padāni vipassanaṃ bhajanti| imāni nava padāni kusalāni dvīsu padaisu yaujitāni, tattha akusalapakkhai navan‍naṃ akusalamūlapadānaṃ yā ca taṇhā yau ca laubhau yau ca dausau yā ca subhasañ‍ñā yā ca sukhasañ‍ñā, imāni pañ‍ca padāni taṇhaṃ bhajanti| yā ca avij‍jā yau ca mauhau yā ca nic‍casañ‍ñā yā ca attasañ‍ñā, imāni cattāri padāni avij‍jaṃ bhajanti| aitāni nava padāni akusalāni susaṃkhittāni| iti tayau nayā aikaṃ nayaṃ na paviṭṭhā| aivaṃ aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni nandiyāvaṭṭanayai niddisitabbāni|

kathaṃ  aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni, tipukkhalai  nayai yuj‍janti? navan‍naṃ padānaṃ kusalānaṃ, vipassanā ca amauhau ca anic‍casañ‍ñā ca anattasañ‍ñā ca, imāni cattāri padāni; amauhau ca samathau ca alaubhau ca asubhasañ‍ñā ca, imāni cattāri padāni; laubhau ca dausau ca, aivaṃ imāni nava padāni tīsu kusalaisu yaujaitabbāni| tattha navan‍naṃ padānaṃ akusalānaṃ taṇhā ca laubhau ca subhasañ‍ñā ca sukhasañ‍ñā ca, imāni cattāri padāni laubhau akusalamūlaṃ; avij‍jā ca mauhau ca nic‍casañ‍ñā ca attasañ‍ñā ca ayaṃ mauhau ayaṃ dausau, yai ca imāni nava padāni tīsu akusalaisu yaujitāni| aivaṃ aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni kusalamūlaisu ca yaujaitvā tipukkhalaina nayaina niddisitabbāni|

kathaṃ aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni sīhavik‍kīḷitai nayai yuj‍janti? taṇhā ca subhasañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ paṭhamau vipal‍lāsau| laubhau ca sukhasañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ dutiyau vipal‍lāsau| avij‍jā ca nic‍casañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ tatiyau vipal‍lāsau| mauhau ca attasañ‍ñā ca, ayaṃ catutthau vipal‍lāsau| iti nava padāni akusalamūlāni catūsu padaisu yaujitāni| tattha navan‍naṃ mūlapadānaṃ kusalānaṃ samathau ca asubhasañ‍ñā ca, idaṃ paṭhamaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ| alaubhau ca dukkhasañ‍ñā ca, idaṃ dutiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ| vipassanā ca anic‍casañ‍ñā ca, idaṃ tatiyaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ| amauhau ca anattasañ‍ñā ca, idaṃ catutthaṃ satipaṭṭhānaṃ| imāni aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni sīhavik‍kīḷitanayaṃ anupaviṭṭhāni| imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ nayānaṃ yā bhūmi ca yau rāgau ca yau dausau ca aikaṃ nayaṃ pavisati| aikassa nayassa akusalai vā dhammai kusalai vā dhammai viñ‍ñātai paṭipakkhau anvaisitabbau, paṭipakkhai anvaisitvā sau nayau niddisitabbau, tamhi nayai  niddiṭṭhau| yathā aikamhi nayai sabbai nayā paviṭṭhā tathā niddisitabbā| aikamhi ca nayai aṭṭhārasa mūlapadāni paviṭṭhāni, tamhi dhammai viñ‍ñātai sabbai dhammā viñ‍ñātā haunti| imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ nayānaṃ sīhavik‍kīḷitanayassa cattāri phalāni pariyausānaṃ| paṭhamāya disāya paṭhamaṃ phalaṃ, dutiyāya disāya dutiyaṃ phalaṃ, tatiyāya disāya tatiyaṃ phalaṃ, catutthāya disāya catutthaṃ phalaṃ| tipukkhalassa nayassa tīṇi vimaukkhamukhāni pariyausānaṃ| paṭhamāya disāya appaṇihitaṃ, dutiyāya disāya suñ‍ñataṃ, tatiyāya disāya animittaṃ| nandiyāvaṭṭassa nayassa rāgavirāgā caitauvimutti avij‍jāvirāgā ca pañ‍ñāvimutti pariyausānaṃ| paṭhamāya disāya rāgavirāgā  caitauvimutti, dutiyāya disāya avij‍jāvirāgā pañ‍ñāvimutti| imai tayau nayā imaisaṃ tiṇṇaṃ nayānaṃ aṭṭhārasan‍naṃ mūlapadānaṃ ālaukanā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati disālaukanau disālaucanau (ka॰) nayau| ālaukaitvāna jānāti ‘‘ayaṃ dhammau imaṃ dhammaṃ bhajatī’’ti sammā yaujanā| kusalapakkhai akusalapakkhai ca ayaṃ nayau aṅkusau nāma| imai pañ‍ca nayā|

tatthimā uddānagāthā

taṇhā ca avij‍jāpi ca, laubhau dausau tathaiva mauhau ca|

cattārau ca vipal‍lāsā, kilaisabhūmī nava padāni||

yai  ca satipaṭṭhānā, samathau ca vipassanā kusalamūlā|

aitaṃ sabbaṃ kusalaṃ, indriyabhūmī nava padāni||

sabbakusalaṃ navahi padaihi yuj‍jati, navahi caiva akusalaṃ|

aitai tai mūlapadā, ubhatau aṭṭhārasa padāni||

taṇhā caiva avij‍jā ca, samathau ca vipassanā|

yau naiti sabbaisu yaugayuttau, ayaṃ nayau nandiyāvaṭṭau||

yaṃ kusalamūlaihi, nayati kusalaakusalamūlaihi|

bhūtaṃ tathaṃ avitathaṃ, tipukkhalaṃ taṃ nayaṃ āhu||

sau naiti vipal‍lāsaihi, kilaisaindriyaihi ca|

dhammai taṃ nayaṃ vinayamāhu, sīhavik‍kīḷitaṃ nāma||

vaiyyākaraṇai vuttai, kusalatāhi akusalatāhi ca|

tayau ālaukayati, ayaṃ nayau disālaucanau nāma||

aulaukaitvā disālaucanaina, ukkhipiya yaṃ samānaiti|

sabbai kusalākusalai, ayaṃ nayau aṅkusau nāma||

nayasamuṭṭhānaṃ|

paiṭakaupadaisai  mahākac‍cāyanassa thairassa suttavibhaṅgassa


vaibhaṅgissa (pī॰ ka॰) dassanaṃ samattaṃ|

yāni  catuk‍kāni akusalāni kusalāni ca sīhavik‍kīḷitai nayai niddiṭṭhāni, tikāni kusalāni ca akusalāni ca tipukkhalai nayai niddiṭṭhāni, dukāni kusalāni ca akusalāni ca nandiyāvaṭṭai nayai niddiṭṭhāni| yaisu  dvīsu dhammaisu visuddhīsu (ka॰) kusalaisu sau atthau tikaisu vibhaj‍jamānassa bhavabhūmi, atha ca sabbau pubbau (ka॰) ca atthau tīhi byañ‍janaihi niddisati| tattakāni vuc‍cati| yau atthau catūhi padaihi aṭṭhavīsatibhāgaihi natthibhūmi niddisituṃ, avacarantauva catūhi padaihi niddisati| iti yaṃ yathāniddiṭṭhassa avikausanā idaṃ pamāṇaṃ| yathā sabbai samādhayau tīsu samādhīsu pariyaisitabbā, savitak‍kasavicārai avitak‍kavicāramattai avitak‍kaavicārai idaṃ pamāṇaṃ, natthi catutthau samādhi| tathā tissau pañ‍ñā cintāmayī sutamayī bhāvanāmayī sabbāsu pañ‍ñāsu niddisati, natthi catutthī pañ‍ñā na cintāmayī na sutamayī na bhāvanāmayī, pañ‍ñā nāssa atthi imaisaṃ dhammānaṃ yā avikkhaipanā, idaṃ vuc‍cati pamāṇanti|

thairassa mahākac‍cāyanassa jambuvanavāsinau paiṭakaupadaisau

samattau|

paiṭakaupadaisapakaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|
